--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\app\controller\game\PlayScreenController.java ---
package app.controller.game;
import app.enums.plant.PlantName;
import app.model.App;
import app.view.game.PlayScreen;
import app.view.hud.NormalHud;
import com.badlogic.gdx.InputAdapter;
import com.badlogic.gdx.InputProcessor;
import com.badlogic.gdx.math.Vector3;
import game.engine.RealTimeGameEngine;
import game.service.plant.PlantPlacementResult;
import game.service.plant.PlantPlacementService;
import game.world.GameWorld;
import game.world.playground.Cell;
public class PlayScreenController
    extends GameController
    implements WorldListener {
    private final PlayScreen screen;
    private final GameWorld world;
    private final RealTimeGameEngine engine;
    private final NormalHud hud;
    private final PlantPlacementService placementService;
    private PlantName selectedPlantName;
    private Cell hoveredCell;
    private boolean plantingMode;
    public PlayScreenController(PlayScreen screen) {
        this.screen = screen;
        this.engine = screen.getEngine();
        this.world = engine.getWorld();
        this.hud = screen.getHud();
        this.placementService =
            new PlantPlacementService(world, engine);
        hud.setForwardListener(this);
    }
    public InputProcessor createGameInputProcessor() {
        return new InputAdapter() {
            @Override
            public boolean touchDown(
                int screenX,
                int screenY,
                int pointer,
                int button
            ) {
                Vector3 position =
                    screenToWorld(screenX, screenY);
                return handleWorldClick(
                    position.x,
                    position.y
                );
            }
            @Override
            public boolean mouseMoved(
                int screenX,
                int screenY
            ) {
                updateHoveredCell(screenX, screenY);
                return false;
            }
            @Override
            public boolean touchDragged(
                int screenX,
                int screenY,
                int pointer
            ) {
                updateHoveredCell(screenX, screenY);
                return false;
            }
        };
    }
    private void updateHoveredCell(
        int screenX,
        int screenY
    ) {
        Vector3 position =
            screenToWorld(screenX, screenY);
        hoveredCell =
            world.getMap()
                .getGrid()
                .getCellAt(position.x, position.y);
    }
    private Vector3 screenToWorld(
        int screenX,
        int screenY
    ) {
        Vector3 position =
            new Vector3(screenX, screenY, 0);
        screen.getViewport().unproject(position);
        return position;
    }
    public boolean handleWorldClick(
        float worldX,
        float worldY
    ) {
        if (plantingMode && selectedPlantName != null) {
            return handlePlantPlacement(
                worldX,
                worldY
            );
        }
        return tryCollectSun(worldX, worldY);
    }
    private boolean handlePlantPlacement(
        float worldX,
        float worldY
    ) {
        Cell targetCell =
            world.getMap()
                .getGrid()
                .getCellAt(worldX, worldY);
        if (targetCell == null) {
            hud.showPlantPlacementMessage(
                PlantPlacementResult.INVALID_CELL.getMessage()
            );
            cancelPlanting();
            return false;
        }
        PlantPlacementResult result =
            placementService.placePlant(
                selectedPlantName,
                targetCell
            );
        if (result.isSuccess()) {
            cancelPlanting();
            return true;
        }
        hud.showPlantPlacementMessage(
            result.getMessage()
        );
        cancelPlanting();
        return false;
    }
    public boolean canPlantOnCell(Cell cell) {
        if (!plantingMode || selectedPlantName == null) {
            return false;
        }
        return placementService
            .getValidator()
            .canPlant(
                selectedPlantName,
                cell
            );
    }
    private boolean tryCollectSun(
        float worldX,
        float worldY
    ) {
        return false;
    }
    @Override
    public void onPlantCardSelected(
        PlantName plantName
    ) {
        if (plantName == null) {
            return;
        }
        selectedPlantName = plantName;
        plantingMode = true;
    }
    @Override
    public void onPlantCardDeselected(
        PlantName plantName
    ) {
        if (selectedPlantName == plantName) {
            cancelPlanting();
        }
    }
    public void cancelPlanting() {
        selectedPlantName = null;
        plantingMode = false;
        hoveredCell = null;
        hud.deselectAllCards();
    }
    public void update(float delta) {
        hud.update(delta);
        hud.updateSunDisplay(
            world.getSunAmount()
        );
        ChapterData.updateWaveDisplay(
            hud,
            world
        );
        updateProgressBar();
    }
    private void updateProgressBar() {
        int currentWave =
            world.getCurrentWaveNumber();
        int totalWaves =
            App.getPendingGameSession()
                .getSelectedChapter()
                .getTotalWaves();
        if (totalWaves <= 0) {
            return;
        }
        float baseProgress =
            (float) (currentWave - 1) / totalWaves;
        float totalProgress =
            baseProgress +
                getCurrentWaveProgress() / totalWaves;
        hud.updateProgressBar(
            Math.min(totalProgress, 1f)
        );
    }
    private float getCurrentWaveProgress() {
        int totalZombies =
            world.getCurrentWave()
                .getZombies()
                .size();
        if (totalZombies <= 0) {
            return 0f;
        }
        int zombiesKilled =
            totalZombies -
                world.getZombies().size();
        return Math.max(
            0f,
            Math.min(
                1f,
                (float) zombiesKilled / totalZombies
            )
        );
    }
    public boolean isPlantingMode() {
        return plantingMode;
    }
    public PlantName getSelectedPlantName() {
        return selectedPlantName;
    }
    public Cell getHoveredCell() {
        return hoveredCell;
    }
    private static class ChapterData {
        private static void updateWaveDisplay(
            NormalHud hud,
            GameWorld world
        ) {
            if (App.getPendingGameSession() == null ||
                App.getPendingGameSession().getSelectedChapter() == null) {
                return;
            }
            hud.updateWaveDisplay(
                world.getCurrentWaveNumber(),
                App.getPendingGameSession()
                    .getSelectedChapter()
                    .getTotalWaves()
            );
        }
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\app\controller\greenhouse\GreenhouseController.java ---
package app.controller.greenhouse;
import app.enums.greenhouse.PotStatus;
import app.model.App;
import app.model.greenhouse.Greenhouse;
import app.model.greenhouse.GreenhousePlant;
import app.model.greenhouse.GreenhousePot;
import app.model.user.User;
import app.service.greenhouse.GreenhouseService;
import common.Result;
public class GreenhouseController
{
    private final static Greenhouse greenhouse = App.getCurrentUser().getGreenhouse();
    private final static GreenhouseService greenhouseService= new GreenhouseService(greenhouse);
    public static Result showGreenhouse()
    {
        return new Result(true , greenhouseService.showGreenhouse() );
    }
    public static Result plantPot(String X_coordinates , String Y_coordinates)
    {
        X_coordinates = X_coordinates.trim();
        Y_coordinates = Y_coordinates.trim();
        if (X_coordinates.isEmpty() || Y_coordinates.isEmpty())
            return new Result(false , "The coordinate value is empty.");
        int x , y;
        try {
            x = Integer.parseInt(X_coordinates);
            y = Integer.parseInt(Y_coordinates);
        } catch (NumberFormatException n)
        {
            return new Result(false , "The coordinate values must be Integer.");
        }
        if (x > greenhouse.getCol() || x < 1)
            return new Result(false , "The value of x must be from 1 to " + greenhouse.getCol() + ".");
        if (y > greenhouse.getRow() || y < 1)
            return new Result(true , "The value of x must be from 1 to " + greenhouse.getCol() + ".");
        GreenhousePot pot = greenhouse.getPot(x , y);
        if(!pot.isEmpty()) return new Result( false,"The pot is not suitable for planting. " +
                "Check the greenhouse conditions for more information.");
        if (greenhouseService.plant(x , y))
        {
            return new Result(true , pot.getPlant().getPlantName() + " was successfully planted " +
                    "in the pot at position ("+ x +", "+ y + "). " +
                    "The remaining time to grow is " + pot.getPlant().getRemainingTimeText());
        }
        return new Result(false , "Error. Please try again.");
    }
    public static Result unlockPot(String X_coordinates , String Y_coordinates)
    {
        X_coordinates = X_coordinates.trim();
        Y_coordinates = Y_coordinates.trim();
        if (X_coordinates.isEmpty() || Y_coordinates.isEmpty())
            return new Result(false , "The coordinate value is empty.");
        int x , y;
        try {
            x = Integer.parseInt(X_coordinates);
            y = Integer.parseInt(Y_coordinates);
        } catch (NumberFormatException n)
        {
            return new Result(false , "The coordinate values must be Integer.");
        }
        if (x > greenhouse.getCol() || x < 1)
            return new Result(false , "The value of x must be from 1 to " + greenhouse.getCol() + ".");
        if (y > greenhouse.getRow() || y < 1)
            return new Result(true , "The value of x must be from 1 to " + greenhouse.getCol() + ".");
        GreenhousePot pot = greenhouse.getPot(x , y);
        if(pot.isUnlocked()) return new Result( false,"The selected pot is already unlocked.");
        int cost = pot.getCost();
        if (App.getCurrentUser().getCoins() < cost)
            return new Result(false , "Your coins are not enough to unlock the pot.");
        if (greenhouseService.unlockPot(x , y))
        {
            return new Result(true , "The pot was successfully planted " +
                    "at position ("+ x +", "+ y + "). ");
        }
        return new Result(false , "Error. Please try again.");
    }
    public static Result collect(String X_coordinates , String Y_coordinates)
    {
        X_coordinates = X_coordinates.trim();
        Y_coordinates = Y_coordinates.trim();
        if (X_coordinates.isEmpty() || Y_coordinates.isEmpty())
            return new Result(false , "The coordinate value is empty.");
        int x , y;
        try {
            x = Integer.parseInt(X_coordinates);
            y = Integer.parseInt(Y_coordinates);
        } catch (NumberFormatException n)
        {
            return new Result(false , "The coordinate values must be Integer.");
        }
        if (x > greenhouse.getCol() || x < 1)
            return new Result(false , "The value of x must be from 1 to " + greenhouse.getCol() + ".");
        if (y > greenhouse.getRow() || y < 1)
            return new Result(true , "The value of x must be from 1 to " + greenhouse.getCol() + ".");
        GreenhousePot pot = greenhouse.getPot(x , y);
        if(pot.isEmpty()) return new Result( false,"No plants were found in this pot.");
        if(!pot.isUnlocked()) return new Result( false,"The pot is locked.");
        if (pot.getPotStatus() == PotStatus.GROWING)
            return new Result( false,"The remaining time to grow is "
                    + pot.getPlant().getRemainingTimeText());
        if (greenhouseService.collect(x , y))
        {
            return new Result(true , "The collection was successful.");
        }
        return new Result(false , "Error. Please try again.");
    }
    public static Result grow(String X_coordinates , String Y_coordinates)
    {
        X_coordinates = X_coordinates.trim();
        Y_coordinates = Y_coordinates.trim();
        if (X_coordinates.isEmpty() || Y_coordinates.isEmpty())
            return new Result(false , "The coordinate value is empty.");
        int x , y;
        try {
            x = Integer.parseInt(X_coordinates);
            y = Integer.parseInt(Y_coordinates);
        } catch (NumberFormatException n)
        {
            return new Result(false , "The coordinate values must be Integer.");
        }
        if (x > greenhouse.getCol() || x < 1)
            return new Result(false , "The value of x must be from 1 to " + greenhouse.getCol() + ".");
        if (y > greenhouse.getRow() || y < 1)
            return new Result(true , "The value of x must be from 1 to " + greenhouse.getCol() + ".");
        GreenhousePot pot = greenhouse.getPot(x , y);
        if(pot.isEmpty()) return new Result( false,"No plants were found in this pot.");
        if(!pot.isUnlocked()) return new Result( false,"The pot is locked.");
        if (pot.getPotStatus() == PotStatus.READY)
            return new Result( false,"The plant is ready to collect.");
        GreenhousePlant plant = pot.getPlant();
        int diamondCost = greenhouseService.calculateGrowCost(plant);
        User user = App.getCurrentUser();
        if (user.getGems() < diamondCost )
            return new Result(false , "There are not enough diamonds.");
        if (greenhouseService.grow(x , y))
        {
            return new Result(true , "Your plant is now ready.");
        }
        return new Result(false , "Error. Please try again.");
    }
    public static Result grow(String X_coordinates , String Y_coordinates , String hoursAmount)
    {
        X_coordinates = X_coordinates.trim();
        Y_coordinates = Y_coordinates.trim();
        hoursAmount = hoursAmount.trim();
        if (X_coordinates.isEmpty() || Y_coordinates.isEmpty())
            return new Result(false , "The coordinate value is empty.");
        if (hoursAmount.isEmpty())
            return new Result(false , "The hours value is empty.");
        int x , y;
        try {
            x = Integer.parseInt(X_coordinates);
            y = Integer.parseInt(Y_coordinates);
        } catch (NumberFormatException n)
        {
            return new Result(false , "The coordinate values must be Integer.");
        }
        int hours;
        try {
            hours = Integer.parseInt(hoursAmount);
        } catch (NumberFormatException n)
        {
            return new Result(false , "The hours values must be Integer.");
        }
        if (x > greenhouse.getCol() || x < 1)
            return new Result(false , "The value of x must be from 1 to " + greenhouse.getCol() + ".");
        if (y > greenhouse.getRow() || y < 1)
            return new Result(true , "The value of x must be from 1 to " + greenhouse.getCol() + ".");
        GreenhousePot pot = greenhouse.getPot(x , y);
        if(pot.isEmpty()) return new Result( false,"No plants were found in this pot.");
        if(!pot.isUnlocked()) return new Result( false,"The pot is locked.");
        if (pot.getPotStatus() == PotStatus.READY)
            return new Result( false,"The plant is ready to collect.");
        GreenhousePlant plant = pot.getPlant();
        int diamondCost = greenhouseService.calculateGrowCost(plant , hours);
        User user = App.getCurrentUser();
        if (user.getGems() < diamondCost )
            return new Result(false , "There are not enough diamonds.");
        if (greenhouseService.grow(x , y))
        {
            return new Result(true , "Your plant is now ready.");
        }
        return new Result(false , "Error. Please try again.");
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\app\controller\menu\CollectionMenuController.java ---
package app.controller.menu;
import app.enums.plant.PlantName;
import app.model.App;
import app.model.Collection;
import app.model.user.User;
import app.service.plantselection.PlantSelectionService;
import common.Result;
import game.entity.plant.Plant;
import game.entity.plant.PlantDefinition;
import game.entity.plant.PlantLevel;
import game.entity.registry.PlantRegistry;
import game.entity.registry.ZombieRegistry;
import game.entity.zombie.Zombie;
import game.entity.zombie.data.properties.Property;
public class CollectionMenuController {
    private Collection getCollection() {
        return App.getCurrentProgress().getCollection();
    }
    public Result showPlants() {
        StringBuilder output = new StringBuilder();
        for (PlantName plant : App.getCurrentUser().getUnlockedPlants()) {
            PlantDefinition definition = PlantRegistry.get(plant);
            output.append(definition.printInfo());
            output.append("\n");
        }
        return new Result(true, output.toString());
    }
    public Result showAllPlants() {
        StringBuilder output = new StringBuilder();
        PlantSelectionService service = new PlantSelectionService();
        return new Result(true, service.showAllPlants());
    }
    public Result showZombies() {
        StringBuilder output = new StringBuilder();
        for (Zombie zombie : getCollection().getSeenZombies()) {
            output.append(zombie.getName());
            output.append("\n");
        }
        return new Result(true, output.toString());
    }
    public Result showAllZombies() {
        StringBuilder output = new StringBuilder();
        for (Property zombie : ZombieRegistry.getAllZombies()) {
            output.append(zombie.printInfo());
            output.append("\n");
        }
        return new Result(true, output.toString());
    }
    public Result showPlant(String plantName) {
        game.entity.plant.PlantDefinition definition = PlantRegistry.get(PlantName.parsePlantName(plantName));
        if (definition == null)
            return new Result(false, "Plant not found.");
        return new Result(true, definition.printInfo());
    }
    public Result showZombie(String zombieName) {
        game.entity.zombie.data.properties.Property property = ZombieRegistry.getZombie(zombieName);
        if (property == null)
            return new Result(false, "Zombie not found.");
        return new Result(true, property.printInfo());
    }
    public Result upgradePlant(String plantName) {
        PlantName name = PlantName.parsePlantName(plantName);
        if (name == null)
            return new Result(false, "Plant not found.");
        User user = App.getCurrentUser();
        PlantLevel level = user.getPlantLevelMap().get(name);
        if (!level.canUpgrade())
            return new Result(false, "Upgrade not allowed.");
        level.levelUp();
        return new Result(true, "Plant upgrade successfully.");
    }
    public Result purchasePlant(String plantName) {
        PlantName name = PlantName.parsePlantName(plantName);
        if (name == null)
            return new Result(false, "Plant not found");
        User user = App.getCurrentUser();
        if (user.isUnlockPlant(name))
            return new Result(false, "You already have this plant");
        if (App.getCurrentUser().getCoins() < 2000)
            return new Result(false, "Not enough coins");
        user.unlockPlant(name);
        App.getCurrentUser().deductCoins(2000);
        return new Result(true, "Plant purchased");
    }
    private String showPlantStats(Plant plant) {
        return plant.getDefinition().printInfo();
    }
    private String showZombieStats(Zombie zombie) {
        return zombie.printInfo();
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\app\controller\menu\GameMenuController.java ---
package app.controller.menu;
import app.enums.Menu;
import app.enums.plant.PlantName;
import app.model.App;
import common.Result;
import game.world.chapter.Chapter;
import game.world.chapter.ChapterRegistry;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;
public class GameMenuController {
    public Result manageEnteringChapter(String chapterName){
        Chapter chapter = ChapterRegistry.getChapterByName(chapterName);
        if (chapter == null) {
            return new Result(false, "Chapter " + chapterName + " does not exist.");
        }
        if (chapter.isLocked(App.getCurrentUser())) {
            return new Result(false, "This chapter is currently locked.");
        }
        List<PlantName> newChapter = new ArrayList<>();
        newChapter = chapter.getAvailablePlants();
        for (PlantName name: newChapter)
        {
            App.getCurrentUser().unlockPlant(name);
        }
        App.getPendingGameSession().clear();
        App.getPendingGameSession().setSelectedChapter(chapter);
        MenuNavigator.goTo(Menu.PlantSelectionMenu);
        return new Result(true, "Entering selection for " + chapterName);
    }
    public Result manageCheatCode(String amount, String currency) {
        int amountNumber = 0;
        try {
            amountNumber = Integer.parseInt(amount);
        } catch (NumberFormatException e) {
            return new Result(false, "invalid amount");
        }
        switch (currency) {
            case "coin":
                App.getCurrentUser().addCoins(amountNumber);
                break;
            case "diamond":
                App.getCurrentUser().addGems(amountNumber);
                break;
            default:
                return new Result(false, "invalid currency");
        }
        return new Result(true, "cheat code applied");
    }
    public int getCoins() {
        return App.getCurrentUser().getCoins();
    }
    public int getGems() {
        return App.getCurrentUser().getGems();
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\app\controller\menu\LeaderBoardMenuController.java ---
package app.controller.menu;
import app.enums.SortType;
import app.model.App;
import app.model.user.User;
import common.Result;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;
public class LeaderBoardMenuController {
    public Result manageSort(String type) {
        type = type.trim();
        SortType sortType;
        try {
            sortType = SortType.valueOf(type.toUpperCase());
        }
        catch (Exception e) {
            return new Result(false, "Invalid sort type.");
        }
        List<User> sortedUsers = new ArrayList<>(App.getAllUsers());
        sortedUsers.sort((user1,user2) -> {
            switch (sortType) {
                case GEM ->         {return Integer.compare(user2.getGems(), user1.getGems());}
                case COIN ->        {return Integer.compare(user2.getCoins(), user1.getCoins());}
                case GAMESPLAYED -> {return Integer.compare(user2.getGamesPlayed(), user1.getGamesPlayed());}
                case MEOWPOINT ->   {return Integer.compare(user2.getMioPoint(), user1.getMioPoint());}
                default -> {return 0;}
            }
        });
        String output = giveOutputString(sortedUsers, sortType);
        return new Result(true, output);
    }
    private String giveOutputString(List<User> sortedUsers, SortType sortType) {
        StringBuilder output = new StringBuilder();
        for(int i = 0; i<sortedUsers.size(); i++) {
            User user = sortedUsers.get(i);
            int value = 0;
            switch (sortType){
                case GAMESPLAYED -> value = user.getGamesPlayed();
                case GEM -> value = user.getGems();
                case COIN -> value = user.getCoins();
                case MEOWPOINT -> value = user.getMioPoint();
            }
            output.append(i+1).append(". ").append(user.getUsername());
            output.append(" : ").append(value).append("\n");
        }
        return output.toString();
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\app\controller\menu\LoginMenuController.java ---
package app.controller.menu;
import app.enums.SecurityQuestions;
import app.model.user.User;
import app.service.AppService;
import app.validator.AppValidator;
import common.Result;
import common.utils.HashUtil;
public class LoginMenuController {
    public Result manageLogin
            (String username,
             String password,
             boolean saveMe)
    {
        username = username.trim();
        password = password.trim();
        User user = AppService.getUserByUsername(username);
        password = HashUtil.hash(password);
        if(user == null) {
            return new Result(false, "Username doesn't exist.");
        }
        else if(!user.getPassword().equals(password)) {
            return new Result(false, "Password is not correct.");
        }
        AppService.loginUser(user, saveMe);
        return new Result(true, "Welcome " + username);
    }
    public Result manageForgotPassword
            (String username,
             String email)
    {
        username = username.trim();
        email = email.trim();
        User user = AppService.getUserByUsername(username);
        if(user == null) {
            return new Result(false, "Username doesn't exist.");
        }
        else if(!user.getEmail().equals(email)) {
            return new Result(false, "Incorrect email.");
        }
        int questionNumber = user.getSecurityQuestionNumber();
        String securityQuestion = SecurityQuestions.getQuestionByNumber(questionNumber).getQuestion();
        return new Result(true, "Please answer security question :\n" + securityQuestion);
    }
    public Result checkSecurityAnswer(String username, String answer) {
        User user = AppService.getUserByUsername(username);
        if (user.getSecurityQuestionAnswer().equals(answer)) {
            return new Result(true, "Answer is correct. Please enter your new password:");
        }
        return new Result(false, "Incorrect answer. Returning to menu.");
    }
    public Result updatePassword(String username, String newPassword) {
        User user = AppService.getUserByUsername(username);
        if(!AppValidator.isPasswordCorrect(newPassword)) {
            return new Result(false, "Invalid password format, returning back to menu.");
        }
        String hashedPass = HashUtil.hash(newPassword);
        user.setPassword(hashedPass);
        AppService.updateAllUsers();
        return new Result(true, "Password changed successfully.");
    }
}

package app.controller.menu;

public class MenuController {
    private static final Map<Menu, AppMenu> menus = new HashMap<>();
    static {
        menus.put(Menu.SignupMenu, new SignupMenu());
        menus.put(Menu.LoginMenu, new LoginMenu());
        menus.put(Menu.MainMenu, new MainMenu());
        menus.put(Menu.GameMenu, new GameMenu());

        menus.put(Menu.IZombieMenu, new IZombieScreen());
        menus.put(Menu.ConveyorMenu, new ConveyorBeltScreen());
        menus.put(Menu.TravelLogMenu, new TravelLogMenu());
        menus.put(Menu.Shop, new ShopMenu());
    }
    public static AppMenu getMenu(Menu menu) {
        return menus.get(menu);
    }
    public static Result menuEnter(Matcher matcher)
    {
        String menuName = matcher.group("menuName");
        Menu menu = Menu.parseMenu(menuName.trim());
        if (menuName.trim().isEmpty() || menu == null)
        {
            System.out.println();
            return new Result(false , "Please enter a valid menu name.");
        }
        return MenuNavigator.goTo(menu);
    }
    public static Result showMenu()
    {

    }
    public static Result menuExit(Matcher matcher)

        try{App.getCurrentUser().getQuestManager().saveQuests();} catch (Exception e){}
        switch (App.getCurrentMenu())

            }
            case TravelLogMenu -> {
                App.setCurrentMenu(Menu.MainMenu);
                return new Result(true , "Current menu: " + App.getCurrentMenu().toString());
            }
            case NewsMenu -> {
                App.setCurrentMenu(Menu.MainMenu);
                return new Result(true , "Current menu: " + App.getCurrentMenu().toString());
            }
            case ProfileMenu -> {
                App.setCurrentMenu(Menu.MainMenu);
                return new Result(true , "Current menu: " + App.getCurrentMenu().toString());
            }
            case CollectionMenu -> {

                return new Result(true , "Current menu: " + App.getCurrentMenu().toString());
            }
            case Shop -> {

            }
            case ConveyorMenu -> {
                App.setCurrentMenu(Menu.GameMenu);
                return new Result(true , "Current menu: " + App.getCurrentMenu().toString());
            }

                App.setCurrentMenu(Menu.SignupMenu);

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\app\controller\menu\MenuNavigator.java ---
package app.controller.menu;
import app.enums.Menu;
import app.model.App;
import common.Result;
public class MenuNavigator {
    public static Result goTo(Menu menu) {
        if (possibilityToChangeMenu(menu , App.getCurrentMenu()))
        {
            App.setCurrentMenu(menu);
            return new Result(true , "Current Menu :" + App.getCurrentMenu());
        }
        String massage = "It is not possible to change the menu.\n" +
                "\n" +
                "The user will be able to go to specific menus in any menu:\n" +
                "Registration menu: Login menu.\n" +
                "Login menu: Only by logging in to the account will he be able to go to the main menu.\n" +
                "Main menu: Game menu, Options menu, Network menu, News menu, Profile menu\n" +
                "Game menu: Collection menu";
        return new Result(false , massage);
    }
    private static boolean possibilityToChangeMenu(Menu menu , Menu currentMnue) {
        if (menu == null) return false;
        switch (currentMnue) {
            case SignupMenu: {
                return menu == Menu.LoginMenu;
            }
            case LoginMenu: {
                return false;
            }
            case MainMenu: {
                return menu == Menu.GameMenu ||
                        menu == Menu.SettingsMenu ||
                        menu == Menu.NetworkMenu ||
                        menu == Menu.NewsMenu ||
                        menu == Menu.ProfileMenu;
            }
            case GameMenu: {
                return menu == Menu.CollectionMenu ||
                        menu == Menu.GreenhousMenu ||
                        menu == Menu.TravelLogMenu ||
                        menu == Menu.LeaderBoardMenu ||
                        menu == Menu.PlantSelectionMenu;
            }
            case GreenhousMenu:
            {
                return menu == Menu.Shop;
            }
            case PlantSelectionMenu:
            {
                return menu == Menu.PlayMenu ||
                        menu == Menu.VaseBreakerMenu ||
                        menu == Menu.IZombieMenu ||
                        menu == Menu.ConveyorMenu ||
                        menu == Menu.GameMenu;
            }
            case PlayMenu, Shop, ConveyorMenu, IZombieMenu, VaseBreakerMenu: {
                return menu == Menu.GameMenu;
            }
            case TravelLogMenu: {
                return menu == Menu.PlantSelectionMenu;
            }
            default:
                return false;
        }
    }
    public static Result successfulLogin(boolean loginResult)
    {
        if (App.getCurrentMenu() != Menu.LoginMenu) return new Result(false ,
                "ERROR: This command is executed at login time.");
        App.setCurrentMenu(Menu.MainMenu);
        return new Result(true , "Current Menu :" + App.getCurrentMenu());
    }
    public static Result logout()
    {
        if (App.getCurrentMenu() != Menu.LoginMenu) return new Result(false ,
                "ERROR: To log out of your account, go to the main menu.");
        App.setCurrentMenu(Menu.SignupMenu);
        return new Result(true , "Current Menu :" + App.getCurrentMenu());
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\app\controller\menu\NewsMenuController.java ---
package app.controller.menu;
import app.model.App;
import app.model.News;
import common.Result;
import java.util.List;
public class NewsMenuController {
    public static Result manageShowUnreadNews(){return null;}
    public static Result manageShowAllNews(){return null;}
    public Result showUnreadNews() {
        StringBuilder output = new StringBuilder();
        List<News> newsList = App.getCurrentUser().getNewsList();
        boolean isThereUnreadNews = false;
        for (News news : newsList) {
            if (!news.isHaveRead()) {
                output.append(news.getMessage()).append("\n");
                news.setHaveRead(true);
                isThereUnreadNews = true;
            }
        }
        if (!isThereUnreadNews)
            return new Result(false, "There are no unread news\n");
        return new Result(true, output.toString());
    }
    public Result showAllNews() {
        StringBuilder output = new StringBuilder();
        List<News> newsList = App.getCurrentUser().getNewsList();
        if (newsList.isEmpty())
            return new Result(false, "There are no news\n");
        for (News news : newsList) {
            output.append(news.getMessage()).append("\n");
            if (!news.isHaveRead()) {
                news.setHaveRead(true);
            }
        }
        return new Result(true, output.toString());
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\app\controller\menu\PlantSelectionController.java ---
package app.controller.menu;
import app.enums.Menu;
import app.enums.plant.PlantName;
import app.model.App;
import app.model.game.PendingGameSession;
import app.service.plantselection.PlantSelectionService;
import app.view.game.PlayScreen;
import com.badlogic.gdx.Gdx;
import common.Result;
import game.engine.GameSimulationEngine;
import game.world.GameWorld;
import game.world.chapter.Chapter;
import game.world.chapter.ChapterWorldBuilder;
import game.world.minigame.izombie.IZombieMode;
import game.world.minigame.vasebreaker.VaseBreakerMode;
import plants_vs_zombies.PlantsVSZombies;
public class PlantSelectionController {
    private static final PlantSelectionService plantSelectionService = new PlantSelectionService();
    public static Result showAllPlants() {
        return new Result(true, plantSelectionService.showAllPlants());
    }
    public static Result showAvailablePlants() {
        return new Result(true,  plantSelectionService.showAvailablePlants());
    }
    public static Result addPlant(String plantName) {
        if (plantName == null || plantName.trim().isEmpty()) {
            return new Result(false, "Plant type cannot be empty.");
        }
        PlantName name = PlantName.parsePlantName(plantName);
        if (!plantSelectionService.isPlantInRegistry(name)) return new Result(false, "Unknown plant.");
        Chapter currentChapter = App.getPendingGameSession().getSelectedChapter();
        App.getPendingGameSession().addPlant(name);
        return new Result(true, plantName + " added.");
    }
    public static Result removePlant(String plantName) {
        if (plantName == null || plantName.trim().isEmpty()) {
            return new Result(false, "Plant type cannot be empty.");
        }
        PlantName name = PlantName.parsePlantName(plantName);
        if (!plantSelectionService.isPlantInRegistry(name)) return new Result(false, "Unknown plant.");
        if (!plantSelectionService.isPlantAlreadySelected(name))
            return new Result(false ,"Such a plant is not added to your current list.");
        App.getPendingGameSession().getSelectedPlants().remove(name);
        return new Result(true, "Plant " + plantName.trim() + " removed from selection.");
    }
    public static Result boostPlant(String plantName) {
        if (plantName == null || plantName.trim().isEmpty()) {
            return new Result(false, "Plant type cannot be empty.");
        }
        PlantName name = PlantName.parsePlantName(plantName);
        if (!plantSelectionService.isPlantInRegistry(name)) return new Result(false, "Unknown plant.");
        if (!plantSelectionService.isPlantAlreadySelected(name))
            return new Result(false ,"This plant has not been selected.");
        int gems = App.getCurrentUser().getGems();
        if(gems < 2) {
            return new Result(false, "You don't have enough diamonds");
        }
        App.getCurrentUser().deductGems(2);
        return new Result(true, "Plant " + plantName.trim() + " boosted. (2 diamonds spent)");
    }
    public static Result startGame() {
        PendingGameSession session = App.getPendingGameSession();
        if (session.getSelectedPlants().isEmpty() && session.getSelectedChapter().isNeedsPlantSelection()) {
            return new Result(false, "Select at least one plant.");
        }
        GameWorld world = ChapterWorldBuilder.buildWorld(session.getSelectedChapter());
        world.loadSelectedGamePlants(session.getSelectedPlants());
        GameSimulationEngine engine = new GameSimulationEngine(world);
        App.setCurrentGameEngine(engine);
        if(session.getSelectedChapter().getMinigameLogic() instanceof VaseBreakerMode) {
            MenuNavigator.goTo(Menu.VaseBreakerMenu);
        }
        if(session.getSelectedChapter().getMinigameLogic() instanceof IZombieMode) {
            MenuNavigator.goTo(Menu.IZombieMenu);
        }
        if(world.isConveyorActive()) {
            MenuNavigator.goTo(Menu.ConveyorMenu);
        }
        else {
            Gdx.app.postRunnable(() -> {
                PlayScreen screen = new PlayScreen(App.getPendingGameSession().getSelectedChapter());
                PlantsVSZombies.getPvz().setScreen(screen);
                System.out.println("I set the screen to play screen");
            });
        }
        return new Result(true, "Game started!");
    }
    public static Result manageExit() {
        MenuNavigator.goTo(Menu.GameMenu);
        return new Result(true, "Exited from game successfully");
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\app\controller\menu\ProfileMenuController.java ---
package app.controller.menu;
import app.model.App;
import app.model.user.User;
import app.service.AppService;
import app.validator.AppValidator;
import common.Result;
import common.utils.HashUtil;
public class ProfileMenuController {
    public Result manageChangeUsername(String username) {
        username = username.trim();
        User currentUser = App.getCurrentUser();
        if(currentUser.getUsername().equals(username)) {
            return new Result(false, "You already have this username.");
        }
        else if(!AppValidator.isUsernameCorrect(username)) {
            return new Result(false, "Invalid username format, Please try again.");
        }
        currentUser.setUsername(username);
        AppService.updateAllUsers();
        return new Result(true, "Username changed successfully.");
    }
    public Result manageChangePassword(String newPassword, String oldPassword) {
        newPassword = newPassword.trim();
        oldPassword = oldPassword.trim();
        String hashedOldPass = HashUtil.hash(oldPassword);
        User currentUser = App.getCurrentUser();
        if(!currentUser.getPassword().equals(hashedOldPass)) {
            return new Result(false, "Wrong old password, Please try again.");
        }
        else if(!AppValidator.isPasswordCorrect(newPassword)) {
            return new Result(false, "Invalid new password format, Please try again.");
        }
        else if(!AppValidator.isPasswordStrong(newPassword)) {
            return new Result(false, "Password is not strong, Please try again.");
        }
        String hashNewPass = HashUtil.hash(newPassword);
        currentUser.setPassword(hashNewPass);
        AppService.updateAllUsers();
        return new Result(true, "Password changed successfully.");
    }
    public Result manageChangeEmail(String email) {
        email = email.trim();
        User currentUser = App.getCurrentUser();
        if(currentUser.getEmail().equals(email)) {
            return new Result(false, "You already have this email.");
        }
        else if(AppValidator.isEmailCorrect(email)) {
            return new Result(false, "Invalid email format, Please try again");
        }
        currentUser.setEmail(email);
        AppService.updateAllUsers();
        return new Result(true, "Email changed successfully.");
    }
    public Result manageChangeNickname(String nickname) {
        nickname = nickname.trim();
        User currentUser = App.getCurrentUser();
        if(currentUser.getNickname().equals(nickname)) {
            return new Result(false, "You already have this nickname.");
        }
        else if(!AppValidator.isNicknameCorrect(nickname)) {
            return new Result(false, "Invalid nickname format, Please try again.");
        }
        currentUser.setNickname(nickname);
        AppService.updateAllUsers();
        return new Result(true, "Nickname changed successfully.");
    }
    public Result manageShowingInfo() {
        User user = App.getCurrentUser();
        int chaptersPassed = (user.getGameProgress() != null) ? user.getGameProgress().getNumberOfLevelsCompleted() : 0;
        String output = "-------------------------------------------\n" +
                "username : " + user.getUsername() + "\n" +
                "nickname : " + user.getNickname() + "\n" +
                "games played : " + user.getGamesPlayed() + "\n" +
                "coins : " + user.getCoins() + "$" + "\n" +
                "gems : " + user.getGems() + "\n" +
                "chapters passed : " + chaptersPassed + "\n" +
                "maximum meow point : " + user.getMioPoint() + "\n" +
                "email : " + user.getEmail();
        return new Result(true, output);
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\app\controller\menu\SignupMenuController.java ---
package app.controller.menu;
import app.enums.SecurityQuestions;
import app.model.user.User;
import app.service.AppService;
import app.validator.AppValidator;
import common.Result;
import common.utils.HashUtil;
public class SignupMenuController {
    public Result manageRegister
            (String username,
             String password,
             String passwordConfirm,
             String nickName,
             String email,
             String gender)
    {
        User user = AppService.getUserByUsername(username);
        if(!AppValidator.isUsernameCorrect(username)) {
            return new Result(false, "Invalid username format, Please try again.");
        }
        else if(user != null) {
            return new Result(false, "Suggested username already exist.");
        }
        else if(!AppValidator.isPasswordCorrect(password)) {
            return new Result(false, "Invalid password format, Please try again.");
        }
        else if(!password.equals(passwordConfirm)) {
            return new Result(false, "Password and its confirmation are not equal.\n" +
                                                      "Please enter password again.");
        }
        else if(!AppValidator.isPasswordStrong(password)) {
            return new Result(false, "Password is not strong, Please try again.");
        }
        else if(!AppValidator.isNicknameCorrect(nickName)) {
            return new Result(false, "Invalid nickname format, Please try again.");
        }
        else if(!AppValidator.isEmailCorrect(email)) {
            return new Result(false, "Invalid email format, Please try again.");
        }
        else if(!AppValidator.isGenderValid(gender)) {
            return new Result(false, "Invalid gender format, Please try again.");
        }
        String securityQuestions = createSecurityQuestionsList();
        return new Result(true, securityQuestions);
    }
    private String createSecurityQuestionsList() {
        StringBuilder questions = new StringBuilder();
        questions.append("Please choose and answer a security question :").append("\n");
        for(SecurityQuestions question : SecurityQuestions.values()) {
            questions.append(question.getNumber()).append(". ");
            questions.append(question.getQuestion()).append("\n");
        }
        return questions.toString();
    }
    public Result finalizeRegister
            (String username,
             String password,
             String passwordConfirm,
             String nickName,
             String email,
             String gender,
             String qNumber,
             String answer,
             String answerConfirm) {
        if(!AppValidator.isNumberValid(qNumber)){
            return new Result(false, "Question number must be integer, Please try again");
        }
        int questionNumber = Integer.parseInt(qNumber);
        if(SecurityQuestions.getQuestionByNumber(questionNumber) == null) {
            return new Result(false, "Invalid security question number, Please try again");
        }
        else if(!answer.equals(answerConfirm)) {
            return new Result(false, "Answers do not match, Please try again");
        }
        String hashedPassword = HashUtil.hash(password);
        User user = new User(username, hashedPassword, nickName, email, gender, questionNumber, answer);
        AppService.addUser(user);
        return new Result(true, "Registering finalized!, welcome " + nickName + ".");
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\app\controller\menu\TravelLogMenuController.java ---
package app.controller.menu;
import app.enums.Menu;
import app.model.App;
import common.Result;
import game.entity.quest.Quest;
import game.world.chapter.Chapter;
import game.world.chapter.ChapterRegistry;
import java.util.List;
public class TravelLogMenuController {
    public Result manageEnteringMinigame(String name) {
        name = name.trim().toUpperCase();
        Chapter chapter = ChapterRegistry.getMinigameChapterByName(name);
        if (chapter == null) {
            return new Result(false, "Chapter " + name + " does not exist.");
        }
        App.getPendingGameSession().clear();
        App.getPendingGameSession().setSelectedChapter(chapter);
        MenuNavigator.goTo(Menu.PlantSelectionMenu);
        return new Result(true, "Entering plant selection for " + name);
    }
    public Result manageShowQuests() {
        List<Quest> activeQuests = App.getCurrentUser().getQuestManager().getQuestsByPriority();
        if (activeQuests.isEmpty()) {
            return new Result(true, "📭 No active quests at the moment.");
        }
        StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
        sb.append("🌟 ====== ACTIVE QUESTS ====== 🌟\n\n");
        for (Quest quest : activeQuests) {
            sb.append("╔══════════════════════════════════════════════╗\n");
            sb.append("║ 📜 ").append(quest.getName().name()).append("\n");
            sb.append("╠══════════════════════════════════════════════╣\n");
            sb.append("  ⚠️ Priority: ").append(quest.getPriority()).append("\n");
            sb.append("  📌 Title: ").append(quest.getTitle()).append("\n");
            sb.append("  📝 Desc: ").append(quest.getDescription()).append("\n");
            sb.append("  --------------------------------------------\n");
            int progress = quest.getCondition() != null ? quest.getCondition().getCurrentProgress() : 0;
            sb.append("  📊 Progress: ").append(progress).append("\n");
            sb.append("  📈 Tier Index: ").append(quest.getCurrentTierIndex()).append("\n");
            sb.append("  ✅ Completed: ").append(quest.isCompleted() ? "Yes" : "No").append("\n");
            if (quest.getTargetPlant() != null && !quest.getTargetPlant().isEmpty()) {
                sb.append("  🌿 Target Plant: ").append(quest.getTargetPlant()).append("\n");
            }
            if (quest.getTargetCategory() != null && !quest.getTargetCategory().isEmpty()) {
                sb.append("  🏷️ Target Category: ").append(quest.getTargetCategory()).append("\n");
            }
            if (quest.getTargetValue() != -1) {
                sb.append("  🎯 Target Value: ").append(quest.getTargetValue()).append("\n");
            }
            sb.append("╚══════════════════════════════════════════════╝\n\n");
        }
        return new Result(true, sb.toString());
    }
}

package app.controller.shop;
import app.model.App;
import app.model.shop.DailyOffer;
import app.model.shop.Shop;
import app.model.shop.ShopItem;
import app.model.shop.items.RandomSeedPacketItem;
import app.model.user.User;
import common.Result;
public class ShopController {
    public Result showList() {
        StringBuilder output = new StringBuilder();
        Shop shop = App.getCurrentUser().getShop();
        for (ShopItem permanentItem : shop.getPermanentItems()) {
            output.append(permanentItem.toString());
            output.append("\n");
        }
        return new Result(true, output.toString());
    }
    public Result showDaily() {
        Shop shop = App.getCurrentUser().getShop();
        DailyOffer offer = shop.getDailyOffer();
        RandomSeedPacketItem item = (RandomSeedPacketItem) offer.getItem();
        return new Result(true, "Seed Packet of " + item.getPlantName());
    }
    public Result buy(String itemID, int count, String PlantName) {
        User user =  App.getCurrentUser();
        Shop shop = user.getShop();
        return shop.buy(itemID, user, count, PlantName);
    }
    public Result buyDaily() {
        User user =  App.getCurrentUser();
        Shop shop = user.getShop();
        return shop.buyDaily(user);
    }
    public void getMoney() {
        App.getCurrentUser().addCoins(10000);
    }
    public void getGem() {
        App.getCurrentUser().addGems(10000);
    }
}

package app.enums;
public enum Color {
    RED("Red"),
    GREEN("Green"),
    BLUE("Blue"),
    YELLOW("Yellow"),
    CYAN("Cyan"),
    MAGENTA("Magenta"),
    ORANGE("Orange"),
    PURPLE("Purple"),
    PINK("Pink"),
    BROWN("Brown"),
    BLACK("Black"),
    WHITE("White"),
    GRAY("Gray"),
    LIME("Lime"),
    INDIGO("Indigo"),
    VIOLET("Violet"),
    TEAL("Teal"),
    MAROON("Maroon"),
    NAVY("Navy"),
    OLIVE("Olive")
    ;
    private final String label;
    Color(String label) {
        this.label = label;
    }
    @Override
    public String toString() {
        return this.label;
    }
    public static Color parseColor(String input) {
        if (input == null)
            return null;
        input = input.trim();
        for (Color color : Color.values()) {
            if (color.toString().equalsIgnoreCase(input))
                return color;
        }
        return null;
    }
}

package app.enums;
import app.view.*;
import app.view.game.ConveyorBeltScreen;
import app.view.game.PlantSelectionMenu;
import app.view.game.PlayScreen;
import app.view.game.minigame.IZombieScreen;
import app.view.game.minigame.VaseBreakerScreen;
public enum Menu {
    SignupMenu(new SignupMenu()),
    LoginMenu(new LoginMenu()),
    MainMenu(new MainMenu()),
    GameMenu(new GameMenu()),
    EnterMenu(new LoginMenu()),
    SettingsMenu(new SettingsMenu()),
    ProfileMenu(new ProfileMenu()),
    NewsMenu(new NewsMenu()),
    TravelLogMenu(new TravelLogMenu()) ,
    CollectionMenu (new CollectionMenu()),
    LeaderBoardMenu(new LeaderBoardMenu()) ,
    GreenhousMenu(new Greenhouse()) ,
    NetworkMenu(new NetworkMenu()) ,
    PlantSelectionMenu(new PlantSelectionMenu()) ,
    Shop (new ShopMenu()),
    PlayMenu(new PlayScreen(null)),
    VaseBreakerMenu(new VaseBreakerScreen()),
    IZombieMenu(new IZombieScreen()),
    ConveyorMenu(new ConveyorBeltScreen())
    ;
    private final AppMenu menu;
    Menu(AppMenu menu) {
        this.menu = menu;
    }
    @Override
    public String toString() {
        return this.name();
    }
    public static Menu parseMenu(String input) {
        if (input == null)
            return null;
        input = input.trim();
        for (Menu menu : Menu.values()) {
            if (menu.toString().equalsIgnoreCase(input))
                return menu;
        }
        return null;
    }
}

package app.enums;
public enum SecurityQuestions {
    FAVORITE_COLOR(1, "What is your favorite color?"),
    FAVORITE_FOOD(2, "What is your favorite food?"),
    FAVORITE_BOOK(3, "What is your favorite movie?"),
    PET_NAME(4, "What was your first pet’s name?"),
    MOTHER_NAME(5, "What was your mother’s maiden name?"),
    SCHOOL_NAME(6, "What was the name of your first school?"),
    CITY_NAME(7, "What city were you born in?")
    ;
    private final int number;
    private final String question;
    SecurityQuestions(int number, String question) {
        this.number = number;
        this.question = question;
    }
    public int getNumber() {
        return number;
    }
    public String getQuestion() {
        return question;
    }
    public static SecurityQuestions getQuestionByNumber(int number) {
        for(SecurityQuestions question : values()) {
            if(question.getNumber() == number) {
                return question;
            }
        }
        return null;
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\app\enums\commands\Commands.java ---
package app.enums.commands;
import java.util.regex.Matcher;
import java.util.regex.Pattern;
public interface Commands {
    String getPattern();
    default Matcher getMatcher(String input) {
        Matcher matcher = Pattern.compile(getPattern()).matcher(input);
        matcher.matches();
        return matcher;
    }
    default boolean matches(String input) {
        return getMatcher(input).matches();
    }
    default String getGroup(String input, String group) {
        return getMatcher(input).group(group);
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\app\enums\commands\ValidatingCommands.java ---
package app.enums.commands;
public enum ValidatingCommands implements Commands{
    USERNAME_FORMAT("[a-zA-Z0-9\\-]+"),
    NICKNAME_FORMAT(".{3,30}"),
    PASSWORD_FORMAT("^[a-zA-Z0-9!#$%^&*)(=+{}\\[\\]\\|\\\\/:;'\"<>,?]+$"),
    EMAIL_FORMAT("[a-zA-Z0-9]+([._-][a-zA-Z0-9]+)*@[a-zA-Z0-9]+([-][a-zA-Z0-9]+)*(\\.[a-zA-Z0-9]+([-][a-zA-Z0-9]+)*)*\\.[a-zA-Z0-9]{2,}$"),
    GENDER_FORMAT("\\s*man|woman|male|female\\s*"),
    PASSWORD_STRENGTH("^(?=.*[a-z])(?=.*[A-Z])(?=.*\\d)(?=.*[!#$%^&*()=+{}\\[\\]|/\\\\:;'\",<>?]).{8,}$")
    ;
    private final String pattern;
    ValidatingCommands(String pattern) {
        this.pattern = pattern;
    }
    @Override
    public String getPattern() {
        return pattern;
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\app\enums\greenhouse\GreenhousePlantType.java ---
package app.enums.greenhouse;
public enum GreenhousePlantType {
    MARIGOLD ("Marigold plant"),
    BOOST ("Boost plant")
    ;
    private final String label;
    GreenhousePlantType(String label) {
        this.label = label;
    }
    @Override
    public String toString() {
        return this.label;
    }
    public static GreenhousePlantType parseGreenhousePlantType(String input) {
        if (input == null)
            return null;
        input = input.trim();
        for (GreenhousePlantType type : GreenhousePlantType.values()) {
            if (type.toString().equalsIgnoreCase(input))
                return type;
        }
        return null;
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\app\enums\greenhouse\PotStatus.java ---
package app.enums.greenhouse;
public enum PotStatus
{
    LOCKED ("Locked") ,
    EMPTY ("Empty") ,
    GROWING ("Growing") ,
    READY ("Ready")
    ;
    private final String label;
    PotStatus(String label) {
        this.label = label;
    }
    @Override
    public String toString() {
        return this.label;
    }
    public static PotStatus parsePotStatus(String input) {
        if (input == null)
            return null;
        input = input.trim();
        for (PotStatus status : PotStatus.values()) {
            if (status.toString().equalsIgnoreCase(input))
                return status;
        }
        return null;
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\app\enums\minigame\WalnutPlantType.java ---
package app.enums.minigame;
public enum WalnutPlantType {
    BOWLING_WALLNUT("Bowling Wallnut"),
    EXPLODE_O_NUT("Explode O' Nut"),
    GIANT_WALLNUT("Giant Wallnut");
    private final String label;
    WalnutPlantType(String label) {
        this.label = label;
    }
    public String getLabel() {
        return label;
    }
    @Override
    public String toString() {
        return label;
    }
    public static WalnutPlantType fromLabel(String label) {
        if (label == null) return null;
        for (WalnutPlantType type : values()) {
            if (type.label.equalsIgnoreCase(label.trim())) {
                return type;
            }
        }
        return null;
    }
}

    }

    public String toString() {
        return label;
    }
    public static PlantCategory fromLabel(String label) {
        if (label == null) return null;
        for (PlantCategory cat : values()) {
            if (cat.label.equalsIgnoreCase(label.trim())) {
                return cat;
            }
        }
        return null;
    }
}

    IMITATER("Imitater", 250, 50000, 0.0f),
    ICE_SHROOM("Ice-shroom", 150, 30000, 0.0f),
    LILY_PAD("Lily Pad", 0, 0, 2.0f),
    HOT_POTATO("Hot Potato", 10, 2000, 0.0f),
    GRAVE_BUSTER("Grave Buster", 10, 2000, 0.0f),
    ENLIGHTEN_MINT("Enlighten-mint", 100, 25000, 0.0f),
    APPEASE_MINT("Appease-mint", 100, 25000, 0.0f),

    ;
    private final String label;
    private final int requiredSeedPackets;
    private final int requiredCoins;
    private final float plantFoodDuration;
    PlantName(String label, int requiredSeedPackets, int requiredCoins, float plantFoodDuration) {
        this.label = label;
        this.requiredSeedPackets = requiredSeedPackets;
        this.requiredCoins = requiredCoins;
        this.plantFoodDuration = plantFoodDuration;
    }
    public String getLabel() {
        return label;
    }
    public int getRequiredSeedPackets() {
        return requiredSeedPackets;
    }
    public int getRequiredCoins() {
        return requiredCoins;
    }
    public float getPlantFoodDuration() {
        return plantFoodDuration;
    }
    @Override
    public String toString() {
        return label;
    }
    public static PlantName fromLabel(String label) {
        if (label == null) return null;
        for (PlantName plant : values()) {
            if (plant.label.equalsIgnoreCase(label.trim())) {
                return plant;
            }
        }
        return null;
    }
    public static PlantName parsePlantName(String input) {
        return fromLabel(input);
    }
    public boolean hasPlantFoodEffect() {
        return switch (this) {
            case GOLD_BLOOM, CHERRY_BOMB, GRAPESHOT, JALAPENO, DOOM_SHROOM,
                 IMITATER, ICE_SHROOM, HOT_POTATO, GRAVE_BUSTER,
                 ENLIGHTEN_MINT, APPEASE_MINT, ARMA_MINT, BOMBARD_MINT,
                 ENFORCE_MINT, REINFORCE_MINT, ENCHANT_MINT, PIERCE_MINT,
                 CAT_TAIL_MINT -> false;
            default -> true;
        };
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\app\enums\quest\QuestName.java ---
package app.enums.quest;
public enum QuestName {
    DAILY_SUN_COLLECTOR("Daily Sun Collector"),
    CHAPTER_HUNTER("Chapter Hunter"),
    PLANT_EXPERT("Plant Expert"),
    CACTUS_ONLY("Cactus Only"),
    ECONOMICAL_GARDENER("Economical Gardener"),
    DEFENSE_MASTER("Defense Master"),
    QUICK_ACTION("Quick Action"),
    DEMOLITION_EXPERT("Demolition Expert"),
    SYMMETRY("Symmetry"),
    FAMILY_HUNTER("Family Hunter"),
    LIMITED_BLOOM("Limited Bloom"),
    DAY_OR_NIGHT("Day or Night"),
    WIN_STREAK("Win Streak"),
    ALMOST_VICTORY("Almost Victory"),
    OCD_MASTER("OCD Master"),
    CLOUDY_DAY("Cloudy Day"),
    ONE_COLUMN_LESS("One Column Less"),
    DEFENSELESS_ROW("Defenseless Row"),
    DEFENSELESS_CROSS("Defenseless Cross"),
    LAWN_MOWER_TIME("Lawn Mower Time");
    private final String label;
    QuestName(String label) {
        this.label = label;
    }
    @Override
    public String toString() {
        return label;
    }
    public static QuestName parseQuestName(String input) {
        if (input == null) return null;
        input = input.trim();
        for (QuestName quest : QuestName.values()) {
            if (quest.toString().equalsIgnoreCase(input)) return quest;
        }
        return null;
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\app\enums\zombie\animation\ZombieAnimationType.java ---
package app.enums.zombie.animation;
import game.graphics.AnimationEntry;
import game.graphics.AnimationRegistry;
public enum ZombieAnimationType {
    ZOMBIE_BASIC("Zombie", "ZOMBIE"),
    ZOMBIE_FLAG("ZombieFlag", "ZOMBIE_FLAG"),
    ZOMBIE_CONE("ZombieCone", "ZOMBIE_CONE"),
    ZOMBIE_BUCKET("ZombieBucket", "ZOMBIE_BUCKET"),
    GARGANTUAR("ZombieGargantuar", "GARGANTUAR"),
    IMP("ZombieImp", "IMP"),
    KING("ZombieKing", "KING"),
    WIZARD("ZombieWizard", "WIZARD"),
    OCTOPUS("ZombieBeachOctopus", "OCTOPUS"),
    FISHERMAN("ZombieBeachFisherman", "FISHERMAN"),
    PIANO("ZombiePiano", "PIANO"),
    NEWSPAPER("ZombieNewspaper", "NEWSPAPER"),
    ALLSTAR("ZombieAllStar", "ALLSTAR"),
    PROSPECTOR("ZombieProspector", "PROSPECTOR"),
    TOMB_RAISER("ZombieTombRaiser", "TOMB_RAISER"),
    EXPLORER("ZombieExplorer", "EXPLORER"),
    DODO("ZombieDodo", "DODO"),
    HUNTER("ZombieHunter", "HUNTER"),
    TROGLOBITE("ZombieTroglobite", "TROGLOBITE"),
    JUGGLER("ZombieJuggler", "JUGGLER"),
    DARKING("ZombieDarking", "DARKING"),
    ARCANE("ZombieArcane", "ARCANE"),
    SNAIL("ZombieSnail", "SNAIL"),
    EIGHTIES_ARCADE("ZombieEightiesArcade", "EIGHTIES_ARCADE"),
    TURQUOISE("ZombieTurquoise", "TURQUOISE"),
    SNORKEL("ZombieSnorkel", "SNORKEL"),
    SURFBOARD("ZombieSurfboard", "SURFBOARD"),
    BOSS_EGYPT("ZombieBossEgypt", "BOSS_EGYPT"),
    CRYSTAL_SKULL("ZombieCrystalSkull", "CRYSTAL_SKULL");
    private final String alias;
    private final String manifestName;
    ZombieAnimationType(String alias, String manifestName) {
        this.alias = alias;
        this.manifestName = manifestName;
    }
    public String getAlias() { return alias; }
    public String getManifestName() { return manifestName; }
    public static ZombieAnimationType fromAlias(String alias) {
        for (ZombieAnimationType type : values()) {
            if (type.alias.equalsIgnoreCase(alias)) {
                return type;
            }
        }
        return ZOMBIE_BASIC;
    }
    public AnimationEntry getAnimationEntry() {
        return AnimationRegistry.getInstance().getByName(manifestName);
    }
    public boolean hasState(ZombieAnimationState state) {
        AnimationEntry entry = getAnimationEntry();
        if (entry == null) return false;
        return entry.hasClip(state.getClipKey());
    }
    public float getClipDuration(ZombieAnimationState state) {
        AnimationEntry entry = getAnimationEntry();
        if (entry == null) return 1.0f;
        return entry.getClipDuration(state.getClipKey());
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\app\model\App.java ---
package app.model;
import app.enums.Menu;
import app.enums.SeasonType;
import app.model.game.PendingGameSession;
import app.model.user.GameProgress;
import app.model.user.User;
import game.engine.GameSimulationEngine;
import game.engine.RealTimeGameEngine;
import game.entity.plant.Plant;
import game.entity.zombie.Zombie;
import game.world.GameWorld;
import java.util.List;
public class App {
    private static Menu currentMenu;
    private static List<User> allUsers;
    private static List<Zombie> allZombies;
    private static List<Plant> allPlants;
    private static User currentUser;
    private static GameProgress currentProgress;
    private static SeasonType currentSeason;
    private static final PendingGameSession pendingGameSession = new PendingGameSession();
    private static GameSimulationEngine currentGameEngine;
    private static RealTimeGameEngine realTimeEngine;
    public static PendingGameSession getPendingGameSession() { return pendingGameSession; }
    public static GameSimulationEngine getCurrentGameEngine() { return currentGameEngine; }
    public static void setCurrentGameEngine(GameSimulationEngine engine) { currentGameEngine = engine; }
    public static User getCurrentUser() { return currentUser; }
    public static void setCurrentUser(User user) { currentUser = user; }
    public static GameProgress getCurrentProgress() { return currentProgress; }
    public static Menu getCurrentMenu() {return currentMenu;}
    public static List<User> getAllUsers() {return allUsers;}
    public static void setCurrentMenu(Menu currentMenu) {App.currentMenu = currentMenu;}
    public static void setCurrentProgress(GameProgress currentProgress) {App.currentProgress = currentProgress;}
    public static void setAllUsers(List<User> allUsers) {App.allUsers = allUsers;}
    public static void setRealTimeEngine(RealTimeGameEngine engine) {
        realTimeEngine = engine;
        if (engine != null) {
            currentGameEngine = engine.getSimulationEngine();
        }
    }
    public static RealTimeGameEngine getRealTimeEngine() {
        return realTimeEngine;
    }
    public static GameWorld getCurrentWorld() {
        return realTimeEngine != null ? realTimeEngine.getWorld() : null;
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\app\model\game\PendingGameSession.java ---
package app.model.game;
import app.enums.plant.PlantName;
import game.world.chapter.Chapter;
import game.world.minigame.Minigame;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;
public class PendingGameSession {
    private Chapter selectedChapter;
    private final List<PlantName> selectedPlants = new ArrayList<>();
    public Chapter getSelectedChapter() { return selectedChapter; }
    public void setSelectedChapter(Chapter chapter) {
        this.selectedChapter = chapter;
    }
    public boolean isMinigame() {
        return selectedChapter != null && selectedChapter.isMinigame();
    }
    public Minigame getMinigameLogic() {
        if (!isMinigame()) return null;
        return selectedChapter.getMinigameLogic();
    }
    public List<PlantName> getSelectedPlants() {
        return selectedPlants;
    }
    public void clear() {
        selectedChapter = null;
        selectedPlants.clear();
    }
    public void addPlant(PlantName plantName) {
        selectedPlants.add(plantName);
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\app\model\greenhouse\Greenhouse.java ---
package app.model.greenhouse;
import app.enums.greenhouse.PotStatus;
import common.model.Position;
public class Greenhouse
{
    private final GreenhousePot[][] pots;
    private final int row = 4;
    private final int col = 5;
    public Greenhouse() {
        pots = new GreenhousePot[row][col];
        for (int rowCursor = 0; rowCursor < row; rowCursor++) {
            for (int colCursor = 0; colCursor < col; colCursor++) {
                Position position = new Position(colCursor + 1, rowCursor + 1);
                PotStatus potStatus = (rowCursor == 0) ? PotStatus.EMPTY : PotStatus.LOCKED;
                int cost = switch (rowCursor) {
                    case 0 -> 0;
                    case 1 -> 300;
                    case 2 -> 500;
                    case 3 -> 750;
                    default -> 0;
                };
                pots[rowCursor][colCursor] = new GreenhousePot(position, potStatus, cost);
            }
        }
    }
    public GreenhousePot[][] getPots()
    {
        return pots;
    }
    public GreenhousePot getPot(int x , int y)
    {
        return pots[y-1][x-1];
    }
    public GreenhousePot getPot(Position position)
    {
        return pots[(int)position.getY()-1][(int)position.getX()-1];
    }
    public int getPotCost(int x , int y)
    {
        return getPot(x , y).getCost();
    }
    public int getPotCost(Position position)
    {
        return getPot(position).getCost();
    }
    public int getRow() {
        return row;
    }
    public int getCol() {
        return col;
    }
    public boolean isPotAvailable(int count) {
        return false;
    }
    public void addPot() {
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\app\model\greenhouse\GreenhousePlant.java ---
package app.model.greenhouse;
import app.enums.greenhouse.GreenhousePlantType;
import java.time.Duration;
import java.time.LocalDateTime;
public abstract class GreenhousePlant {
    protected final GreenhousePlantType GreenhousePlantType;
    protected final LocalDateTime plantedAt;
    protected LocalDateTime readyAt;
    public GreenhousePlant(GreenhousePlantType GreenhousePlantType,
                           LocalDateTime plantedAt,
                           Duration growthDuration) {
        this.GreenhousePlantType = GreenhousePlantType;
        this.plantedAt = plantedAt;
        this.readyAt = plantedAt.plus(growthDuration);
    }
    public GreenhousePlant(GreenhousePlantType GreenhousePlantType,
                           LocalDateTime plantedAt,
                           LocalDateTime readyAt) {
        this.GreenhousePlantType = GreenhousePlantType;
        this.plantedAt = plantedAt;
        this.readyAt = readyAt;
    }
    public GreenhousePlantType getGreenhousePlantType() {
        return GreenhousePlantType;
    }
    public LocalDateTime getPlantedAt() {
        return plantedAt;
    }
    public LocalDateTime getReadyAt() {
        return readyAt;
    }
    public boolean isReady() {
        return !LocalDateTime.now().isBefore(readyAt);
    }
    public Duration getRemainingTime() {
        if (isReady()) {
            return Duration.ZERO;
        }
        return Duration.between(LocalDateTime.now(), readyAt);
    }
    public String getRemainingTimeText() {
        if (isReady()) {
            return "The plant is ready for harvest.";
        }
        Duration remaining = getRemainingTime();
        long totalSeconds = remaining.getSeconds();
        long minutesRounded = (long) Math.ceil(totalSeconds / 60.0);
        long hours = minutesRounded / 60;
        long minutes = minutesRounded % 60;
        StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
        if (hours > 0) {
            sb.append(hours)
                    .append(" ")
                    .append(hours == 1 ? "hour" : "hours");
        }
        if (minutes > 0) {
            if (hours > 0) {
                sb.append(" and ");
            }
            sb.append(minutes)
                    .append(" ")
                    .append(minutes == 1 ? "minute" : "minutes");
        }
        sb.append(" remaining.");
        return sb.toString();
    }
    public void completeGrowth() {
        this.readyAt = LocalDateTime.now();
    }
    public void reduceRemainingTime(long hours) {
        if (hours <= 0) return;
        this.readyAt = this.readyAt.minusHours(hours);
        if (this.readyAt.isBefore(LocalDateTime.now())) {
            this.readyAt = LocalDateTime.now();
        }
    }
    public abstract String getPlantName();
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\app\model\greenhouse\GreenhousePot.java ---
package app.model.greenhouse;
import app.enums.greenhouse.PotStatus;
import common.model.Position;
public class GreenhousePot
{
    private Position position;
    private PotStatus potStatus;
    private GreenhousePlant plant;
    private final int cost;
    public GreenhousePot(Position position, PotStatus potStatus , int cost) {
        setPosition(position);
        this.potStatus = potStatus;
        this.plant = null;
        this.cost = cost;
    }
    public Position getPosition()
    {
        return position;
    }
    public boolean isUnlocked()
    {
        return potStatus != PotStatus.LOCKED;
    }
    public void unlock()
    {
        if (isUnlocked()) return;
        this.potStatus = PotStatus.EMPTY;
    }
    public GreenhousePlant getPlant()
    {
        return plant;
    }
    public void setPlant(GreenhousePlant plant)
    {
        if (this.potStatus != PotStatus.EMPTY || plant == null) return;
        this.plant = plant;
        this.potStatus = PotStatus.GROWING;
    }
    public boolean isEmpty()
    {
        return this.potStatus == PotStatus.EMPTY;
    }
    public boolean isReady()
    {
        return isPlantReady();
    }
    public void setPosition(Position position) {
        if (position == null) return;
        if (position.getX() > 5 || position.getX() < 1) return;
        if (position.getY() > 4 || position.getY() < 1) return;
        this.position = position;
    }
    public void collect()
    {
        if (plant == null) return;
        if (!isPlantReady()) return;
        this.plant = null;
        this.potStatus = PotStatus.EMPTY;
    }
    public boolean isPlantReady()
    {
        if (plant == null) return false;
        if (plant.isReady())
        {
            this.potStatus = PotStatus.READY;
            return true;
        }
        return false;
    }
    public PotStatus getPotStatus() {
        return potStatus;
    }
    public int getCost() {
        return cost;
    }
}

        this.color = getRandomColor();
        this.coinValue = 500;
    }

        super(app.enums.greenhouse.GreenhousePlantType.MARIGOLD, plantedAt, readyAt);
        this.color = color;
        this.coinValue = coinValue;
    }

        Color[] colors = Color.values();
        Random random = new Random();
        return colors[random.nextInt(colors.length)];
    }

    }

        return coinValue;
    }

    }

    }

    }

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\app\model\shop\Shop.java ---
package app.model.shop;
import app.enums.plant.PlantName;
import app.model.App;
import app.model.shop.enums.CurrencyType;
import app.model.shop.items.*;
import app.model.user.User;
import common.Result;
import java.time.LocalDate;
import java.time.LocalTime;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Random;
public class Shop {
    private final ArrayList<ShopItem> permanentItems;
    private DailyOffer dailyOffer;
    private LocalTime lastResetTime;
    public Shop() {
        permanentItems = new ArrayList<>();
        initializePermanentItems();
    }
    private void initializePermanentItems() {
        permanentItems.add(new PotItem("1"));
        permanentItems.add(new PlantFoodItem("2"));
        permanentItems.add(new RandomSeedPacketItem("3", null));
        permanentItems.add(new SelectedSeedPacketItem("4", null));
        permanentItems.add(new CurrencyExchangeItem("5"));
    }
    public Result buy(String itemID, User user, int count, String plantName) {
        ShopItem item = fetchItem(itemID);
        if (item == null)
            return new Result(false, "Item ID is invalid");
        if (!canPay(user, item, count)) {
            return new Result(false, "Not enough money");
        }
        if (itemID.equals("3")) {
            PlantName randomPlant = selectRandomPlant();
            if (randomPlant == null) {
                return new Result(false, "No plants available");
            }
            Price price = new Price(1000, CurrencyType.COIN);
            if (!canPay(user, item, count)) {
                return new Result(false, "Not enough money");
            }
            pay(user, price, count);
            user.getSeedBank().addPacket(randomPlant, count);
            return new Result(true, "Random seed packet bought successfully");
        }
        if (itemID.equals("4")) {
            if (plantName == null)
                return new Result(false, "plantName is invalid");
            PlantName name = app.enums.plant.PlantName.parsePlantName(plantName.trim());
            if (name == null) {
                return new Result(false, "plantName is invalid");
            }
            pay(user, item.getPrice(), count);
            user.getSeedBank().addPacket(name , count);
            return new Result(true, "Item bought successfully");
        }
        if (!item.canApply(user, count)) {
            return new Result(false, "Not enough items");
        }
        pay(user, item.getPrice(), count);
        for (int i = 0; i < count; i++)
            item.apply(user);
        return new Result(true, "Item bought successfully");
    }
    public Result buyDaily(User user) {
        if (dailyOffer == null || !dailyOffer.getDate().equals(LocalDate.now())) {
            resetDailyItem();
        }
        if (dailyOffer.isSold())
            return new Result(false, "Daily item is sold");
        pay(user, dailyOffer.getItem().getPrice(), 1);
        dailyOffer.setSold(true);
        dailyOffer.apply(user);
        return new Result(true, "Daily item bought successfully");
    }
    private boolean canPay(User user, ShopItem item, int count) {
        Price price = item.getPrice();
        boolean canPay = true;
        switch (price.currencyType()) {
            case COIN -> canPay = user.getCoins() >= (price.amount() * count);
            case GEM -> canPay = user.getGems() >= (price.amount() * count);
        }
        return canPay;
    }
    private void pay(User user, Price price, int count) {
        switch (price.currencyType()) {
            case COIN ->
                    user.deductCoins(price.amount() * count);
            case GEM ->
                    user.deductGems(price.amount() * count);
        }
    }
    public void resetDailyItem() {
        if (dailyOffer != null && dailyOffer.getDate().equals(LocalDate.now())) {
            return;
        }
        List<PlantName> availablePlants = new ArrayList<>();
        if(App.getCurrentUser() != null)
        {
            availablePlants = App.getCurrentUser().getUnlockedPlants();
            if (availablePlants.isEmpty()) {
                dailyOffer = null;
                return;
            }
            Random random = new Random();
            PlantName randomPlant = availablePlants.get(random.nextInt(availablePlants.size()));
            ShopItem item = new RandomSeedPacketItem("6", randomPlant);
            dailyOffer = new DailyOffer(item, LocalDate.now());
        }
    }
    public ArrayList<ShopItem> getPermanentItems() {
        return permanentItems;
    }
    public ShopItem fetchItem(String itemID) {
        for (ShopItem item : permanentItems) {
            if (item.itemID.equals(itemID))
                return item;
        }
        return null;
    }
    public DailyOffer getDailyOffer() {
        if (dailyOffer == null || !dailyOffer.getDate().equals(LocalDate.now())) {
            resetDailyItem();
        }
        return dailyOffer;
    }
    private PlantName selectRandomPlant() {
        List<PlantName> availablePlants = new ArrayList<>();
        if (App.getCurrentUser() != null)
        {
            availablePlants = App.getCurrentUser().getUnlockedPlants();
            Random random = new Random();
            return availablePlants.get(random.nextInt(availablePlants.size()));
        }
        return null;
    }
}
