--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\app\model\user\GameProgress.java ---
package app.model.user;
import app.enums.SeasonType;
import app.model.Collection;
import java.util.HashMap;
import java.util.Map;
public class GameProgress {
    private Collection collection;
    private final Map<SeasonType, Integer> seasonProgress;
    public GameProgress() {
        this.seasonProgress = new HashMap<>();
        this.collection = new Collection();
        for(SeasonType type : SeasonType.values()) {
            seasonProgress.put(type, 0);
        }
    }
    public void markLevelCompleted(SeasonType season, int level) {
        int current = seasonProgress.getOrDefault(season, 0);
        if (level > current && level >= 1 && level <= 4) {
            seasonProgress.put(season, level);
        }
    }
    public int getHighestCompletedLevel(SeasonType season) {
        return seasonProgress.getOrDefault(season, 0);
    }
    public boolean isLevelCompleted(SeasonType season, int level) {
        return level <= getHighestCompletedLevel(season);
    }
    public Collection getCollection() {
        return collection;
    }
    public int getNumberOfLevelsCompleted() {
        int completedLevels = 0;
        for(SeasonType type : SeasonType.values()) {
            completedLevels += getHighestCompletedLevel(type);
        }
        return completedLevels;
    }
}

        if (amount < 0) return;
        if (this.coins - amount < 0) return;
        this.coins -= amount;
    }
    public void addCoins(int amount) {
        if (amount <= 0) {return;}
        this.coins += amount;
    }
    public void unlockPlant(PlantName plant) {
        if (!plantBoostStatus.containsKey(plant) && !plantLevelMap.containsKey(plant)) {
            plantBoostStatus.put(plant, false);
            plantLevelMap.put(plant , new PlantLevel(plant));
        } else if (!plantBoostStatus.containsKey(plant)) {
            plantBoostStatus.put(plant, false);
        } else if (!plantLevelMap.containsKey(plant)) {
            plantLevelMap.put(plant , new PlantLevel(plant));
        }
    }
    public boolean isUnlockPlant(PlantName name)
    {
        if (plantBoostStatus.containsKey(name)) return true;
        return false;
    }

        return newsList;
    }

        return plantBoostStatus.containsKey(plant);
    }
    public void setBoost(PlantName plant, boolean status) {
        if (isUnlocked(plant)) {
            plantBoostStatus.put(plant, status);
        }
    }
    public boolean isBoosted(PlantName plant) {
        return plantBoostStatus.getOrDefault(plant, false);
    }
    public void deductGems(int amount) {
        if (amount < 0) return;
        if (this.gems - amount < 0) return;
        this.gems -= amount;
    }
    public void addGems(int amount) {
        if (amount <= 0) return;
        this.gems += amount;
    }

        newsList.add(news);

    }

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\app\repository\Repository.java ---
package app.repository;
import app.enums.Color;
import app.enums.greenhouse.GreenhousePlantType;
import app.enums.plant.PlantCategory;
import app.enums.plant.PlantName;
import app.enums.quest.QuestName;
import app.model.App;
import app.model.greenhouse.*;
import app.model.user.User;
import app.service.AppService;
import com.google.gson.*;
import com.google.gson.reflect.TypeToken;
import game.entity.quest.Quest;
import game.service.quest.QuestFactory;
import game.service.quest.QuestManager;
import java.io.BufferedReader;
import java.io.File;
import java.io.FileReader;
import java.io.FileWriter;
import java.lang.reflect.Type;
import java.time.LocalDate;
import java.time.LocalDateTime;
import java.time.LocalTime;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;
public class Repository {
    private static final String LOGIN_FILE = "login.txt";
    private static final String USERS_FILE = "users.json";
    private static final Gson gson = new GsonBuilder()
            .setPrettyPrinting()
            .registerTypeAdapter(LocalDateTime.class, (JsonSerializer<LocalDateTime>) (src, type, context) -> new JsonPrimitive(src.toString()))
            .registerTypeAdapter(LocalDateTime.class, (JsonDeserializer<LocalDateTime>) (json, type, context) -> LocalDateTime.parse(json.getAsString()))
            .registerTypeAdapter(LocalDate.class, (JsonSerializer<LocalDate>) (src, type, context) -> new JsonPrimitive(src.toString()))
            .registerTypeAdapter(LocalDate.class, (JsonDeserializer<LocalDate>) (json, type, context) -> LocalDate.parse(json.getAsString()))
            .registerTypeAdapter(LocalTime.class, (JsonSerializer<LocalTime>) (src, type, context) -> new JsonPrimitive(src.toString()))
            .registerTypeAdapter(LocalTime.class, (JsonDeserializer<LocalTime>) (json, type, context) -> LocalTime.parse(json.getAsString()))
            .registerTypeAdapter(QuestManager.class, new QuestManagerAdapter())
            .registerTypeAdapter(GreenhousePlant.class, new GreenhousePlantAdapter())
            .create();
    public static void saveLogin(String username) {
        try (FileWriter fileWriter = new FileWriter(LOGIN_FILE)){
            fileWriter.write(username);
        }
        catch (Exception e) {
            System.err.println("Unable to save logged in user");
        }
    }
    public static User loadLoginedUser() {
        loadAllUsers();
        File file = new File(LOGIN_FILE);
        if(!file.exists()) {return null;}
        String loggedInUsername;
        try (BufferedReader reader = new BufferedReader(new FileReader(file))){
            loggedInUsername = reader.readLine();
        }
        catch (Exception e) {
            return null;
        }
        return AppService.getUserByUsername(loggedInUsername);
    }
    public static void clearLoggedInUser() {
        File file = new File(LOGIN_FILE);
        if(file.exists()) {
            file.delete();
        }
    }
    public static void saveAllUsers() {
        try (FileWriter writer = new FileWriter(USERS_FILE)) {
            gson.toJson(App.getAllUsers(), writer);
        }
        catch (Exception e) {
            System.err.println("unable to save all users.");
            e.printStackTrace();
        }
    }
    public static void loadAllUsers() {
        File file = new File(USERS_FILE);
        if(!file.exists()) {
            App.setAllUsers(new ArrayList<>());
            return;
        }
        try (FileReader reader = new FileReader(file)){
            Type userListType = new TypeToken<ArrayList<User>>(){}.getType();
            List<User> users = gson.fromJson(reader, userListType);
            if (users == null) {
                users = new ArrayList<>();
            }
            App.setAllUsers(users);
        }
        catch (Exception e) {
            System.err.println("unable to load users. Creating a new list.");
            App.setAllUsers(new ArrayList<>());
        }
    }
    private static class GreenhousePlantAdapter implements JsonSerializer<GreenhousePlant>, JsonDeserializer<GreenhousePlant> {
        @Override
        public JsonElement serialize(GreenhousePlant src, Type typeOfSrc, JsonSerializationContext context) {
            JsonObject obj = new JsonObject();
            obj.addProperty("plantedAt", src.getPlantedAt().toString());
            obj.addProperty("readyAt", src.getReadyAt().toString());
            if (src instanceof BoostPlant boost) {
                obj.addProperty("type", GreenhousePlantType.BOOST.name());
                obj.addProperty("plant", boost.getPlant().name());
            } else if (src instanceof MarigoldPlant marigold) {
                obj.addProperty("type", GreenhousePlantType.MARIGOLD.name());
                obj.addProperty("color", marigold.getColor().name());
                obj.addProperty("coinValue", marigold.getCoinValue());
            }
            return obj;
        }
        @Override
        public GreenhousePlant deserialize(JsonElement json, Type typeOfT, JsonDeserializationContext context) throws JsonParseException {
            JsonObject obj = json.getAsJsonObject();
            GreenhousePlantType type = GreenhousePlantType.valueOf(obj.get("type").getAsString());
            LocalDateTime plantedAt = LocalDateTime.parse(obj.get("plantedAt").getAsString());
            LocalDateTime readyAt = LocalDateTime.parse(obj.get("readyAt").getAsString());
            if (type == GreenhousePlantType.BOOST) {
                PlantName plant = PlantName.valueOf(obj.get("plant").getAsString());
                return new BoostPlant(plant, plantedAt, readyAt);
            } else if (type == GreenhousePlantType.MARIGOLD) {
                Color color = Color.valueOf(obj.get("color").getAsString());
                int coinValue = obj.get("coinValue").getAsInt();
                return new MarigoldPlant(color, coinValue, plantedAt, readyAt);
            }
            throw new JsonParseException("Unknown GreenhousePlantType: " + type);
        }
    }
    private static class QuestManagerAdapter implements JsonSerializer<QuestManager>, JsonDeserializer<QuestManager> {
        @Override
        public JsonElement serialize(QuestManager src, Type typeOfSrc, JsonSerializationContext context) {
            JsonObject root = new JsonObject();
            root.addProperty("lastResetDate",
                    src.getLastResetDate() != null ? src.getLastResetDate().toString() : null);
            JsonArray activeQuests = new JsonArray();
            for (Quest quest : src.getActiveQuests()) {
                activeQuests.add(serializeQuest(quest));
            }
            JsonArray completedQuests = new JsonArray();
            for (Quest quest : src.getCompletedQuests()) {
                completedQuests.add(serializeQuest(quest));
            }
            root.add("activeQuests", activeQuests);
            root.add("completedQuests", completedQuests);
            return root;
        }
        @Override
        public QuestManager deserialize(JsonElement json, Type typeOfT, JsonDeserializationContext context)
                throws JsonParseException {
            JsonObject root = json.getAsJsonObject();
            QuestManager manager = new QuestManager();
            JsonElement lastResetDateElement = root.get("lastResetDate");
            if (lastResetDateElement != null && !lastResetDateElement.isJsonNull()) {
                manager.setLastResetDate(LocalDate.parse(lastResetDateElement.getAsString()));
            }
            JsonArray activeQuests = root.getAsJsonArray("activeQuests");
            if (activeQuests != null) {
                for (JsonElement element : activeQuests) {
                    Quest quest = deserializeQuest(element.getAsJsonObject());
                    if (quest != null) {
                        manager.getActiveQuests().add(quest);
                    }
                }
            }
            JsonArray completedQuests = root.getAsJsonArray("completedQuests");
            if (completedQuests != null) {
                for (JsonElement element : completedQuests) {
                    Quest quest = deserializeQuest(element.getAsJsonObject());
                    if (quest != null) {
                        manager.getCompletedQuests().add(quest);
                    }
                }
            }
            return manager;
        }
        private JsonObject serializeQuest(Quest quest) {
            JsonObject object = new JsonObject();
            object.addProperty("questName", quest.getName().name());
            object.addProperty("currentProgress",
                    quest.getCondition() != null ? quest.getCondition().getCurrentProgress() : 0);
            object.addProperty("currentTierIndex", quest.getCurrentTierIndex());
            object.addProperty("completed", quest.isCompleted());
            object.addProperty("targetPlant", quest.getTargetPlant());
            object.addProperty("targetCategory", quest.getTargetCategory());
            object.addProperty("targetValue", quest.getTargetValue());
            return object;
        }
        private Quest deserializeQuest(JsonObject object) {
            if (object == null || !object.has("questName")) {
                return null;
            }
            try {
                QuestName questName = QuestName.valueOf(object.get("questName").getAsString());
                String targetPlant = getAsString(object, "targetPlant");
                String targetCategory = getAsString(object, "targetCategory");
                int targetValue = getAsInt(object, "targetValue", -1);
                int currentProgress = getAsInt(object, "currentProgress", 0);
                int currentTierIndex = getAsInt(object, "currentTierIndex", 0);
                boolean completed = getAsBoolean(object, "completed", false);
                Quest quest = recreateQuest(questName, targetPlant, targetCategory, targetValue);
                if (quest == null) {
                    return null;
                }
                restoreQuestState(quest, currentProgress, currentTierIndex, completed);
                return quest;
            } catch (Exception e) {
                return null;
            }
        }
        private Quest recreateQuest(QuestName name, String targetPlant, String targetCategory, int targetValue) {
            switch (name) {
                case DAILY_SUN_COLLECTOR:
                    return QuestFactory.createDailySunCollector();
                case CHAPTER_HUNTER:
                    return QuestFactory.createChapterHunter(targetValue != -1 ? targetValue : 1);
                case PLANT_EXPERT:
                    return QuestFactory.createPlantExpert(parsePlantName(targetPlant));
                case CACTUS_ONLY:
                    return QuestFactory.createCactusOnly();
                case ECONOMICAL_GARDENER:
                    return QuestFactory.createEconomicalGardener(targetValue != -1 ? targetValue : 3);
                case DEFENSE_MASTER:
                    return QuestFactory.createDefenseMaster();
                case QUICK_ACTION:
                    return QuestFactory.createQuickAction();
                case DEMOLITION_EXPERT:
                    return QuestFactory.createDemolitionExpert();
                case SYMMETRY:
                    return QuestFactory.createSymmetry();
                case FAMILY_HUNTER:
                    return QuestFactory.createFamilyHunter(parsePlantCategory(targetCategory));
                case LIMITED_BLOOM:
                    return QuestFactory.createLimitedBloom(parsePlantCategory(targetCategory));
                case DAY_OR_NIGHT:
                    return QuestFactory.createDayOrNight();
                case WIN_STREAK:
                    return QuestFactory.createWinStreak();
                case ALMOST_VICTORY:
                    return QuestFactory.createAlmostVictory();
                case OCD_MASTER:
                    return QuestFactory.createOcdMaster();
                case CLOUDY_DAY:
                    return QuestFactory.createCloudyDay();
                case ONE_COLUMN_LESS:
                    return QuestFactory.createOneColumnLess(targetValue != -1 ? targetValue : 8);
                case DEFENSELESS_ROW:
                    return QuestFactory.createDefenselessRow(targetValue != -1 ? targetValue : 4);
                case DEFENSELESS_CROSS:
                    return QuestFactory.createDefenselessCross(targetValue != -1 ? targetValue : 2);
                case LAWN_MOWER_TIME:
                    return QuestFactory.createLawnMowerTime(targetValue != -1 ? targetValue : 10);
                default:
                    return null;
            }
        }
        private void restoreQuestState(Quest quest, int currentProgress, int currentTierIndex, boolean completed) {
            for (int i = 0; i < currentTierIndex; i++) {
                quest.checkAndAdvanceTier();
            }
            if (quest.getCondition() != null && currentProgress > 0) {
                quest.getCondition().setCurrentProgress(currentProgress);
            }
            if (completed) {
                quest.checkAndAdvanceTier();
            }
        }
        private String getAsString(JsonObject object, String memberName) {
            JsonElement element = object.get(memberName);
            if (element == null || element.isJsonNull()) {
                return null;
            }
            return element.getAsString();
        }
        private int getAsInt(JsonObject object, String memberName, int defaultValue) {
            JsonElement element = object.get(memberName);
            if (element == null || element.isJsonNull()) {
                return defaultValue;
            }
            return element.getAsInt();
        }
        private boolean getAsBoolean(JsonObject object, String memberName, boolean defaultValue) {
            JsonElement element = object.get(memberName);
            if (element == null || element.isJsonNull()) {
                return defaultValue;
            }
            return element.getAsBoolean();
        }
        private PlantName parsePlantName(String raw) {
            if (raw != null) {
                try {
                    return PlantName.valueOf(raw);
                } catch (Exception ignored) {
                }
            }
            return PlantName.values()[0];
        }
        private PlantCategory parsePlantCategory(String raw) {
            if (raw != null) {
                try {
                    return PlantCategory.valueOf(raw);
                } catch (Exception ignored) {
                }
            }
            return PlantCategory.values()[0];
        }
    }
}

package app.service;
import app.enums.Menu;
import app.model.App;
import app.model.user.User;
import app.repository.Repository;
public class AppService {
    public static User getUserByUsername(String username)
    {
        for(User user : App.getAllUsers()) {
            if(user.getUsername().equals(username))
            {
                return user;
            }
        }
        return null;
    }
    public static void addUser(User user){
        App.getAllUsers().add(user);
        updateAllUsers();
    }
    public static void loginUser(User user, boolean stayLoggedIn) {
        App.setCurrentUser(user);
        if(stayLoggedIn) {
            Repository.saveLogin(user.getUsername());
        }
        else {
            Repository.clearLoggedInUser();
        }
    }
    public static void updateAllUsers() {
        Repository.saveAllUsers();
    }
    public static void logout() {
        App.setCurrentUser(null);
        App.setCurrentMenu(Menu.SignupMenu);
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\app\service\greenhouse\GreenhouseService.java ---
package app.service.greenhouse;
import app.enums.greenhouse.GreenhousePlantType;
import app.enums.greenhouse.PotStatus;
import app.enums.plant.PlantName;
import app.model.App;
import app.model.greenhouse.*;
import app.model.user.User;
import java.time.Duration;
import java.time.LocalDateTime;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Random;
public class GreenhouseService
{
    private final Greenhouse greenhouse;
    private static final Random RANDOM = new Random();
    public GreenhouseService(Greenhouse greenhouse) {
        this.greenhouse = greenhouse;
    }
    public String showGreenhouse() {
        StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
        sb.append("----------------------------------------------------\n");
        sb.append("|||||||||||||||||||||||||GREENHOUSE||||||||||||||||||||||||||||\n");
        sb.append("----------------------------------------------------\n");
        GreenhousePot[][] pots = greenhouse.getPots();
        int rows = pots.length;
        int cols = pots[0].length;
        for (int row = 0; row < rows; row++) {
            for (int col = 0; col < cols; col++) {
                GreenhousePot pot = pots[row][col];
                int x = col + 1;
                int y = row + 1;
                sb.append("(").append(x).append(",").append(y).append("): ");
                switch (pot.getPotStatus()) {
                    case LOCKED:
                        sb.append("locked\n");
                        break;
                    case EMPTY:
                        sb.append("empty\n");
                        break;
                    case READY:
                        GreenhousePlant plantReady = pot.getPlant();
                        if (plantReady instanceof MarigoldPlant marigold) {
                            sb.append("marigold (")
                                    .append(marigold.getColor())
                                    .append(") - ready\n");
                        } else if (plantReady instanceof BoostPlant boost) {
                            sb.append(boost.getPlant().name().toLowerCase())
                                    .append(" - ready\n");
                        } else {
                            sb.append("ready\n");
                        }
                        break;
                    case GROWING:
                        GreenhousePlant plant = pot.getPlant();
                        if (plant instanceof MarigoldPlant marigold) {
                            sb.append("marigold (")
                                    .append(marigold.getColor())
                                    .append(") - ")
                                    .append(plant.getRemainingTimeText())
                                    .append("\n");
                        } else if (plant instanceof BoostPlant boost) {
                            sb.append(boost.getPlant().name().toLowerCase())
                                    .append(" - ")
                                    .append(plant.getRemainingTimeText())
                                    .append("\n");
                        } else {
                            sb.append("growing - ")
                                    .append(plant.getRemainingTimeText())
                                    .append("\n");
                        }
                        break;
                    default:
                        sb.append("unknown\n");
                }
            }
        }
        sb.append("----------------------------------------------------");
        return sb.toString();
    }
    public boolean unlockPot(int x, int y)
    {
        if (x > greenhouse.getCol() || x < 1) return false;
        if (y > greenhouse.getRow() || y < 1) return false;
        GreenhousePot pot = greenhouse.getPot(x , y);
        if(pot.isUnlocked()) return true;
        int cost = pot.getCost();
        if (App.getCurrentUser().getCoins() < cost) return false;
        pot.unlock();
        App.getCurrentUser().deductCoins(cost);
        return true;
    }
    public boolean plant(int x, int y)
    {
        if (canPlant(x , y)) {
            GreenhousePot pot = greenhouse.getPot(x , y);
            pot.setPlant(generateRandomPlant(App.getCurrentUser()));
            return true;
        }
        return false;
    }
    public boolean collect(int x, int y)
    {
        if (x > greenhouse.getCol() || x < 1) return false;
        if (y > greenhouse.getRow() || y < 1) return false;
        GreenhousePot pot = greenhouse.getPot(x, y);
        if (!pot.isReady()) return false;
        GreenhousePlant plant = pot.getPlant();
        User user = App.getCurrentUser();
        user.addCoins(500);
        if (plant.getGreenhousePlantType() == GreenhousePlantType.BOOST) {
            BoostPlant boost = (BoostPlant) plant;
            if (!user.getPlantBoostStatus().containsKey(boost.getPlant()) ||
                    !user.getPlantBoostStatus().get(boost.getPlant())) {
                user.getPlantBoostStatus().put(boost.getPlant(), true);
            }
        }
        pot.collect();
        return true;
    }
    public boolean grow(int x, int y)
    {
        if (x > greenhouse.getCol() || x < 1) return false;
        if (y > greenhouse.getRow() || y < 1) return false;
        GreenhousePot pot = greenhouse.getPot(x, y);
        if (pot.getPotStatus()!= PotStatus.GROWING) return false;
        GreenhousePlant plant = pot.getPlant();
        int diamondCost = calculateGrowCost(plant);
        User user = App.getCurrentUser();
        if (user.getGems() < diamondCost ) return false;
        plant.completeGrowth();
        user.deductGems(diamondCost);
        pot.isPlantReady();
        return true;
    }
    public boolean grow(int x, int y , int hours)
    {
        if (hours <= 0) return false;
        if (x > greenhouse.getCol() || x < 1) return false;
        if (y > greenhouse.getRow() || y < 1) return false;
        GreenhousePot pot = greenhouse.getPot(x, y);
        if (pot.getPotStatus()!= PotStatus.GROWING) return false;
        GreenhousePlant plant = pot.getPlant();
        int diamondCost = calculateGrowCost(plant , hours);
        User user = App.getCurrentUser();
        if (user.getGems() < diamondCost ) return false;
        plant.reduceRemainingTime(hours);
        user.deductGems(diamondCost);
        pot.isPlantReady();
        return true;
    }
    public int calculateGrowCost(GreenhousePlant plant)
    {
        Duration remaining = plant.getRemainingTime();
        long secondes = remaining.toSeconds();
        if (secondes % 3600 == 0) return (int)secondes/3600;
        else return ((int)secondes/3600)+1;
    }
    public int calculateGrowCost(GreenhousePlant plant , int hours)
    {
        Duration remaining = plant.getRemainingTime();
        long secondes = Math.min(hours* 3600L, remaining.toSeconds());
        if (secondes % 3600 == 0) return (int)secondes/3600;
        else return ((int)secondes/3600)+1;
    }
    public boolean canPlant(int x, int y)
    {
        if (x > greenhouse.getCol() || x < 1) return false;
        if (y > greenhouse.getRow() || y < 1) return false;
        GreenhousePot pot = greenhouse.getPot(x , y);
        return pot.isEmpty();
    }
    public GreenhousePlant generateRandomPlant(User user) {
        int chance = RANDOM.nextInt(100);
        if (chance < 50) {
            return new MarigoldPlant(LocalDateTime.now());
        }
        else {
            List<PlantName> eligiblePlants = user.getUnlockedPlants().stream()
                    .filter(PlantName::hasPlantFoodEffect)
                    .toList();
            if (eligiblePlants.isEmpty()) {
                return new MarigoldPlant(LocalDateTime.now());
            }
            PlantName randomType = eligiblePlants.get(RANDOM.nextInt(eligiblePlants.size()));
            return new BoostPlant(randomType, LocalDateTime.now());
        }
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\app\service\plantselection\PlantSelectionService.java ---
package app.service.plantselection;
import app.enums.plant.PlantName;
import app.model.App;
import app.model.game.PendingGameSession;
import game.entity.plant.Plant;
import game.entity.plant.PlantCooldown;
import game.entity.plant.PlantDefinition;
import game.entity.plant.ability.PlantAbility;
import game.entity.registry.PlantRegistry;
import game.service.plant.PlantFactory;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;
public class PlantSelectionService {
    private static final int TABLE_WIDTH = 100;
    private String padRight(String s, int width) {
        if (s.length() >= width) return s.substring(0, width);
        return s + " ".repeat(width - s.length());
    }
    private String truncate(String s, int maxLength) {
        if (s.length() <= maxLength) return s;
        return s.substring(0, maxLength - 3) + "...";
    }
    public String showAllPlants() {
        Map<PlantName, PlantDefinition> definitions = PlantRegistry.getDefinitions();
        if (definitions == null || definitions.isEmpty()) {
            return "╔" + "═".repeat(TABLE_WIDTH - 2) + "╗\n" +
                    "║" + padRight("No plants registered.", TABLE_WIDTH - 2) + "║\n" +
                    "╚" + "═".repeat(TABLE_WIDTH - 2) + "╝";
        }
        StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
        String borderTop = "╔" + "═".repeat(TABLE_WIDTH - 2) + "╗\n";
        String separator = "╠" + "═".repeat(TABLE_WIDTH - 2) + "╣\n";
        String borderBottom = "╚" + "═".repeat(TABLE_WIDTH - 2) + "╝";
        sb.append(borderTop);
        sb.append("║").append(padRight("🌱  PLANT_PLANT COLLECTION  🌱", TABLE_WIDTH - 2)).append("║\n");
        sb.append(separator);
        int index = 1;
        for (PlantDefinition def : definitions.values()) {
            Plant plant = PlantFactory.create(def.getName());
            String name = def.getName() != null ? def.getName().name() : "Unknown";
            String line1 = String.format("%-2d. %-20s │ Cost: %-4d │ HP: %-5d │ Cooldown: %-4d │ Damage: %-4d",
                    index++,
                    truncate(name, 20),
                    plant.getSunCost(),
                    plant.getHealth().getMaxHealth(),
                    PlantCooldown.getCoolDown(plant.getDefinition().getName()),
                    plant.getAttackDamage()
            );
            sb.append("║").append(padRight(line1, TABLE_WIDTH - 2)).append("║\n");
            String type = def.getName() != null ? def.getName().name() : "N/A";
            String category = def.getCategory() != null ? def.getCategory().name() : "N/A";
            String line2 = String.format("Type: %-12s │ Category: %-16s",
                    truncate(type, 12),
                    truncate(category, 16)
            );
            sb.append("║").append(padRight(line2, TABLE_WIDTH - 2)).append("║\n");
            String tagsStr = "None";
            if (def.getTags() != null && !def.getTags().tags().isEmpty()) {
                tagsStr = def.getTags().toString();
            }
            String line3 = String.format("Tags: %-68s", truncate(tagsStr, 68));
            sb.append("║").append(padRight(line3, TABLE_WIDTH - 2)).append("║\n");
            String abilitiesStr = "None";
            if (def.getAbilities() != null && !def.getAbilities().isEmpty()) {
                List<String> abilityNames = new ArrayList<>();
                for (PlantAbility ability : def.getAbilities()) {
                    abilityNames.add(ability.getClass().getSimpleName());
                }
                abilitiesStr = String.join(", ", abilityNames);
            }
            String line4 = String.format("Abilities: %-60s", truncate(abilitiesStr, 60));
            sb.append("║").append(padRight(line4, TABLE_WIDTH - 2)).append("║\n");
            if (index - 1 < definitions.size()) {
                sb.append(separator);
            }
        }
        sb.append(borderBottom);
        return sb.toString();
    }
    public String showAvailablePlants() {
        PendingGameSession session = App.getPendingGameSession();
        List<PlantName> availablePlants = new ArrayList<>();
        if (session.getSelectedChapter() != null) {
            availablePlants = session.getSelectedChapter().getAvailablePlants();
        }
        if (availablePlants == null || availablePlants.isEmpty()) {
            return "╔" + "═".repeat(TABLE_WIDTH - 2) + "╗\n" +
                    "║" + padRight("No plants available.", TABLE_WIDTH - 2) + "║\n" +
                    "╚" + "═".repeat(TABLE_WIDTH - 2) + "╝";
        }
        StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
        String borderTop = "╔" + "═".repeat(TABLE_WIDTH - 2) + "╗\n";
        String separator = "╠" + "═".repeat(TABLE_WIDTH - 2) + "╣\n";
        String borderBottom = "╚" + "═".repeat(TABLE_WIDTH - 2) + "╝";
        sb.append(borderTop);
        sb.append("║").append(padRight("🌱  PLANT_PLANT COLLECTION  🌱", TABLE_WIDTH - 2)).append("║\n");
        sb.append(separator);
        int index = 1;
        for (PlantName plantName : availablePlants) {
            PlantDefinition def = PlantRegistry.get(plantName);
            Plant plant = PlantFactory.create(def.getName());
            if (def == null) continue;
            String name = def.getName() != null ? def.getName().name() : "Unknown";
            String line1 = String.format("%-2d. %-20s │ Cost: %-4d │ HP: %-5d │ Cooldown: %-4d │ Damage: %-4d",
                    index++,
                    truncate(name, 20),
                    plant.getSunCost(),
                    plant.getHealth().getMaxHealth(),
                    PlantCooldown.getCoolDown(plant.getDefinition().getName()),
                    plant.getAttackDamage()
            );
            sb.append("║").append(padRight(line1, TABLE_WIDTH - 2)).append("║\n");
            String category = def.getCategory() != null ? def.getCategory().name() : "N/A";
            String line2 = String.format("Category: %-16s", truncate(category, 16));
            sb.append("║").append(padRight(line2, TABLE_WIDTH - 2)).append("║\n");
            String tagsStr = "None";
            if (def.getTags() != null && !def.getTags().tags().isEmpty()) {
                tagsStr = def.getTags().toString();
            }
            String line3 = String.format("Tags: %-68s", truncate(tagsStr, 68));
            sb.append("║").append(padRight(line3, TABLE_WIDTH - 2)).append("║\n");
            String abilitiesStr = "None";
            if (def.getAbilities() != null && !def.getAbilities().isEmpty()) {
                List<String> abilityNames = new ArrayList<>();
                for (PlantAbility ability : def.getAbilities()) {
                    abilityNames.add(ability.getClass().getSimpleName());
                }
                abilitiesStr = String.join(", ", abilityNames);
            }
            String line4 = String.format("Abilities: %-60s", truncate(abilitiesStr, 60));
            sb.append("║").append(padRight(line4, TABLE_WIDTH - 2)).append("║\n");
            if (index - 1 < availablePlants.size()) {
                sb.append(separator);
            }
        }
        sb.append(borderBottom);
        return sb.toString();
    }
    public boolean isPlantInRegistry(PlantName plantName) {
        if (plantName == null) return false;
        return PlantRegistry.getDefinitions().containsKey(plantName);
    }
    public boolean isPlantAvailableInGame(PlantName plantName) {
        PendingGameSession session = App.getPendingGameSession();
        if (session.getSelectedChapter() == null) return false;
        List<PlantName> available = session.getSelectedChapter().getAvailablePlants();
        return available != null && available.contains(plantName);
    }
    public boolean isPlantAlreadySelected(PlantName plantName) {
        if (plantName == null) return false;
        return App.getPendingGameSession().getSelectedPlants().contains(plantName);
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\app\validator\AppValidator.java ---
package app.validator;
import app.enums.commands.ValidatingCommands;
public class AppValidator {
    public static boolean isUsernameCorrect(String username) {
        return ValidatingCommands.USERNAME_FORMAT.matches(username);
    }
    public static boolean isPasswordCorrect(String password){
        return ValidatingCommands.PASSWORD_FORMAT.matches(password);
    }
    public static boolean isNicknameCorrect(String nickName) {
        return ValidatingCommands.NICKNAME_FORMAT.matches(nickName);
    }
    public static boolean isEmailCorrect(String email) {
        if (email == null || email.isEmpty()) {
            return false;
        }
        if (email.contains("..")) {
            return false;
        }
        int firstAt = email.indexOf('@');
        int lastAt = email.lastIndexOf('@');
        if (firstAt == -1 || firstAt != lastAt) {
            return false;
        }
        String localPart = email.substring(0, firstAt);
        if (localPart.length() < 5) {
            return false;
        }
        return ValidatingCommands.EMAIL_FORMAT.matches(email);
    }
    public static boolean isGenderValid(String gender) {
        gender = gender.toLowerCase();
        return ValidatingCommands.GENDER_FORMAT.matches(gender);
    }
    public static boolean isNumberValid(String number) {
        number = number.trim();
        try {
            Integer.parseInt(number);
        }
        catch (Exception e) {
            return false;
        }
        return true;
    }
    public static boolean isPasswordStrong(String password) {
        return ValidatingCommands.PASSWORD_STRENGTH.matches(password);
    }
    public static boolean isCoordinateValid(String posX, String posY) {
        posX = posX.trim();
        posY = posY.trim();
        float x,y;
        try {
            x = Float.parseFloat(posX);
            y = Float.parseFloat(posY);
        }
        catch (Exception e) {
            return false;
        }
        return !(x < 0) && !(y < 0);
    }
}

package app.view;
import app.controller.menu.GameMenuController;
import app.controller.menu.MenuController;
import app.controller.menu.PlantSelectionController;
import app.enums.Menu;
import app.model.App;
import app.model.user.User;
import app.repository.Repository;
import app.view.game.PlayScreen;
import game.component.AssetManager;
import game.entity.registry.PlantDefinitionLoader;
import game.service.Zombie.ZombieJsonParserService;
import game.service.quest.QuestLoad;
public class AppLoop {
    private static GameMenuController gameMenuController = new GameMenuController();
    public static void init() {
        PlantDefinitionLoader.load();
        ZombieJsonParserService.loadZombiesFromJson("zombies.json");
        ZombieJsonParserService.loadArmorsFromJson("armor.json");
        User loggedInUser = Repository.loadLoginedUser();
        App.setCurrentUser(loggedInUser);
        if (loggedInUser != null) {
            QuestLoad.load();
            App.setCurrentMenu(Menu.GameMenu);
            gameMenuController.manageEnteringChapter("1.1");
            PlantSelectionController.addPlant("sunflower");
            PlantSelectionController.addPlant("peashooter");
            PlantSelectionController.addPlant("Repeater");
            PlantSelectionController.addPlant("Threepeater");
            PlantSelectionController.addPlant("Citron");
            PlantSelectionController.addPlant("Cactus");
            PlantSelectionController.startGame();
            System.out.println("Welcome back " + loggedInUser.getNickname());
        } else {
            App.setCurrentMenu(Menu.SignupMenu);
        }
        Thread consoleThread = new Thread(() -> {
            while (true) {
                AppMenu menu = MenuController.getMenu(App.getCurrentMenu());
                if (menu != null) {
                    menu.checkCommand();
                }
            }
        });
        consoleThread.setDaemon(true);
        consoleThread.start();
    }
    public static void update() {}
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\app\view\AppMenu.java ---
package app.view;
import app.controller.menu.MenuController;
import app.enums.commands.GlobalCommand;
import common.Result;
import java.util.regex.Matcher;
public interface AppMenu {
    void checkCommand();
    default void invalidCommand() {
        System.out.println("Invalid command.");
    }
    static boolean menuEnterMatches(String input)
    {
        Matcher menuenterMatcher = GlobalCommand.CHANGE_MENU.getMatcher(input);
        if (menuenterMatcher.matches())
        {
            Result result = MenuController.menuEnter(menuenterMatcher);
            System.out.println(result.message());
            return true;
        }
        return false;
    }
    static boolean reviewCommonCommandsMatches(String input)
    {
        return menuEnterMatches(input)|| showMenuMatches(input)|| menuExitMatches(input);
    }
    static boolean showMenuMatches(String input)
    {
        Matcher menuShowMatcher = GlobalCommand.SHOW_MENU.getMatcher(input);
        if (menuShowMatcher.matches())
        {
            Result result = MenuController.showMenu();
            System.out.println(result.message());
            return true;
        }
        return false;
    }
    private static boolean menuExitMatches(String input)
    {
        Matcher menuExitMatcher = GlobalCommand.EXIT_MENU.getMatcher(input);
        if (menuExitMatcher.matches())
        {
            Result result = MenuController.menuExit(menuExitMatcher);
            System.out.println(result.message());
            return true;
        }
        return false;
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\app\view\CollectionMenu.java ---
package app.view;
import app.controller.menu.CollectionMenuController;
import app.enums.commands.CollectionMenuCommands;
import common.Input;
import java.util.regex.Matcher;
public class CollectionMenu implements AppMenu {
    private final CollectionMenuController controller = new CollectionMenuController();
    @Override
    public void checkCommand() {
        String input = Input.nextLine();
        if (AppMenu.reviewCommonCommandsMatches(input)) {
            return;
        }
        if (CollectionMenuCommands.ShowPlants.matches(input)) {
            System.out.print(controller.showPlants());
            return;
        }
        if (CollectionMenuCommands.ShowAllPlants.matches(input)) {
            System.out.print(controller.showAllPlants());
            return;
        }
        if (CollectionMenuCommands.ShowZombies.matches(input)) {
            System.out.println(controller.showZombies());
            return;
        }
        if (CollectionMenuCommands.ShowAllZombies.matches(input)) {
            System.out.print(controller.showAllZombies());
            return;
        }
        Matcher matcher = CollectionMenuCommands.ShowPlant.getMatcher(input);
        if (matcher.matches()) {
            System.out.println(controller.showPlant(matcher.group("plantName")));
            return;
        }
        matcher = CollectionMenuCommands.ShowZombie.getMatcher(input);
        if (matcher.matches()) {
            System.out.println(controller.showZombie(matcher.group("zombieName")));
            return;
        }
        matcher = CollectionMenuCommands.UpgradePlant.getMatcher(input);
        if (matcher.matches()) {
            System.out.println(controller.upgradePlant(matcher.group("plantName")));
            return;
        }
        matcher = CollectionMenuCommands.PurchasePlant.getMatcher(input);
        if (matcher.matches()) {
            System.out.println(controller.purchasePlant(matcher.group("plantName")));
            return;
        }
        invalidCommand();
    }
}

    package app.view;
    import app.controller.menu.GameMenuController;
    import app.controller.menu.MenuController;
    import app.enums.commands.GameMenuCommands;
    import app.enums.commands.GlobalCommand;
    import com.badlogic.gdx.Gdx;
    import common.Input;
    import common.Result;
    import java.util.regex.Matcher;
    public class GameMenu implements AppMenu{
        private final GameMenuController controller = new GameMenuController();
        private boolean menuEnterMatches(String input)
        {
            Matcher menuenterMatcher = GlobalCommand.CHANGE_MENU.getMatcher(input);
            Matcher menuMatcher = GlobalCommand.CHANGE_MENU_IN_GAME_MENU.getMatcher(input);
            if (menuenterMatcher.matches())
            {
                Result result = MenuController.menuEnter(menuenterMatcher);
                System.out.println(result.message());
                return true;
            }
            if (menuMatcher.matches())
            {
                Result result = MenuController.menuEnter(menuMatcher);
                System.out.println(result.message());
                return true;
            }
            return false;
        }
        @Override
        public void checkCommand() {
            String input = Input.nextLine();
            if (menuEnterMatches(input)) return;
            if (AppMenu.showMenuMatches(input) || AppMenu.reviewCommonCommandsMatches(input)) return;
            if (GameMenuCommands.EnterChapter.matches(input)) {
                String chapterName = GameMenuCommands.EnterChapter.getGroup(input,"chaptername");
                handleEnteringChapter(chapterName);
                return;
            }
            if (GameMenuCommands.CoinWallet.matches(input)) {
                System.out.println(controller.getCoins());
                return;
            }
            if (GameMenuCommands.GemWallet.matches(input)) {
                System.out.println(controller.getGems());
                return;
            }
            if (GameMenuCommands.CheatAddCoin.matches(input)) {
                String amount = GameMenuCommands.CheatAddCoin.getGroup(input,"amount");
                String currency = GameMenuCommands.CheatAddCoin.getGroup(input, "currency");
                System.out.println(controller.manageCheatCode(amount, currency));
                return;
            }
            invalidCommand();
        }
        private void handleEnteringChapter(String chapterName){
            Gdx.app.postRunnable(() -> {
                Result result = controller.manageEnteringChapter(chapterName);
                System.out.println(result);
            });
        }
    }

package app.view;
import app.controller.greenhouse.GreenhouseController;
import app.enums.commands.GreenhouseCommands;
import common.Input;
import common.Result;
public class Greenhouse implements AppMenu {
    @Override
    public void checkCommand() {
        String input = Input.nextLine();
        if (AppMenu.reviewCommonCommandsMatches(input)) return;
        if (GreenhouseCommands.SHOW_GREENHOUSE.matches(input)) {
            handleShowGreenhouse();
        } else if (GreenhouseCommands.UNLOCK_POT.matches(input)) {
            handleUnlockPot(input);
        } else if (GreenhouseCommands.PLANT_POT.matches(input)) {
            handlePlantPot(input);
        } else if (GreenhouseCommands.COLLECT.matches(input)) {
            handleCollect(input);
        } else if (GreenhouseCommands.GROW.matches(input)) {
            handleGrowWithHours(input);
        } else if (GreenhouseCommands.Full_GROW.matches(input)) {
            handleGrow(input);
        } else {
            invalidCommand();
        }
    }
    private void handleShowGreenhouse() {
        Result result = GreenhouseController.showGreenhouse();
        System.out.println(result.message());
    }
    private void handleUnlockPot(String input) {
        String xCoord = GreenhouseCommands.UNLOCK_POT.getGroup(input, "x");
        String yCoord = GreenhouseCommands.UNLOCK_POT.getGroup(input, "y");
        Result result = GreenhouseController.unlockPot(xCoord, yCoord);
        System.out.println(result.message());
    }
    private void handlePlantPot(String input) {
        String xCoord = GreenhouseCommands.PLANT_POT.getGroup(input, "x");
        String yCoord = GreenhouseCommands.PLANT_POT.getGroup(input, "y");
        Result result = GreenhouseController.plantPot(xCoord, yCoord);
        System.out.println(result.message());
    }
    private void handleCollect(String input) {
        String xCoord = GreenhouseCommands.COLLECT.getGroup(input, "x");
        String yCoord = GreenhouseCommands.COLLECT.getGroup(input, "y");
        Result result = GreenhouseController.collect(xCoord, yCoord);
        System.out.println(result.message());
    }
    private void handleGrow(String input) {
        String xCoord = GreenhouseCommands.Full_GROW.getGroup(input, "x");
        String yCoord = GreenhouseCommands.Full_GROW.getGroup(input, "y");
        Result result = GreenhouseController.grow(xCoord, yCoord);
        System.out.println(result.message());
    }
    private void handleGrowWithHours(String input) {
        String xCoord = GreenhouseCommands.GROW.getGroup(input, "x");
        String yCoord = GreenhouseCommands.GROW.getGroup(input, "y");
        String hours = GreenhouseCommands.GROW.getGroup(input, "hours");
        Result result = GreenhouseController.grow(xCoord, yCoord, hours);
        System.out.println(result.message());
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\app\view\LoginMenu.java ---
    package app.view;
    import app.controller.menu.LoginMenuController;
    import app.controller.menu.MenuNavigator;
    import app.enums.commands.LoginMenuCommands;
    import common.Input;
    import common.Result;
    import java.util.Scanner;
    public class LoginMenu implements AppMenu{
        private final LoginMenuController controller = new LoginMenuController();
        @Override
        public void checkCommand() {
            String input = Input.nextLine();
            if (AppMenu.reviewCommonCommandsMatches(input)) return;
            if(LoginMenuCommands.FORGOT_PASSWORD.matches(input)) {
                handleForgotPassword(input, Input.getScanner());
            }
            else if(LoginMenuCommands.LOGIN.matches(input)) {
                handleLogin(input);
            }
            else
            {
                invalidCommand();
            }
        }
        private void handleLogin(String input) {
            String username = LoginMenuCommands.LOGIN.getGroup(input, "username");
            String password = LoginMenuCommands.LOGIN.getGroup(input, "password");
            String flag = LoginMenuCommands.LOGIN.getGroup(input, "stay");
            Result result = controller.manageLogin(username, password, flag != null);
            System.out.println(result.message());
            if (result.success()) MenuNavigator.successfulLogin(result.success());
        }
        private void handleForgotPassword(String input, Scanner scanner){
            String username  = LoginMenuCommands.FORGOT_PASSWORD.getGroup(input, "username");
            String email = LoginMenuCommands.FORGOT_PASSWORD.getGroup(input, "email");
            Result step1Result = controller.manageForgotPassword(username, email);
            System.out.println(step1Result.message());
            if (!step1Result.success()) return;
            String answerInput = scanner.nextLine().trim();
            if (!LoginMenuCommands.ANSWER.matches(answerInput)) {
                System.out.println("Invalid command. Returning to menu.");
                return;
            }
            String answer = LoginMenuCommands.ANSWER.getGroup(answerInput, "answer").trim();
            Result step2Result = controller.checkSecurityAnswer(username, answer);
            System.out.println(step2Result.message());
            if (!step2Result.success()) return;
            String newPassword = scanner.nextLine().trim();
            Result finalResult = controller.updatePassword(username, newPassword);
            System.out.println(finalResult.message());
        }
    }

package app.view;
import app.controller.menu.NewsMenuController;
import app.enums.commands.NewsMenuCommands;
import common.Input;
public class NewsMenu implements AppMenu{
    private final NewsMenuController controller = new NewsMenuController();
    @Override
    public void checkCommand() {
        String input = Input.nextLine();
        if (AppMenu.reviewCommonCommandsMatches(input)) return;
        if (NewsMenuCommands.ShowUnreadNews.matches(input)) {
            System.out.print(controller.showUnreadNews());
            return;
        }
        if (NewsMenuCommands.ShowAllNews.matches(input)) {
            System.out.print(controller.showAllNews());
            return;
        }
        invalidCommand();
    }
}

package app.view;
import app.controller.menu.ProfileMenuController;
import app.enums.commands.ProfileMenuCommands;
import common.Input;
import common.Result;
public class ProfileMenu implements AppMenu{
    private final ProfileMenuController controller = new ProfileMenuController();
    @Override
    public void checkCommand() {
        String input = Input.nextLine();
        if (AppMenu.reviewCommonCommandsMatches(input)) return;
        if(ProfileMenuCommands.CHANGE_USERNAME.matches(input)) {
            handleChangingUsername(input);
        }
        else if(ProfileMenuCommands.CHANGE_EMAIL.matches(input)) {
            handleChangingEmail(input);
        }
        else if(ProfileMenuCommands.CHANGE_NICKNAME.matches(input)) {
            handleChangingNickname(input);
        }
        else if(ProfileMenuCommands.CHANGE_PASSWORD.matches(input)) {
            handleChangingPassword(input);
        }
        else if(ProfileMenuCommands.SHOW_INFO.matches(input)) {
            handleShowUserInfo();
        }
        else
        {
            invalidCommand();
        }
    }
    private void handleChangingUsername(String input){
        String username = ProfileMenuCommands.CHANGE_USERNAME.getGroup(input, "username");
        Result result = controller.manageChangeUsername(username);
        System.out.println(result.message());
    }
    private void handleChangingNickname(String input){
        String nickname = ProfileMenuCommands.CHANGE_NICKNAME.getGroup(input, "nickname");
        Result result = controller.manageChangeNickname(nickname);
        System.out.println(result.message());
    }
    private void handleChangingEmail(String input){
        String email = ProfileMenuCommands.CHANGE_EMAIL.getGroup(input, "email");
        Result result = controller.manageChangeEmail(email);
        System.out.println(result.message());
    }
    private void handleChangingPassword(String input){
        String newPassword = ProfileMenuCommands.CHANGE_PASSWORD.getGroup(input, "new");
        String oldPassword = ProfileMenuCommands.CHANGE_PASSWORD.getGroup(input, "old");
        Result result = controller.manageChangePassword(newPassword, oldPassword);
        System.out.println(result.message());
    }
    private void handleShowUserInfo(){
        Result result = controller.manageShowingInfo();
        System.out.println(result.message());
    }
}

package app.view;
import app.controller.menu.SettingsMenuController;
import app.enums.commands.SettingsMenuCommands;
import common.Input;
import common.Result;
import java.util.regex.Matcher;
public class SettingsMenu implements AppMenu{
    private final SettingsMenuController controller = new SettingsMenuController();
    @Override
    public void checkCommand() {
        String input = Input.nextLine();
        if (AppMenu.reviewCommonCommandsMatches(input)) return;
        Matcher matcher;
        if ((matcher = SettingsMenuCommands.ChangeDifficulty.getMatcher(input)) != null) {
            handleChangingDifficulty(matcher);
            return;
        }
        invalidCommand();
    }
    private void handleChangingDifficulty(Matcher matcher){
        try {
            int level = Integer.parseInt(matcher.group("level"));
            Result result = controller.changeDifficulty(level);
            System.out.println(result);
        } catch (Exception e) {
            invalidCommand();
        }
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\app\view\ShopMenu.java ---
package app.view;
import app.controller.shop.ShopController;
import app.enums.commands.ShopCommands;
import common.Input;
import java.util.regex.Matcher;
public class ShopMenu implements AppMenu {
    private final ShopController controller = new ShopController();
    @Override
    public void checkCommand() {
        String input = Input.nextLine();
        if (AppMenu.reviewCommonCommandsMatches(input)) return;
        Matcher matcher;
        if (ShopCommands.List.matches(input.trim())) {
            System.out.print(controller.showList());
            return;
        }
        if (ShopCommands.Daily.matches(input)) {
            System.out.println(controller.showDaily());
            return;
        }
        if (ShopCommands.BuyDaily.matches(input)) {
            System.out.println(controller.buyDaily());
            return;
        }
        if ((matcher = ShopCommands.Buy.getMatcher(input)) != null) {
            String itemID = matcher.group("itemID");
            int count = 0;
            try {
                count = Integer.parseInt(matcher.group("count"));
            } catch (Exception e) {
                System.out.println("Wrong Number");
                return;
            }
            String PlantName = matcher.group("type");
            System.out.println(controller.buy(itemID, count, PlantName));
            return;
        }
        invalidCommand();
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\app\view\SignupMenu.java ---
package app.view;
import app.controller.menu.MenuNavigator;
import app.controller.menu.SignupMenuController;
import app.enums.Menu;
import app.enums.commands.SignupMenuCommands;
import common.Input;
import common.Result;
import java.util.Scanner;
public class SignupMenu implements AppMenu{
    private final SignupMenuController controller = new SignupMenuController();
    @Override
    public void checkCommand() {
        String input = Input.nextLine();
        if (AppMenu.reviewCommonCommandsMatches(input)) return;
        if(SignupMenuCommands.REGISTER.matches(input))
        {
            handleRegister(input, Input.getScanner());
        }
        else
        {
            invalidCommand();
        }
    }
    private void handleRegister(String input, Scanner scanner) {
        String username = SignupMenuCommands.REGISTER.getGroup(input, "username");
        String password = SignupMenuCommands.REGISTER.getGroup(input, "password1");
        String passwordConfirm = SignupMenuCommands.REGISTER.getGroup(input, "password2");
        String nickname = SignupMenuCommands.REGISTER.getGroup(input, "nickname");
        String email = SignupMenuCommands.REGISTER.getGroup(input, "email");
        String gender = SignupMenuCommands.REGISTER.getGroup(input, "gender");
        username = username.trim();
        password = password.trim();
        passwordConfirm = passwordConfirm.trim();
        nickname = nickname.trim();
        email = email.trim();
        gender = gender.trim();
        Result result = controller.manageRegister
                (username,
                password,
                passwordConfirm,
                nickname,
                email,
                gender);
        System.out.println(result.message().trim());
        if(!result.success()) {return;}
        while (true) {
            String securityQInput = scanner.nextLine().trim();
            if(SignupMenuCommands.PICK_QUESTION.matches(securityQInput))
            {
                boolean success = handleFinalizingRegister
                        (securityQInput,
                                username,
                                password,
                                passwordConfirm,
                                nickname,
                                email,
                                gender);
                if(success) {
                    MenuNavigator.goTo(Menu.LoginMenu);
                    break;
                }
            }
            else
            {
                System.out.println("Invalid format, Please use this format: \n" +
                        "pick question -q <question_number> -a <answer> -c <answer_confirm>");
            }
        }
    }
    private boolean handleFinalizingRegister(String input,
                                             String username,
                                             String password,
                                             String passwordConfirm,
                                             String nickname,
                                             String email,
                                             String gender) {
        String questionNumber = SignupMenuCommands.PICK_QUESTION.getGroup(input, "number");
        String answer = SignupMenuCommands.PICK_QUESTION.getGroup(input, "answer");
        String answerConfirm = SignupMenuCommands.PICK_QUESTION.getGroup(input, "confirm");
        questionNumber = questionNumber.trim();
        answer = answer.trim();
        answerConfirm = answerConfirm.trim();
        Result result = controller.finalizeRegister
                        (username,
                         password,
                         passwordConfirm,
                         nickname,
                         email,
                         gender,
                         questionNumber,
                         answer,
                         answerConfirm);
        System.out.println(result.message().trim());
        return result.success();
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\app\view\game\PlantSelectionMenu.java ---
package app.view.game;
import app.controller.menu.PlantSelectionController;
import app.enums.commands.PlantSelectionMenuCommand;
import app.view.AppMenu;
import common.Input;
import common.Result;
public class PlantSelectionMenu implements AppMenu {
    @Override
    public void checkCommand() {
        String input = Input.nextLine();
        if (AppMenu.reviewCommonCommandsMatches(input)) return;
        if (PlantSelectionMenuCommand.SHOW_ALL.matches(input)) {
            handleShowAll();
        } else if (PlantSelectionMenuCommand.SHOW_AVAILABLE.matches(input)) {
            handleShowAvailable();
        } else if (PlantSelectionMenuCommand.ADD_PLANT.matches(input)) {
            handleAddPlant(input);
        } else if (PlantSelectionMenuCommand.REMOVE_PLANT.matches(input)) {
            handleRemovePlant(input);
        } else if (PlantSelectionMenuCommand.BOOST_PLANT.matches(input)) {
            handleBoostPlant(input);
        } else if (PlantSelectionMenuCommand.START_GAME.matches(input)) {
            handleStartGame();
        } else if (PlantSelectionMenuCommand.EXIT_GAME.matches(input)) {
            handleExit();
        } else {
            invalidCommand();
        }
    }
    private void handleShowAll() {
        Result result = PlantSelectionController.showAllPlants();
        System.out.println(result.message());
    }
    private void handleShowAvailable() {
        Result result = PlantSelectionController.showAvailablePlants();
        System.out.println(result.message());
    }
    private void handleAddPlant(String input) {
        String type = PlantSelectionMenuCommand.ADD_PLANT.getGroup(input, "type");
        Result result = PlantSelectionController.addPlant(type);
        System.out.println(result.message());
    }
    private void handleRemovePlant(String input) {
        String type = PlantSelectionMenuCommand.REMOVE_PLANT.getGroup(input, "type");
        Result result = PlantSelectionController.removePlant(type);
        System.out.println(result.message());
    }
    private void handleBoostPlant(String input) {
        String type = PlantSelectionMenuCommand.BOOST_PLANT.getGroup(input, "type");
        Result result = PlantSelectionController.boostPlant(type);
        System.out.println(result.message());
    }
    private void handleStartGame() {
        Result result = PlantSelectionController.startGame();
        System.out.println(result.message());
    }
    private void handleExit() {
        Result result = PlantSelectionController.manageExit();
        System.out.println(result.message());
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\app\view\game\PlayScreen.java ---
package app.view.game;
import app.controller.game.PlayScreenController;
import app.enums.plant.PlantName;
import app.model.App;
import app.view.AppMenu;
import app.view.hud.NormalHud;
import com.badlogic.gdx.Gdx;
import com.badlogic.gdx.InputMultiplexer;
import com.badlogic.gdx.Screen;
import com.badlogic.gdx.graphics.GL20;
import com.badlogic.gdx.graphics.OrthographicCamera;
import com.badlogic.gdx.maps.MapLayer;
import com.badlogic.gdx.maps.MapObjects;
import com.badlogic.gdx.maps.objects.RectangleMapObject;
import com.badlogic.gdx.maps.tiled.TiledMap;
import com.badlogic.gdx.maps.tiled.renderers.OrthogonalTiledMapRenderer;
import com.badlogic.gdx.math.Rectangle;
import com.badlogic.gdx.physics.box2d.Box2DDebugRenderer;
import com.badlogic.gdx.scenes.scene2d.Stage;
import com.badlogic.gdx.utils.Array;
import com.badlogic.gdx.utils.viewport.Viewport;
import game.component.AssetManager;
import game.component.physic.PhysicService;
import game.engine.RealTimeGameEngine;
import game.entity.zombie.data.properties.Property;
import game.world.chapter.Chapter;
import game.world.playground.Cell;
import game.world.playground.Grid;
import graphics.GraphicsEngine;
import graphics.map.hover.CellHoverRenderer;
import plants_vs_zombies.PlantsVSZombies;
import java.util.HashSet;
import java.util.Set;
import java.util.stream.Collectors;
public class PlayScreen implements Screen, AppMenu {
    private Viewport viewport;
    private Stage stage;
    private OrthographicCamera camera;
    private PlantsVSZombies game;
    private RealTimeGameEngine engine;
    private OrthogonalTiledMapRenderer renderer;
    private Rectangle mapBounds;
    private GraphicsEngine graphicsEngine;
    private Box2DDebugRenderer b2dr;
    private InputMultiplexer inputMultiplexer;
    private PlayScreenController controller;
    private NormalHud hud;
    private CellHoverRenderer cellHoverRenderer;
    public PlayScreen(Chapter chapter) {
        if (chapter == null) {
            return;
        }
        game = PlantsVSZombies.getPvz();
        renderer = new OrthogonalTiledMapRenderer(chapter.getMap());
        mapBounds = extractMapBounds(chapter.getMap());
        hud = new NormalHud();
        cellHoverRenderer = new CellHoverRenderer();
        initializeGraphics();
        initializeEngine(chapter);
        hud.initialize(engine);
        controller = new PlayScreenController(this);
        initializeInputHandling();
        b2dr = new Box2DDebugRenderer();
    }
    @Override
    public void show() {
        Gdx.input.setInputProcessor(inputMultiplexer);
    }
    @Override
    public void render(float delta) {
        clearScreen();
        camera.update();
        engine.update(delta);
        controller.update(delta);
        renderer.setView(camera);
        renderer.render();
        renderHover(delta);
        renderPhysics();
        game.batch.setProjectionMatrix(camera.combined);
        game.batch.begin();
        graphicsEngine.updateAndRender(game.batch, delta);
        game.batch.end();
        stage.act(delta);
        hud.render();
    }
    private void clearScreen() {
        Gdx.gl.glClearColor(0, 0, 0, 1);
        Gdx.gl.glClear(GL20.GL_COLOR_BUFFER_BIT);
    }
    private void renderHover(float delta) {
        if (!controller.isPlantingMode()) {
            return;
        }
        Cell hoveredCell = controller.getHoveredCell();
        if (hoveredCell == null) {
            return;
        }
        Grid grid = engine.getWorld().getGrid();
        cellHoverRenderer.render(
            camera,
            grid,
            hoveredCell,
            controller.canPlantOnCell(hoveredCell),
            delta
        );
    }
    private void renderPhysics() {
        b2dr.render(
            engine.getB2dWorld(),
            camera.combined.cpy().scl(
                PhysicService.PIXEL_PER_METER
            )
        );
    }
    @Override
    public void resize(int width, int height) {
        if (width <= 0 || height <= 0) {
            return;
        }
        float previousLeft =
            camera.position.x - camera.viewportWidth / 2f;
        viewport.update(width, height, false);
        float minLeft = mapBounds.x;
        float maxLeft = Math.max(
            minLeft,
            mapBounds.x + mapBounds.width - camera.viewportWidth
        );
        float left = Math.max(
            minLeft,
            Math.min(previousLeft, maxLeft)
        );
        camera.position.x =
            left + camera.viewportWidth / 2f;
        camera.position.y =
            mapBounds.y + mapBounds.height / 2f;
        camera.update();
        hud.resize(width, height);
    }
    @Override
    public void pause() {
    }
    @Override
    public void resume() {
    }
    @Override
    public void hide() {
    }
    @Override
    public void dispose() {
        stage.dispose();
        b2dr.dispose();
        cellHoverRenderer.dispose();
        renderer.dispose();
    }
    private Rectangle extractMapBounds(TiledMap map) {
        MapLayer layer = map.getLayers().get("map");
        if (layer == null) {
            throw new IllegalStateException("Map layer not found: map");
        }
        MapObjects objects = layer.getObjects();
        Array<RectangleMapObject> mapObjects =
            objects.getByType(RectangleMapObject.class);
        if (mapObjects.size == 0) {
            throw new IllegalStateException(
                "No rectangle found in map layer"
            );
        }
        return mapObjects.first().getRectangle();
    }
    private void initializeGraphics() {
        camera = new OrthographicCamera();
        viewport = new MapViewport(
            camera,
            mapBounds.height
        );
        viewport.update(
            Gdx.graphics.getWidth(),
            Gdx.graphics.getHeight(),
            false
        );
        camera.position.set(
            mapBounds.x + camera.viewportWidth / 2f,
            mapBounds.y + mapBounds.height / 2f,
            0
        );
        camera.update();
        stage = new Stage(viewport);
    }
    private void initializeEngine(Chapter chapter) {
        Set<String> chapterZombieAliases = chapter
            .getAvailableZombies()
            .stream()
            .map(Property::getAlias)
            .collect(Collectors.toSet());
        Set<PlantName> chapterPlants =
            new HashSet<>(chapter.getAvailablePlants());
        graphicsEngine = new GraphicsEngine(AssetManager.player);
        graphicsEngine.prepareLevelAssets(
            chapterPlants,
            chapterZombieAliases
        );
        engine = new RealTimeGameEngine(graphicsEngine);
        App.setRealTimeEngine(engine);
    }
    private void initializeInputHandling() {
        inputMultiplexer = new InputMultiplexer();
        inputMultiplexer.addProcessor(hud.getStage());
        inputMultiplexer.addProcessor(
            controller.createGameInputProcessor()
        );
    }
    @Override
    public void checkCommand() {
    }
    public Stage getStage() {
        return stage;
    }
    public OrthographicCamera getCamera() {
        return camera;
    }
    public RealTimeGameEngine getEngine() {
        return engine;
    }
    public NormalHud getHud() {
        return hud;
    }
    public Viewport getViewport() {
        return viewport;
    }
    private static class MapViewport extends Viewport {
        private final float worldHeight;
        private MapViewport(
            OrthographicCamera camera,
            float worldHeight
        ) {
            this.worldHeight = worldHeight;
            setCamera(camera);
            setWorldHeight(worldHeight);
        }
        @Override
        public void update(
            int screenWidth,
            int screenHeight,
            boolean centerCamera
        ) {
            if (screenWidth <= 0 || screenHeight <= 0) {
                return;
            }
            float worldWidth =
                worldHeight * screenWidth / (float) screenHeight;
            setWorldSize(worldWidth, worldHeight);
            setScreenBounds(
                0,
                0,
                screenWidth,
                screenHeight
            );
            apply(centerCamera);
        }
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\app\view\hud\AbstractHud.java ---
package app.view.hud;
import com.badlogic.gdx.graphics.g2d.SpriteBatch;
import com.badlogic.gdx.scenes.scene2d.Stage;
import com.badlogic.gdx.utils.viewport.FitViewport;
import game.engine.RealTimeGameEngine;
import game.world.GameWorld;
public abstract class AbstractHud implements Hud{
    protected Stage stage;
    protected FitViewport hudViewport;
    protected GameWorld world;
    protected RealTimeGameEngine engine;
    protected boolean initialized = false;
    protected static final float HUD_WIDTH = 1280f;
    protected static final float HUD_HEIGHT = 720f;
    @Override
    public void initialize(RealTimeGameEngine engine) {
        this.world = engine.getWorld();
        this.engine = engine;
        this.hudViewport = new FitViewport(HUD_WIDTH, HUD_HEIGHT);
        this.stage = new Stage(hudViewport);
        this.initialized = true;
        buildUI();
        System.out.println("HUD initialized with stage: " + stage);
        System.out.println("Stage viewport: " + stage.getViewport());
    }
    protected abstract void buildUI();
    @Override
    public void update(float deltaTime) {
        if (!initialized) return;
        stage.act(deltaTime);
    }
    @Override
    public void render() {
        if (!initialized) return;
        stage.draw();
    }
    @Override
    public void resize(int width, int height) {
        hudViewport.update(width, height, true);
    }
    @Override
    public boolean onTouchDown(float x, float y, int pointer, int button) {
        return stage.hit(x, y, true) != null;
    }
    @Override
    public void dispose() {
        if (stage != null) {
            stage.dispose();
        }
    }
    @Override
    public Stage getStage() {
        return stage;
    }
}

package app.view.hud;
import com.badlogic.gdx.graphics.g2d.SpriteBatch;
import com.badlogic.gdx.scenes.scene2d.Stage;
import game.engine.RealTimeGameEngine;
import game.world.GameWorld;
public interface Hud {
    void initialize(RealTimeGameEngine engine);
    void update(float deltaTime);
    void render();
    void resize(int width, int height);
    boolean onTouchDown(float x, float y, int pointer, int button);
    void dispose();
    HudType getType();
    Stage getStage();
}
