--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\app\view\hud\NormalHud.java ---
package app.view.hud;
import app.controller.game.WorldListener;
import app.enums.plant.PlantName;
import app.model.App;
import app.view.hud.elements.PlantCard;
import app.view.hud.elements.NormalTextPopup;
import app.view.hud.elements.ProgressZombieBar;
import com.badlogic.gdx.graphics.Color;
import com.badlogic.gdx.scenes.scene2d.ui.Label;
import com.badlogic.gdx.scenes.scene2d.ui.Table;
import com.badlogic.gdx.utils.Align;
import game.component.AssetManager;
import game.engine.RealTimeGameEngine;
import game.world.chapter.Chapter;
import pvz.skin.BorderedTable;
import java.util.List;
public class NormalHud extends AbstractHud implements WorldListener {
    private static final float TOP_BAR_HEIGHT = 70f;
    private Table rootTable;
    private PlantCardBar plantCardBar;
    private ProgressZombieBar progressBar;
    private Label sunLabel;
    private Label waveLabel;
    private WorldListener forwardListener;
    @Override
    public void initialize(RealTimeGameEngine engine) {
        super.initialize(engine);
    }
    @Override
    protected void buildUI() {
        rootTable = new Table();
        rootTable.setFillParent(true);
        stage.addActor(rootTable);
        buildTopBar();
        buildProgressBar();
        buildCardBar();
    }
    private void buildTopBar() {
        BorderedTable topBar = new BorderedTable();
        sunLabel = new Label("☀ 0", AssetManager.skin);
        sunLabel.setColor(Color.YELLOW);
        waveLabel = new Label("Wave 0/0", AssetManager.skin);
        waveLabel.setColor(Color.WHITE);
        topBar.add(sunLabel).padLeft(15).padRight(30);
        topBar.add(waveLabel)
            .expandX()
            .align(Align.right)
            .padRight(15);
        rootTable.add(topBar)
            .top()
            .growX()
            .height(TOP_BAR_HEIGHT)
            .colspan(2)
            .row();
    }
    private void buildProgressBar() {
        Chapter chapter =
            App.getPendingGameSession().getSelectedChapter();
        int totalWaves =
            chapter != null ? chapter.getTotalWaves() : 1;
        progressBar =
            ProgressZombieBar.createRaidProgressBar();
        progressBar.setSize(
            ProgressZombieBar.WIDTH,
            ProgressZombieBar.HEIGHT
        );
        if (totalWaves > 1) {
            for (int i = 1; i < totalWaves; i++) {
                progressBar.addWaveFlag((float) i / totalWaves);
            }
        }
        Table barContainer = new Table();
        barContainer.add(progressBar)
            .size(
                ProgressZombieBar.WIDTH,
                ProgressZombieBar.HEIGHT
            )
            .pad(5);
        rootTable.add(barContainer)
            .colspan(2)
            .center()
            .row();
    }
    private void buildCardBar() {
        plantCardBar =
            new PlantCardBar(1.0f, 6f, 520f);
        plantCardBar.setWorldListener(this);
        List<PlantName> selectedPlants =
            App.getPendingGameSession().getSelectedPlants();
        if (selectedPlants != null) {
            plantCardBar.setPlants(selectedPlants);
        }
        rootTable.add(plantCardBar)
            .top()
            .left()
            .padLeft(15)
            .padTop(10)
            .expandY();
        rootTable.add().expand();
    }
    public void showPlantPlacementMessage(String message) {
        if (message == null || message.isBlank()) {
            return;
        }
        NormalTextPopup popup =
            new NormalTextPopup(
                message,
                stage.getViewport().getWorldWidth()
            );
        stage.addActor(popup);
        popup.toFront();
    }
    @Override
    public void onPlantCardSelected(PlantName plantName) {
        if (forwardListener != null) {
            forwardListener.onPlantCardSelected(plantName);
        }
    }
    @Override
    public void onPlantCardDeselected(PlantName plantName) {
        if (forwardListener != null) {
            forwardListener.onPlantCardDeselected(plantName);
        }
    }
    public void deselectAllCards() {
        if (plantCardBar != null) {
            plantCardBar.deselectAll();
        }
    }
    public void updateSunDisplay(int sunAmount) {
        if (sunLabel != null) {
            sunLabel.setText("☀ " + sunAmount);
        }
    }
    public void updateWaveDisplay(
        int currentWave,
        int totalWaves
    ) {
        if (waveLabel != null) {
            waveLabel.setText(
                String.format(
                    "Wave %d/%d",
                    currentWave,
                    totalWaves
                )
            );
        }
    }
    public void updateProgressBar(float progress) {
        if (progressBar != null) {
            progressBar.setProgress(progress);
        }
    }
    public void setForwardListener(WorldListener listener) {
        forwardListener = listener;
    }
    @Override
    public HudType getType() {
        return HudType.NORMAL;
    }
    public PlantCardBar getPlantCardBar() {
        return plantCardBar;
    }
    public List<PlantCard> getPlantCards() {
        return plantCardBar != null
            ? plantCardBar.getPlantCards()
            : null;
    }
    @Override
    public void update(float deltaTime) {
        super.update(deltaTime);
        plantCardBar.update(deltaTime);
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\app\view\hud\elements\NormalTextPopup.java ---
package app.view.hud.elements;
import com.badlogic.gdx.math.Interpolation;
import com.badlogic.gdx.scenes.scene2d.actions.Actions;
import com.badlogic.gdx.scenes.scene2d.ui.Label;
import com.badlogic.gdx.utils.Align;
import game.component.AssetManager;
public class NormalTextPopup extends Label {
    private static final float DISPLAY_TIME = 1f;
    private static final float ENTER_ANIMATION_TIME = 0.45f;
    private static final float EXIT_LIFT_TIME = 0.12f;
    private static final float EXIT_ANIMATION_TIME = 0.35f;
    private static final float EXIT_LIFT_HEIGHT = 18f;
    private static final float HORIZONTAL_PADDING = 30f;
    private static final float VERTICAL_PADDING = 12f;
    private static final float BOTTOM_OFFSET = 80f;
    private final float targetY;
    public NormalTextPopup(String message, float worldWidth) {
        super(message, AssetManager.skin, "bundle_reward_multiplier");
        setAlignment(Align.center);
        setWrap(false);
        float width = getPrefWidth() + HORIZONTAL_PADDING;
        float height = getPrefHeight() + VERTICAL_PADDING;
        setSize(width, height);
        targetY = BOTTOM_OFFSET;
        float centerX = (worldWidth - width) / 2f;
        float hiddenY = -height;
        setPosition(centerX, hiddenY);
        setColor(1, 1, 1, 0);
        addAction(
            Actions.sequence(
                Actions.alpha(0),
                Actions.parallel(
                    Actions.moveTo(centerX, targetY, ENTER_ANIMATION_TIME, Interpolation.pow2Out),
                    Actions.fadeIn(ENTER_ANIMATION_TIME, Interpolation.fade)
                ),
                Actions.delay(DISPLAY_TIME),
                Actions.parallel(
                    Actions.moveTo(centerX, hiddenY, EXIT_ANIMATION_TIME, Interpolation.pow2In),
                    Actions.fadeOut(EXIT_ANIMATION_TIME, Interpolation.fade)
                ),
                Actions.removeActor()
            )
        );
    }
    public float getTargetY() {
        return targetY;
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\app\view\hud\elements\PlantCard.java ---
package app.view.hud.elements;
import app.controller.game.WorldListener;
import app.enums.plant.PlantName;
import app.model.App;
import com.badlogic.gdx.graphics.Color;
import com.badlogic.gdx.graphics.g2d.Batch;
import com.badlogic.gdx.graphics.g2d.BitmapFont;
import com.badlogic.gdx.graphics.g2d.GlyphLayout;
import com.badlogic.gdx.graphics.g2d.TextureRegion;
import com.badlogic.gdx.math.MathUtils;
import com.badlogic.gdx.scenes.scene2d.Actor;
import com.badlogic.gdx.scenes.scene2d.InputEvent;
import com.badlogic.gdx.scenes.scene2d.Touchable;
import com.badlogic.gdx.scenes.scene2d.utils.ClickListener;
import game.component.AssetManager;
import game.entity.plant.Plant;
import game.entity.plant.PlantCooldown;
import game.entity.plant.PlantLevel;
import game.service.plant.PlantFactory;
public class PlantCard extends Actor {
    private static final float PLANT_X_OFFSET_RATIO = 0.22f;
    private static final float PLANT_Y_OFFSET_RATIO = 0.02f;
    private static final float COST_PADDING_X = 6f;
    private static final float COST_PADDING_Y = 5f;
    private static final float BASE_COST_FONT_SCALE = 0.80f;
    private static final float LEVEL_PADDING_X = 6f;
    private static final float LEVEL_PADDING_Y = 5f;
    private static final float BASE_LEVEL_FONT_SCALE = 0.50f;
    private static final float COOLDOWN_ALPHA = 0.65f;
    private static final Color COOLDOWN_TINT = new Color(0.15f, 0.15f, 0.15f, COOLDOWN_ALPHA);
    private static final Color SELECTION_GLOW = new Color(1f, 0.92f, 0.2f, 1f);
    private final PlantName plantName;
    private final int sunCost;
    private final PlantCooldown cooldown;
    private final PlantCardConfigLoader.PlantCardConfig cardConfig;
    private TextureRegion selectionBorder;
    private float cardScale = 1.0f;
    private boolean isSelected = false;
    private float stateTime = 0f;
    private WorldListener listener;
    private BitmapFont font;
    private final GlyphLayout glyphLayout = new GlyphLayout();
    public PlantCard(PlantName plantName) {
        this(plantName, 1.0f);
    }
    public PlantCard(PlantName plantName, float cardScale) {
        this.plantName = plantName;
        this.cardScale = cardScale;
        this.cardConfig = PlantCardConfigLoader.getConfig(plantName);
        Plant plant = PlantFactory.create(plantName);
        this.sunCost = (plant != null) ? plant.getSunCost() : 0;
        this.cooldown = App.getCurrentGameEngine() != null && App.getCurrentGameEngine().getGameWorld() != null
            ? App.getCurrentGameEngine().getGameWorld().getCoolDownPlant(plantName)
            : null;
        loadUIAssets();
        updateCardBounds();
        setupInputListener();
    }
    private void loadUIAssets() {
        this.selectionBorder = AssetManager.textureBank.region("IMAGE_UI_PACKETS_SELECT");
        this.font = AssetManager.skin.getFont("FBUSV8C5EI_1");
    }
    private void updateCardBounds() {
        if (cardConfig.cachedBgRegion != null) {
            float width = cardConfig.cachedBgRegion.getRegionWidth() * cardScale;
            float height = cardConfig.cachedBgRegion.getRegionHeight() * cardScale;
            setSize(width, height);
        }
    }
    private void setupInputListener() {
        setTouchable(Touchable.enabled);
        addListener(new ClickListener() {
            @Override
            public void clicked(InputEvent event, float x, float y) {
                if (listener == null) return;
                if (isSelected) {
                    listener.onPlantCardDeselected(plantName);
                } else {
                    listener.onPlantCardSelected(plantName);
                }
            }
        });
    }
    public void setCardScale(float scale) {
        this.cardScale = scale;
        updateCardBounds();
    }
    public float getCardScale() {
        return cardScale;
    }
    @Override
    public void act(float delta) {
        super.act(delta);
        stateTime += delta;
        if (cooldown != null && App.getCurrentGameEngine() != null) {
            cooldown.update(App.getRealTimeEngine().getClock().getCurrentTick());
        } else {
            System.out.println("Debug: cooldown is null");
        }
    }
    @Override
    public void draw(Batch batch, float parentAlpha) {
        float x = getX();
        float y = getY();
        float w = getWidth();
        float h = getHeight();
        Color color = getColor();
        batch.setColor(color.r, color.g, color.b, color.a * parentAlpha);
        drawBackground(batch, x, y, w, h);
        drawPlantIcon(batch, x, y, w, h);
        drawCooldownOverlay(batch, x, y, w, h);
        drawLevelText(batch, x, y, w, h);
        drawCostText(batch, x, y, w, h);
        if (isSelected) {
            drawSelectionHighlight(batch, x, y, w, h);
        }
        batch.setColor(Color.WHITE);
    }
    private void drawBackground(Batch batch, float x, float y, float w, float h) {
        if (cardConfig.cachedBgRegion != null) {
            batch.draw(cardConfig.cachedBgRegion, x, y, w, h);
        }
    }
    private void drawPlantIcon(Batch batch, float x, float y, float w, float h) {
        if (cardConfig.cachedPlantRegion == null) return;
        float imgW = cardConfig.cachedPlantRegion.getRegionWidth() * cardScale * cardConfig.scale;
        float imgH = cardConfig.cachedPlantRegion.getRegionHeight() * cardScale * cardConfig.scale;
        float imgX = x + (w - imgW) / 2f - (w * PLANT_X_OFFSET_RATIO) + (cardConfig.offsetX * cardScale);
        float imgY = y + h - imgH + (h * PLANT_Y_OFFSET_RATIO) + (cardConfig.offsetY * cardScale);
        batch.draw(cardConfig.cachedPlantRegion, imgX, imgY, imgW, imgH);
    }
    private void drawCooldownOverlay(Batch batch, float x, float y, float w, float h) {
        if (cooldown == null || cooldown.isAvailable()) return;
        float progress = (float) cooldown.getProgress();
        float remainingRatio = 1.0f - progress;
        if (remainingRatio <= 0f) return;
        float overlayH = h * remainingRatio;
        float overlayY = y + (h * progress);
        batch.setColor(COOLDOWN_TINT);
        if (cardConfig.cachedBgRegion != null) {
            float u = cardConfig.cachedBgRegion.getU();
            float v = cardConfig.cachedBgRegion.getV();
            float u2 = cardConfig.cachedBgRegion.getU2();
            float v2 = cardConfig.cachedBgRegion.getV2();
            float slicedV = v + (v2 - v) * progress;
            batch.draw(cardConfig.cachedBgRegion.getTexture(), x, overlayY, w, overlayH, u, slicedV, u2, v2);
        }
    }
    private void drawCostText(Batch batch, float x, float y, float w, float h) {
        if (font == null) return;
        String costStr = String.valueOf(sunCost);
        float scaleX = font.getScaleX();
        float scaleY = font.getScaleY();
        font.getData().setScale(BASE_COST_FONT_SCALE * cardScale);
        glyphLayout.setText(font, costStr);
        float textX = x + w - (COST_PADDING_X * cardScale) - glyphLayout.width;
        float textY = y + (COST_PADDING_Y * cardScale) + glyphLayout.height;
        font.setColor(getCurrentSun() >= sunCost ? Color.WHITE : Color.RED);
        font.draw(batch, costStr, textX, textY);
        font.getData().setScale(scaleX, scaleY);
    }
    private void drawLevelText(Batch batch, float x, float y, float w, float h) {
        if (font == null) return;
        String levelStr = "Lv." + getPlantLevel();
        float scaleX = font.getScaleX();
        float scaleY = font.getScaleY();
        font.getData().setScale(BASE_LEVEL_FONT_SCALE * cardScale);
        glyphLayout.setText(font, levelStr);
        float textX = x + w - (LEVEL_PADDING_X * cardScale) - glyphLayout.width;
        float textY = y + h - (LEVEL_PADDING_Y * cardScale);
        font.setColor(Color.WHITE);
        font.draw(batch, levelStr, textX, textY);
        font.getData().setScale(scaleX, scaleY);
    }
    private void drawSelectionHighlight(Batch batch, float x, float y, float w, float h) {
        if (selectionBorder == null) return;
        float alpha = 0.75f + 0.25f * MathUtils.sin(stateTime * 6f);
        batch.setColor(SELECTION_GLOW.r, SELECTION_GLOW.g, SELECTION_GLOW.b, alpha);
        float borderThick = 3f * cardScale;
        batch.draw(
            selectionBorder,
            x - borderThick,
            y - borderThick,
            w + (borderThick * 2f),
            h + (borderThick * 2f)
        );
    }
    private int getCurrentSun() {
        if (App.getCurrentGameEngine() != null && App.getCurrentGameEngine().getGameWorld() != null) {
            return App.getCurrentGameEngine().getGameWorld().getSunAmount();
        }
        return 0;
    }
    private int getPlantLevel() {
        if (App.getCurrentUser() != null && App.getCurrentUser().getPlantLevelMap() != null) {
            PlantLevel levelObj = App.getCurrentUser().getPlantLevelMap().get(plantName);
            if (levelObj != null) {
                return levelObj.getLevel();
            }
        }
        return 1;
    }
    public void setListener(WorldListener listener) {
        this.listener = listener;
    }
    public void setSelected(boolean selected) {
        this.isSelected = selected;
    }
    public boolean isSelected() {
        return isSelected;
    }
    public PlantName getPlantName() {
        return plantName;
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\app\view\hud\elements\PlantCardBar.java ---
package app.view.hud.elements;
import app.controller.game.WorldListener;
import app.enums.plant.PlantName;
import com.badlogic.gdx.scenes.scene2d.ui.ScrollPane;
import com.badlogic.gdx.scenes.scene2d.ui.Table;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;
public class PlantCardBar extends Table implements WorldListener {
    private final Table contentTable;
    private final ScrollPane scrollPane;
    private final List<PlantCard> plantCards = new ArrayList<>();
    private float cardScale = 1.0f;
    private float cardSpacing = 6f;
    private float maxBarHeight = 500f;
    private PlantCard selectedCard = null;
    private WorldListener worldListener;
    public PlantCardBar() {
        this(1.0f, 6f, 500f);
    }
    public void update(float deltaTime) {
    }
    public PlantCardBar(float cardScale, float cardSpacing, float maxBarHeight) {
        this.cardScale = cardScale;
        this.cardSpacing = cardSpacing;
        this.maxBarHeight = maxBarHeight;
        this.contentTable = new Table();
        this.contentTable.top();
        ScrollPane.ScrollPaneStyle style = new ScrollPane.ScrollPaneStyle();
        this.scrollPane = new ScrollPane(contentTable, style);
        this.scrollPane.setScrollingDisabled(true, false);
        this.scrollPane.setOverscroll(false, false);
        this.scrollPane.setSmoothScrolling(true);
        this.scrollPane.setFadeScrollBars(true);
        this.add(scrollPane).top().expandY().fillY();
        this.top();
    }
    public void setPlants(List<PlantName> plantNames) {
        clearCards();
        if (plantNames == null) return;
        for (PlantName name : plantNames) {
            addPlantCard(name);
        }
        rebuildLayout();
    }
    public void addPlantCard(PlantName plantName) {
        PlantCard card = new PlantCard(plantName, cardScale);
        card.setListener(this);
        plantCards.add(card);
        rebuildLayout();
    }
    @Override
    public void onPlantCardSelected(PlantName plantName) {
        PlantCard clickedCard = getCardByName(plantName);
        if (clickedCard != null) {
            selectCard(clickedCard);
        }
        if (worldListener != null) {
            worldListener.onPlantCardSelected(plantName);
        }
    }
    @Override
    public void onPlantCardDeselected(PlantName plantName) {
        deselectAll();
    }
    public void selectCard(PlantCard card) {
        if (selectedCard != null && selectedCard != card) {
            selectedCard.setSelected(false);
        }
        selectedCard = card;
        if (selectedCard != null) {
            selectedCard.setSelected(true);
        }
    }
    public void deselectAll() {
        if (selectedCard != null) {
            PlantName deselectedPlant = selectedCard.getPlantName();
            selectedCard.setSelected(false);
            selectedCard = null;
            if (worldListener != null) {
                worldListener.onPlantCardDeselected(deselectedPlant);
            }
        }
    }
    public void setCardScale(float scale) {
        this.cardScale = scale;
        for (PlantCard card : plantCards) {
            card.setCardScale(scale);
        }
        rebuildLayout();
    }
    public void setCardSpacing(float spacing) {
        this.cardSpacing = spacing;
        rebuildLayout();
    }
    public void setMaxBarHeight(float height) {
        this.maxBarHeight = height;
        rebuildLayout();
    }
    private void rebuildLayout() {
        contentTable.clearChildren();
        float maxCardWidth = 0f;
        for (PlantCard card : plantCards) {
            contentTable.add(card).padBottom(cardSpacing).row();
            if (card.getWidth() > maxCardWidth) {
                maxCardWidth = card.getWidth();
            }
        }
        contentTable.pack();
        float calculatedHeight = Math.min(contentTable.getHeight(), maxBarHeight);
        getCell(scrollPane).width(maxCardWidth + 10f).height(calculatedHeight);
        invalidateHierarchy();
    }
    public void clearCards() {
        plantCards.clear();
        contentTable.clearChildren();
        selectedCard = null;
    }
    public PlantCard getCardByName(PlantName plantName) {
        for (PlantCard card : plantCards) {
            if (card.getPlantName() == plantName) {
                return card;
            }
        }
        return null;
    }
    public void setWorldListener(WorldListener listener) {
        this.worldListener = listener;
    }
    public List<PlantCard> getPlantCards() {
        return plantCards;
    }
    public PlantCard getSelectedCard() {
        return selectedCard;
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\app\view\hud\elements\PlantCardConfigLoader.java ---
package app.view.hud.elements;
import app.enums.plant.PlantName;
import com.badlogic.gdx.Gdx;
import com.badlogic.gdx.graphics.g2d.TextureRegion;
import com.badlogic.gdx.utils.Json;
import com.badlogic.gdx.utils.JsonValue;
import game.component.AssetManager;
import java.util.EnumMap;
import java.util.Map;
public class PlantCardConfigLoader {
    public static class PlantCardConfig {
        public String bgKey = "IMAGE_UI_PACKETS_MODERNDAY";
        public String textureKey = "";
        public float scale = 1.0f;
        public float offsetX = 0.0f;
        public float offsetY = 0.0f;
        public float cropLeft = 0.0f;
        public float cropRight = 0.0f;
        public float cropTop = 0.0f;
        public float cropBottom = 0.0f;
        public TextureRegion cachedBgRegion;
        public TextureRegion cachedPlantRegion;
    }
    private static final Map<PlantName, PlantCardConfig> CONFIG_MAP = new EnumMap<>(PlantName.class);
    private static boolean loaded = false;
    public static synchronized void load() {
        if (loaded) return;
        try {
            Json json = new Json();
            JsonValue root = json.fromJson(null, Gdx.files.internal("plants/plant_cards.json"));
            if (root != null) {
                for (JsonValue child = root.child; child != null; child = child.next) {
                    try {
                        PlantName plantName = PlantName.valueOf(child.name);
                        PlantCardConfig config = json.readValue(PlantCardConfig.class, child);
                        if (config.textureKey == null || config.textureKey.isEmpty()) {
                            config.textureKey = "IMAGE_UI_PACKETS_" + plantName.name();
                        }
                        prepareCachedRegions(config, plantName);
                        CONFIG_MAP.put(plantName, config);
                    } catch (IllegalArgumentException e) {
                        Gdx.app.error("PlantCardConfigLoader", "Invalid PlantName in JSON config: " + child.name);
                    }
                }
            }
            Gdx.app.log("PlantCardConfigLoader", "Successfully loaded configs for " + CONFIG_MAP.size() + " plants.");
        } catch (Exception e) {
            Gdx.app.error("PlantCardConfigLoader", "Failed to load plant_cards.json file!", e);
        }
        loaded = true;
    }
    private static void prepareCachedRegions(PlantCardConfig config, PlantName plantName) {
        config.cachedBgRegion = AssetManager.textureBank.region(config.bgKey);
        if (config.cachedBgRegion == null) {
            Gdx.app.error("PlantCardConfigLoader", "Missing background texture key: " + config.bgKey + " for " + plantName);
            config.cachedBgRegion = AssetManager.textureBank.region("IMAGE_UI_PACKETS_MODERNDAY");
        }
        TextureRegion rawPlant = AssetManager.textureBank.region(config.textureKey);
        if (rawPlant == null) {
            Gdx.app.error("PlantCardConfigLoader", "Missing plant texture key: " + config.textureKey + " for " + plantName);
            config.cachedPlantRegion = null;
            return;
        }
        if (config.cropLeft + config.cropRight >= 100f || config.cropTop + config.cropBottom >= 100f) {
            Gdx.app.error("PlantCardConfigLoader", "Invalid crop values (>100%) for plant: " + plantName);
            config.cachedPlantRegion = rawPlant;
            return;
        }
        int origX = rawPlant.getRegionX();
        int origY = rawPlant.getRegionY();
        int origW = rawPlant.getRegionWidth();
        int origH = rawPlant.getRegionHeight();
        int cutLeft = Math.round(origW * (config.cropLeft / 100f));
        int cutRight = Math.round(origW * (config.cropRight / 100f));
        int cutTop = Math.round(origH * (config.cropTop / 100f));
        int cutBottom = Math.round(origH * (config.cropBottom / 100f));
        int newW = Math.max(1, origW - cutLeft - cutRight);
        int newH = Math.max(1, origH - cutTop - cutBottom);
        config.cachedPlantRegion = new TextureRegion(
            rawPlant.getTexture(),
            origX + cutLeft,
            origY + cutTop,
            newW,
            newH
        );
    }
    public static PlantCardConfig getConfig(PlantName plantName) {
        if (!loaded) load();
        PlantCardConfig config = CONFIG_MAP.get(plantName);
        if (config == null) {
            Gdx.app.error("PlantCardConfigLoader", "No config found for " + plantName + ", fallback created.");
            config = new PlantCardConfig();
            config.bgKey = "IMAGE_UI_PACKETS_MODERNDAY";
            config.textureKey = "IMAGE_UI_PACKETS_" + plantName.name();
            prepareCachedRegions(config, plantName);
        }
        return config;
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\app\view\hud\elements\ProgressZombieBar.java ---
package app.view.hud.elements;
import com.badlogic.gdx.graphics.Color;
import com.badlogic.gdx.graphics.Texture;
import com.badlogic.gdx.graphics.g2d.Batch;
import com.badlogic.gdx.graphics.g2d.BitmapFont;
import com.badlogic.gdx.graphics.g2d.TextureRegion;
import com.badlogic.gdx.math.MathUtils;
import com.badlogic.gdx.scenes.scene2d.Actor;
import com.badlogic.gdx.scenes.scene2d.ui.ProgressBar;
import com.badlogic.gdx.utils.Array;
import game.component.AssetManager;
public class ProgressZombieBar extends Actor {
    public static final float WIDTH = 250f;
    public static final float HEIGHT = 24f;
    public enum Direction {
        LEFT_TO_RIGHT,
        RIGHT_TO_LEFT
    }
    public static class WaveFlag {
        public float position;
        public TextureRegion region;
        public WaveFlag(float position, TextureRegion region) {
            this.position = MathUtils.clamp(position, 0f, 1f);
            this.region = region;
        }
    }
    private Direction direction = Direction.LEFT_TO_RIGHT;
    private float progress = 0f;
    private float displayedProgress = 0f;
    private TextureRegion backgroundTexture;
    private TextureRegion fillTexture;
    private TextureRegion zombieHeadTexture;
    private TextureRegion flagTexture;
    private final Array<WaveFlag> waveFlags = new Array<>();
    private float barHeight = 20f;
    private float headSize = 48f;
    private float flagWidth = 16f;
    private float flagHeight = 24f;
    private float smoothingSpeed = 5f;
    private BitmapFont font;
    private String label = "";
    private boolean showLabel = false;
    public ProgressZombieBar() {
        this(Direction.LEFT_TO_RIGHT);
    }
    public ProgressZombieBar(Direction direction) {
        this.direction = direction;
        this.backgroundTexture = AssetManager.textureBank.region("IMAGE_UI_HUD_INGAME_ZOMBOSS_PROGRESS_METER");
        this.fillTexture = AssetManager.textureBank.region("IMAGE_UI_HUD_INGAME_PROGRESS_METER_FILL");
        this.zombieHeadTexture = AssetManager.textureBank.region("IMAGE_UI_HUD_INGAME_PROGRESS_METER_ZOMBOSS_HEAD");
        this.flagTexture = AssetManager.textureBank.region("IMAGE_ZOMBIE_ZOMBIE_TUTORIAL_FLAG_ZOMBIE_TUTORIAL_FLAG_123X95");
        this.font = AssetManager.skin.getFont("FBUSV8C5EI_2");
    }
    public void setDirection(Direction direction) {
        this.direction = direction;
    }
    public void setZombieHeadTexture(TextureRegion texture) {
        this.zombieHeadTexture = texture;
    }
    public void setZombieHeadTexture(String assetPath) {
        this.zombieHeadTexture =  AssetManager.textureBank.region(assetPath);
    }
    public void setFlagTexture(TextureRegion texture) {
        this.flagTexture = texture;
    }
    public void setLabel(String label) {
        this.label = label;
        this.showLabel = label != null && !label.isEmpty();
    }
    public void setSmoothingSpeed(float speed) {
        this.smoothingSpeed = speed;
    }
    public void addWaveFlag(float position) {
        waveFlags.add(new WaveFlag(position, flagTexture));
    }
    public void addWaveFlag(float position, TextureRegion texture) {
        waveFlags.add(new WaveFlag(position, texture));
    }
    public void clearWaveFlags() {
        waveFlags.clear();
    }
    public void setWaveFlags(float... positions) {
        waveFlags.clear();
        for (float pos : positions) {
            waveFlags.add(new WaveFlag(pos, flagTexture));
        }
    }
    public void setProgress(float progress) {
        this.progress = MathUtils.clamp(progress, 0f, 1f);
    }
    public void setProgressImmediate(float progress) {
        this.progress = MathUtils.clamp(progress, 0f, 1f);
        this.displayedProgress = this.progress;
    }
    public float getProgress() {
        return progress;
    }
    public float getDisplayedProgress() {
        return displayedProgress;
    }
    @Override
    public void act(float delta) {
        super.act(delta);
        if (Math.abs(displayedProgress - progress) > 0.001f) {
            displayedProgress += (progress - displayedProgress)
                * Math.min(smoothingSpeed * delta, 1f);
        } else {
            displayedProgress = progress;
        }
    }
    @Override
    public void draw(Batch batch, float parentAlpha) {
        float x = getX();
        float y = getY();
        float width = getWidth();
        float height = getHeight();
        if (backgroundTexture != null) {
            batch.setColor(getColor());
            batch.draw(backgroundTexture, x, y, width, height);
        }
        float fillWidth = width * displayedProgress;
        float fillX = x;
        if (direction == Direction.RIGHT_TO_LEFT) {
            fillWidth = width * displayedProgress;
            fillX = x;
        }
        if (fillTexture != null && fillWidth > 0) {
            batch.setColor(getFillColor());
            batch.draw(fillTexture, fillX, y, fillWidth, height);
        }
        drawWaveFlags(batch, x, y, width, height);
        drawZombieHead(batch, x, y, width, height);
        if (showLabel && font != null) {
            drawLabel(batch, x, y, width, height);
        }
        batch.setColor(Color.WHITE);
    }
    private void drawLabel(Batch batch, float barX, float barY,
                           float barWidth, float barHeight) {
        float textWidth = font.getScaleX() * label.length() * 8f;
        float textX = barX + (barWidth - textWidth) / 2f;
        float textY = barY + barHeight / 2f + font.getLineHeight() / 4f;
        font.setColor(Color.WHITE);
        font.draw(batch, label, textX, textY);
    }
    protected Color getFillColor() {
        if (displayedProgress > 0.6f) {
            return Color.GREEN;
        } else if (displayedProgress > 0.3f) {
            return Color.YELLOW;
        } else {
            return Color.RED;
        }
    }
    private void drawZombieHead(Batch batch, float barX, float barY,
                                float barWidth, float barHeight) {
        if (zombieHeadTexture == null) return;
        float headX;
        float headY = barY + (barHeight - headSize) / 2f;
        if (direction == Direction.LEFT_TO_RIGHT) {
            headX = barX + (barWidth * displayedProgress) - headSize / 2f;
        } else {
            headX = barX + (barWidth * displayedProgress) - headSize / 2f;
        }
        headX = MathUtils.clamp(headX, barX - headSize / 4f,
            barX + barWidth - headSize * 0.75f);
        batch.setColor(Color.WHITE);
        batch.draw(zombieHeadTexture, headX, headY, headSize, headSize);
    }
    private void drawWaveFlags(Batch batch, float barX, float barY,
                               float barWidth, float barHeight) {
        for (WaveFlag flag : waveFlags) {
            TextureRegion tex = flag.region != null ? flag.region : flagTexture;
            if (tex == null) continue;
            float flagX = barX + (barWidth * flag.position) - (flagWidth / 2f);
            float flagY = barY + barHeight;
            batch.setColor(Color.WHITE);
            batch.draw(tex, flagX, flagY, flagWidth, flagHeight);
        }
    }
    public static ProgressZombieBar createBossHealthBar() {
        ProgressZombieBar bar = new ProgressZombieBar(Direction.RIGHT_TO_LEFT);
        bar.setProgressImmediate(1f);
        bar.setLabel("BOSS");
        return bar;
    }
    public static ProgressZombieBar createRaidProgressBar() {
        ProgressZombieBar bar = new ProgressZombieBar(Direction.LEFT_TO_RIGHT);
        bar.setProgressImmediate(0f);
        return bar;
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\common\model\Position.java ---
package common.model;
public class Position
{
    private static final float EPSILON = 0.5f;
    private double x;
    private double y;
    public Position(double x, double y) {
        this.x = x;
        this.y = y;
    }
    public double getX()
    {
        return x;
    }
    public double getY()
    {
        return y;
    }
    public void setX(double x)
    {
        this.x = x;
    }
    public void setY(double y)
    {
        this.y = y;
    }
    @Override
    public boolean equals(Object obj)
    {
        if (!(obj instanceof Position position)) return false;
        double diffX = Math.abs(this.getX() - position.getX());
        double diffY = Math.abs(this.getY() - position.getY());
        return diffX < EPSILON && diffY < EPSILON;
    }
    @Override
    public String toString() {
        return "( " + x + " , " + y + " )";
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\common\model\Rect.java ---
package common.model;
public class Rect {
    public Position position;
    public float width;
    public float height;
    public Rect(Position position, float width, float height) {
        this.position = position;
        this.width = width;
        this.height = height;
    }
    public Rect(float x, float y, float width, float height) {
        this.position = new Position(x, y);
        this.width = width;
        this.height = height;
    }
    public boolean overlaps(Rect other) {
        return this.position.getX() < other.position.getX() + other.width &&
                this.position.getX() + this.width > other.position.getX() &&
                this.position.getY() < other.position.getY() + other.height &&
                this.position.getY() + this.height > other.position.getY();
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\common\model\Vector.java ---
package common.model;
public class Vector {
    public double x;
    public double y;
    public double z;
    public Vector(double x, double y) {
        this.x = x;
        this.y = y;
        this.z = 0;
    }
    public Vector(double x, double y, double z) {
        this.x = x;
        this.y = y;
        this.z = z;
    }
    public static double findDist(Position vec1, Position vec2) {
        double dx = vec1.getX() - vec2.getX();
        double dy = vec1.getY() - vec2.getY();
        return Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
    }
    public Vector() {
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\common\utils\HashUtil.java ---
package common.utils;
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.security.MessageDigest;
public class HashUtil {
    public static String hash(String input) {
        if (input == null) return null;
        byte[] encodedHash = null;
        try {
            MessageDigest messageDigest = MessageDigest.getInstance("SHA-256");
            encodedHash = messageDigest.digest(input.getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
        }
        catch (Exception e) {
            return "";
        }
        StringBuilder hexString = new StringBuilder(2 * encodedHash.length);
        for (byte b : encodedHash) {
            int mask = 0xff & b;
            String hex = Integer.toHexString(mask);
            if (hex.length() == 1) {
                hexString.append('0');
            }
            hexString.append(hex);
        }
        return hexString.toString();
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\game\component\contacts\WorldContactListener.java ---
package game.component.contacts;
import app.model.App;
import com.badlogic.gdx.physics.box2d.*;
import game.entity.Entity;
import game.entity.plant.Plant;
import game.entity.zombie.Zombie;
import game.world.playground.Grid;
public class WorldContactListener implements ContactListener {
    @Override
    public void beginContact(Contact contact) {
        Object userA = contact.getFixtureA().getBody().getUserData();
        Object userB = contact.getFixtureB().getBody().getUserData();
        System.out.println("___________GOT CALLED_________________");
        if (userA instanceof Zombie && userB instanceof Plant) {
            handleBeginContact((Zombie) userA, (Plant) userB);
        } else if (userA instanceof Plant && userB instanceof Zombie) {
            handleBeginContact((Zombie) userB, (Plant) userA);
        }
    }
    private void handleBeginContact(Zombie zombie, Plant plant) {
        if (!zombie.isDead() && !plant.isDead()) {
            zombie.setTargetPlant(plant);
        }
    }
    @Override
    public void endContact(Contact contact) {
        Object userA = contact.getFixtureA().getBody().getUserData();
        Object userB = contact.getFixtureB().getBody().getUserData();
        if (userA instanceof Zombie && userB instanceof Plant) {
            handleEndContact((Zombie) userA, (Plant) userB);
        } else if (userA instanceof Plant && userB instanceof Zombie) {
            handleEndContact((Zombie) userB, (Plant) userA);
        }
    }
    private void handleEndContact(Zombie zombie, Plant plant) {
        if (zombie.getTargetPlant() == plant) {
            zombie.setTargetPlant(null);
        }
    }
    @Override
    public void preSolve(Contact contact, Manifold manifold) {
        Entity entityA = (Entity) contact.getFixtureA().getBody().getUserData();
        Entity entityB = (Entity) contact.getFixtureB().getBody().getUserData();
        Grid grid = App.getRealTimeEngine().getWorld().getGrid();
        if(grid.getEntityLane(entityA) != grid.getEntityLane(entityB)) {
            contact.setEnabled(false);
            System.out.println("disabled");
        }
        else {
            System.out.println("same lane! -> " + grid.getEntityLane(entityA)  + "," + grid.getEntityLane(entityB));
        }
    }
    @Override
    public void postSolve(Contact contact, ContactImpulse contactImpulse) {
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\game\component\physic\BodyFactory.java ---
package game.component.physic;
import app.model.App;
import com.badlogic.gdx.physics.box2d.*;
import game.component.contacts.ContactBits;
import game.entity.Entity;
import game.world.playground.Grid;
public class BodyFactory {
    private static BodyFactory instance;
    private final World world;
    private BodyFactory(World world) {
        this.world = world;
    }
    public static void initialize(World world) {
        instance = new BodyFactory(world);
    }
    public static BodyFactory getInstance() {
        if (instance == null) {
            throw new IllegalStateException("BodyFactory must be initialized with a World first!");
        }
        return instance;
    }
    public Body createZombieBody(float x, float y, float width, float height) {
        float normX = x / PhysicService.PIXEL_PER_METER;
        float normY = y / PhysicService.PIXEL_PER_METER;
        width = width / PhysicService.PIXEL_PER_METER;
        height = height / PhysicService.PIXEL_PER_METER;
        BodyDef bodyDef = new BodyDef();
        bodyDef.type = BodyDef.BodyType.DynamicBody;
        bodyDef.position.set(normX, normY);
        Body body = world.createBody(bodyDef);
        PolygonShape shape = new PolygonShape();
        shape.setAsBox(width, height);
        FixtureDef fixtureDef = new FixtureDef();
        fixtureDef.shape = shape;
        fixtureDef.density = 1.0f;
        fixtureDef.filter.categoryBits = getLineBit(x,y);
        fixtureDef.filter.maskBits = getLineBit(x,y);
        fixtureDef.isSensor = true;
        body.createFixture(fixtureDef);
        shape.dispose();
        return body;
    }
    public Body createPlantBody(float x, float y, float width, float height) {
        float normX = x / PhysicService.PIXEL_PER_METER;
        float normY = y / PhysicService.PIXEL_PER_METER;
        width = width / PhysicService.PIXEL_PER_METER;
        height = height / PhysicService.PIXEL_PER_METER;
        BodyDef bodyDef = new BodyDef();
        bodyDef.type = BodyDef.BodyType.StaticBody;
        bodyDef.position.set(normX, normY);
        Body body = world.createBody(bodyDef);
        PolygonShape shape = new PolygonShape();
        shape.setAsBox(width, height);
        FixtureDef fixtureDef = new FixtureDef();
        fixtureDef.shape = shape;
        fixtureDef.filter.categoryBits = getLineBit(x,y);
        fixtureDef.filter.maskBits = getLineBit(x,y);
        body.createFixture(fixtureDef);
        shape.dispose();
        return body;
    }
    public Body createProjectileBody(float x, float y, float radius) {
        radius = radius / PhysicService.PIXEL_PER_METER;
        return null;
    }
    private short getLineBit(float x, float y) {
        Grid grid = App.getCurrentWorld().getGrid();
        return switch (grid.getEntityLane(x,y)) {
            case 0 -> ContactBits.LINE_0_BIT;
            case 1 -> ContactBits.LINE_1_BIT;
            case 2 -> ContactBits.LINE_2_BIT;
            case 3 -> ContactBits.LINE_3_BIT;
            case 4 -> ContactBits.LINE_4_BIT;
            default -> ContactBits.LINE_5_BIT;
        };
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\game\component\physic\PhysicService.java ---
package game.component.physic;
import com.badlogic.gdx.math.Vector2;
import com.badlogic.gdx.physics.box2d.Body;
import com.badlogic.gdx.physics.box2d.BodyDef;
import com.badlogic.gdx.physics.box2d.World;
import game.component.contacts.WorldContactListener;
public class PhysicService {
    public static final float PIXEL_PER_METER = 100;
    private final World b2world;
    private final float timeStep = 1/60f;
    private float accumulator = 0f;
    public PhysicService() {
        this.b2world = new World(new Vector2(0, 0), true);
        b2world.setContactListener(new WorldContactListener());
    }
    public World getB2dWorld() {
        return b2world;
    }
    public Body createBody(BodyDef bodyDef) {
        return b2world.createBody(bodyDef);
    }
    public void destroyBody(Body body) {
        if (body != null) {
            b2world.destroyBody(body);
        }
    }
    public void updateWorld(float delta) {
        b2world.step(timeStep, 6, 2);
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\game\engine\GameSimulationEngine.java ---
package game.engine;
import app.enums.GameState;
import game.engine.system.lawnmower.LawnMowerSimulationSystem;
import game.engine.system.plant.PlantSimulationSystem;
import game.engine.system.projectile.ProjectileSimulationSystem;
import game.engine.system.sun.SunSimulationSystem;
import game.engine.system.wave.WaveSimulationSystem;
import game.engine.system.zombie.ZombieSimulationSystem;
import game.engine.time.GameClock;
import game.engine.time.TickProcessor;
import game.engine.time.TickSystem;
import game.entity.registry.ZombieRegistry;
import game.service.Zombie.ZombieService;
import game.world.GameWorld;
import game.world.chapter.rule.ChapterRule;
import game.world.minigame.MinigameWorld;
public class GameSimulationEngine
{
    private final GameWorld gameWorld;
    private final GameClock gameClock;
    private final TickProcessor tickProcessor;
    public GameSimulationEngine(GameWorld gameWorld) {
        this.gameWorld = gameWorld;
        this.gameClock = new GameClock();
        this.tickProcessor = new TickProcessor(this.gameWorld);
        System.out.println("Loaded zombies: " + ZombieRegistry.DEFINITIONS.size());
        loadSystems();
        if (gameWorld.getCurrentChapter() != null) {
            for (ChapterRule rule : gameWorld.getCurrentChapter().getRules()) {
                rule.onEngineStart(this);
            }
        }
        if (gameWorld instanceof MinigameWorld) {
            ((MinigameWorld) gameWorld).getLogic().onEngineStart(this);
        }
    }
    private void loadSystems()
    {
        ZombieService zombieService = new ZombieService(gameWorld);
        tickProcessor.addSystem(new SunSimulationSystem(gameWorld));
        tickProcessor.addSystem(new ZombieSimulationSystem(gameWorld));
        tickProcessor.addSystem(new WaveSimulationSystem(gameWorld, zombieService));
        tickProcessor.addSystem(new PlantSimulationSystem(gameWorld));
        tickProcessor.addSystem(new ProjectileSimulationSystem(gameWorld));
        tickProcessor.addSystem(new LawnMowerSimulationSystem(gameWorld));
    }
    public void removeSystem(Class<? extends TickSystem> systemClass) {
        tickProcessor.removeSystem(systemClass);
    }
    public void addSystem(TickSystem system) {
        tickProcessor.addSystem(system);
    }
    public void advanceTime(long ticks)
    {
        for (long i = 0; i < ticks; i++) {
            if (gameWorld.getGameState() == GameState.WON ||gameWorld.getGameState() == GameState.LOST) return;
            tickProcessor.update(gameClock.getCurrentTick());
            for(String event : gameWorld.getEvents()) {
                System.out.println("|EVENT LOG: tick #"+gameClock.getCurrentTick()+"|  " + event);
            }
            gameWorld.clearEventLog();
        }
    }
    public GameWorld getGameWorld()
    {
        return gameWorld;
    }
    public GameClock getGameClock()
    {
        return gameClock;
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\game\engine\RealTimeGameEngine.java ---
package game.engine;
import app.enums.GameState;
import app.model.App;
import com.badlogic.gdx.physics.box2d.World;
import game.component.AssetManager;
import game.component.physic.BodyFactory;
import game.component.physic.PhysicService;
import game.engine.time.GameClock;
import game.entity.zombie.data.properties.Property;
import game.world.GameWorld;
import game.world.chapter.Chapter;
import graphics.GraphicsEngine;
import java.util.HashSet;
import java.util.Set;
import java.util.stream.Collectors;
public class RealTimeGameEngine {
    private final GameWorld world;
    private final GameClock clock;
    private final GameSimulationEngine simulationEngine;
    private final PhysicService physicService;
    private final GraphicsEngine graphicsEngine;
    private boolean isPaused = false;
    public RealTimeGameEngine(GraphicsEngine graphicsEngine ) {
        this.simulationEngine = App.getCurrentGameEngine();
        this.world = simulationEngine.getGameWorld();
        this.clock = simulationEngine.getGameClock();
        this.physicService = new PhysicService();
        BodyFactory.initialize(getB2dWorld());
        this.graphicsEngine = graphicsEngine;
    }
    public void update(float deltaSeconds) {
        if (isPaused || world.getGameState() == GameState.WON || world.getGameState() == GameState.LOST) {
            return;
        }
        physicService.updateWorld(deltaSeconds);
        int ticks = clock.update(deltaSeconds);
        if (ticks > 0) {
            simulationEngine.advanceTime(ticks);
        }
    }
    public void pause() {
        isPaused = true;
    }
    public void resume() {
        isPaused = false;
    }
    public boolean isPaused() {
        return isPaused;
    }
    public GameSimulationEngine getSimulationEngine() {
        return simulationEngine;
    }
    public GameWorld getWorld() {
        return world;
    }
    public GameClock getClock() {
        return clock;
    }
    public World getB2dWorld() {
        return physicService.getB2dWorld();
    }
    public GraphicsEngine getGraphicsEngine() {
        return graphicsEngine;
    }
    public PhysicService getPhysicService() {
        return physicService;
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\game\engine\system\environment\EnvironmentSystem.java ---
package game.engine.system.environment;
import game.engine.time.TickSystem;
import game.entity.obstacle.Obstacle;
import game.entity.obstacle.TombStone;
import game.world.GameWorld;
import game.world.playground.Cell;
import game.world.playground.Grid;
public class EnvironmentSystem implements TickSystem {
    private final GameWorld world;
    public EnvironmentSystem(GameWorld world) {
        this.world = world;
    }
    @Override
    public void update(long currentTick) {
        destroyDeadGraves(world.getGrid());
        world.getSeason().updateEnvironment(world.getGrid());
    }
    private void destroyDeadGraves(Grid grid) {
        for (Cell cell : grid.getAllCells()) {
            Obstacle obstacle = cell.getObstacle();
            if (obstacle instanceof TombStone tombstone) {
                if (tombstone.isDestroyed()){}
            }
        }
    }
}

package game.engine.system.minigame;
import game.engine.time.TickSystem;
import game.entity.zombie.Zombie;
import game.world.minigame.scoringgame.ScoringGameWorld;
import game.world.minigame.scoringgame.patterns.ExactDamagePattern;
import game.world.minigame.scoringgame.patterns.LastStandRecoveryPattern;
import game.world.minigame.scoringgame.patterns.LowSunVictoryPattern;
import game.world.minigame.scoringgame.patterns.ScoringPattern;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;
public class ScoringGameSystem implements TickSystem {
    private final ScoringGameWorld world;
    private final List<ScoringPattern> patterns = new ArrayList<>();
    public ScoringGameSystem(ScoringGameWorld world) {
        this.world = world;
        patterns.add(new ExactDamagePattern());
        patterns.add(new LastStandRecoveryPattern());
        patterns.add(new LowSunVictoryPattern());
    }
    @Override
    public void update(long currentTick) {
        for (ScoringPattern pattern : patterns) {
            pattern.processTick(world, currentTick);
        }
        world.getZombieHealthSnapshot().keySet().removeIf(Zombie::isDead);
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\game\engine\system\plant\PlantSimulationSystem.java ---
package game.engine.system.plant;
import game.engine.time.TickSystem;
import game.entity.plant.Plant;
import game.entity.plant.PlantCooldown;
import game.service.plant.PlantService;
import game.world.GameWorld;
import java.util.ArrayList;
public class PlantSimulationSystem implements TickSystem
{
    private final GameWorld gameWorld;
    private final PlantService plantService;
    private final ArrayList<Plant> plants;
    private final ArrayList<PlantCooldown> cooldownPlants;
    public PlantSimulationSystem(GameWorld gameWorld) {
        this.gameWorld = gameWorld;
        this.plantService = new PlantService(this.gameWorld);
        this.plants = gameWorld.getPlants();
        this.cooldownPlants = gameWorld.getPlantCooldowns();
    }
    @Override
    public void update(long currentTick)
    {
        for (Plant plant: plants)
            plant.update(plantService , currentTick);
        for (PlantCooldown element: cooldownPlants)
            element.update(currentTick);
        gameWorld.getPlants().removeIf(Plant::isDead);
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\game\engine\system\projectile\ProjectileSimulationSystem.java ---
package game.engine.system.projectile;
import game.engine.time.TickSystem;
import game.entity.projectile.Projectile;
import game.world.GameWorld;
import java.util.Iterator;
import java.util.List;
public class ProjectileSimulationSystem implements TickSystem {
    private final GameWorld gameWorld;
    public ProjectileSimulationSystem(GameWorld gameWorld) {
        this.gameWorld = gameWorld;
    }
    @Override
    public void update(long currentTick) {
        List<Projectile> projectiles = gameWorld.getProjectiles();
        Iterator<Projectile> iterator = projectiles.iterator();
        while (iterator.hasNext()) {
            Projectile projectile = iterator.next();
            if (!projectile.isActive()) {
                iterator.remove();
                continue;
            }
            projectile.update(gameWorld, currentTick);
            if (!projectile.isActive()) {
                iterator.remove();
            }
        }
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\game\engine\system\special_levels\ConveyorBeltSystem.java ---

    private int tickCounter = 0;
    public ConveyorBeltSystem(ConveyorBeltService service) {
        this.service = service;
    }
    @Override
    public void update(long currentTick) {
        tickCounter++;
        if (tickCounter >= service.getTickInterval()) {
            tickCounter = 0;
            service.rollPlant();
        }
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\game\engine\system\wave\WaveSimulationSystem.java ---
package game.engine.system.wave;
import app.enums.GameState;
import game.engine.time.TickSystem;
import game.service.Zombie.WaveService;
import game.service.Zombie.ZombieService;
import game.world.GameWorld;
import game.world.chapter.Chapter;
import game.world.chapter.wave.WaveConfig;
public class WaveSimulationSystem implements TickSystem
{
    private final GameWorld world;
    private final WaveService waveService;
    private final WaveConfig config;
    private final int totalWaves;
    private final long[] waveStartTicks;
    private int nextWaveIndex = 0;
    private double spawnDeposit = 0;
    private double spawnThreshold;
    private boolean finalWaveStarted = false;
    private boolean victoryTriggered = false;
    public WaveSimulationSystem(GameWorld world, ZombieService zombieService) {
        this.world = world;
        Chapter chapter = world.getCurrentChapter();
        this.waveService = new WaveService(world, zombieService, chapter);
        this.config = chapter.getWaveConfig();
        this.totalWaves = chapter.getTotalWaves();
        waveStartTicks = new long[totalWaves];
        long nextTick = config.firstWaveTicks();
        for (int i = 0; i < totalWaves; i++) {
            waveStartTicks[i] = nextTick;
            nextTick += config.waveIntervalTicks();
        }
        this.spawnThreshold = config.RSInitialDeposit();
    }
    @Override
    public void update(long currentTick) {
        if (world.getGameState() != GameState.PLAYING || victoryTriggered) return;
        while (nextWaveIndex < totalWaves && currentTick >= waveStartTicks[nextWaveIndex]) {
            startWave(nextWaveIndex + 1);
            nextWaveIndex++;
            if (nextWaveIndex == totalWaves) {
                finalWaveStarted = true;
            }
        }
        updateRandomSpawner(currentTick);
        if (finalWaveStarted && world.getZombies().isEmpty()) {
            victoryTriggered = true;
            world.triggerPlantVictory();
        }
    }
    private void startWave(int waveNumber) {
        world.getCurrentSeason().onWaveStart(world.getGrid());
        waveService.startNextWave(waveNumber);
        world.setCurrentWaveNumber(waveNumber);
    }
    private void updateRandomSpawner(long currentTick) {
        if (finalWaveStarted && world.getZombies().isEmpty()) return;
        double timeFactor = 1.0 + currentTick * config.RSTimeScaling();
        spawnDeposit += config.RSGrowthRate() * timeFactor;
        double maxDeposit = spawnThreshold * config.RSMaxDepositFactor();
        if (spawnDeposit > maxDeposit) spawnDeposit = maxDeposit;
        while (spawnDeposit >= spawnThreshold) {
            if (waveService.spawnRandomZombie() != null) {
                spawnDeposit -= spawnThreshold;
                spawnThreshold *= config.RSDepositGrowthFactor();
            } else {
                break;
            }
        }
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\game\engine\system\zombie\ZombieSimulationSystem.java ---
package game.engine.system.zombie;
import game.engine.time.TickSystem;
import game.entity.zombie.Zombie;
import game.service.Zombie.ZombieService;
import game.world.GameWorld;
import java.util.ArrayList;
public class ZombieSimulationSystem implements TickSystem
{
    private final GameWorld world;
    private final ZombieService zombieService;
    private final ArrayList<Zombie> zombies;
    public ZombieSimulationSystem(GameWorld world) {
        this.world = world;
        this.zombieService = new ZombieService(world);
        zombies = world.getZombies();
    }
    @Override
    public void update(long currentTick) {
        for (Zombie zombie : new ArrayList<>(zombies)) {
            zombie.update(zombieService, currentTick);
        }
        world.flushPendingZombies();
        world.getZombies().removeIf(Zombie::isDead);
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\game\engine\time\GameClock.java ---
package game.engine.time;
public class GameClock
{
    private long currentTick = 0;
    private float accumulator = 0f;
    public static final int TICKS_PER_SECOND = 10;
    private static final float SECONDS_PER_TICK = 1f / TICKS_PER_SECOND;
    private static final int MAX_TICKS_PER_FRAME = 5;
    public int update(float deltaSeconds) {
        accumulator += deltaSeconds;
        int ticksToProcess = (int) (accumulator / SECONDS_PER_TICK);
        ticksToProcess = Math.min(ticksToProcess, MAX_TICKS_PER_FRAME);
        if (ticksToProcess > 0) {
            accumulator -= ticksToProcess * SECONDS_PER_TICK;
            currentTick += ticksToProcess;
        }
        return ticksToProcess;
    }
    public long getCurrentTick()
    {
        return currentTick;
    }
    public void advance(long ticks)
    {
        currentTick += ticks;
    }
    public double getCurrentSeconds()
    {
        return (double)currentTick / TICKS_PER_SECOND;
    }
    public float getInterpolationAlpha() {
        return accumulator / SECONDS_PER_TICK;
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\game\engine\time\TickProcessor.java ---
package game.engine.time;
import game.world.GameWorld;
import game.world.chapter.rule.ChapterRule;
import game.world.minigame.MinigameWorld;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;
public class TickProcessor
{
    private final List<TickSystem> systems = new ArrayList<>();
    private final GameWorld gameWorld;
    public TickProcessor(GameWorld gameWorld) {
        this.gameWorld = gameWorld;
    }
    public void update(long currentTick)
    {
        for (TickSystem system : systems) {
            system.update(currentTick);
        }
        if (gameWorld.getCurrentChapter() != null) {
            for (ChapterRule rule : gameWorld.getCurrentChapter().getRules()) {
                rule.onTick(gameWorld, currentTick);
            }
        }
        if (gameWorld instanceof MinigameWorld) {
            ((MinigameWorld) gameWorld).getLogic().onTick(gameWorld, currentTick);
        }
    }
    public void addSystem(TickSystem system)
    {
        if(system != null && !systems.contains(system)) {
            systems.add(system);
        }
    }
    public void removeSystem(Class<? extends TickSystem> systemClass) {
        systems.removeIf(systemClass::isInstance);
    }
}

package game.engine.time;

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\game\entity\Entity.java ---
package game.entity;
import com.badlogic.gdx.math.Vector2;
import com.badlogic.gdx.physics.box2d.Body;
import common.model.Position;
import game.component.physic.PhysicService;
public abstract class Entity {
    protected Body body;
    public Body getBody() {
        return body;
    }
    public void setBody(Body body) {
        this.body = body;
    }
    public Vector2 getPhysicsPosition() {
        if (body != null) {
            return body.getPosition();
        }
        return Vector2.Zero;
    }
    public void destroyBody(PhysicService physicsService) {
        if (body != null) {
            physicsService.destroyBody(body);
            body = null;
        }
    }
    public abstract Position getPosition();
}

import app.model.App;
import game.entity.plant.Plant;
public class NoActEffect extends PermanentEffect{
    @Override
    public void onApply(Plant plant) {
        plant.setCanAct(false);
        App.getCurrentGameEngine().getGameWorld().addEvent(
                "plant " + plant.getName() + " at position " + plant.getPosition().toString() + " can not act anymore!"
        );

    public void onRemove(Plant plant) {
        plant.setCanAct(true);
        App.getCurrentGameEngine().getGameWorld().addEvent(
                "plant " + plant.getName() + " at position " + plant.getPosition().toString() + " can act from now!"
        );
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\game\entity\effect\SlowEffect.java ---
package game.entity.effect;
import app.enums.effect.EffectType;
import game.entity.plant.Plant;
import game.entity.zombie.Zombie;
public class SlowEffect
        extends TimedEffect
{
    private final double slowFactor;
    public SlowEffect(long startTick, long durationTicks, double slowFactor , EffectType type) {
        super(startTick, durationTicks , type);
        this.slowFactor = slowFactor;
    }
    @Override
    public void applyModifier(Zombie zombie) {
        if(!applied) {
            zombie.setMoveSpeedMultiplier(
                    zombie.getMoveSpeedMultiplier() * slowFactor
            );
            zombie.setEatSpeedMultiplier(
                    zombie.getEatSpeedMultiplier() * slowFactor
            );
            zombie.getWorld().addEvent("Zombie at position " + zombie.getPosition().toString() + " got slow effect.");
            applied = true;
        }
    }
    public double getSlowFactor()
    {
        return slowFactor;
    }
    @Override
    public void onApply(Plant plant)
    {
    }
    @Override
    public void onRemove(Zombie zombie) {
        zombie.setMoveSpeedMultiplier(zombie.getMoveSpeedMultiplier() * (1f/slowFactor));
        zombie.setEatSpeedMultiplier(zombie.getEatSpeedMultiplier() * (1f/slowFactor));
        zombie.getWorld().addEvent("Slow effect got removed from zombie at position : " + zombie.getPosition().toString());
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\game\entity\effect\TimedEffect.java ---
package game.entity.effect;
import app.enums.effect.EffectType;
public abstract class TimedEffect extends Effect
{
    protected long startTick;
    protected long durationTicks;
    public TimedEffect(long startTick, long durationTicks , EffectType type) {
        this.startTick = startTick;
        this.durationTicks = durationTicks;
        this.type = type;
    }
    @Override
    public boolean isExpired(long currentTick)
    {
        return currentTick >= startTick + durationTicks;
    }
    public long getStartTick() {
        return startTick;
    }
    public long getDurationTicks() {
        return durationTicks;
    }
}

import app.model.App;
import common.model.Position;
import game.world.GameWorld;
public class ArcadeCabinet extends Obstacle implements MoveAble{
    private double health = 200;
    public ArcadeCabinet(Position position) {
        super(position, "Cabinet");

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\game\entity\obstacle\Obstacle.java ---
package game.entity.obstacle;
import app.model.App;
import common.model.Position;
import game.world.GameWorld;
import game.world.playground.Cell;
public abstract class Obstacle {
    private int  currentHitPoints;
    protected String name;
    protected Position position;
    public Obstacle(Position position, String name) {
        this.position = position;
        this.name = name;
    }
    public String getName() {
        return name;
    }
    public abstract void onHit(long currentTick, int damage);
    public void die() {
        GameWorld world = App.getCurrentGameEngine().getGameWorld();
        Cell cell = world.getGrid().getCellAt(position);
        if(cell != null) {
            cell.setObstacle(null);
            world.addEvent(name + " Destroyed in position : " + position.toString());
        }
    }
    public Position getPosition() {
        return position;
    }
    public void setPosition(Position position) {
        this.position = position;
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\game\entity\obstacle\Octopus.java ---
package game.entity.obstacle;
import app.model.App;
import common.model.Position;
import game.entity.effect.Effect;
import game.entity.effect.NoActEffect;
import game.entity.plant.Plant;
import game.world.GameWorld;
import game.world.playground.Cell;
public class Octopus extends Obstacle{
    private double health = 100;
    public Octopus(Position position) {
        super(position, "octopus");
        Cell cell = App.getCurrentGameEngine().getGameWorld().getGrid().getCellAt(position);
        if(cell.getPlant() != null) {
            cell.getPlant().addEffect(new NoActEffect());
        }
    }
    public void onHit(long currentTick, int damage) {
        health -= damage;
        GameWorld world = App.getCurrentGameEngine().getGameWorld();
        world.addEvent("*** Octopus took " + damage + " damage. ***");
        if(health <= 0) {
            die();
        }
    }
    @Override
    public void die() {
        super.die();
        Cell cell = App.getCurrentGameEngine().getGameWorld().getGrid().getCellAt(position);
        if(cell.getPlant() != null) {
            Plant plant = cell.getPlant();
            for(Effect effect : plant.getEffects()) {
                if(effect instanceof NoActEffect noAct) {
                    noAct.setExpired(true);
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\game\entity\plant\Plant.java ---
package game.entity.plant;
import app.enums.plant.PlantState;
import app.model.App;
import common.model.Position;
import game.component.physic.PhysicService;
import game.entity.Entity;
import game.entity.effect.Effect;
import game.entity.plant.ability.PlantAbility;
import game.entity.zombie.Zombie;
import game.service.plant.PlantService;
import game.service.plant.UpgradePlant;
import game.world.GameWorld;
import game.world.playground.Cell;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;
public class Plant extends Entity {
    private PlantDefinition definition;
    private PlantHealth health;
    private PlantLevel level;
    private PlantFoodState plantFoodState;
    private Position position;
    private PlantState state;
    private long plantedTick;
    private boolean isBoosted;
    private final List<Effect> effects;
    private final PlantDamage plantDamage;
    private boolean canAct = true;
    private List<PlantAbility> abilities;
    public Plant() {
        abilities = new ArrayList<>();
        effects = new ArrayList<>();
        this.plantDamage = new PlantDamage();
        this.plantFoodState = new PlantFoodState();
    }
    public PlantDefinition getDefinition()
    {
        return definition;
    }
    @Override
    public Position getPosition()
    {
        return position;
    }
    public void setPosition(Position position)
    {
        this.position = position;
    }
    public PlantHealth getCurrentHealth()
    {
        return health;
    }
    public void takeDamage(double damage , Zombie attacker , long currentTick)
    {
        this.health.takeDamage(damage , plantDamage , attacker , currentTick);
    }
    public void heal(int amount)
    {
        this.health.heal(amount);
    }
    public boolean isAlive()
    {
        return state != PlantState.DEAD && !this.health.isDead();
    }
    public int getLevel()
    {
        return UpgradePlant.getLevel(definition.getName());
    }
    public void levelUp()
    {
    }
    public PlantState getState()
    {
        return state;
    }
    public void setState(PlantState state)
    {
        this.state = state;
    }
    public long getPlantTick()
    {
        return plantedTick;
    }
    public List<PlantAbility> getAbilities()
    {
        return abilities;
    }
    public int getAttackDamage() {
        if (definition == null) return 0;
        return definition.getAttackDamage()+UpgradePlant.getExtraDamage(definition.getName());
    }
    public void setDefinition(PlantDefinition deginition) {
        this.definition = deginition;
        for (PlantAbility ability: this.definition.getAbilities()){
            abilities.add(ability);
        }
    }
    public float getActionInterval() {
        return definition.getActionInterval();
    }
    public int getSunCost()
    {
        return definition.getSunCost() - UpgradePlant.getCostReduction(definition.getName());
    }
    public void setHealth(PlantHealth health) {
        this.health = health;
    }
    public String getName() {
        return this.definition.getName().toString();
    }
    public PlantHealth getHealth() {
        return health;
    }
    public PlantFoodState getPlantFoodState() {
        return plantFoodState;
    }
    public long getPlantedTick() {
        return plantedTick;
    }
    public void setPlantedTick(long plantedTick) {
        this.plantedTick = plantedTick;
    }
    public boolean isBoosted() {
        return isBoosted;
    }
    public void setBoosted(boolean boosted) {
        isBoosted = boosted;
    }
    public void update(PlantService plantService , long currentTick )
    {
        updateEffects(plantService , currentTick);
        if(canAct) {
            plantFoodState.update(currentTick);
            for (PlantAbility ability : abilities) {
                ability.update(this, plantService, currentTick);
            }
            if (health.isDead()) {
                markDead(plantService.getGameWorld());
            }
        }
    }
    private void updateEffects(PlantService plantService, long currentTick) {
        for(Effect effect : effects) {
            effect.tick(this, plantService, currentTick);
        }
        effects.removeIf(effect -> {
            if(effect.isExpired(currentTick)) {
                effect.onRemove(this);
                return true;
            }
            return false;
        });
    }
    public void setAbilities(List<PlantAbility> abilities) {
        this.abilities = abilities;
    }
    public boolean isDead() {
        return state == PlantState.DEAD || health.isDead();
    }
    public void addEffect(Effect effect) {
        effects.add(effect);
        effect.onApply(this);
    }
    public void markDead(GameWorld world) {
        if (state == PlantState.DEAD) {
            return;
        }
        state = PlantState.DEAD;
        this.health.die();
        App.getRealTimeEngine().getGraphicsEngine().unregisterPlant(this);
        world.getObserver().addLostPlantsCount(1);
        world.addEvent(">>> Plant " + getName() + " died (!) at position : " + position.toString());
        Cell currentCell = world.getGrid().getCellAt(position);
        if(currentCell != null && currentCell.getPlant() != null) {
            currentCell.setPlant(null);
        }
        PhysicService physicService = App.getRealTimeEngine().getPhysicService();
        destroyBody(physicService);
    }
    public int getRow()
    {
        return (int) position.getY();
    }
    public int getCol()
    {
        return (int) position.getX();
    }
    public PlantDamage getPlantDamage() {
        return plantDamage;
    }
    public List<Effect> getEffects() {
        return effects;
    }
    public boolean isCanAct() {
        return canAct;
    }
    public void setCanAct(boolean canAct) {
        this.canAct = canAct;
    }
    public boolean isFrozen() {
        return false;
    }
    public boolean isSleeping() {
        return false;
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\game\entity\plant\PlantCooldown.java ---
package game.entity.plant;
import app.enums.plant.PlantName;
import game.entity.registry.PlantRegistry;
import game.service.plant.UpgradePlant;
public class PlantCooldown
{
    private final PlantName name;
    private final long cooldownTicks;
    private long lastPlantTick;
    private long currentTick;
    private boolean clearCooldown;
    public PlantCooldown(PlantName name) {
        this.name = name;
        this.cooldownTicks = getCoolDown(name);
        this.clearCooldown = true;
    }
    public void update(long currentTick) {
        this.currentTick = currentTick;
    }
    public boolean isAvailable() {
        if (clearCooldown) return true;
        return (currentTick - lastPlantTick) >= cooldownTicks;
    }
    public void startCooldown(long currentTick) {
        this.lastPlantTick = currentTick;
        this.clearCooldown = false;
    }
    public void clearCooldown() {
        this.clearCooldown = true;
    }
    public double getProgress() {
        if (clearCooldown || isAvailable()) return 1.0;
        long effective = cooldownTicks;
        if (effective <= 0) return 1.0;
        long elapsed = currentTick - lastPlantTick;
        return Math.min(1.0, (double) elapsed / effective);
    }
    public long getRemainingTicks() {
        if (clearCooldown || isAvailable()) return 0;
        long effective = cooldownTicks;
        long elapsed = currentTick - lastPlantTick;
        return Math.max(0, effective - elapsed);
    }
    public static int getCoolDown(PlantName name){
        return Math.max(PlantRegistry.get(name).getCooldown() - UpgradePlant.getCooldownReduction(name) , 0);
    }
}
