--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\game\entity\plant\PlantDefinition.java ---
package game.entity.plant;
import app.enums.plant.*;
import game.entity.plant.ability.PlantAbility;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.stream.Collectors;
public class PlantDefinition {
    private final PlantName name;
    private final PlantCategory category;
    private final int sunCost;
    private final int maxHealth;
    private final int cooldown;
    private final PlantTags tags;
    private final List<PlantAbility> abilities;
    private final int attackDamage;
    private float actionInterval;
    public PlantDefinition(PlantName name, PlantCategory category, int sunCost,
                           int maxHealth, int cooldown, PlantTags tags,
                           List<PlantAbility> abilities, int attackDamage,
                           float actionInterval) {
        this.name = name;
        this.category = category;
        this.sunCost = sunCost;
        this.maxHealth = maxHealth;
        this.cooldown = cooldown;
        this.tags = tags;
        this.abilities = abilities;
        this.attackDamage = attackDamage;
        this.actionInterval = actionInterval;
    }
    public PlantName getName() {
        return name;
    }
    public PlantCategory getCategory() {
        return category;
    }
    public int getSunCost() {
        return sunCost;
    }
    public int getMaxHealth() {
        return maxHealth;
    }
    public int getCooldown() {
        return cooldown;
    }
    public PlantTags getTags() {
        return tags;
    }
    public List<PlantAbility> getAbilities() {
        return abilities;
    }
    public int getAttackDamage() {
        return attackDamage;
    }
    public float getActionInterval() {
        return actionInterval;
    }
    public void setActionInterval(float actionInterval) {
        this.actionInterval = actionInterval;
    }
    public boolean hasTag(PlantTag tag) {
        return tags != null && tags.hasTag(tag);
    }
    public boolean update() {
        return true;
    }
    public String printInfo() {
        String nameVal = String.valueOf(getName());
        String catVal = String.valueOf(getCategory());
        String costVal = String.valueOf(getSunCost());
        String healthVal = String.valueOf(getMaxHealth());
        String cooldownVal = getCooldown() + " ticks";
        String tagsVal = (getTags() != null) ? getTags().toString() : "None";
        String abilityVal = (getAbilities() != null) ?
                getAbilities().stream()
                        .map(PlantAbility::toString)
                        .collect(Collectors.joining(", ")) : "None";
        String attackVal = String.valueOf(getAttackDamage());
        String intervalVal = String.format("%.1f s", getActionInterval());
        String levelVal = "1";
        String[] keys = {"Name", "Category", "Sun Cost", "Max Health",
                "Cooldown", "Tags", "Abilities", "Attack Damage", "Action Interval", "Level"};
        String[] vals = {nameVal, catVal, costVal, healthVal,
                cooldownVal, tagsVal, abilityVal, attackVal, intervalVal, levelVal};
        int maxKeyLen = 0, maxValLen = 0;
        for (int i = 0; i < keys.length; i++) {
            maxKeyLen = Math.max(maxKeyLen, keys[i].length());
            maxValLen = Math.max(maxValLen, vals[i].length());
        }
        int padding = 2;
        int keyColWidth = maxKeyLen + padding * 2;
        int valColWidth = maxValLen + padding * 2;
        int totalWidth = keyColWidth + valColWidth + 3;
        StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
        String topLine = "+" + "-".repeat(keyColWidth) + "+" + "-".repeat(valColWidth) + "+";
        sb.append(topLine).append("\n");
        String title = " Plant Information ";
        int titleWidth = totalWidth - 2;
        sb.append("|").append(centerString(title, titleWidth)).append("|\n");
        sb.append(topLine).append("\n");
        for (int i = 0; i < keys.length; i++) {
            sb.append(formatRow(keys[i], vals[i], maxKeyLen, maxValLen, padding)).append("\n");
        }
        sb.append(topLine);
        return sb.toString();
    }
    private String formatRow(String key, String value, int keyWidth, int valWidth, int padding) {
        return String.format("| %-" + keyWidth + "s | %-" + valWidth + "s |", key, value);
    }
    private String centerString(String str, int width) {
        if (str.length() >= width) return str;
        int left = (width - str.length()) / 2;
        int right = width - str.length() - left;
        return " ".repeat(left) + str + " ".repeat(right);
    }
    public void addAbility(PlantAbility ability)
    {
        abilities.add(ability);
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\game\entity\plant\PlantFoodState.java ---
package game.entity.plant;
import app.enums.plant.PlantName;
public class PlantFoodState
{
    private boolean active = false;
    private long startTick;
    private long durationTicks;
    public boolean isActive()
    {
        return active;
    }
    public void activate(long tick , long durationTicks)
    {
        this.active = true;
        this.startTick = tick;
        this.durationTicks = durationTicks;
    }
    public void activate(long tick , PlantName name)
    {
        this.active = true;
        this.startTick = tick;
        this.durationTicks =(long) name.getPlantFoodDuration()*10;
    }
    public void deactivate()
    {
        this.active = false;
    }
    public long getStartTick()
    {
        return this.startTick;
    }
    public long getDurationTicks()
    {
        return durationTicks;
    }
    public void update(long currentTick)
    {
        if (!active) return;
        if (currentTick - startTick >= durationTicks)
        {
            deactivate();
        }
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\game\entity\plant\PlantHealth.java ---
package game.entity.plant;
import app.model.App;
import game.entity.zombie.Zombie;
public class PlantHealth {
    private double currentHealth;
    private final int maxHealth;
    public PlantHealth(int maxHealth) {
        this.maxHealth = maxHealth;
        this.currentHealth = maxHealth <= 0 ? 1 : maxHealth;
    }
    public double getCurrentHealth() {
        return currentHealth;
    }
    public int getMaxHealth() {
        return maxHealth;
    }
    public void takeDamage(double damage , PlantDamage plantDamage , Zombie attacker , long currentTick) {
        if (damage < 0) return;
        plantDamage.setLastDamageTick(currentTick);
        plantDamage.setZombie(attacker);
        currentHealth = Math.max(0, currentHealth - damage);
    }
    public void heal(int amount) {
        if (amount < 0) return;
        currentHealth = Math.min(maxHealth, currentHealth + amount);
    }
    public void die()
    {
        this.currentHealth = 0;
    }
    public boolean isDead() {
        return currentHealth <= 0;
    }
    public float getHealthRatio() {
        return (float) currentHealth / maxHealth;
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\game\entity\plant\PlantLevel.java ---
package game.entity.plant;
import app.enums.plant.PlantName;
import app.model.App;
public class PlantLevel  {
    private final PlantName plantName;
    private int level;
    private int requiredSeedPackets;
    private int requiredCoins;
    public PlantLevel(PlantName plantName) {
        this.plantName = plantName;
        this.level = 1;
        this.requiredSeedPackets = plantName.getRequiredSeedPackets();
        this.requiredCoins = plantName.getRequiredCoins();
    }
    public PlantName getPlantName() { return plantName; }
    public int getLevel() { return level; }
    public int getRequiredSeedPackets() { return requiredSeedPackets; }
    public int getRequiredCoins() { return requiredCoins; }
    public boolean canUpgrade() {
        int seedPacketAmount = App.getCurrentUser().getSeedBank().getAmount(plantName);
        int coin = App.getCurrentUser().getCoins();
        return seedPacketAmount >= requiredSeedPackets && coin >= requiredCoins;
    }
    public void levelUp() {
        if (!canUpgrade()) return;
        App.getCurrentUser().getSeedBank().removePacket(plantName , requiredSeedPackets);
        this.level++;
        this.requiredSeedPackets = calculateRequiredSeedPackets(level);
        this.requiredCoins = calculateRequiredCoins(level);
    }
    private int calculateRequiredSeedPackets(int nextLevel) {
        return requiredCoins * nextLevel;
    }
    private int calculateRequiredCoins(int nextLevel) {
        return requiredCoins * nextLevel ;
    }
}

package game.entity.plant;
import app.enums.plant.PlantTag;
import java.util.EnumSet;
import java.util.Set;
public record PlantTags(Set<PlantTag> tags) {
    public PlantTags() {
        this(EnumSet.noneOf(PlantTag.class));
    }
    public PlantTags(Set<PlantTag> tags) {
        this.tags = EnumSet.copyOf(tags);
    }
    public boolean hasTag(PlantTag tag) {
        return tags.contains(tag);
    }
    public void addTag(PlantTag tag) {
        tags.add(tag);
    }
    public void removeTag(PlantTag tag) {
        tags.remove(tag);
    }
    @Override
    public Set<PlantTag> tags() {
        return EnumSet.copyOf(tags);
    }
    @Override
    public String toString() {
        return tags.toString();
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\game\entity\plant\ability\AbstractPlantAbility.java ---
package game.entity.plant.ability;
import game.entity.plant.Plant;
import game.service.plant.PlantService;
public abstract class AbstractPlantAbility implements PlantAbility {
    protected long lastUpdateTick;
    protected boolean active;
    public AbstractPlantAbility() {
        this.lastUpdateTick = 0;
        this.active = true;
    }
    @Override
    public void update(Plant plant, PlantService plantService, long currentTick) {
        if (!active) return;
        doUpdate(plant, plantService, currentTick);
        lastUpdateTick = currentTick;
    }
    protected abstract void doUpdate(Plant plant, PlantService plantService, long currentTick);
    public void setActive(boolean active) {
        this.active = active;
    }
    public boolean isActive() {
        return active;
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\game\entity\plant\ability\ApplyPlantFoodAbility.java ---
package game.entity.plant.ability;
import app.enums.plant.PlantCategory;
import app.enums.plant.PlantName;
import app.model.App;
import game.entity.plant.Plant;
import game.service.plant.PlantService;
import game.world.GameWorld;
import java.util.ArrayList;
public class ApplyPlantFoodAbility extends AbstractPlantAbility {
    private final long durationTicks;
    private boolean activated;
    public ApplyPlantFoodAbility(long durationTicks) {
        this.durationTicks = durationTicks;
        this.activated = false;
    }
    @Override
    protected void doUpdate(Plant plant, PlantService plantService, long currentTick) {
        if (plant == null || !plant.isAlive()) return;
        applyPlantFood(plant, plantService, currentTick);
    }
    public void applyPlantFood(Plant plant, PlantService plantService, long currentTick) {
        if (activated) return;
        PlantName name = plant.getDefinition().getName();
        GameWorld gameWorld = App.getCurrentGameEngine().getGameWorld();
        ArrayList<Plant> familyPlants = getFamilyPlants(gameWorld.getPlants(), name);
        for (Plant element : familyPlants) {
            if (element != null && element.isAlive()) {
                element.getPlantFoodState().activate(currentTick, durationTicks);
            }
        }
        activated = true;
        plant.markDead(gameWorld);
    }
    public ArrayList<Plant> getFamilyPlants(ArrayList<Plant> plants, PlantName mintName) {
        ArrayList<Plant> group = new ArrayList<>();
        if (plants == null) return group;
        for (Plant plant : plants) {
            if (plant != null && plant.isAlive() && isSameFamily(mintName, plant)) {
                group.add(plant);
            }
        }
        return group;
    }
    private boolean isSameFamily(PlantName mintName, Plant targetPlant) {
        if (targetPlant == null || targetPlant.getDefinition() == null) return false;
        PlantCategory targetCategory = targetPlant.getDefinition().getCategory();
        if (targetCategory == null) return false;
        switch (mintName) {
            case ENLIGHTEN_MINT:
                return targetCategory == PlantCategory.SUN_PRODUCER;
            case APPEASE_MINT:
                return targetCategory == PlantCategory.SHOOTER;
            case PIERCE_MINT:
                return targetCategory == PlantCategory.STRIKE_THROUGH;
            case ARMA_MINT:
                return targetCategory == PlantCategory.LOBBER;
            case BOMBARD_MINT:
                return targetCategory == PlantCategory.EXPLOSIVE;
            case ENFORCE_MINT:
                return targetCategory == PlantCategory.MELEE;
            case REINFORCE_MINT:
                return targetCategory == PlantCategory.WALL_NUT;
            case ENCHANT_MINT:
                return targetCategory == PlantCategory.MODIFIER;
            default:
                return false;
        }
    }
    public boolean isActivated() {
        return activated;
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\game\entity\plant\ability\AttackAbility.java ---
package game.entity.plant.ability;
import app.enums.ProjectilleEffect.ProjectileEffect;
import app.enums.plant.PlantCategory;
import app.enums.plant.PlantName;
import app.model.App;
import common.model.Position;
import game.entity.plant.Plant;
import game.entity.projectile.LobbedProjectile;
import game.entity.projectile.NormalShooterProjectile;
import game.entity.projectile.ShooterProjectile;
import game.entity.zombie.Zombie;
import game.service.plant.AttackService;
import game.service.plant.PlantService;
import game.world.GameWorld;
import game.world.playground.Grid;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;
public class AttackAbility extends AbstractPlantAbility {
    private final long attackCooldown;
    private long lastAttackTick;
    private AttackService attackService;
    public AttackAbility(long intervalTicks) {
        this.attackCooldown = intervalTicks;
        this.lastAttackTick = 0;
    }
    @Override
    protected void doUpdate(Plant plant, PlantService plantService , long currentTick) {
        if (plant == null || !plant.isAlive()) return;
        if (plant.getPlantFoodState().isActive()) return;
        PlantName name = plant.getDefinition().getName();
        Position position = plant.getPosition();
        int damage = plant.getAttackDamage();
        GameWorld gameWorld = App.getCurrentGameEngine().getGameWorld();
        this.attackService = new AttackService(gameWorld);
        Grid grid = gameWorld.getGrid();
        if (currentTick - lastAttackTick < attackCooldown && lastAttackTick != 0) return;
        PlantCategory category = plant.getDefinition().getCategory();
        if (category == PlantCategory.SUN_PRODUCER ||
                category == PlantCategory.WALL_NUT ||
                category == PlantCategory.MODIFIER) return;
        boolean attacked = performAttack(plant, currentTick , plantService);
        if (attacked) lastAttackTick = currentTick;
    }
    private boolean performAttack(Plant plant,  long currentTick , PlantService plantService) {
        PlantName name = plant.getDefinition().getName();
        Position position = new Position(plant.getPosition().getX() , plant.getPosition().getY());
        int damage = plant.getAttackDamage();
        GameWorld gameWorld = App.getCurrentGameEngine().getGameWorld();
        Grid grid = gameWorld.getGrid();
        int plantRow = (int) (plant.getPosition().getY() / grid.getConfig().getCellHeight());
        switch (name) {
            case PEASHOOTER:
                plantService.addProjectile(new NormalShooterProjectile(plant, plant.getAttackDamage() , position , getSpeed(name) , 0));
                break;
            case REPEATER:
                plantService.addProjectile(new NormalShooterProjectile(plant, plant.getAttackDamage() , position , getSpeed(name) , 0));
                plantService.addProjectile(new NormalShooterProjectile(plant, plant.getAttackDamage() , position , getSpeed(name) , 0 , 5));
                break;
            case THREEPEATER:
                plantService.addProjectile(new NormalShooterProjectile(plant, plant.getAttackDamage() , position , getSpeed(name) , 0));
                Position topPosition = new Position(position.getX() , position.getY()+grid.getConfig().getCellHeight());
                Position bottomPosition = new Position(position.getX() , position.getY()-grid.getConfig().getCellHeight());
                if (grid.isInsideGrid(topPosition))
                    plantService.addProjectile(new NormalShooterProjectile(plant, plant.getAttackDamage() , topPosition , getSpeed(name), 0));
                if (grid.isInsideGrid(bottomPosition))
                    plantService.addProjectile(new NormalShooterProjectile(plant, plant.getAttackDamage() , bottomPosition , getSpeed(name), 0));
                break;
            case SNOW_PEA:
                ShooterProjectile snowPeaProjectile = new NormalShooterProjectile(plant, plant.getAttackDamage() , position , getSpeed(name) , 0);
                snowPeaProjectile.addEffect(ProjectileEffect.ICE);
                plantService.addProjectile(snowPeaProjectile);
                break;
            case FIRE_PEASHOOTER:
                ShooterProjectile firePeaShooterProjectile = new NormalShooterProjectile(plant, plant.getAttackDamage() , position , getSpeed(name) , 0);
                firePeaShooterProjectile.addEffect(ProjectileEffect.FIRE);
                plantService.addProjectile(firePeaShooterProjectile);
                break;
            case GOO_PEASHOOTER:
                ShooterProjectile gooProjectile = new NormalShooterProjectile(plant, damage , position , getSpeed(name) , 0);
                gooProjectile.enableIgnoresArmor();
                plantService.addProjectile(gooProjectile);
                break;
            case MEGA_GATLING_PEA:
                plantService.addProjectile(new NormalShooterProjectile(plant, plant.getAttackDamage() , position , getSpeed(name) , 0));
                plantService.addProjectile(new NormalShooterProjectile(plant, plant.getAttackDamage() , position , getSpeed(name) , 0 , 2));
                plantService.addProjectile(new NormalShooterProjectile(plant, plant.getAttackDamage() , position , getSpeed(name) , 0 , 4));
                plantService.addProjectile(new NormalShooterProjectile(plant, plant.getAttackDamage() , position , getSpeed(name) , 0 , 6));
                break;
            case ROTOBAGA:
                plantService.addProjectile(new NormalShooterProjectile(plant, plant.getAttackDamage() , position , getSpeed(name) , 40));
                plantService.addProjectile(new NormalShooterProjectile(plant, plant.getAttackDamage() , position , getSpeed(name) , -40));
                plantService.addProjectile(new NormalShooterProjectile(plant, plant.getAttackDamage() , position , getSpeed(name) , 180+40));
                plantService.addProjectile(new NormalShooterProjectile(plant, plant.getAttackDamage() , position , getSpeed(name) , 180-40));
                break;
            case PEA_POD:
                plantService.addProjectile(new NormalShooterProjectile(plant, plant.getAttackDamage() , position , getSpeed(name) , 0));
                break;
            case SPLIT_PEA:
                plantService.addProjectile(new NormalShooterProjectile(plant, plant.getAttackDamage() , position , getSpeed(name) , 0));
                plantService.addProjectile(new NormalShooterProjectile(plant, plant.getAttackDamage() , position , getSpeed(name) , 180));
                plantService.addProjectile(new NormalShooterProjectile(plant, plant.getAttackDamage() , position , getSpeed(name) , 180 , 3));
                break;
            case CITRON:
                plantService.addProjectile(new NormalShooterProjectile(plant, plant.getAttackDamage() , position , getSpeed(name) , 0));
                break;
            case BOWLING_BULB:
                break;
            case STARFRUIT:
                plantService.addProjectile(new NormalShooterProjectile(plant, plant.getAttackDamage() , position , getSpeed(name) , 180));
                plantService.addProjectile(new NormalShooterProjectile(plant, plant.getAttackDamage() , position , getSpeed(name) , 180+72));
                plantService.addProjectile(new NormalShooterProjectile(plant, plant.getAttackDamage() , position , getSpeed(name) , 180+72+72));
                plantService.addProjectile(new NormalShooterProjectile(plant, plant.getAttackDamage() , position , getSpeed(name) , 180+72+72+72));
                plantService.addProjectile(new NormalShooterProjectile(plant, plant.getAttackDamage() , position , getSpeed(name) , 180+72+72+72+72));
                break;
            case SEA_SHROOM:
                ShooterProjectile seaShroomProjectile = new NormalShooterProjectile(plant, damage , position , getSpeed(name) , 0);
                seaShroomProjectile.setMaxLifetimeTicks(600);
                seaShroomProjectile.setMaxRange(grid.getPlaygroundWidth()/2.5);
                plantService.addProjectile(seaShroomProjectile);
                break;
            case PUFF_SHROOM:
                ShooterProjectile puffShroomProjectile = new NormalShooterProjectile(plant, damage , position , getSpeed(name) , 0);
                puffShroomProjectile.setMaxLifetimeTicks(600);
                puffShroomProjectile.setMaxRange(grid.getPlaygroundWidth()/2.5);
                plantService.addProjectile(puffShroomProjectile);
                break;
            case CABBAGE_PULT: {
                Zombie targetZombie = findFrontmostActiveZombie(gameWorld, grid, plantRow);
                if (targetZombie != null) {
                    System.out.println("Debug: CABBAGE_PULT !!!!!!!!!!! ");
                    plantService.addProjectile(new LobbedProjectile(plant, damage, position, getSpeed(name), targetZombie));
                }
                break;
            }
            case KERNEL_PULT: {
                Zombie targetZombie = findFrontmostActiveZombie(gameWorld, grid, plantRow);
                if (targetZombie != null) {
                    if (Math.random() < 0.5) {
                        LobbedProjectile butter = new LobbedProjectile(plant, damage, position, getSpeed(name), targetZombie);
                        butter.addEffect(ProjectileEffect.SLOWER);
                        plantService.addProjectile(butter);
                    } else {
                        plantService.addProjectile(new LobbedProjectile(plant, damage / 2, position, getSpeed(name), targetZombie));
                    }
                }
                break;
            }
            case MELON_PULT: {
                Zombie targetZombie = findFrontmostActiveZombie(gameWorld, grid, plantRow);
                if (targetZombie != null) {
                    LobbedProjectile melon = new LobbedProjectile(plant, damage, position, getSpeed(name), targetZombie);
                    melon.setSplash(true);
                    plantService.addProjectile(melon);
                }
                break;
            }
            case WINTER_MELON: {
                Zombie targetZombie = findFrontmostActiveZombie(gameWorld, grid, plantRow);
                if (targetZombie != null) {
                    LobbedProjectile winterMelon = new LobbedProjectile(plant, damage, position, getSpeed(name), targetZombie);
                    winterMelon.setSplash(true);
                    winterMelon.addEffect(ProjectileEffect.ICE);
                    plantService.addProjectile(winterMelon);
                }
                break;
            }
            case PEPPER_PULT: {
                Zombie targetZombie = findFrontmostActiveZombie(gameWorld, grid, plantRow);
                if (targetZombie != null) {
                    LobbedProjectile pepper = new LobbedProjectile(plant, damage, position, getSpeed(name), targetZombie);
                    pepper.setSplash(true);
                    pepper.addEffect(ProjectileEffect.FIRE);
                    plantService.addProjectile(pepper);
                }
                break;
            }
            case CACTUS:
                ShooterProjectile cactusProjectile = new NormalShooterProjectile(plant, damage , position , getSpeed(name) , 0);
                cactusProjectile.setMaxTargets(3);
                plantService.addProjectile(cactusProjectile);
                break;
            case FUME_SHROOM:
                ShooterProjectile fumeShroomProjectile = new NormalShooterProjectile(plant, damage , position , getSpeed(name) , 0);
                fumeShroomProjectile.enableInfinitePierce();
                fumeShroomProjectile.setMaxRange(grid.getPlaygroundWidth()/1.5);
                plantService.addProjectile(fumeShroomProjectile);
                break;
            default:
                return false;
        }
        return true;
    }
    private double getSpeed(PlantName plantName)
    {
        return switch (plantName)
        {
            case PEASHOOTER -> 0.4;
            case REPEATER -> 0.35;
            case THREEPEATER -> 0.4;
            case SNOW_PEA -> 0.5;
            case ROTOBAGA -> 0.3;
            case SPLIT_PEA -> 0.4;
            case CITRON -> 0.25;
            case FIRE_PEASHOOTER -> 0.4;
            case CACTUS -> 0.6;
            case STARFRUIT -> 0.37;
            case GOO_PEASHOOTER -> 0.35;
            case MEGA_GATLING_PEA -> 1;
            case SEA_SHROOM -> 0.3;
            case PUFF_SHROOM -> 0.32;
            case FUME_SHROOM -> 0.4;
            case CABBAGE_PULT -> 1.0;
            case KERNEL_PULT -> 1.0;
            case MELON_PULT -> 1.25;
            case WINTER_MELON -> 0.25;
            case PEPPER_PULT -> 0.25;
            default -> 0.5;
        };
    }
    private boolean hasTargetInLanes(Plant plant, Grid grid, GameWorld gameWorld) {
        int plantRow = (int) (plant.getPosition().getY() / grid.getConfig().getCellHeight());
        PlantName name = plant.getDefinition().getName();
        if (name == PlantName.THREEPEATER || name == PlantName.ROTOBAGA || name == PlantName.STARFRUIT) {
            return hasActiveZombieInRow(gameWorld, grid, plantRow) ||
                    hasActiveZombieInRow(gameWorld, grid, plantRow - 1) ||
                    hasActiveZombieInRow(gameWorld, grid, plantRow + 1);
        }
        return hasActiveZombieInRow(gameWorld, grid, plantRow);
    }
    private boolean hasActiveZombieInRow(GameWorld gameWorld, Grid grid, int row) {
        List<Zombie> zombies = getSafeZombiesInRow(gameWorld, grid, row);
        for (Zombie zombie : zombies) {
            if (zombie != null && !zombie.isDead()) {
                return true;
            }
        }
        return false;
    }
    private List<Zombie> getSafeZombiesInRow(GameWorld gameWorld, Grid grid, int row) {
        Position temporaryPosition = new Position(0, row * grid.getConfig().getCellHeight());
        if (grid.isInsideGrid(temporaryPosition)) {
            List<Zombie> zombies = gameWorld.getZombiesInRow(row);
            return zombies != null ? zombies : new ArrayList<>();
        }
        return new ArrayList<>();
    }
    private Zombie findFrontmostActiveZombie(GameWorld gameWorld, Grid grid, int row) {
        Zombie frontmostZombie = null;
        for (Zombie zombie : getSafeZombiesInRow(gameWorld, grid, row)) {
            if (zombie != null && !zombie.isDead()) {
                if (frontmostZombie == null || zombie.getPosition().getX() < frontmostZombie.getPosition().getX()) {
                    frontmostZombie = zombie;
                }
            }
        }
        return frontmostZombie;
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\game\entity\plant\ability\AttackPlantFood.java ---
package game.entity.plant.ability;
import app.enums.ProjectilleEffect.ProjectileEffect;
import app.enums.plant.PlantName;
import app.model.App;
import common.model.Position;
import game.entity.plant.Plant;
import game.entity.projectile.LobbedProjectile;
import game.entity.projectile.NormalShooterProjectile;
import game.entity.projectile.ShooterProjectile;
import game.entity.zombie.Zombie;
import game.service.plant.AttackService;
import game.service.plant.PlantService;
import game.world.GameWorld;
import game.world.playground.Grid;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;
public class AttackPlantFood extends AbstractPlantAbility {
    private long attackCooldown = 10;
    private long lastAttackTick = 0;
    private AttackService attackService;
    private boolean flag = false;
    public AttackPlantFood() {
        super();
    }
    public AttackPlantFood(long intervalTicks) {
        super();
        this.attackCooldown = intervalTicks;
        this.lastAttackTick = 0;
    }
    @Override
    protected void doUpdate(Plant plant, PlantService plantService, long currentTick) {
        if (plant == null || !plant.isAlive()) return;
        if (!plant.getPlantFoodState().isActive()) return;
        GameWorld gameWorld = App.getCurrentGameEngine().getGameWorld();
        if (this.attackService == null) {
            this.attackService = new AttackService(gameWorld);
        }
        Grid grid = gameWorld.getGrid();
        if (!hasTargetInLanes(plant, grid, gameWorld)) return;
        long actualCooldown = (long) getAttackCooldown(plant.getDefinition().getName());
        if (currentTick - lastAttackTick < actualCooldown && lastAttackTick != 0) return;
        boolean attacked = performAttack(plant, currentTick, plantService);
        if (attacked) {
            lastAttackTick = currentTick;
        }
    }
    private boolean performAttack(Plant plant, long currentTick, PlantService plantService) {
        PlantName name = plant.getDefinition().getName();
        Position position = new Position(plant.getPosition().getX(), plant.getPosition().getY());
        int damage = plant.getAttackDamage();
        GameWorld gameWorld = App.getCurrentGameEngine().getGameWorld();
        Grid grid = gameWorld.getGrid();
        int plantRow = (int) (plant.getPosition().getY() / grid.getConfig().getCellHeight());
        switch (name) {
            case PEASHOOTER -> {
                plantService.addProjectile(new NormalShooterProjectile(plant, damage, position, getSpeed(name), 0));
            }
            case REPEATER -> {
                plantService.addProjectile(new NormalShooterProjectile(plant, damage, position, getSpeed(name), 0));
                if (!flag) {
                    plantService.addProjectile(new NormalShooterProjectile(plant, damage * 20, position, getSpeed(name), 0));
                    flag = true;
                }
            }
            case THREEPEATER -> {
                for (int i = 0; i < grid.getRowCount(); i++) {
                    Position rowPos = new Position(position.getX(), i * grid.getConfig().getCellHeight());
                    plantService.addProjectile(new NormalShooterProjectile(plant, damage, rowPos, getSpeed(name), 0));
                }
            }
            case SNOW_PEA -> {
                ShooterProjectile snowPeaProjectile = new NormalShooterProjectile(plant, plant.getAttackDamage(), position, getSpeed(name), 0);
                snowPeaProjectile.addEffect(ProjectileEffect.ICE);
                plantService.addProjectile(snowPeaProjectile);
                attackService.damageZombiesInRowWithIce(plantRow, plant.getAttackDamage(), currentTick, 0.0, (int) plant.getPlantFoodState().getDurationTicks());
            }
            case FIRE_PEASHOOTER -> {
                ShooterProjectile firePeaShooterProjectile = new NormalShooterProjectile(plant, plant.getAttackDamage(), position, getSpeed(name), 0);
                firePeaShooterProjectile.addEffect(ProjectileEffect.FIRE);
                plantService.addProjectile(firePeaShooterProjectile);
            }
            case GOO_PEASHOOTER -> {
                ShooterProjectile gooProjectile = new NormalShooterProjectile(plant, damage, position, getSpeed(name), 0);
                gooProjectile.enableIgnoresArmor();
                plantService.addProjectile(gooProjectile);
            }
            case MEGA_GATLING_PEA -> {
                plantService.addProjectile(new NormalShooterProjectile(plant, plant.getAttackDamage(), position, getSpeed(name), 0));
                if (!flag) {
                    plantService.addProjectile(new NormalShooterProjectile(plant, plant.getAttackDamage(), position, getSpeed(name), 0, 3));
                    plantService.addProjectile(new NormalShooterProjectile(plant, plant.getAttackDamage(), position, getSpeed(name), 0, 6));
                    plantService.addProjectile(new NormalShooterProjectile(plant, plant.getAttackDamage(), position, getSpeed(name), 0, 9));
                    plantService.addProjectile(new NormalShooterProjectile(plant, plant.getAttackDamage(), position, getSpeed(name), 0, 12));
                    flag = true;
                }
            }
            case ROTOBAGA -> {
                plantService.addProjectile(new NormalShooterProjectile(plant, plant.getAttackDamage(), position, getSpeed(name), 40));
                plantService.addProjectile(new NormalShooterProjectile(plant, plant.getAttackDamage(), position, getSpeed(name), -40));
                plantService.addProjectile(new NormalShooterProjectile(plant, plant.getAttackDamage(), position, getSpeed(name), 180 + 40));
                plantService.addProjectile(new NormalShooterProjectile(plant, plant.getAttackDamage(), position, getSpeed(name), 180 - 40));
            }
            case SPLIT_PEA -> {
                plantService.addProjectile(new NormalShooterProjectile(plant, plant.getAttackDamage(), position, getSpeed(name), 0));
                plantService.addProjectile(new NormalShooterProjectile(plant, plant.getAttackDamage(), position, getSpeed(name), 180));
                plantService.addProjectile(new NormalShooterProjectile(plant, plant.getAttackDamage(), position, getSpeed(name), 180));
            }
            case CITRON -> {
                if (!flag) {
                    plantService.addProjectile(new NormalShooterProjectile(plant, plant.getAttackDamage() * 50, position, getSpeed(name), 0));
                    flag = true;
                }
            }
            case STARFRUIT -> {
                plantService.addProjectile(new NormalShooterProjectile(plant, plant.getAttackDamage(), position, getSpeed(name), 180));
                plantService.addProjectile(new NormalShooterProjectile(plant, plant.getAttackDamage(), position, getSpeed(name), 180 + 72));
                plantService.addProjectile(new NormalShooterProjectile(plant, plant.getAttackDamage(), position, getSpeed(name), 180 + 144));
                plantService.addProjectile(new NormalShooterProjectile(plant, plant.getAttackDamage(), position, getSpeed(name), 180 + 216));
                plantService.addProjectile(new NormalShooterProjectile(plant, plant.getAttackDamage(), position, getSpeed(name), 180 + 288));
            }
            case SEA_SHROOM -> {
                ShooterProjectile seaShroomProjectile = new NormalShooterProjectile(plant, damage, position, getSpeed(name), 0);
                seaShroomProjectile.setMaxLifetimeTicks(600);
                seaShroomProjectile.setMaxRange(grid.getPlaygroundWidth() / 2.5);
                plantService.addProjectile(seaShroomProjectile);
            }
            case PUFF_SHROOM -> {
                ShooterProjectile puffShroomProjectile = new NormalShooterProjectile(plant, damage, position, getSpeed(name), 0);
                puffShroomProjectile.setMaxLifetimeTicks(600);
                puffShroomProjectile.setMaxRange(grid.getPlaygroundWidth() / 2.5);
                plantService.addProjectile(puffShroomProjectile);
            }
            case CABBAGE_PULT -> {
                Zombie targetZombie = findFrontmostActiveZombie(gameWorld, grid, plantRow);
                if (targetZombie != null) {
                    plantService.addProjectile(new LobbedProjectile(plant, damage, position, getSpeed(name), targetZombie));
                }
            }
            case KERNEL_PULT -> {
                Zombie targetZombie = findFrontmostActiveZombie(gameWorld, grid, plantRow);
                if (targetZombie != null) {
                    if (Math.random() < 0.5) {
                        LobbedProjectile butter = new LobbedProjectile(plant, damage, position, getSpeed(name), targetZombie);
                        butter.addEffect(ProjectileEffect.SLOWER);
                        plantService.addProjectile(butter);
                    } else {
                        plantService.addProjectile(new LobbedProjectile(plant, damage / 2, position, getSpeed(name), targetZombie));
                    }
                }
            }
            case MELON_PULT -> {
                Zombie targetZombie = findFrontmostActiveZombie(gameWorld, grid, plantRow);
                if (targetZombie != null) {
                    LobbedProjectile melon = new LobbedProjectile(plant, damage, position, getSpeed(name), targetZombie);
                    melon.setSplash(true);
                    plantService.addProjectile(melon);
                }
            }
            case WINTER_MELON -> {
                Zombie targetZombie = findFrontmostActiveZombie(gameWorld, grid, plantRow);
                if (targetZombie != null) {
                    LobbedProjectile winterMelon = new LobbedProjectile(plant, damage, position, getSpeed(name), targetZombie);
                    winterMelon.setSplash(true);
                    winterMelon.addEffect(ProjectileEffect.ICE);
                    plantService.addProjectile(winterMelon);
                }
            }
            case PEPPER_PULT -> {
                Zombie targetZombie = findFrontmostActiveZombie(gameWorld, grid, plantRow);
                if (targetZombie != null) {
                    LobbedProjectile pepper = new LobbedProjectile(plant, damage, position, getSpeed(name), targetZombie);
                    pepper.setSplash(true);
                    pepper.addEffect(ProjectileEffect.FIRE);
                    plantService.addProjectile(pepper);
                }
            }
            case CACTUS -> {
                ShooterProjectile cactusProjectile = new NormalShooterProjectile(plant, damage * 5, position, getSpeed(name), 0);
                cactusProjectile.enableInfinitePierce();
                plantService.addProjectile(cactusProjectile);
            }
            case FUME_SHROOM -> {
                ShooterProjectile fumeShroomProjectile = new NormalShooterProjectile(plant, damage, position, getSpeed(name), 0);
                fumeShroomProjectile.enableInfinitePierce();
                fumeShroomProjectile.setMaxRange(grid.getPlaygroundWidth() / 1.5);
                plantService.addProjectile(fumeShroomProjectile);
            }
            default -> {
                return false;
            }
        }
        return true;
    }
    private double getSpeed(PlantName plantName) {
        return 1.6 * switch (plantName) {
            case PEASHOOTER -> 0.4;
            case REPEATER -> 0.35;
            case THREEPEATER -> 0.4;
            case SNOW_PEA -> 0.5;
            case ROTOBAGA -> 0.3;
            case SPLIT_PEA -> 0.4;
            case CITRON -> 0.25;
            case FIRE_PEASHOOTER -> 0.4;
            case CACTUS -> 0.6;
            case STARFRUIT -> 0.37;
            case GOO_PEASHOOTER -> 0.35;
            case MEGA_GATLING_PEA -> 1.0;
            case SEA_SHROOM -> 0.3;
            case PUFF_SHROOM -> 0.32;
            case FUME_SHROOM -> 0.4;
            case CABBAGE_PULT -> 0.3;
            case KERNEL_PULT -> 0.3;
            case MELON_PULT -> 0.25;
            case WINTER_MELON -> 0.25;
            case PEPPER_PULT -> 0.25;
            default -> 0.5;
        };
    }
    private boolean hasTargetInLanes(Plant plant, Grid grid, GameWorld gameWorld) {
        int plantRow = (int) (plant.getPosition().getY() / grid.getConfig().getCellHeight());
        PlantName name = plant.getDefinition().getName();
        if (name == PlantName.THREEPEATER) {
            return hasActiveZombieInRow(gameWorld, grid, plantRow) ||
                    hasActiveZombieInRow(gameWorld, grid, plantRow - 1) ||
                    hasActiveZombieInRow(gameWorld, grid, plantRow + 1);
        }
        if (name == PlantName.ROTOBAGA || name == PlantName.STARFRUIT) {
            return true;
        }
        return hasActiveZombieInRow(gameWorld, grid, plantRow);
    }
    private boolean hasActiveZombieInRow(GameWorld gameWorld, Grid grid, int row) {
        List<Zombie> zombies = getSafeZombiesInRow(gameWorld, grid, row);
        for (Zombie zombie : zombies) {
            if (zombie != null && !zombie.isDead()) {
                return true;
            }
        }
        return false;
    }
    private List<Zombie> getSafeZombiesInRow(GameWorld gameWorld, Grid grid, int row) {
        Position temporaryPosition = new Position(0, row * grid.getConfig().getCellHeight());
        if (grid.isInsideGrid(temporaryPosition)) {
            List<Zombie> zombies = gameWorld.getZombiesInRow(row);
            return zombies != null ? zombies : new ArrayList<>();
        }
        return new ArrayList<>();
    }
    private Zombie findFrontmostActiveZombie(GameWorld gameWorld, Grid grid, int row) {
        Zombie frontmostZombie = null;
        for (Zombie zombie : getSafeZombiesInRow(gameWorld, grid, row)) {
            if (zombie != null && !zombie.isDead()) {
                if (frontmostZombie == null || zombie.getPosition().getX() < frontmostZombie.getPosition().getX()) {
                    frontmostZombie = zombie;
                }
            }
        }
        return frontmostZombie;
    }
    private double getAttackCooldown(PlantName name) {
        return switch (name) {
            case PEASHOOTER -> attackCooldown / 3.0;
            case REPEATER -> attackCooldown / 5.0;
            case THREEPEATER -> attackCooldown / 3.0;
            case ROTOBAGA -> attackCooldown / 3.0;
            case SNOW_PEA -> attackCooldown / 4.0;
            case SPLIT_PEA -> attackCooldown / 3.0;
            case CITRON -> attackCooldown;
            case FIRE_PEASHOOTER -> attackCooldown / 4.0;
            case CACTUS -> attackCooldown / 2.0;
            case STARFRUIT -> attackCooldown / 3.0;
            case GOO_PEASHOOTER -> attackCooldown / 2.5;
            case MEGA_GATLING_PEA -> attackCooldown / 3.0;
            case SEA_SHROOM, PUFF_SHROOM -> attackCooldown / 5.0;
            case FUME_SHROOM, CABBAGE_PULT, KERNEL_PULT, MELON_PULT, WINTER_MELON, PEPPER_PULT -> attackCooldown;
            default -> 5.0;
        };
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\game\entity\plant\ability\ExplodeAbility.java ---
package game.entity.plant.ability;
import app.enums.ProjectilleEffect.ProjectileEffect;
import app.enums.plant.PlantName;
import app.enums.quest.EventType;
import app.model.App;
import common.model.Position;
import game.entity.plant.Plant;
import game.entity.projectile.NormalShooterProjectile;
import game.entity.quest.GameEvent;
import game.entity.zombie.Zombie;
import game.service.plant.AttackService;
import game.service.plant.PlantService;
import game.world.GameWorld;
import game.world.playground.Cell;
import game.world.playground.Grid;
public class ExplodeAbility extends AbstractPlantAbility {
    private long potatoMineArmTicks = 150;
    private long primalPotatoMineArmTicks = 50;
    private long squashDelayTicks = 10;
    private int icebergFreezeDurationTicks = 100;
    private long instantExplosionDelayTicks = 2;
    private int grapeshotProjectileCount = 8;
    private final double grapeshotProjectileSpeed = 0.5;
    private final int grapeshotProjectileDamage = 20;
    private final int normalExplosionRadius = 1;
    private final int doomShroomRadius = 2;
    private boolean triggered = false;
    private long triggerTick = -1;
    private int targetRow = -1;
    private int targetCol = -1;
    private boolean isEnableExplodeONut;
    private AttackService attackService;
    public ExplodeAbility() {
        super();
    }
    @Override
    protected void doUpdate(Plant plant, PlantService plantService, long currentTick) {
        if (plant == null) return;
        GameWorld gameWorld = App.getCurrentGameEngine().getGameWorld();
        if (attackService == null) {
            attackService = new AttackService(gameWorld);
        }
        PlantName name = plant.getDefinition().getName();
        if (name == PlantName.EXPLODE_O_NUT) {
            explodeONutHandler(plant, plantService, gameWorld, currentTick);
            return;
        }
        if (!plant.isAlive()) return;
        if (plant.getPlantFoodState().isActive()) return;
        switch (name) {
            case POTATO_MINE -> potatoMineHandler(plant, plantService, gameWorld, currentTick);
            case PRIMAL_POTATO_MINE -> primalPotatoMineHandler(plant, plantService, gameWorld, currentTick);
            case CHERRY_BOMB -> cherryBombHandler(plant, plantService, gameWorld, currentTick);
            case SQUASH -> squashHandler(plant, plantService, gameWorld, currentTick);
            case GRAPESHOT -> grapeshotHandler(plant, plantService, gameWorld, currentTick);
            case JALAPENO -> jalapenoHandler(plant, plantService, gameWorld, currentTick);
            case DOOM_SHROOM -> doomShroomHandler(plant, plantService, gameWorld, currentTick);
            case TANGLE_KELP -> tangleKelpHandler(plant, plantService, gameWorld, currentTick);
            case ICEBERG_LETTUCE -> icebergLettuceHandler(plant, plantService, gameWorld, currentTick);
            case ICE_SHROOM -> iceShroomHandler(plant, plantService, gameWorld, currentTick);
        }
    }
    private void iceShroomHandler(Plant plant, PlantService plantService, GameWorld gameWorld, long currentTick) {
        if (currentTick - plant.getPlantedTick() < instantExplosionDelayTicks) return;
        Grid grid = gameWorld.getGrid();
        Cell cell = grid.getCellAt(plant.getPosition());
        if (cell != null) {
            gameWorld.addEvent("Ice-shroom froze all zombies on the map!");
            App.getCurrentUser().getQuestManager().fireEvent(new GameEvent(EventType.PLANT_EXPLODED, 1));
            attackService.freezeZombiesInMap(currentTick, 30);
        }
        plantService.removePlant(plant);
    }
    private void explodeONutHandler(Plant plant, PlantService plantService, GameWorld gameWorld, long currentTick) {
        Grid grid = gameWorld.getGrid();
        Cell cell = grid.getCellAt(plant.getPosition());
        if (cell != null && (plant.getHealth().isDead() || isEnableExplodeONut)) {
            App.getCurrentUser().getQuestManager().fireEvent(new GameEvent(EventType.PLANT_EXPLODED, 1));
            attackService.damageArea(cell.getRow(), cell.getCol(), 1, plant.getAttackDamage(), currentTick);
            isEnableExplodeONut = false;
            plantService.removePlant(plant);
        }
    }
    private void potatoMineHandler(Plant plant, PlantService plantService, GameWorld gameWorld, long currentTick) {
        if (currentTick - plant.getPlantedTick() < potatoMineArmTicks) return;
        Grid grid = gameWorld.getGrid();
        Cell cell = grid.getCellAt(plant.getPosition());
        if (cell != null && attackService.hasActiveZombieInCell(cell)) {
            gameWorld.addEvent("Potato Mine exploded on contact!");
            App.getCurrentUser().getQuestManager().fireEvent(new GameEvent(EventType.PLANT_EXPLODED, 1));
            attackService.damageZombiesInCell(cell.getRow(), cell.getCol(), plant.getAttackDamage(), currentTick);
            plantService.removePlant(plant);
        }
    }
    private void primalPotatoMineHandler(Plant plant, PlantService plantService, GameWorld gameWorld, long currentTick) {
        if (currentTick - plant.getPlantedTick() < primalPotatoMineArmTicks) return;
        Grid grid = gameWorld.getGrid();
        Cell cell = grid.getCellAt(plant.getPosition());
        if (cell != null && attackService.hasActiveZombieInCell(cell)) {
            gameWorld.addEvent("Primal Potato Mine exploded in a 3x3 area!");
            App.getCurrentUser().getQuestManager().fireEvent(new GameEvent(EventType.PLANT_EXPLODED, 1));
            attackService.damageArea(cell.getRow(), cell.getCol(), normalExplosionRadius, plant.getAttackDamage(), currentTick);
            plantService.removePlant(plant);
        }
    }
    private void cherryBombHandler(Plant plant, PlantService plantService, GameWorld gameWorld, long currentTick) {
        if (currentTick - plant.getPlantedTick() < instantExplosionDelayTicks) return;
        Grid grid = gameWorld.getGrid();
        Cell cell = grid.getCellAt(plant.getPosition());
        if (cell != null) {
            gameWorld.addEvent("Cherry Bomb exploded instantly in a 3x3 area!");
            App.getCurrentUser().getQuestManager().fireEvent(new GameEvent(EventType.PLANT_EXPLODED, 1));
            attackService.damageArea(cell.getRow(), cell.getCol(), normalExplosionRadius, plant.getAttackDamage(), currentTick);
        }
        plantService.removePlant(plant);
    }
    private void squashHandler(Plant plant, PlantService plantService, GameWorld gameWorld, long currentTick) {
        Grid grid = gameWorld.getGrid();
        Cell cell = grid.getCellAt(plant.getPosition());
        if (cell == null) return;
        int row = cell.getRow();
        int col = cell.getCol();
        if (!triggered) {
            Cell ownCell = grid.getCell(row, col);
            Cell frontCell = grid.getCell(row, col + 1);
            if (ownCell != null && attackService.hasActiveZombieInCell(ownCell)) {
                startSquashTrigger(currentTick, row, col);
            } else if (frontCell != null && attackService.hasActiveZombieInCell(frontCell)) {
                startSquashTrigger(currentTick, row, col + 1);
            }
        } else {
            if (currentTick - triggerTick >= squashDelayTicks) {
                gameWorld.addEvent("Squash smashed all zombies in cell (" + targetRow + ", " + targetCol + ")");
                App.getCurrentUser().getQuestManager().fireEvent(new GameEvent(EventType.PLANT_EXPLODED, 1));
                attackService.damageZombiesInCell(targetRow, targetCol, plant.getAttackDamage(), currentTick);
                plantService.removePlant(plant);
            }
        }
    }
    private void grapeshotHandler(Plant plant, PlantService plantService, GameWorld gameWorld, long currentTick) {
        if (currentTick - plant.getPlantedTick() < instantExplosionDelayTicks) return;
        Grid grid = gameWorld.getGrid();
        Cell cell = grid.getCellAt(plant.getPosition());
        if (cell != null) {
            gameWorld.addEvent("Grapeshot exploded in 3x3 and scattered bouncing grapes!");
            App.getCurrentUser().getQuestManager().fireEvent(new GameEvent(EventType.PLANT_EXPLODED, 1));
            attackService.damageArea(cell.getRow(), cell.getCol(), normalExplosionRadius, plant.getAttackDamage(), currentTick);
            double angleStep = 360.0 / grapeshotProjectileCount;
            for (int i = 0; i < grapeshotProjectileCount; i++) {
                double angle = i * angleStep;
                Position spawnPos = new Position(plant.getPosition().getX(), plant.getPosition().getY());
                NormalShooterProjectile grape = new NormalShooterProjectile(
                        plant,
                        grapeshotProjectileDamage,
                        spawnPos,
                        grapeshotProjectileSpeed,
                        angle
                );
                grape.addEffect(ProjectileEffect.POISONOUS);
                plantService.addProjectile(grape);
            }
        }
        plantService.removePlant(plant);
    }
    private void jalapenoHandler(Plant plant, PlantService plantService, GameWorld gameWorld, long currentTick) {
        if (currentTick - plant.getPlantedTick() < instantExplosionDelayTicks) return;
        Grid grid = gameWorld.getGrid();
        Cell cell = grid.getCellAt(plant.getPosition());
        if (cell != null) {
            int row = cell.getRow();
            gameWorld.addEvent("Jalapeno scorched the entire lane " + row);
            App.getCurrentUser().getQuestManager().fireEvent(new GameEvent(EventType.PLANT_EXPLODED, 1));
            attackService.damageZombiesInRowWithFire(row, plant.getAttackDamage(), currentTick, 20);
        }
        plantService.removePlant(plant);
    }
    private void doomShroomHandler(Plant plant, PlantService plantService, GameWorld gameWorld, long currentTick) {
        if (currentTick - plant.getPlantedTick() < instantExplosionDelayTicks) return;
        Grid grid = gameWorld.getGrid();
        Cell cell = grid.getCellAt(plant.getPosition());
        if (cell != null) {
            gameWorld.addEvent("Doom-shroom triggered a massive explosion and created a crater!");
            App.getCurrentUser().getQuestManager().fireEvent(new GameEvent(EventType.PLANT_EXPLODED, 1));
            attackService.damageArea(cell.getRow(), cell.getCol(), doomShroomRadius, plant.getAttackDamage(), currentTick);
        }
        plantService.removePlant(plant);
    }
    private void tangleKelpHandler(Plant plant, PlantService plantService, GameWorld gameWorld, long currentTick) {
    }
    private void icebergLettuceHandler(Plant plant, PlantService plantService, GameWorld gameWorld, long currentTick) {
        Grid grid = gameWorld.getGrid();
        Cell cell = grid.getCellAt(plant.getPosition());
        if (cell != null && attackService.hasActiveZombieInCell(cell)) {
            gameWorld.addEvent("Iceberg Lettuce froze the target cell!");
            App.getCurrentUser().getQuestManager().fireEvent(new GameEvent(EventType.PLANT_EXPLODED, 1));
            attackService.freezeZombieInCell(cell, currentTick, icebergFreezeDurationTicks);
            plantService.removePlant(plant);
        }
    }
    private void startSquashTrigger(long currentTick, int row, int col) {
        this.triggered = true;
        this.triggerTick = currentTick;
        this.targetRow = row;
        this.targetCol = col;
    }
    public void setPotatoMineArmTicks(long ticks) { this.potatoMineArmTicks = ticks; }
    public void setPrimalPotatoMineArmTicks(long ticks) { this.primalPotatoMineArmTicks = ticks; }
    public void setSquashDelayTicks(long ticks) { this.squashDelayTicks = ticks; }
    public void setIcebergFreezeDurationTicks(int ticks) { this.icebergFreezeDurationTicks = ticks; }
    public void setGrapeshotProjectileCount(int count) { this.grapeshotProjectileCount = count; }
    public void setInstantExplosionDelayTicks(long ticks) { this.instantExplosionDelayTicks = ticks; }
    public void setEnableExplodeONut(boolean enableExplodeONut) {
        isEnableExplodeONut = enableExplodeONut;
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\game\entity\plant\ability\ExplodePlantFood.java ---
package game.entity.plant.ability;
import app.enums.ProjectilleEffect.ProjectileEffect;
import app.enums.plant.PlantName;
import app.enums.quest.EventType;
import app.model.App;
import common.model.Position;
import game.entity.plant.Plant;
import game.entity.projectile.NormalShooterProjectile;
import game.entity.quest.GameEvent;
import game.entity.zombie.Zombie;
import game.service.plant.AttackService;
import game.service.plant.PlantService;
import game.world.GameWorld;
import game.world.playground.Cell;
import game.world.playground.Grid;
public class ExplodePlantFood extends AbstractPlantAbility {
    private long potatoMineArmTicks = 150;
    private long primalPotatoMineArmTicks = 50;
    private long squashDelayTicks = 10;
    private int icebergFreezeDurationTicks = 100;
    private int grapeshotProjectileCount = 8;
    private final double grapeshotProjectileSpeed = 0.5;
    private final int grapeshotProjectileDamage = 20;
    private final int normalExplosionRadius = 1;
    private final int doomShroomRadius = 2;
    private boolean triggered = false;
    private long triggerTick = -1;
    private int targetRow = -1;
    private int targetCol = -1;
    private boolean isEnableExplodeONut;
    private AttackService attackService;
    public ExplodePlantFood() {
        super();
    }
    @Override
    protected void doUpdate(Plant plant, PlantService plantService, long currentTick) {
        if (plant == null || !plant.isAlive()) return;
        if (!plant.getPlantFoodState().isActive()) return;
        GameWorld gameWorld = App.getCurrentGameEngine().getGameWorld();
        if (this.attackService == null) {
            this.attackService = new AttackService(gameWorld);
        }
        PlantName name = plant.getDefinition().getName();
        switch (name) {
            case POTATO_MINE -> potatoMineHandler(plant, plantService, gameWorld, currentTick);
            case PRIMAL_POTATO_MINE -> primalPotatoMineHandler(plant, plantService, gameWorld, currentTick);
            case CHERRY_BOMB -> cherryBombHandler(plant, plantService, gameWorld, currentTick);
            case SQUASH -> squashHandler(plant, plantService, gameWorld, currentTick);
            case GRAPESHOT -> grapeshotHandler(plant, plantService, gameWorld, currentTick);
            case JALAPENO -> jalapenoHandler(plant, plantService, gameWorld, currentTick);
            case DOOM_SHROOM -> doomShroomHandler(plant, plantService, gameWorld, currentTick);
            case TANGLE_KELP -> tangleKelpHandler(plant, plantService, gameWorld, currentTick);
            case ICEBERG_LETTUCE -> icebergLettuceHandler(plant, plantService, gameWorld, currentTick);
            case EXPLODE_O_NUT -> explodeONutHandler(plant, plantService, gameWorld, currentTick);
            case ICE_SHROOM -> iceShroomHandler(plant, plantService, gameWorld, currentTick);
        }
    }
    private void iceShroomHandler(Plant plant, PlantService plantService, GameWorld gameWorld, long currentTick) {
        Grid grid = gameWorld.getGrid();
        Cell cell = grid.getCellAt(plant.getPosition());
        if (cell != null) {
            gameWorld.addEvent("Ice-shroom froze all zombies on the map during Plant Food!");
            App.getCurrentUser().getQuestManager().fireEvent(new GameEvent(EventType.PLANT_EXPLODED, 1));
            attackService.freezeZombiesInMap(currentTick, 30);
        }
        plantService.removePlant(plant);
    }
    private void explodeONutHandler(Plant plant, PlantService plantService, GameWorld gameWorld, long currentTick) {
        Grid grid = gameWorld.getGrid();
        Cell cell = grid.getCellAt(plant.getPosition());
        if (cell != null && (plant.getHealth().isDead() || isEnableExplodeONut)) {
            App.getCurrentUser().getQuestManager().fireEvent(new GameEvent(EventType.PLANT_EXPLODED, 1));
            attackService.damageArea(cell.getRow(), cell.getCol(), 1, plant.getAttackDamage(), currentTick);
            isEnableExplodeONut = false;
            plantService.removePlant(plant);
        }
    }
    private void potatoMineHandler(Plant plant, PlantService plantService, GameWorld gameWorld, long currentTick) {
        Grid grid = gameWorld.getGrid();
        Cell cell = grid.getCellAt(plant.getPosition());
        if (cell != null && attackService.hasActiveZombieInCell(cell)) {
            gameWorld.addEvent("Potato Mine exploded via Plant Food!");
            App.getCurrentUser().getQuestManager().fireEvent(new GameEvent(EventType.PLANT_EXPLODED, 1));
            attackService.damageZombiesInCell(cell.getRow(), cell.getCol(), plant.getAttackDamage(), currentTick);
            plantService.removePlant(plant);
        }
    }
    private void primalPotatoMineHandler(Plant plant, PlantService plantService, GameWorld gameWorld, long currentTick) {
        Grid grid = gameWorld.getGrid();
        Cell cell = grid.getCellAt(plant.getPosition());
        if (cell != null && attackService.hasActiveZombieInCell(cell)) {
            gameWorld.addEvent("Primal Potato Mine exploded in a 3x3 area via Plant Food!");
            App.getCurrentUser().getQuestManager().fireEvent(new GameEvent(EventType.PLANT_EXPLODED, 1));
            attackService.damageArea(cell.getRow(), cell.getCol(), normalExplosionRadius, plant.getAttackDamage(), currentTick);
            plantService.removePlant(plant);
        }
    }
    private void cherryBombHandler(Plant plant, PlantService plantService, GameWorld gameWorld, long currentTick) {
        Grid grid = gameWorld.getGrid();
        Cell cell = grid.getCellAt(plant.getPosition());
        if (cell != null) {
            gameWorld.addEvent("Cherry Bomb exploded instantly in a 3x3 area!");
            App.getCurrentUser().getQuestManager().fireEvent(new GameEvent(EventType.PLANT_EXPLODED, 1));
            attackService.damageArea(cell.getRow(), cell.getCol(), normalExplosionRadius, plant.getAttackDamage(), currentTick);
        }
        plantService.removePlant(plant);
    }
    private void squashHandler(Plant plant, PlantService plantService, GameWorld gameWorld, long currentTick) {
        Grid grid = gameWorld.getGrid();
        Cell cell = grid.getCellAt(plant.getPosition());
        if (cell == null) return;
        int row = cell.getRow();
        int col = cell.getCol();
        if (!triggered) {
            Cell ownCell = grid.getCell(row, col);
            Cell frontCell = grid.getCell(row, col + 1);
            if (ownCell != null && attackService.hasActiveZombieInCell(ownCell)) {
                startSquashTrigger(currentTick, row, col);
            } else if (frontCell != null && attackService.hasActiveZombieInCell(frontCell)) {
                startSquashTrigger(currentTick, row, col + 1);
            }
        } else {
            if (currentTick - triggerTick >= squashDelayTicks) {
                gameWorld.addEvent("Squash smashed all zombies in cell (" + targetRow + ", " + targetCol + ")");
                App.getCurrentUser().getQuestManager().fireEvent(new GameEvent(EventType.PLANT_EXPLODED, 1));
                attackService.damageZombiesInCell(targetRow, targetCol, plant.getAttackDamage(), currentTick);
                plantService.removePlant(plant);
            }
        }
    }
    private void grapeshotHandler(Plant plant, PlantService plantService, GameWorld gameWorld, long currentTick) {
        Grid grid = gameWorld.getGrid();
        Cell cell = grid.getCellAt(plant.getPosition());
        if (cell != null) {
            gameWorld.addEvent("Grapeshot exploded in 3x3 and scattered bouncing grapes!");
            App.getCurrentUser().getQuestManager().fireEvent(new GameEvent(EventType.PLANT_EXPLODED, 1));
            attackService.damageArea(cell.getRow(), cell.getCol(), normalExplosionRadius, plant.getAttackDamage(), currentTick);
            double angleStep = 360.0 / grapeshotProjectileCount;
            for (int i = 0; i < grapeshotProjectileCount; i++) {
                double angle = i * angleStep;
                Position spawnPos = new Position(plant.getPosition().getX(), plant.getPosition().getY());
                NormalShooterProjectile grape = new NormalShooterProjectile(
                        plant,
                        grapeshotProjectileDamage,
                        spawnPos,
                        grapeshotProjectileSpeed,
                        angle
                );
                grape.addEffect(ProjectileEffect.POISONOUS);
                plantService.addProjectile(grape);
            }
        }
        plantService.removePlant(plant);
    }
    private void jalapenoHandler(Plant plant, PlantService plantService, GameWorld gameWorld, long currentTick) {
        Grid grid = gameWorld.getGrid();
        Cell cell = grid.getCellAt(plant.getPosition());
        if (cell != null) {
            int row = cell.getRow();
            gameWorld.addEvent("Jalapeno scorched the entire lane " + row);
            App.getCurrentUser().getQuestManager().fireEvent(new GameEvent(EventType.PLANT_EXPLODED, 1));
            attackService.damageZombiesInRowWithFire(row, plant.getAttackDamage(), currentTick, 20);
        }
        plantService.removePlant(plant);
    }
    private void doomShroomHandler(Plant plant, PlantService plantService, GameWorld gameWorld, long currentTick) {
        Grid grid = gameWorld.getGrid();
        Cell cell = grid.getCellAt(plant.getPosition());
        if (cell != null) {
            gameWorld.addEvent("Doom-shroom triggered a massive explosion and created a crater!");
            App.getCurrentUser().getQuestManager().fireEvent(new GameEvent(EventType.PLANT_EXPLODED, 1));
            attackService.damageArea(cell.getRow(), cell.getCol(), doomShroomRadius, plant.getAttackDamage(), currentTick);
        }
        plantService.removePlant(plant);
    }
    private void tangleKelpHandler(Plant plant, PlantService plantService, GameWorld gameWorld, long currentTick) {
    }
    private void icebergLettuceHandler(Plant plant, PlantService plantService, GameWorld gameWorld, long currentTick) {
        Grid grid = gameWorld.getGrid();
        Cell cell = grid.getCellAt(plant.getPosition());
        if (cell != null && attackService.hasActiveZombieInCell(cell)) {
            gameWorld.addEvent("Iceberg Lettuce froze the target cell!");
            App.getCurrentUser().getQuestManager().fireEvent(new GameEvent(EventType.PLANT_EXPLODED, 1));
            attackService.freezeZombieInCell(cell, currentTick, icebergFreezeDurationTicks);
            plantService.removePlant(plant);
        }
    }
    private void startSquashTrigger(long currentTick, int row, int col) {
        this.triggered = true;
        this.triggerTick = currentTick;
        this.targetRow = row;
        this.targetCol = col;
    }
    public void setPotatoMineArmTicks(long ticks) { this.potatoMineArmTicks = ticks; }
    public void setPrimalPotatoMineArmTicks(long ticks) { this.primalPotatoMineArmTicks = ticks; }
    public void setSquashDelayTicks(long ticks) { this.squashDelayTicks = ticks; }
    public void setIcebergFreezeDurationTicks(int ticks) { this.icebergFreezeDurationTicks = ticks; }
    public void setGrapeshotProjectileCount(int count) { this.grapeshotProjectileCount = count; }
    public void setEnableExplodeONut(boolean enableExplodeONut) {
        isEnableExplodeONut = enableExplodeONut;
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\game\entity\plant\ability\MeleeAbility.java ---
package game.entity.plant.ability;
import app.enums.plant.PlantName;
import app.model.App;
import game.entity.effect.FireEffect;
import game.entity.plant.Plant;
import game.entity.zombie.Zombie;
import game.service.plant.AttackService;
import game.service.plant.PlantService;
import game.world.GameWorld;
import game.world.playground.Cell;
import game.world.playground.Grid;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;
public class MeleeAbility extends AbstractPlantAbility {
    private final long attackCooldown;
    private long lastAttackTick;
    private static final int TICKS_PER_SECOND = 50;
    private boolean isDigesting;
    private long digestionEndTick;
    public MeleeAbility(long intervalTicks) {
        this.attackCooldown = intervalTicks;
        this.lastAttackTick = 0;
        this.isDigesting = false;
        this.digestionEndTick = 0;
    }
    @Override
    protected void doUpdate(Plant plant, PlantService plantService, long currentTick) {
        if (plant == null || !plant.isAlive() || plant.getHealth().getCurrentHealth() <= 0) return;
        if (plant.getPlantFoodState() != null && plant.getPlantFoodState().isActive()) return;
        PlantName name = plant.getDefinition().getName();
        GameWorld gameWorld = App.getCurrentGameEngine().getGameWorld();
        AttackService attackService = new AttackService(gameWorld);
        if (name == PlantName.CHOMPER) {
            if (isDigesting) {
                if (currentTick >= digestionEndTick) {
                    isDigesting = false;
                }
                return;
            }
        }
        if (lastAttackTick != 0 && (currentTick - lastAttackTick < attackCooldown)) {
            return;
        }
        boolean attacked = performAttack(plant, currentTick, attackService, gameWorld);
        if (attacked) {
            lastAttackTick = currentTick;
        }
    }
    private boolean performAttack(Plant plant, long currentTick, AttackService attackService, GameWorld gameWorld) {
        PlantName name = plant.getDefinition().getName();
        int damage = plant.getAttackDamage();
        Grid grid = gameWorld.getGrid();
        int plantRow = (int) (plant.getPosition().getY() / grid.getConfig().getCellHeight());
        int plantCol = (int) (plant.getPosition().getX() / grid.getConfig().getCellWidth());
        switch (name) {
            case BONK_CHOY: {
                Zombie target = getFrontMostZombieInCell(grid, plantRow, plantCol + 1);
                if (target == null) {
                    target = getFrontMostZombieInCell(grid, plantRow, plantCol);
                }
                if (target == null) {
                    target = getFrontMostZombieInCell(grid, plantRow, plantCol - 1);
                }
                if (target != null) {
                    attackService.damageZombie(target, currentTick, damage);
                    return true;
                }
                return false;
            }
            case PHAT_BEET: {
                return damageZombiesInArea(grid, attackService, plantRow, plantCol, 1, damage, currentTick);
            }
            case CHOMPER: {
                Zombie target = getFrontMostZombieInCell(grid, plantRow, plantCol + 1);
                if (target == null) {
                    target = getFrontMostZombieInCell(grid, plantRow, plantCol);
                }
                if (target != null) {
                    target.getTakingDamageStrategy().takeDamage(target, currentTick, 99999);
                    gameWorld.addEvent(plant.getName() + " swallowed a zombie!");
                    this.isDigesting = true;
                    this.digestionEndTick = currentTick + (40L * TICKS_PER_SECOND);
                    return true;
                }
                return false;
            }
            case WASABI_WHIP: {
                Zombie target = getFrontMostZombieInCell(grid, plantRow, plantCol + 1);
                if (target == null) {
                    target = getFrontMostZombieInCell(grid, plantRow, plantCol);
                }
                if (target == null) {
                    target = getFrontMostZombieInCell(grid, plantRow, plantCol - 1);
                }
                if (target != null) {
                    target.addEffect(new FireEffect(currentTick, 4 * TICKS_PER_SECOND));
                    attackService.damageZombie(target, currentTick, damage);
                    return true;
                }
                return false;
            }
            case KIWIBEAST: {
                long ageInTicks = currentTick - plant.getPlantedTick();
                long ageInSeconds = ageInTicks / TICKS_PER_SECOND;
                int currentDamage = damage;
                int radius = 0;
                if (ageInSeconds >= 72) {
                    currentDamage = damage * 3;
                    radius = 1;
                } else if (ageInSeconds >= 24) {
                    currentDamage = damage * 2;
                    radius = 0;
                }
                return damageZombiesInArea(grid, attackService, plantRow, plantCol, radius, currentDamage, currentTick);
            }
            default:
                return false;
        }
    }
    private Zombie getFrontMostZombieInCell(Grid grid, int row, int col) {
        Zombie frontMost = null;
        return frontMost;
    }
    private boolean damageZombiesInArea(Grid grid, AttackService attackService, int centerRow, int centerCol, int radius, int damage, long currentTick) {
        boolean attacked = false;
        return attacked;
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\game\entity\plant\ability\MeleePlantFood.java ---
package game.entity.plant.ability;
import app.enums.plant.PlantName;
import app.model.App;
import game.entity.effect.FireEffect;
import game.entity.plant.Plant;
import game.entity.zombie.Zombie;
import game.service.plant.AttackService;
import game.service.plant.PlantService;
import game.world.GameWorld;
import game.world.playground.Cell;
import game.world.playground.Grid;
import java.util.ArrayList;
public class MeleePlantFood extends AbstractPlantAbility {
    private static final int TICKS_PER_SECOND = 50;
    @Override
    protected void doUpdate(Plant plant, PlantService plantService, long currentTick) {
        if (plant == null || !plant.isAlive() || plant.getHealth().getCurrentHealth() <= 0) return;
        if (plant.getPlantFoodState() == null || !plant.getPlantFoodState().isActive()) return;
        GameWorld gameWorld = App.getCurrentGameEngine().getGameWorld();
        AttackService attackService = new AttackService(gameWorld);
        executePlantFoodEffect(plant, currentTick, attackService, gameWorld);
        plant.getPlantFoodState().deactivate();
    }
    private void executePlantFoodEffect(Plant plant, long currentTick, AttackService attackService, GameWorld gameWorld) {
        PlantName name = plant.getDefinition().getName();
        int baseDamage = plant.getAttackDamage();
        Grid grid = gameWorld.getGrid();
        int plantRow = (int) (plant.getPosition().getY() / grid.getConfig().getCellHeight());
        int plantCol = (int) (plant.getPosition().getX() / grid.getConfig().getCellWidth());
        switch (name) {
            case BONK_CHOY: {
                damageZombiesInArea(grid, attackService, plantRow, plantCol, 1, baseDamage * 5, currentTick);
                gameWorld.addEvent(plant.getName() + " unleashed a flurry of punches!");
                break;
            }
            case PHAT_BEET: {
                damageZombiesInArea(grid, attackService, plantRow, plantCol, 1, baseDamage * 6, currentTick);
                gameWorld.addEvent(plant.getName() + " created a powerful sonic shockwave!");
                break;
            }
            case CHOMPER: {
                int count = 0;
                for (Zombie z : new ArrayList<>(gameWorld.getZombies())) {
                    if (z != null && !z.isDead()) {
                        z.getTakingDamageStrategy().takeDamage(z, currentTick, 99999);
                        count++;
                        if (count >= 3) break;
                    }
                }
                gameWorld.addEvent(plant.getName() + " swallowed 3 zombies from distance!");
                break;
            }
            case WASABI_WHIP: {
                gameWorld.addEvent(plant.getName() + " spun its flaming whip in all directions!");
                break;
            }
            case KIWIBEAST: {
                damageZombiesInArea(grid, attackService, plantRow, plantCol, 1, baseDamage * 5, currentTick);
                gameWorld.addEvent(plant.getName() + " slammed the ground with massive force!");
                break;
            }
            default:
                break;
        }
    }
    private boolean damageZombiesInArea(Grid grid, AttackService attackService, int centerRow, int centerCol, int radius, int damage, long currentTick) {
        boolean attacked = false;
        return attacked;
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\game\entity\plant\ability\ModifierProjectileAbility.java ---
package game.entity.plant.ability;
import app.enums.ProjectilleEffect.ProjectileEffect;
import app.enums.plant.PlantName;
import app.model.App;
import common.model.Position;
import game.entity.plant.Plant;
import game.entity.projectile.Projectile;
import game.entity.projectile.ShooterProjectile;
import game.service.plant.PlantService;
import game.world.GameWorld;
import game.world.playground.Grid;
import java.util.ArrayList;
public class ModifierProjectileAbility extends AbstractPlantAbility {
    @Override
    protected void doUpdate(Plant plant, PlantService plantService , long currentTick) {
        if (plant == null || !plant.isAlive()) return;
        boolean test = abilityHandler(plant, currentTick , plantService);
    }
    private boolean abilityHandler(Plant plant,  long currentTick , PlantService plantService) {
        PlantName name = plant.getDefinition().getName();
        Position position = new Position(plant.getPosition().getX() , plant.getPosition().getY());
        int damage = plant.getAttackDamage();
        GameWorld gameWorld = App.getCurrentGameEngine().getGameWorld();
        ArrayList<Projectile> projectiles = new ArrayList<>(gameWorld.getProjectiles());
        Grid grid = gameWorld.getGrid();
        int plantRow = (int) plant.getPosition().getY();
        switch (name) {
            case TORCHWOOD:
                for (Projectile projectile:projectiles)
                {
                    if (grid.getCellAt(projectile.getPosition()).equals(grid.getCellAt(position)))
                    {
                        if (projectile instanceof ShooterProjectile shooterProjectile)
                        {
                            shooterProjectile.addEffect(ProjectileEffect.FIRE);
                            shooterProjectile.removeEffect(ProjectileEffect.ICE);
                        }
                    }
                }
                break;
            default:
                return false;
        }
        return true;
    }
}
