--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\game\entity\plant\ability\PlantFoodAbility.java ---
package game.entity.plant.ability;
import app.enums.plant.PlantName;
import game.entity.plant.Plant;
import game.service.plant.PlantService;
public class PlantFoodAbility extends AbstractPlantAbility{
    AttackPlantFood attackPlantFood;
    ExplodePlantFood explodePlantFood;
    MeleePlantFood meleePlantFood;
    WallNutPlantFood wallNutPlantFood;
    public PlantFoodAbility(long intervalTicks){
        attackPlantFood = new AttackPlantFood(intervalTicks);
        explodePlantFood = new ExplodePlantFood();
        meleePlantFood = new MeleePlantFood();
        wallNutPlantFood = new WallNutPlantFood();
    }
    @Override
    protected void doUpdate(Plant plant, PlantService plantService, long currentTick) {
        if (plant == null) return;
        if (!plant.getPlantFoodState().isActive()) return;
        PlantName name = plant.getDefinition().getName();
        switch (name)
        {
            case SUNFLOWER -> sunflowerHandler(plant , plantService , currentTick);
            case TWIN_SUNFLOWER -> twinSunflowerHandler(plant , plantService , currentTick);
            case SUN_SHROOM -> sunShroomHandler(plant , plantService , currentTick);
            case PRIMAL_SUNFLOWER -> primalSunflowerHandler(plant , plantService , currentTick);
            case PEASHOOTER, REPEATER, THREEPEATER, SNOW_PEA, FIRE_PEASHOOTER,
                 GOO_PEASHOOTER, MEGA_GATLING_PEA, ROTOBAGA, PEA_POD, SPLIT_PEA,
                 CITRON, BOWLING_BULB, STARFRUIT, SEA_SHROOM, PUFF_SHROOM,
                 CABBAGE_PULT, KERNEL_PULT, MELON_PULT, WINTER_MELON, PEPPER_PULT,
                 CACTUS, FUME_SHROOM ->
                    attackPlantFood.doUpdate(plant , plantService , currentTick);
            case POTATO_MINE, PRIMAL_POTATO_MINE, CHERRY_BOMB, SQUASH, GRAPESHOT,
                 JALAPENO, DOOM_SHROOM, TANGLE_KELP, ICEBERG_LETTUCE,
                 EXPLODE_O_NUT, ICE_SHROOM ->
                    explodePlantFood.doUpdate(plant , plantService , currentTick);
            case BONK_CHOY, PHAT_BEET, CHOMPER, WASABI_WHIP, KIWIBEAST ->
                    meleePlantFood.doUpdate(plant , plantService , currentTick);
            case WALL_NUT, TALL_NUT, ENDURIAN, GARLIC, SWEET_POTATO,
                 PUMPKIN, SUN_BEAN ->
                    wallNutPlantFood.doUpdate(plant , plantService , currentTick);
        }
    }
    private void sunflowerHandler(Plant plant, PlantService plantService, long currentTick)
    {
        plantService.getSunSpawner().spawnPlantSun(plant , 150);
        plant.getPlantFoodState().deactivate();
    }
    private void twinSunflowerHandler(Plant plant, PlantService plantService, long currentTick)
    {
        plantService.getSunSpawner().spawnPlantSun(plant , 250);
        plant.getPlantFoodState().deactivate();
    }
    private void sunShroomHandler(Plant plant, PlantService plantService, long currentTick) {
    }
    private void primalSunflowerHandler(Plant plant, PlantService plantService, long currentTick)
    {
        plantService.getSunSpawner().spawnPlantSun(plant , 225);
        plant.getPlantFoodState().deactivate();
    }
}

package game.entity.plant.ability;
public enum ProductionState {
    PRODUCING,
    WAITING_COLLECTION
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\game\entity\plant\ability\SunProductionAbility.java ---
package game.entity.plant.ability;
import app.enums.plant.PlantName;
import app.enums.sun.SunState;
import game.entity.plant.Plant;
import game.entity.sun.PlantSun;
import game.service.plant.PlantService;
public class SunProductionAbility extends AbstractPlantAbility {
    private final long productionDurationTicks;
    private int currentGrowthStage;
    private final long stageDurationTicks;
    private float stageDurationMultiplier;
    private PlantSun sunProduced;
    private ProductionState productionState;
    private long stateStartTick;
    public SunProductionAbility(long productionDurationTicks, long stageDurationTicks , float stageDurationMultiplier)
    {
        this.productionDurationTicks = productionDurationTicks;
        this.currentGrowthStage = 0;
        this.stageDurationTicks = stageDurationTicks;
        this.stageDurationMultiplier = stageDurationMultiplier;
        this.productionState = ProductionState.PRODUCING;
    }
    public SunProductionAbility(long productionDurationTicks) {
        this.productionDurationTicks = productionDurationTicks;
        this.currentGrowthStage = 1;
        this.stageDurationTicks = 0;
        this.productionState = ProductionState.PRODUCING;
    }
    @Override
    protected void doUpdate(Plant plant, PlantService plantService, long currentTick) {
        if (plant == null ) return;
        PlantName name = plant.getDefinition().getName();
        if (name == PlantName.GOLD_BLOOM) goldBloomHandler(plant,plantService);
        if (!plant.isAlive()) return;
        switch (name){
            case SUNFLOWER -> sunFlowerHandler(plant , currentTick,plantService);
            case TWIN_SUNFLOWER -> twinSunFlowerHandler(plant , currentTick,plantService);
            case SUN_SHROOM -> sunShroomHandler(plant , currentTick , plantService);
            case PRIMAL_SUNFLOWER -> primalSunFlowerHandler(plant , currentTick,plantService);
        }
    }
    private int calculateSunAmount(Plant plant) {
        PlantName name = plant.getDefinition().getName();
        return switch (name) {
            case SUNFLOWER -> 50;
            case TWIN_SUNFLOWER -> 100;
            case PRIMAL_SUNFLOWER -> 75;
            case SUN_SHROOM -> currentGrowthStage * 25;
            case GOLD_BLOOM -> 375;
            default -> 0;
        };
    }
    private void produceSun(Plant plant, PlantService plantService, int amount) {
        sunProduced = (PlantSun) plantService.getSunSpawner().spawnPlantSun(plant , amount);
        productionState = ProductionState.WAITING_COLLECTION;
    }
    private void sunFlowerHandler(Plant plant , long currentTick , PlantService plantService) {
        if (stateStartTick == 0) {
            stateStartTick = plant.getPlantedTick();
        }
        switch (productionState) {
            case WAITING_COLLECTION -> {
                if (sunProduced != null &&
                        sunProduced.getState() == SunState.COLLECTED) {
                    sunProduced = null;
                    productionState = ProductionState.PRODUCING;
                    stateStartTick = currentTick;
                }
            }
            case PRODUCING -> {
                long elapsed = currentTick - stateStartTick;
                if (elapsed >= productionDurationTicks) produceSun(plant , plantService , calculateSunAmount(plant));
            }
        }
    }
    private void twinSunFlowerHandler(Plant plant , long currentTick , PlantService plantService)
    {
        sunFlowerHandler(plant , currentTick,plantService);
    }
    private void sunShroomHandler(Plant plant, long currentTick, PlantService plantService) {
        long elapsedSincePlanted = currentTick - plant.getPlantedTick();
        if (currentGrowthStage < 3) {
            currentGrowthStage = getNewStage(plant , elapsedSincePlanted , plantService);
        } else currentGrowthStage = 3;
        sunFlowerHandler(plant, currentTick, plantService);
    }
    private int getNewStage(Plant plant , long elapsedSincePlanted , PlantService plantService)
    {
        boolean plantFoodActive = plant.getPlantFoodState().isActive();
        int newStage = 1;
        if (plantFoodActive)
        {
            plantService.getSunSpawner().spawnPlantSun(plant , 225);
            newStage = 3;
            plant.getPlantFoodState().deactivate();
        }
        if (newStage >= 3) return 3;
        if (elapsedSincePlanted > stageDurationTicks) {
            newStage = 2;
        }
        if (elapsedSincePlanted > stageDurationTicks + (long)(stageDurationTicks * stageDurationMultiplier)) {
            newStage = 3;
        }
        return newStage;
    }
    private void primalSunFlowerHandler(Plant plant, long currentTick, PlantService plantService)
    {
        sunFlowerHandler(plant, currentTick, plantService);
    }
    private void goldBloomHandler(Plant plant ,PlantService plantService)
    {
        if (stateStartTick == 0) {
            stateStartTick = plant.getPlantedTick();
        }
        produceSun(plant , plantService , calculateSunAmount(plant));
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\game\entity\plant\ability\WallNutAbility.java ---
package game.entity.plant.ability;
import app.enums.plant.PlantName;
import app.model.App;
import common.model.Position;
import game.entity.plant.Plant;
import game.entity.zombie.Zombie;
import game.service.plant.AttackService;
import game.service.plant.PlantService;
import game.world.GameWorld;
import game.world.playground.Cell;
import game.world.playground.Grid;
import java.util.ArrayList;
public class WallNutAbility extends AbstractPlantAbility {
    @Override
    protected void doUpdate(Plant plant, PlantService plantService, long currentTick) {
        if (plant == null || !plant.isAlive() || plant.getHealth().getCurrentHealth() <= 0) return;
        if (plant.getPlantFoodState() != null && plant.getPlantFoodState().isActive()) return;
        abilityHandler(plant, currentTick, plantService);
    }
    private boolean abilityHandler(Plant plant, long currentTick, PlantService plantService) {
        PlantName name = plant.getDefinition().getName();
        GameWorld gameWorld = App.getCurrentGameEngine().getGameWorld();
        Grid grid = gameWorld.getGrid();
        AttackService attackService = new AttackService(gameWorld);
        int plantRow = (int) (plant.getPosition().getY() );
        int plantCol = (int) (plant.getPosition().getX() );
        long damageTick = plant.getPlantDamage() != null ? plant.getPlantDamage().getLastDamageTick() : -1;
        switch (name) {
            case ENDURIAN: {
                if (damageTick == currentTick && plant.getPlantDamage() != null) {
                    Zombie attacker = plant.getPlantDamage().getZombie();
                    if (attacker != null && !attacker.isDead()) {
                        int reflectDamage = plant.getAttackDamage() > 0 ? plant.getAttackDamage() : 20;
                        attackService.damageZombie(attacker, currentTick, reflectDamage);
                    }
                }
                break;
            }
            case GARLIC: {
                if (damageTick == currentTick && plant.getPlantDamage() != null) {
                    Zombie attacker = plant.getPlantDamage().getZombie();
                    if (attacker != null && !attacker.isDead()) {
                        redirectZombieToAdjacentLane(attacker, plantRow, grid);
                    }
                }
                break;
            }
            case SWEET_POTATO: {
                attractZombiesFromAdjacentLanes(plantRow, plantCol, grid);
                break;
            }
            case EXPLODE_O_NUT: {
                if (!plant.isAlive() || plant.getHealth().getCurrentHealth() <= 0) {
                    ExplodeAbility explode = new ExplodeAbility();
                    explode.setEnableExplodeONut(true);
                    plant.getAbilities().add(explode);
                }
                break;
            }
            case SUN_BEAN: {
                if (damageTick == currentTick && plantService.getSunSpawner() != null) {
                    plantService.getSunSpawner().spawnPlantSun(plant, 5);
                }
                break;
            }
            case WALL_NUT:
            case TALL_NUT:
            case PUMPKIN:
            default:
                break;
        }
        return true;
    }
    private void redirectZombieToAdjacentLane(Zombie zombie, int currentRow, Grid grid) {
        double x = zombie.getPosition().getX();
        Position upPos = new Position(x, (currentRow - 1) );
        Position downPos = new Position(x, (currentRow + 1) );
        if (grid.isInsideGrid(upPos)) {
            zombie.setPosition(upPos);
        } else if (grid.isInsideGrid(downPos)) {
            zombie.setPosition(downPos);
        }
    }
    private void attractZombiesFromAdjacentLanes(int plantRow, int plantCol, Grid grid) {
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\game\entity\plant\ability\WallNutPlantFood.java ---
package game.entity.plant.ability;
import app.enums.plant.PlantName;
import app.model.App;
import common.model.Position;
import game.entity.plant.Plant;
import game.entity.plant.PlantHealth;
import game.entity.zombie.Zombie;
import game.service.plant.PlantService;
import game.world.GameWorld;
import game.world.playground.Cell;
import game.world.playground.Grid;
import java.util.ArrayList;
public class WallNutPlantFood extends AbstractPlantAbility {
    @Override
    protected void doUpdate(Plant plant, PlantService plantService, long currentTick) {
        if (plant == null || !plant.isAlive() || plant.getHealth().getCurrentHealth() <= 0) return;
        if (plant.getPlantFoodState() == null || !plant.getPlantFoodState().isActive()) return;
        executePlantFoodEffect(plant, currentTick, plantService);
        plant.getPlantFoodState().deactivate();
    }
    private boolean executePlantFoodEffect(Plant plant, long currentTick, PlantService plantService) {
        PlantName name = plant.getDefinition().getName();
        GameWorld gameWorld = App.getCurrentGameEngine().getGameWorld();
        Grid grid = gameWorld.getGrid();
        int plantRow = plant.getRow();
        int plantCol = plant.getCol();
        switch (name) {
            case WALL_NUT: {
                int newMaxHp = plant.getHealth().getMaxHealth() + 4000;
                plant.setHealth(new PlantHealth(newMaxHp));
                gameWorld.addEvent(plant.getName() + " gained permanent armor (+4000 HP)!");
                break;
            }
            case TALL_NUT: {
                int newMaxHp = plant.getHealth().getMaxHealth() + 8000;
                plant.setHealth(new PlantHealth(newMaxHp));
                gameWorld.addEvent(plant.getName() + " gained heavy permanent armor (+8000 HP)!");
                break;
            }
            case ENDURIAN: {
                int newMaxHp = plant.getHealth().getMaxHealth() + 3000;
                plant.setHealth(new PlantHealth(newMaxHp));
                gameWorld.addEvent(plant.getName() + " gained metal armor (+3000 HP)!");
                break;
            }
            case GARLIC: {
                gameWorld.addEvent(plant.getName() + " diverted all zombies in its lane!");
                break;
            }
            case SWEET_POTATO: {
                plant.heal(plant.getHealth().getMaxHealth());
                attractZombiesSurrounding(plantRow, plantCol, grid);
                gameWorld.addEvent(plant.getName() + " fully healed and pulled all surrounding zombies!");
                break;
            }
            case EXPLODE_O_NUT: {
                int newMaxHp = plant.getHealth().getMaxHealth() + 4000;
                plant.setHealth(new PlantHealth(newMaxHp));
                gameWorld.addEvent(plant.getName() + " gained explosive metal armor (+4000 HP)!");
                break;
            }
            case PUMPKIN: {
                int newMaxHp = plant.getHealth().getMaxHealth() + 4000;
                plant.setHealth(new PlantHealth(newMaxHp));
                gameWorld.addEvent(plant.getName() + " gained heavy metal shield (+4000 HP)!");
                break;
            }
            case SUN_BEAN: {
                int newMaxHp = plant.getHealth().getMaxHealth() + 1000;
                plant.setHealth(new PlantHealth(newMaxHp));
                gameWorld.addEvent(plant.getName() + " gained protective armor (+1000 HP)!");
                break;
            }
            default:
                return false;
        }
        return true;
    }
    private void redirectZombieToAdjacentLane(Zombie zombie, int currentRow, Grid grid) {
        double x = zombie.getPosition().getX();
        Position upPos = new Position(x, currentRow - 1);
        Position downPos = new Position(x, currentRow + 1);
        if (grid.isInsideGrid(upPos)) {
            zombie.setPosition(upPos);
        } else if (grid.isInsideGrid(downPos)) {
            zombie.setPosition(downPos);
        }
    }
    private void attractZombiesSurrounding(int centerRow, int centerCol, Grid grid) {
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\game\entity\plant\seedpacket\SeedBank.java ---
package game.entity.plant.seedpacket;
import app.enums.plant.PlantName;
import java.util.Collections;
import java.util.HashMap;
import java.util.Map;
public class SeedBank {
    private final Map<PlantName, SeedPacket> packets;
    private int capacity;
    public SeedBank() {
        this(500);
    }
    public SeedBank(int capacity) {
        this.packets = new HashMap<>();
        this.capacity = capacity;
    }
    public boolean addPacket(PlantName name, int amount) {
        if (amount <= 0) {
            return false;
        }
        if (!packets.containsKey(name) && isFull()) {
            return false;
        }
        packets.computeIfAbsent(name, k -> new SeedPacket(k, 0)).addPacket(amount);
        return true;
    }
    public boolean addPacket(SeedPacket packet) {
        if (packet == null) {
            return false;
        }
        return addPacket(packet.getPlantName(), packet.getAmount());
    }
    public boolean removePacket(PlantName name, int amount) {
        if (amount <= 0) {
            return true;
        }
        SeedPacket seedPacket = packets.get(name);
        if (seedPacket == null) {
            return false;
        }
        if (seedPacket.getAmount() < amount) {
            return false;
        }
        seedPacket.removePacket(amount);
        if (seedPacket.isEmpty()) {
            packets.remove(name);
        }
        return true;
    }
    public int getAmount(PlantName name) {
        SeedPacket packet = packets.get(name);
        return packet != null ? packet.getAmount() : 0;
    }
    public SeedPacket getPacket(PlantName name) {
        return packets.get(name);
    }
    public boolean hasPacket(PlantName name) {
        return getAmount(name) > 0;
    }
    public boolean hasEnough(PlantName name, int amount) {
        return getAmount(name) >= amount;
    }
    public boolean isFull() {
        return packets.size() >= capacity;
    }
    public boolean isEmpty() {
        return packets.isEmpty();
    }
    public int getCapacity() {
        return capacity;
    }
    public void setCapacity(int capacity) {
        this.capacity = capacity;
    }
    public int getSize() {
        return packets.size();
    }
    public Map<PlantName, SeedPacket> getPackets() {
        return Collections.unmodifiableMap(packets);
    }
    public void clear() {
        packets.clear();
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\game\entity\plant\seedpacket\SeedPacket.java ---
package game.entity.plant.seedpacket;
import app.enums.plant.PlantName;
public class SeedPacket
{
    private final PlantName plantName;
    private int amount;
    public SeedPacket(PlantName name)
    {
        this(name, 1);
    }
    public SeedPacket(PlantName name, int amount)
    {
        this.plantName = name;
        this.amount = Math.max(0, amount);
    }
    public void addPacket(int amount)
    {
        if (amount > 0)
        {
            this.amount += amount;
        }
    }
    public boolean removePacket(int amount)
    {
        if (amount <= 0 ) return false;
        this.amount = Math.max(0 , this.amount - amount);
        return true;
    }
    public int getAmount()
    {
        return amount;
    }
    public PlantName getPlantName()
    {
        return plantName;
    }
    public boolean isEmpty()
    {
        return amount <= 0;
    }
    public boolean hasEnough(int requiredAmount)
    {
        return amount >= requiredAmount;
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\game\entity\plantfood\PlantFoodInventory.java ---
package game.entity.plantfood;
public class PlantFoodInventory
{
    private static final int MAX_AMOUNT = 3;
    private int amount;
    public PlantFoodInventory() {
        this.amount = 0;
    }
    public int getAmount()
    {
        return amount;
    }
    public int getMaxAmount()
    {
        return MAX_AMOUNT;
    }
    public boolean isFull()
    {
        return amount >= MAX_AMOUNT;
    }
    public boolean willBeFull(int count) {
        return amount + count - 1 >= MAX_AMOUNT;
    }
    public boolean hasPlantFood()
    {
        return amount > 0;
    }
    public void addPlantFood()
    {
        amount = Math.min(amount+1 , 3);
    }
    public void consumePlantFood()
    {
        amount = Math.max(amount-1 , 0);
    }
    public void clear()
    {
        amount = 0;
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\game\entity\projectile\LobbedProjectile.java ---
package game.entity.projectile;
import app.enums.ProjectilleEffect.ProjectileEffect;
import app.enums.effect.EffectType;
import app.enums.projectile.ProjectileType;
import common.model.Position;
import game.entity.effect.CharmEffect;
import game.entity.effect.PoisonEffect;
import game.entity.effect.SlowEffect;
import game.entity.plant.Plant;
import game.entity.zombie.Zombie;
import game.world.GameWorld;
import game.world.playground.Cell;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;
public class LobbedProjectile extends Projectile {
    private double arcHeight;
    private final Zombie targetZombie;
    private final Position targetLastPosition;
    private final double initialDistance;
    private boolean hasLanded = false;
    private boolean isSplash = false;
    private double splashDamageMultiplier = 0.5;
    private int splashRadius = 1;
    protected List<ProjectileEffect> effects = new ArrayList<>();
    public LobbedProjectile(Plant ownerPlant, int damage, Position position, double speed, Zombie targetZombie) {
        this(ownerPlant, damage, position, speed, targetZombie, 0);
    }
    public LobbedProjectile(Plant ownerPlant, int damage, Position position, double speed, Zombie targetZombie, int delayOffset) {
        super(ownerPlant, damage, position, speed, ProjectileType.LOB, delayOffset);
        this.targetZombie = targetZombie;
        this.arcHeight = 2.0;
        this.canBypassObstacles = true;
        if (targetZombie != null) {
            this.targetLastPosition = new Position(targetZombie.getPosition().getX(), targetZombie.getPosition().getY());
        } else {
            this.targetLastPosition = new Position(position != null ? position.getX() : 0, position != null ? position.getY() : 0);
        }
        this.initialDistance = calculateDistance(position, targetLastPosition);
    }
    @Override
    protected void move(long currentTick) {
        if (!active) {
            return;
        }
        if (targetZombie != null && !targetZombie.isDead()) {
            targetLastPosition.setX(targetZombie.getPosition().getX());
            targetLastPosition.setY(targetZombie.getPosition().getY());
        }
        double differenceX = targetLastPosition.getX() - position.getX();
        double differenceY = targetLastPosition.getY() - position.getY();
        double distance = Math.sqrt(differenceX * differenceX + differenceY * differenceY);
        double step = speed / 10.0;
        if (distance <= step) {
            position.setX(targetLastPosition.getX());
            position.setY(targetLastPosition.getY());
            hasLanded = true;
        } else {
            double ratio = step / distance;
            position.setX(position.getX() + differenceX * ratio);
            position.setY(position.getY() + differenceY * ratio);
        }
    }
    @Override
    protected void onHit(GameWorld gameWorld, long currentTick) {
        if (!active || !hasLanded) {
            return;
        }
        Zombie primaryTarget = findPrimaryTarget(gameWorld);
        if (primaryTarget != null) {
            applyDamageAndEffects(primaryTarget, damage, currentTick, false, gameWorld);
        }
        if (isSplash && gameWorld != null && gameWorld.getGrid() != null && position != null) {
            applySplashDamage(gameWorld, primaryTarget, currentTick);
        }
        destroy();
    }
    private Zombie findPrimaryTarget(GameWorld gameWorld) {
        if (targetZombie != null && !targetZombie.isDead()) {
            return targetZombie;
        }
        return null;
    }
    private void applySplashDamage(GameWorld gameWorld, Zombie primaryTarget, long currentTick) {
        int splashDamage = (int) (damage * splashDamageMultiplier);
        int centerRow = (int) position.getY();
        int centerColumn = (int) position.getX();
    }
    private void applyDamageAndEffects(Zombie zombie, int damageAmount, long currentTick, boolean isSplash, GameWorld gameWorld) {
        effectHandler(zombie, currentTick);
        zombie.getTakingDamageStrategy().takeDamage(zombie, currentTick, damageAmount);
        String logType = isSplash ? "splash damage" : "primary damage from lobbed projectile";
        gameWorld.addEvent(zombie.getName() + " took " + damageAmount + " " + logType);
        hitZombies.add(zombie);
    }
    public double getCurrentVisualHeight() {
        if (hasLanded || initialDistance == 0) {
            return 0.0;
        }
        double currentDistance = calculateDistance(position, targetLastPosition);
        double progress = 1.0 - (currentDistance / initialDistance);
        progress = Math.max(0.0, Math.min(1.0, progress));
        return 4.0 * arcHeight * progress * (1.0 - progress);
    }
    private double calculateDistance(Position positionOne, Position positionTwo) {
        if (positionOne == null || positionTwo == null) {
            return 0.0;
        }
        double differenceX = positionOne.getX() - positionTwo.getX();
        double differenceY = positionOne.getY() - positionTwo.getY();
        return Math.sqrt(differenceX * differenceX + differenceY * differenceY);
    }
    public void addEffect(ProjectileEffect effect) {
        if (!effects.contains(effect)) {
            effects.add(effect);
        }
    }
    public void removeEffect(ProjectileEffect effect) {
        effects.remove(effect);
    }
    protected void effectHandler(Zombie zombie, long currentTick) {
        if (effects.contains(ProjectileEffect.FIRE)) {
            damage *= 2;
            zombie.removeEffect(EffectType.ICE);
        }
        if (effects.contains(ProjectileEffect.ICE)) {
            zombie.addEffect(new SlowEffect(currentTick, 40, 0.3f, EffectType.ICE));
        }
        if (effects.contains(ProjectileEffect.SLOWER)) {
            zombie.addEffect(new SlowEffect(currentTick, 40, 0.5f, EffectType.SLOW));
        }
        if (effects.contains(ProjectileEffect.HYPNOSIS)) {
            zombie.addEffect(new CharmEffect(currentTick, 10));
        }
        if (effects.contains(ProjectileEffect.POISONOUS)) {
            zombie.addEffect(new PoisonEffect(currentTick, 10, 5));
        }
    }
    public double getArcHeight() {
        return arcHeight;
    }
    public void setArcHeight(double arcHeight) {
        this.arcHeight = arcHeight;
    }
    public boolean isSplash() {
        return isSplash;
    }
    public void setSplash(boolean splash) {
        isSplash = splash;
    }
    public double getSplashDamageMultiplier() {
        return splashDamageMultiplier;
    }
    public void setSplashDamageMultiplier(double splashDamageMultiplier) {
        this.splashDamageMultiplier = splashDamageMultiplier;
    }
    public int getSplashRadius() {
        return splashRadius;
    }
    public void setSplashRadius(int splashRadius) {
        this.splashRadius = splashRadius;
    }
    public Zombie getTargetZombie() {
        return targetZombie;
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\game\entity\projectile\NormalShooterProjectile.java ---
package game.entity.projectile;
import common.model.Position;
import game.entity.plant.Plant;
import game.entity.zombie.Zombie;
import game.world.GameWorld;
import game.world.playground.Cell;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;
public class NormalShooterProjectile extends ShooterProjectile {
    public NormalShooterProjectile(Plant ownerPlant, int damage, Position position, double speed, double angle) {
        super(ownerPlant, damage, position, speed);
        this.angle = angle;
    }
    public NormalShooterProjectile(Plant ownerPlant, int damage, Position position, double speed, double angle, int delayOffset) {
        super(ownerPlant, damage, position, speed, delayOffset);
        this.angle = angle;
    }
    @Override
    protected void move(long currentTick) {
        if (!active || !launched) {
            return;
        }
        double normalizedAngle = ((angle % 360) + 360) % 360;
        double radians = Math.toRadians(normalizedAngle);
        double distance = speed / 10.0;
        double deltaX = distance * Math.cos(radians);
        double deltaY = -distance * Math.sin(radians);
        position.setX(position.getX() + deltaX);
        position.setY(position.getY() + deltaY);
    }
    @Override
    protected void onHit(GameWorld gameWorld, long currentTick) {
        if (!active || !launched) {
            return;
        }
        Cell cell = getCell(gameWorld);
        if (cell == null) {
            destroy();
            return;
        }
        if (cell.hasObstacle() && !canBypassObstacles) {
            cell.getObstacle().onHit(currentTick , damage);
            destroy();
            return;
        }
        List<Zombie> zombies = cell.getZombies();
        if (zombies == null || zombies.isEmpty()) {
            return;
        }
        List<Zombie> eligibleZombies = new ArrayList<>();
        for (Zombie z : zombies) {
            if (!hitZombies.contains(z)) {
                eligibleZombies.add(z);
            }
        }
        if (eligibleZombies.isEmpty()) {
            return;
        }
        Zombie frontZombie = eligibleZombies.get(0);
        for (Zombie z : eligibleZombies) {
            if (z.getPosition().getX() < frontZombie.getPosition().getX()) {
                frontZombie = z;
            }
        }
        effectHandler(frontZombie, currentTick);
        frontZombie.getTakingDamageStrategy().takeDamage(
                frontZombie,
                currentTick,
                damage
        );
        hitZombies.add(frontZombie);
        gameWorld.addEvent("Zombie " + frontZombie.getName() + " took " + damage + " damage.");
        if (!infinitePierce && hitZombies.size() >= maxTargets) {
            destroy();
        }
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\game\entity\projectile\Projectile.java ---
package game.entity.projectile;
import app.enums.projectile.ProjectileType;
import common.model.Position;
import game.entity.Entity;
import game.entity.plant.Plant;
import game.entity.zombie.Zombie;
import game.world.GameWorld;
import game.world.playground.Cell;
import java.util.HashSet;
import java.util.Set;
public abstract class Projectile extends Entity {
    protected Plant ownerPlant;
    protected int damage;
    protected Position position;
    protected double speed;
    protected ProjectileType type;
    protected boolean active;
    protected boolean launched;
    protected boolean canBypassObstacles = false;
    protected int maxTargets = 1;
    protected Set<Zombie> hitZombies;
    protected boolean ignoresArmor = false;
    protected int delayOffset;
    protected int maxLifetimeTicks = -1;
    protected int ticksActive = 0;
    protected double maxRange = -1;
    protected double startX;
    protected double startY;
    protected boolean infinitePierce = false;
    public Projectile(Plant ownerPlant, int damage, Position position, double speed, ProjectileType type) {
        this(ownerPlant, damage, position, speed, type, 0);
    }
    public Projectile(Plant ownerPlant, int damage, Position position, double speed, ProjectileType type, int delayOffset) {
        this.ownerPlant = ownerPlant;
        this.damage = damage;
        this.position = (position != null) ? new Position(position.getX(), position.getY()) : null;
        this.speed = speed;
        this.type = type;
        this.active = true;
        this.delayOffset = delayOffset;
        this.launched = (delayOffset <= 0);
        this.hitZombies = new HashSet<>();
        if (this.position != null) {
            this.startX = this.position.getX();
            this.startY = this.position.getY();
        }
    }
    public Plant getOwnerPlant() {
        return ownerPlant;
    }
    public void setOwnerPlant(Plant ownerPlant) {
        this.ownerPlant = ownerPlant;
    }
    public int getDamage() {
        return damage;
    }
    public void setDamage(int damage) {
        this.damage = damage;
    }
    @Override
    public Position getPosition() {
        return position;
    }
    public void setPosition(Position position) {
        this.position = (position != null) ? new Position(position.getX(), position.getY()) : null;
        if (this.position != null) {
            this.startX = this.position.getX();
            this.startY = this.position.getY();
        }
    }
    public double getSpeed() {
        return speed;
    }
    public void setSpeed(double speed) {
        this.speed = speed;
    }
    public ProjectileType getType() {
        return type;
    }
    public boolean isActive() {
        return active;
    }
    public void setActive(boolean active) {
        this.active = active;
    }
    public boolean isLaunched() {
        return launched;
    }
    public void setLaunched(boolean launched) {
        this.launched = launched;
    }
    public void update(GameWorld gameWorld, long currentTick) {
        if (!active) {
            return;
        }
        if (delayOffset > 0) {
            delayOffset--;
            return;
        }
        if (!launched) {
            launched = true;
            if (position != null) {
                this.startX = position.getX();
                this.startY = position.getY();
            }
        }
        if (maxLifetimeTicks != -1 && ticksActive >= maxLifetimeTicks) {
            destroy();
            return;
        }
        ticksActive++;
        move(currentTick);
        if (maxRange != -1 && position != null) {
            double deltaX = position.getX() - startX;
            double deltaY = position.getY() - startY;
            double distance = Math.sqrt(deltaX * deltaX + deltaY * deltaY);
            if (distance >= maxRange) {
                destroy();
                return;
            }
        }
        onHit(gameWorld, currentTick);
    }
    protected abstract void move(long currentTick);
    protected abstract void onHit(GameWorld gameWorld, long currentTick);
    protected Cell getCell(GameWorld gameWorld) {
        if (position == null || gameWorld == null || gameWorld.getGrid() == null) {
            return null;
        }
        return gameWorld.getGrid().getCellAt(position);
    }
    public boolean isCanBypassObstacles() {
        return canBypassObstacles;
    }
    public int getMaxTargets() {
        return maxTargets;
    }
    public void setMaxTargets(int maxTargets) {
        this.maxTargets = maxTargets;
    }
    public Set<Zombie> getHitZombies() {
        return hitZombies;
    }
    public void destroy() {
        this.active = false;
    }
    public boolean isIgnoresArmor() {
        return ignoresArmor;
    }
    public void enableIgnoresArmor() {
        this.ignoresArmor = true;
    }
    public void enableObstacleBypass() {
        this.canBypassObstacles = true;
    }
    public int getDelayOffset() {
        return delayOffset;
    }
    public void setDelayOffset(int delayOffset) {
        this.delayOffset = delayOffset;
        if (delayOffset <= 0) {
            this.launched = true;
        }
    }
    public int getMaxLifetimeTicks() {
        return maxLifetimeTicks;
    }
    public void setMaxLifetimeTicks(int maxLifetimeTicks) {
        this.maxLifetimeTicks = maxLifetimeTicks;
    }
    public double getMaxRange() {
        return maxRange;
    }
    public void setMaxRange(double maxRange) {
        this.maxRange = maxRange;
    }
    public boolean isInfinitePierce() {
        return infinitePierce;
    }
    public void enableInfinitePierce() {
        this.infinitePierce = true;
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\game\entity\projectile\ShooterProjectile.java ---
package game.entity.projectile;
import app.enums.ProjectilleEffect.ProjectileEffect;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;
public abstract class ShooterProjectile extends Projectile {
    protected List<ProjectileEffect> effects = new ArrayList<>();
    protected double angle;
    public ShooterProjectile(Plant ownerPlant, int damage, Position position, double speed) {
        super(ownerPlant, damage, position, speed, ProjectileType.NORMAL);
    }
    public ShooterProjectile(Plant ownerPlant, int damage, Position position, double speed, int delayOffset) {
        super(ownerPlant, damage, position, speed, ProjectileType.NORMAL, delayOffset);
    }
    @Override
    protected abstract void move(long currentTick);
    @Override
    protected abstract void onHit(GameWorld gameWorld, long currentTick);
    public void addEffect(ProjectileEffect effect) {
        if (!effects.contains(effect)) {
            effects.add(effect);
        }
    }
    public void removeEffect(ProjectileEffect effect) {
        effects.remove(effect);
    }
    public List<ProjectileEffect> getEffects() {
        return effects;

    public double getAngle() {
        return angle;
    }
    public void setAngle(double angle) {
        this.angle = angle;
    }
    protected void effectHandler(Zombie zombie, long currentTick) {
        if (effects.contains(ProjectileEffect.FIRE)) {
            damage *= 2;
            zombie.removeEffect(EffectType.ICE);
        }
        if (effects.contains(ProjectileEffect.ICE)) {
            zombie.addEffect(new SlowEffect(currentTick, 40, 0.3f, EffectType.ICE));
        }
        if (effects.contains(ProjectileEffect.SLOWER)) {
            zombie.addEffect(new SlowEffect(currentTick, 40, 0.5f, EffectType.SLOW));
        }
        if (effects.contains(ProjectileEffect.HYPNOSIS)) {
            zombie.addEffect(new CharmEffect(currentTick, 10));
        }
        if (effects.contains(ProjectileEffect.POISONOUS)) {
            zombie.addEffect(new PoisonEffect(currentTick, 10, 5));
        }
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\game\entity\quest\AccumulativeCondition.java ---
package game.entity.quest;
import app.enums.quest.EventType;
public class AccumulativeCondition implements QuestCondition {
    private final EventType eventType;
    private final EventType resetEventType;
    private int requiredProgress;
    private int currentProgress;
    private final String targetPlant;
    private final String targetFamily;
    private final int targetChapter;
    private final int maxTimeInSeconds;
    private final int targetDifficulty;
    private final boolean requireFirstColNoMower;
    public AccumulativeCondition(EventType eventType, EventType resetEventType, int requiredProgress,
                                 String targetPlant, String targetFamily, int targetChapter,
                                 int maxTimeInSeconds, int targetDifficulty, boolean requireFirstColNoMower) {
        this.eventType = eventType;
        this.resetEventType = resetEventType;
        this.requiredProgress = requiredProgress;
        this.currentProgress = 0;
        this.targetPlant = targetPlant;
        this.targetFamily = targetFamily;
        this.targetChapter = targetChapter;
        this.maxTimeInSeconds = maxTimeInSeconds;
        this.targetDifficulty = targetDifficulty;
        this.requireFirstColNoMower = requireFirstColNoMower;
    }
    public AccumulativeCondition(EventType eventType, int requiredProgress, String targetPlant,
                                 String targetFamily, int targetChapter, int maxTimeInSeconds,
                                 int targetDifficulty, boolean requireFirstColNoMower) {
        this(eventType, null, requiredProgress, targetPlant, targetFamily, targetChapter, maxTimeInSeconds, targetDifficulty, requireFirstColNoMower);
    }
    public AccumulativeCondition(EventType eventType, int requiredProgress, String targetPlant,
                                 String targetFamily, int targetChapter, int maxTimeInSeconds, int targetDifficulty) {
        this(eventType, null, requiredProgress, targetPlant, targetFamily, targetChapter, maxTimeInSeconds, targetDifficulty, false);
    }
    @Override
    public void handleEvent(GameEvent event) {
        if (event == null) return;
        if (resetEventType != null && event.type() == resetEventType) {
            reset();
            return;
        }
        if (event.type() != this.eventType) return;
        if (targetPlant != null && !targetPlant.equalsIgnoreCase(event.plantName())) return;
        if (targetFamily != null && !targetFamily.equalsIgnoreCase(event.familyType())) return;
        if (targetChapter != -1 && targetChapter != event.chapter()) return;
        if (maxTimeInSeconds != -1 && event.timeInSeconds() > maxTimeInSeconds) return;
        if (targetDifficulty != -1 && targetDifficulty != event.difficulty()) return;
        if (requireFirstColNoMower && !event.isFirstColNoMower()) return;
        this.currentProgress += event.amount();
    }
    @Override
    public boolean isReached() { return currentProgress >= requiredProgress; }
    @Override
    public void reset() { this.currentProgress = 0; }
    @Override
    public int getCurrentProgress() { return currentProgress; }
    @Override
    public void setCurrentProgress(int currentProgress) { this.currentProgress = currentProgress; }
    @Override
    public void setRequiredProgress(int progress) { this.requiredProgress = progress; }
    @Override
    public boolean verifyFinalState(GameBackup gameBackup) { return false; }
    @Override
    public int getRequiredProgress() { return requiredProgress; }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\game\entity\quest\Quest.java ---
package game.entity.quest;
import app.enums.quest.QuestCategory;
import app.enums.quest.QuestName;
import app.enums.quest.QuestPriority;
import java.util.List;
public class Quest {
    private final QuestName name;
    private final String title;
    private final String description;
    private final QuestCategory category;
    private final QuestPriority priority;
    private final QuestCondition condition;
    private final List<QuestReward> rewards;
    private final List<Integer> progressionTiers;
    private int currentTierIndex;
    private boolean completed;
    private String targetPlant;
    private String targetCategory;
    private int targetValue = -1;
    public Quest(QuestName name, String title, String description, QuestCategory category,
                 QuestPriority priority, QuestCondition condition, List<QuestReward> rewards,
                 List<Integer> progressionTiers) {
        this.name = name;
        this.title = title;
        this.description = description;
        this.category = category;
        this.priority = priority;
        this.condition = condition;
        this.rewards = rewards;
        this.progressionTiers = progressionTiers;
        this.currentTierIndex = 0;
        this.completed = false;
        if (progressionTiers != null && !progressionTiers.isEmpty() && condition != null) {
            this.condition.setRequiredProgress(progressionTiers.get(0));
        }
    }
    public Quest(QuestName name, String title, String description, QuestCategory category,
                 QuestPriority priority, QuestCondition condition, List<QuestReward> rewards) {
        this(name, title, description, category, priority, condition, rewards, null);
    }
    public void updateProgress(GameEvent event) {
        if (completed || condition == null) return;
        condition.handleEvent(event);
    }
    public boolean evaluateEndState(GameBackup gameBackup) {
        if (completed) return true;
        if (condition == null) return false;
        boolean verified = condition.verifyFinalState(gameBackup);
        if (verified && (progressionTiers == null || progressionTiers.isEmpty())) {
            completed = true;
        }
        return verified;
    }
    public boolean checkAndAdvanceTier() {
        if (completed || condition == null) return false;
        if (condition.isReached()) {
            if (progressionTiers != null && currentTierIndex < progressionTiers.size() - 1) {
                currentTierIndex++;
                condition.reset();
                condition.setRequiredProgress(progressionTiers.get(currentTierIndex));
                return false;
            } else {
                completed = true;
                return true;
            }
        }
        return false;
    }
    public QuestName getName() { return name; }
    public String getTitle() { return title; }
    public String getDescription() { return description; }
    public QuestCategory getCategory() { return category; }
    public QuestPriority getPriority() { return priority; }
    public QuestCondition getCondition() { return condition; }
    public List<QuestReward> getRewards() { return rewards; }
    public boolean isCompleted() { return completed; }
    public int getCurrentTier() { return currentTierIndex + 1; }
    public int getCurrentTierIndex() { return currentTierIndex; }
    public List<Integer> getProgressionTiers() { return progressionTiers; }
    public String getTargetPlant() { return targetPlant; }
    public void setTargetPlant(String targetPlant) { this.targetPlant = targetPlant; }
    public String getTargetCategory() { return targetCategory; }
    public void setTargetCategory(String targetCategory) { this.targetCategory = targetCategory; }
    public int getTargetValue() { return targetValue; }
    public void setTargetValue(int targetValue) { this.targetValue = targetValue; }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\game\entity\quest\QuestCondition.java ---
package game.entity.quest;
public interface QuestCondition {
    void handleEvent(GameEvent event);
    boolean verifyFinalState(GameBackup gameBackup);
    int getCurrentProgress();
    void setCurrentProgress(int currentProgress);
    int getRequiredProgress();
    void setRequiredProgress(int requiredProgress);
    boolean isReached();
    void reset();
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\game\entity\quest\SnapshotCondition.java ---
package game.entity.quest;
import java.util.function.Predicate;
public class SnapshotCondition implements QuestCondition {
    private final Predicate<GameBackup> conditionPredicate;
    private boolean isSatisfied;
    public SnapshotCondition(Predicate<GameBackup> conditionPredicate) {
        this.conditionPredicate = conditionPredicate;
        this.isSatisfied = false;
    }
    @Override
    public void handleEvent(GameEvent event) {}
    @Override
    public boolean verifyFinalState(GameBackup gameBackup) {
        if (gameBackup == null) return false;
        isSatisfied = conditionPredicate.test(gameBackup);
        return isSatisfied;
    }
    @Override
    public int getCurrentProgress() { return isSatisfied ? 1 : 0; }
    @Override
    public void setCurrentProgress(int currentProgress) { this.isSatisfied = currentProgress >= 1; }
    @Override
    public int getRequiredProgress() { return 1; }
    @Override
    public void setRequiredProgress(int requiredProgress) {}
    @Override
    public boolean isReached() { return isSatisfied; }
    @Override
    public void reset() { this.isSatisfied = false; }
    public Predicate<GameBackup> getConditionPredicate() { return conditionPredicate; }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\game\entity\registry\PlantRegistry.java ---
package game.entity.registry;
import app.enums.plant.PlantName;
import game.entity.plant.PlantDefinition;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.Collections;
import java.util.HashMap;
import java.util.Map;
public final class PlantRegistry {
    private static final Map<PlantName, PlantDefinition> DEFINITIONS = new HashMap<>();
    private PlantRegistry() {
        PlantDefinitionLoader.load();
    }
    public static void register(PlantDefinition definition) {
        DEFINITIONS.put(definition.getName(), definition);
    }
    public static PlantDefinition get(PlantName name) {
        return DEFINITIONS.get(name);
    }
    public static ArrayList<PlantDefinition> getPlantDefinitions() {
        return new ArrayList<>(DEFINITIONS.values());
    }
    public static Map<PlantName, PlantDefinition> getDefinitions() {
        return Collections.unmodifiableMap(DEFINITIONS);
    }
    public static void clear() {
        DEFINITIONS.clear();
    }
    public static boolean contains(PlantName name) {
        return DEFINITIONS.containsKey(name);
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\game\entity\registry\ZombieRegistry.java ---
package game.entity.registry;
import game.entity.zombie.data.properties.Property;
import game.entity.zombie.data.properties.armor.ArmorProperty;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.HashMap;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;
public class ZombieRegistry {
    public static final Map<String, Property> DEFINITIONS = new HashMap<>();
    public static final Map<String, ArmorProperty> ARMORS = new HashMap<>();
    private ZombieRegistry() {
    }
    public static void register(
            Property definition)
    {
        if (definition != null && definition.getAlias() != null) {
            DEFINITIONS.put(definition.getAlias(), definition);
        }
    }
    public static void register (ArmorProperty armorProperty) {
        if(armorProperty != null && armorProperty.getAlias() != null) {
            ARMORS.put(armorProperty.getAlias(), armorProperty);
        }
    }
    public static Property getZombie(String type)
    {
        return DEFINITIONS.get(type);
    }
    public static ArmorProperty getArmor(String type) {return ARMORS.get(type);}
    public static List<Property> getAllZombies() {
        return new ArrayList<>(DEFINITIONS.values());
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\game\entity\score\DailyZombieSpawnPattern.java ---
package game.entity.score;
import java.time.LocalDate;
import java.util.List;
public class DailyZombieSpawnPattern {
    private final LocalDate date;
    private List<String> zombieTypes;
    private List<Integer> spawnRows;
    private List<Integer> spawnColumns;
    private List<Long> spawnTicks;
    public DailyZombieSpawnPattern(LocalDate date) {
        this.date = date;
    }
    public LocalDate getDate() {
        return date;
    }
    public List<String> getZombieTypes() {
        return zombieTypes;
    }
    public void setZombieTypes(List<String> zombieTypes) {
        this.zombieTypes = zombieTypes;
    }
    public List<Integer> getSpawnRows() {
        return spawnRows;
    }
    public void setSpawnRows(List<Integer> spawnRows) {
        this.spawnRows = spawnRows;
    }
    public List<Integer> getSpawnColumns() {
        return spawnColumns;
    }
    public void setSpawnColumns(List<Integer> spawnColumns) {
        this.spawnColumns = spawnColumns;
    }
    public List<Long> getSpawnTicks() {
        return spawnTicks;
    }
    public void setSpawnTicks(List<Long> spawnTicks) {
        this.spawnTicks = spawnTicks;
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\game\entity\sun\PlantSun.java ---
package game.entity.sun;
import app.enums.sun.*;
import common.model.Position;
public class PlantSun extends Sun
{
    public PlantSun(Position position,
                    int value)
    {
        this.type = SunType.NORMAL;
        this.position = position;
        this.value = value;
        this.state = SunState.ON_PLANT;
        this.sourceType = SunSourceType.PLANT;
    }
    @Override
    public boolean isCollectable()
    {
        return true;
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\game\entity\sun\SkySun.java ---
package game.entity.sun;
import app.enums.sun.SunSourceType;
import app.enums.sun.SunState;
import app.enums.sun.SunType;
import common.model.Position;
public class SkySun extends Sun {
    private long spawnTick;
    private long lastMoveTick;
    public SkySun(SunType type, Position position, int value, long spawnTick) {
        this.type = type;
        this.position = position;
        this.value = value;
        this.spawnTick = spawnTick;
        this.lastMoveTick = spawnTick;
        this.state = SunState.DROPPING;
        this.sourceType = SunSourceType.SKY;
    }
    public long getSpawnTick() {
        return spawnTick;
    }
    public void setSpawnTick(long spawnTick) {
        this.spawnTick = spawnTick;
    }
    public long getLastMoveTick() {
        return lastMoveTick;
    }
    public void setLastMoveTick(long lastMoveTick) {
        this.lastMoveTick = lastMoveTick;
    }
    public void land() {
        if (state == SunState.ON_GROUND) {
            return;
        }
        this.state = SunState.ON_GROUND;
        if (type == SunType.RADIOACTIVE) {
            this.type = SunType.NORMAL;
            this.value = 25;
        }
    }
    @Override
    public boolean isCollectable() {
        return state == SunState.DROPPING || state == SunState.ON_GROUND;
    }
}

package game.entity.sun;
import app.enums.sun.*;
import common.model.Position;
public abstract class Sun
{
    protected SunType type;
    protected SunState state;
    protected SunSourceType sourceType;
    protected Position position;
    protected int value;
    public SunType getType()
    {
        return type;
    }
    public SunState getState()
    {
        return state;
    }
    public void setState(SunState state)
    {
        this.state = state;
    }
    public Position getPosition()
    {
        return position;
    }
    public void setPosition(Position position)
    {
        this.position = position;
    }
    public int getValue()
    {
        return value;
    }
    public SunSourceType getSourceType()
    {
        return sourceType;
    }
    public abstract boolean isCollectable();
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\game\entity\zombie\Zombie.java ---
package game.entity.zombie;
import app.enums.effect.EffectType;
import app.model.App;
import com.badlogic.gdx.math.Vector2;
import common.model.Position;
import game.component.physic.PhysicService;
import game.entity.Entity;
import game.entity.effect.Effect;
import game.entity.plant.Plant;
import game.entity.plantfood.PlantFoodInventory;
import game.entity.zombie.ability.ZombieAbility;
import game.entity.zombie.data.properties.Property;
import game.entity.zombie.strategy.attack.AttackStrategy;
import game.entity.zombie.strategy.movement.MovementStrategy;
import game.entity.zombie.strategy.takedamage.TakingDamageStrategy;
import game.service.Zombie.ZombieService;
import game.world.GameWorld;
import game.world.playground.Cell;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Random;
public class Zombie extends Entity {
    private double currentHealth;
    private MovementStrategy movementStrategy;
    private AttackStrategy attackStrategy;
    private TakingDamageStrategy takingDamageStrategy;
    private Position position;
    private List<ZombieAbility> abilities;
    private GameWorld world;
    private Plant targetPlant;
    private Property propertyData;
    private final List<ZombieArmor> armors;
    private final List<Effect> effects;
    private boolean dead;
    public enum ZombieState {
        WALKING,
        EATING,
        USING_ABILITY,
        DYING
    }
    private ZombieState state;
    private boolean canMove;
    private double moveSpeedMultiplier;
    private double eatSpeedMultiplier;
    private long nextAttackTick;
    public Zombie(double currentHealth,
                  MovementStrategy movementStrategy,
                  AttackStrategy attackStrategy,
                  TakingDamageStrategy takingDamageStrategy,
                  Position position,
                  List<ZombieAbility> abilities,
                  GameWorld world,
                  Plant targetPlant,
                  Property propertyData) {
        this.currentHealth = currentHealth;
        this.movementStrategy = movementStrategy;
        this.attackStrategy = attackStrategy;
        this.takingDamageStrategy = takingDamageStrategy;
        this.position = position;
        this.abilities = abilities;
        this.world = world;
        this.targetPlant = targetPlant;
        this.propertyData = propertyData;
        armors = new ArrayList<>();
        effects = new ArrayList<>();
        this.canMove = true;
        this.moveSpeedMultiplier = 1f;
        this.eatSpeedMultiplier = 1f;
        this.dead = false;
        this.nextAttackTick = 0;
        this.state = ZombieState.WALKING;
    }
    public String getName() {
        return propertyData.getAlias();
    }
    public double getCurrentHealth() {
        return currentHealth;
    }
    public MovementStrategy getMovementStrategy() {
        return movementStrategy;
    }
    public AttackStrategy getAttackStrategy() {
        return attackStrategy;
    }
    public TakingDamageStrategy getTakingDamageStrategy() {
        return takingDamageStrategy;
    }
    @Override
    public Position getPosition() {
        return position;
    }
    public List<ZombieAbility> getAbilities() {
        return abilities;
    }
    public GameWorld getWorld() {
        return world;
    }
    public Plant getTargetPlant() {
        return targetPlant;
    }
    public Property getPropertyData() {
        return propertyData;
    }
    public List<ZombieArmor> getArmors() {
        return armors;
    }
    public void addArmor(ZombieArmor armor) {
        armors.add(armor);
    }
    public List<Effect> getEffects() {
        return effects;
    }
    public void update(ZombieService zombieService, long currentTick) {
        if(dead){
            return;
        }
        if(currentHealth <= 0) {
            die();
            return;
        }
        updateEffects(zombieService,currentTick);
        syncPosition();
        for (ZombieAbility ability : abilities) {
            ability.update(this, zombieService, currentTick);
        }
        if(!canAct()) {
            stopMovement();
            return;
        }
        evaluateState();
        switch (state) {
            case EATING:
                stopMovement();
                attackStrategy.executeAttack(this, targetPlant, zombieService, currentTick);
                if (targetPlant.isDead()) {
                    targetPlant = null;
                }
                break;
            case WALKING:
                movementStrategy.move(this, zombieService, currentTick);
                break;
            case USING_ABILITY:
            case DYING:
                stopMovement();
                break;
        }
        if(hasReachedHouse()){
            world.checkZombieWinCondition(this);
        }
    }
    private void syncPosition() {
        Vector2 b2Pos = body.getPosition();
        setPosition(new Position(b2Pos.x * PhysicService.PIXEL_PER_METER,
                                 b2Pos.y * PhysicService.PIXEL_PER_METER));
    }
    private void evaluateState() {
        if (targetPlant != null && !targetPlant.isDead()) {
            this.state = ZombieState.EATING;
        } else {
            this.targetPlant = null;
            if (this.state != ZombieState.USING_ABILITY) {
                this.state = ZombieState.WALKING;
            }
        }
    }
    private void updateEffects(ZombieService zombieService,long currentTick) {
        for(Effect effect : effects) {
            effect.tick(this, zombieService, currentTick);
        }
        effects.removeIf(effect -> {
            if(effect.isExpired(currentTick)) {
                effect.onRemove(this);
                return true;
            }
            return false;
        });
        for(Effect effect : effects) {
            effect.applyModifier(this);
        }
    }
    public void addEffect(Effect effect) {
        effects.add(effect);
        effect.onApply(this);
    }
    public void setMovementStrategy(MovementStrategy newStrategy) {
        this.movementStrategy = newStrategy;
    }
    private boolean canAct() {
        return canMove;
    }
    private boolean hasReachedHouse() {
        return world.getMap().hasCrossedLoseLine(this);
    }
    public void setCurrentHealth(double currentHealth) {
        this.currentHealth = currentHealth;
    }
    public void setAttackStrategy(AttackStrategy attackStrategy) {
        this.attackStrategy = attackStrategy;
    }
    public void setTakingDamageStrategy(TakingDamageStrategy takingDamageStrategy) {
        this.takingDamageStrategy = takingDamageStrategy;
    }
    public void setPosition(Position newPosition) {
        this.position = newPosition;
    }
    public void die() {
        if (dead) {
            return;
        }
        dead = true;
        manageSpawnPlantFood();
        for (Effect effect : effects) {
            effect.onRemove(this);
        }
        for (ZombieAbility ability : abilities) {
            ability.onDeath(this);
        }
        effects.clear();
        world.addEvent("zombie die at position>>  X:" + position.getX() + " Y:" + position.getY());
        App.getRealTimeEngine().getGraphicsEngine().unregisterZombie(this);
        PhysicService physicService = App.getRealTimeEngine().getPhysicService();
        destroyBody(physicService);
        targetPlant = null;
    }
    public boolean isDead() {
        return dead || currentHealth <= 0;
    }
    public void setAbilities(List<ZombieAbility> abilities) {
        this.abilities = abilities;
    }
    public void setWorld(GameWorld world) {
        this.world = world;
    }
    public void setTargetPlant(Plant targetPlant) {
        this.targetPlant = targetPlant;
    }
    public void setPropertyData(Property propertyData) {
        this.propertyData = propertyData;
    }
    public void setCanMove(boolean canMove) {
        this.canMove = canMove;
    }
    public void setMoveSpeedMultiplier(double moveSpeedMultiplier) {
        this.moveSpeedMultiplier = moveSpeedMultiplier;
    }
    public void addAbility(ZombieAbility ability) {
        abilities.add(ability);
    }
    public double getSpeed() {
        return propertyData.getSpeed() * moveSpeedMultiplier;
    }
    public double getMoveSpeedMultiplier() {
        return moveSpeedMultiplier;
    }
    public double getEatSpeedMultiplier() {
        return eatSpeedMultiplier;
    }
    public void setEatSpeedMultiplier(double eatSpeedMultiplier) {
        this.eatSpeedMultiplier = eatSpeedMultiplier;
    }
    public void removeEffect(EffectType effect) {
        effects.removeIf(element -> element.getType() == effect);
    }
    public long getNextAttackTick() {
        return nextAttackTick;
    }
    public void setNextAttackTick(long nextAttackTick) {
        this.nextAttackTick = nextAttackTick;
    }
    public String printInfo() {
        return propertyData.printInfo();
    }
    public void takeDamage(double amount, long currentTick) {
        takingDamageStrategy.takeDamage(this,currentTick,amount);
    }
    private void manageSpawnPlantFood() {
        boolean spawnedFood = false;
        boolean spawnedCoin = false;
        if(!propertyData.canSpawnPlantFood()) {
            return;
        }
        Random random = new Random();
        if(random.nextFloat() < 0.4) {
            PlantFoodInventory inventory = App.getCurrentUser().getFoodInventory();
            if(!inventory.isFull()) {
                inventory.addPlantFood();
                world.addEvent(
                                "\n╭━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━╮\n" +
                                "│ 🧟 Zombie dropped Plant Food! 🌱 (Saved 🎒)│\n" +
                                "╰━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━╯"
                );
                spawnedFood = true;
            }
        }
        if(spawnedFood)return;
        if(random.nextFloat() < 0.3) {
            App.getCurrentUser().addCoins(50);
            world.addEvent(
                            "\n╭━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━╮\n" +
                            "│ 🧟 Zombie dropped 50 Coins! 💰 (Saved 🎒)  │\n" +
                            "╰━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━╯"
            );
            spawnedCoin = true;
        }
        if(spawnedCoin)return;
        if(random.nextFloat() < 0.05) {
            App.getCurrentUser().addGems(1);
            world.addEvent(
                    "\n╭━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━╮\n" +
                            "│ 🧟 Zombie dropped a GEM! 💎 (Saved 🎒)     │\n" +
                            "╰━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━╯"
            );
        }
    }
    public void stopMovement() {
        body.setLinearVelocity(0f, 0f);;
    }
    public boolean isAttacking() {
        return state == ZombieState.EATING;
    }
    public boolean isWalking() {
        return state == ZombieState.WALKING;
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\game\entity\zombie\ZombieArmor.java ---
package game.entity.zombie;
import game.entity.zombie.data.properties.armor.ArmorProperty;
public class ZombieArmor {
    private final ArmorProperty property;
    private double currentHealth;
    private boolean broken;
    public ZombieArmor(ArmorProperty property) {
        this.property = property;
        this.currentHealth = property.getBaseHealth();
        this.broken = false;
    }
    public String getName() {
        return property.getAlias();
    }
    public double getCurrentHealth() {
        return currentHealth;
    }
    public double takeDamage(double amount) {
        if (broken || amount <= 0) {
            return amount;
        }
        if (amount >= currentHealth) {
            double leftover = amount - currentHealth;
            currentHealth = 0;
            broken = true;
            return leftover;
        }
        currentHealth -= amount;
        return 0;
    }
    public boolean isBroken() {
        return broken;
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\game\entity\zombie\ZombieArmorFactory.java ---
package game.entity.zombie;
import game.entity.registry.ZombieRegistry;
import game.entity.zombie.data.properties.armor.ArmorProperty;
public class ZombieArmorFactory {
    private ZombieArmorFactory() {}
    public static ZombieArmor create(String armorAlias) {
        ArmorProperty property = ZombieRegistry.getArmor(armorAlias);
        if (property == null) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("No armor found with name : " + armorAlias);
        }
        return new ZombieArmor(property);
    }
    public static ZombieArmor create(ArmorProperty property) {
        if (property == null) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("No armor found.");
        }
        return new ZombieArmor(property);
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\game\entity\zombie\ZombieFactory.java ---
package game.entity.zombie;
import app.enums.plant.PlantName;
import com.badlogic.gdx.physics.box2d.Body;
import common.model.Position;
import game.component.physic.BodyFactory;
import game.component.physic.PhysicService;
import game.entity.effect.FreezeEffect;
import game.entity.obstacle.ArcadeCabinet;
import game.entity.obstacle.Barrel;
import game.entity.obstacle.IceBlock;
import game.entity.obstacle.data.BarrelProperty;
import game.entity.registry.ZombieRegistry;
import game.entity.zombie.ability.*;
import game.entity.zombie.data.properties.*;
import game.entity.zombie.data.properties.DarkAges.KingProperty;
import game.entity.zombie.data.properties.DarkAges.WizardProperty;
import game.entity.zombie.data.properties.ancientEgypt.ExplorerProperty;
import game.entity.zombie.data.properties.ancientEgypt.RaProperty;
import game.entity.zombie.data.properties.ancientEgypt.TombRaiserProperty;
import game.entity.zombie.data.properties.beach.FishermanProperty;
import game.entity.zombie.data.properties.beach.OctopusProperty;
import game.entity.zombie.data.properties.iceAge.IceAgeDodoProperty;
import game.entity.zombie.data.properties.iceAge.TroglobiteProperty;
import game.entity.zombie.strategy.attack.*;
import game.entity.zombie.strategy.movement.MovementStrategy;
import game.entity.zombie.strategy.movement.NormalMovement;
import game.entity.zombie.strategy.movement.StationaryMovement;
import game.entity.zombie.strategy.takedamage.StandardTakingDamage;
import game.entity.zombie.strategy.takedamage.TakingDamageStrategy;
import game.world.GameWorld;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;
public class ZombieFactory {
    public static Zombie buildZombie(String alias,
                              GameWorld world,
                              Position position)
    {
        Property property = ZombieRegistry.getZombie(alias);
        if(property == null) {
            System.err.println("Cant find zombie with alias : " + alias);
            return null;
        }
        Zombie zombie;
        double currentHealth = property.getInitialHealth();
        MovementStrategy movement = new NormalMovement();
        TakingDamageStrategy takeDamage = new StandardTakingDamage();
        AttackStrategy attack = new EatAttack();
        List<ZombieAbility> abilities = new ArrayList<>();
        zombie = new Zombie(
                currentHealth,
                movement,
                attack,
                takeDamage,
                position,
                abilities,
                world,
                null,
                property
        );
        float bodyWidth = 20f;
        float bodyHeight = 55f;
        if(property instanceof PianoProperty) {
            zombie.setAttackStrategy( new InstantKillAttack());
            zombie.addAbility(new PianoMusicAbility());
        }
        else if(property instanceof WizardProperty) {
            zombie.addAbility(new WizardAbility());
        }
        else if(property instanceof OctopusProperty) {
            zombie.addAbility(new OctopusThrowAbility());
        }
        else if(property instanceof NewspaperProperty) {
            zombie.addAbility(new NewspaperAbility());
            ZombieArmor armor = ZombieArmorFactory.create("NewspaperDefault");
            zombie.addArmor(armor);
        }
        else if(property instanceof CrystalSkullProperty) {
            zombie.addAbility(new TurquoiseAbility());
        }
        else if(property instanceof TroglobiteProperty) {
            zombie.addAbility(new PushObjectAbility(new IceBlock(zombie.getPosition())));
        }
        else if(property instanceof KingProperty) {
            zombie.addAbility(new KingAuraAbility());
            zombie.setMovementStrategy(new StationaryMovement());
        }
        else if(property instanceof BarrelProperty) {
            zombie.addAbility(new PushObjectAbility(new Barrel(zombie.getPosition())));
        }
        else if(property instanceof LostCityProperty) {
        }
        else if(property instanceof ArcadeProperty) {
            zombie.addAbility(new PushObjectAbility(new ArcadeCabinet(zombie.getPosition())));
        }
        else if(property instanceof GargantuarProperty) {
            zombie.setAttackStrategy(new SmashAttack());
            abilities.add(new ImpThrowAbility());
        }
        else if(property instanceof IceAgeDodoProperty) {
            abilities.add(new FlyingAbility(List.of(PlantName.WALL_NUT, PlantName.POTATO_MINE)));
        }
        else if(property instanceof AllStarProperty) {
            zombie.setMovementStrategy(new NormalMovement());
            zombie.setMoveSpeedMultiplier(3);
            zombie.setAttackStrategy(new SwapAfterAttack(new SmashAttack(), new EatAttack(), new NormalMovement()));
        }
        else if(property instanceof ProspectorProperty) {
            zombie.addAbility(new DynamiteThrowAbility());
        }
        else if(property instanceof ExplorerProperty) {
            zombie.setAttackStrategy(new InstantKillAttack());
        }
        else if(property instanceof RaProperty) {
            zombie.addAbility(new SunStealAbility(property.getMaxClaimedSunCurrency()));
        }
        else if(property instanceof TombRaiserProperty) {
            zombie.addAbility(new TombRaiserAbility(
                    property.getAmmo(),
                    property.getNumberOfTombsToSpawn(),
                    (int)property.getTimeBetweenCasts(),
                    property.getTimeBetweenRaisings()));
        }
        else if(property instanceof FishermanProperty) {
            zombie.addAbility(new FishermanAbility(property.getTimeBetweenCasts()));
            zombie.setMovementStrategy(new StationaryMovement());
        }
        else if(property instanceof OrdinaryZombieProperty) {
            switch (alias) {
                case "ZombieArmor1" -> {
                    ZombieArmor armor = ZombieArmorFactory.create("ConeDefault");
                    zombie.addArmor(armor);
                }
                case "ZombieArmor2" -> {
                    ZombieArmor armor = ZombieArmorFactory.create("BucketDefault");
                    zombie.addArmor(armor);
                }
                case "ZombieArmor4" -> {
                    ZombieArmor armor = ZombieArmorFactory.create("BrickDefault");
                    zombie.addArmor(armor);
                }
                case "ZombieImp" -> {
                    zombie.setMoveSpeedMultiplier(3);
                    zombie.setEatSpeedMultiplier(3);
                }
                case "ZombieBarrel" -> {
                    zombie.addAbility(new PushObjectAbility(new Barrel(zombie.getPosition())));
                }
                case "ZombieFrozen" -> {
                    zombie.addEffect(new FreezeEffect(0, 100));
                }
            }
        }
        Body zombieBody = BodyFactory.getInstance().createZombieBody(
            (float) position.getX(),
            (float) position.getY(),
            bodyWidth,
            bodyHeight
        );
        zombieBody.setUserData(zombie);
        zombie.setBody(zombieBody);
        return zombie;
    }
    public String extractAliasFromRtid(String rtid) {
        if (rtid == null || !rtid.startsWith("RTID(")) {
            return rtid;
        }
        int startIndex = 5;
        int endIndex = rtid.indexOf('@');
        if (endIndex == -1) {
            endIndex = rtid.indexOf(')');
        }
        return rtid.substring(startIndex, endIndex);
    }
}

}

package game.entity.zombie.ability;
import game.entity.zombie.Zombie;
import game.service.Zombie.ZombieService;
public class CamelSegmentAbility extends AbstractZombieAbility{
    @Override

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\game\entity\zombie\ability\DynamiteThrowAbility.java ---
package game.entity.zombie.ability;
import game.entity.zombie.Zombie;
import game.entity.zombie.strategy.movement.ReverseMovement;
import game.service.Zombie.ZombieService;
import game.world.GameWorld;
public class DynamiteThrowAbility extends AbstractZombieAbility{
    private static final long DYNAMITE_DURATION_TICKS = 100;
    private long startTick = -1;
    private boolean exploded = false;
    @Override
    public void update(Zombie zombie, ZombieService zombieService, long currentTick) {
        if(startTick == -1) {
            startTick = currentTick;
        }
        if(exploded || !isTimeToExplode(currentTick)) {
            return;
        }
        executeExplosion(zombie);
    }
    private void executeExplosion(Zombie zombie) {
        exploded = true;
        GameWorld world = zombie.getWorld();
        double targetX = world.getGrid().getConfig().getPlaygroundX() + 0.5;
        zombie.getPosition().setX(targetX);
        zombie.setMovementStrategy(new ReverseMovement());
        world.addEvent("Prospector Zombie exploded and flew to the end of the row!");
    }
    @Override
    public void onDeath(Zombie zombie) {
    }
    private boolean isTimeToExplode(long currentTick) {
        return (currentTick - startTick) > DYNAMITE_DURATION_TICKS;
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\game\entity\zombie\ability\FishermanAbility.java ---
package game.entity.zombie.ability;
import common.model.Position;
import game.entity.plant.Plant;
import game.entity.zombie.Zombie;
import game.service.Zombie.ZombieService;
import game.world.playground.Cell;
public class FishermanAbility extends AbstractZombieAbility{
    private final float timeBetweenCasts;
    private final long lastCastTick = -1;
    public FishermanAbility(float timeBetweenCasts) {
        this.timeBetweenCasts = timeBetweenCasts;
    }
    @Override
    public void update(Zombie zombie, ZombieService zombieService, long currentTick) {
        if(!canCast(currentTick)) {
            return;
        }
        Plant target = zombieService.getFirstPlantInRow((int)zombie.getPosition().getY());
        if(target == null)return;
        Position newPositon = new Position(target.getPosition().getX() + 1, target.getPosition().getY());
        Cell currentCell = zombieService.getWorld().getGrid().getCellAt(target.getPosition());
        Cell nextCell = zombieService.getWorld().getGrid().getCellAt(newPositon);
        if(nextCell == null ||
                (int)newPositon.getX() == (int)zombie.getPosition().getX() ||
                !nextCell.isPlantable()) {
            target.markDead(zombieService.getWorld());
            zombieService.getWorld().addEvent("Plant got killed because of fisherman ability");
        }
        else {
            nextCell.setPlant(target);
            currentCell.setPlant(null);
            target.setPosition(newPositon);
        }
    }
    @Override
    public void onDeath(Zombie zombie) {
    }
    private boolean canCast(long currentTick) {
        return lastCastTick < 0 || (currentTick - lastCastTick) > timeBetweenCasts;
    }
}
