--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\game\entity\zombie\ability\ImpThrowAbility.java ---
package game.entity.zombie.ability;
import common.model.Position;
import game.entity.zombie.Zombie;
import game.service.Zombie.ZombieService;
public class ImpThrowAbility extends AbstractZombieAbility{
    private boolean hasThrownImp = false;
    @Override
    public void update(Zombie zombie, ZombieService zombieService, long currentTick) {
        if(hasThrownImp)return;
        int initialHealth = zombie.getPropertyData().getInitialHealth();
        if(zombie.getCurrentHealth() <= initialHealth / 1.1) {
            throwImp(zombie, zombieService);
        }
    }
    private void throwImp(Zombie zombie, ZombieService zombieService) {
        double cellWidth = zombieService.getWorld().getGrid().getConfig().getCellWidth();
        double targetX = zombie.getWorld().getGrid().getConfig().getPlaygroundX() + 2 * cellWidth;
        double targetY = zombie.getPosition().getY();
        Position spawnPos = new Position(targetX, targetY);
        zombieService.spawnZombie("ZombieImp", spawnPos);
        zombieService.getWorld().addEvent("Gargantuar threw an imp on position (" + targetX + "," + targetY + ")");
        hasThrownImp = true;
    }
    @Override
    public void onDeath(Zombie zombie) {
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\game\entity\zombie\ability\KingAuraAbility.java ---
package game.entity.zombie.ability;
import game.entity.zombie.Zombie;
import game.entity.zombie.ZombieArmor;
import game.entity.zombie.ZombieArmorFactory;
import game.service.Zombie.ZombieService;
import game.world.GameWorld;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.concurrent.ThreadLocalRandom;
public class KingAuraAbility extends AbstractZombieAbility {
    private static final long ACTIVATION_INTERVAL = 100;
    private static final String CROWN_ARMOR_ALIAS   = "CrownDefault";
    private static final String SHOULDER_ARMOR_ALIAS = "ShoulderArmorDefault";
    private static final String TARGET_ZOMBIE_NAME  = "ZombieDefault";
    private long lastActivationTick = -1;
    @Override
    public void update(Zombie king, ZombieService zombieService, long currentTick) {
        if (king.isDead()) {
            return;
        }
        if (lastActivationTick < 0) {
            lastActivationTick = currentTick;
            return;
        }
        if (currentTick - lastActivationTick < ACTIVATION_INTERVAL) {
            return;
        }
        lastActivationTick = currentTick;
        blessRandomDefaultZombie(king, zombieService.getWorld());
    }
    private void blessRandomDefaultZombie(Zombie king, GameWorld world) {
        List<Zombie> candidates = new ArrayList<>();
        for (Zombie zombie : world.getZombies()) {
            if (zombie == king)                                 continue;
            if (zombie.isDead())                                continue;
            if (!TARGET_ZOMBIE_NAME.equals(zombie.getName()))   continue;
            if (hasCrownArmor(zombie))                          continue;
            candidates.add(zombie);
        }
        if (candidates.isEmpty()) {
            return;
        }
        Zombie target = candidates.get(
                ThreadLocalRandom.current().nextInt(candidates.size())
        );
        ZombieArmor crownArmor    = ZombieArmorFactory.create(CROWN_ARMOR_ALIAS);
        ZombieArmor shoulderArmor = ZombieArmorFactory.create(SHOULDER_ARMOR_ALIAS);
        target.addArmor(crownArmor);
        target.addArmor(shoulderArmor);
        world.addEvent(String.format(
                "👑 [Royal Blessing] King Zombie crowned a %s with royal armor! ✨ (🛡️ Crown + Shoulder) at [%.1f, %.1f]",
                target.getName(),
                target.getPosition().getX(),
                target.getPosition().getY()
        ));
    }
    private boolean hasCrownArmor(Zombie zombie) {
        for (ZombieArmor armor : zombie.getArmors()) {
            if (CROWN_ARMOR_ALIAS.equals(armor.getName())) {
                return true;
            }
        }
        return false;
    }
    @Override
    public void onDeath(Zombie king) {
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\game\entity\zombie\ability\OctopusThrowAbility.java ---
package game.entity.zombie.ability;
import game.entity.obstacle.Octopus;
import game.entity.plant.Plant;
import game.entity.zombie.Zombie;
import game.service.Zombie.ZombieService;
import game.world.GameWorld;
import game.world.playground.Cell;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Random;
public class OctopusThrowAbility extends AbstractZombieAbility{
    private long lastThrow = -1;
    private final long cooldown = 150;
    @Override
    public void update(Zombie zombie, ZombieService zombieService, long currentTick) {
        if(canThrow(currentTick)) {
            Cell cell = giveOctopusCell(zombie.getWorld(), zombie);
            if(cell == null) return;
            lastThrow = currentTick;
        }
    }
    private boolean canThrow(long currentTick) {
        return lastThrow < 0 || currentTick - lastThrow >= cooldown;
    }
    @Override
    public void onDeath(Zombie zombie) {
    }
    private Cell giveOctopusCell(GameWorld world, Zombie zombie) {
        List<Plant> plants = new ArrayList<>();
        for(Plant plant : world.getPlants()) {
            if ((int)plant.getPosition().getY() == (int)zombie.getPosition().getY()) {
                Cell cell = world.getGrid().getCellAt(plant.getPosition());
                if(!cell.hasObstacle()) {
                    plants.add(plant);
                }
            }
        }
        Random random = new Random();
        if(plants.isEmpty()) {
            return null;
        }
        Plant plant = plants.get(random.nextInt(plants.size()));
        return world.getGrid().getCellAt(plant.getPosition());
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\game\entity\zombie\ability\PianoMusicAbility.java ---
package game.entity.zombie.ability;
import common.model.Position;
import game.entity.zombie.Zombie;
import game.service.Zombie.ZombieService;
import game.world.GameWorld;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.concurrent.ThreadLocalRandom;
public class PianoMusicAbility extends AbstractZombieAbility{
    public static final long PIANO_MUSIC_COOLDOWN_TICKS = 100;
    private long lastCastTick = -1;
    @Override
    public void update(Zombie zombie, ZombieService zombieService, long currentTick) {
        if (!canCast(currentTick)) {
            return;
        }
        GameWorld world = zombie.getWorld();
        List<Zombie> zombies = new ArrayList<>(world.getZombies());
        for (Zombie other : zombies) {
            if (other == zombie || other.isDead()) {
                continue;
            }
            shiftToNeighborRow(other);
            world.addEvent("Piano ability got activated! ZOMBIES CHANGED LINE!");
        }
        lastCastTick = currentTick;
    }
    @Override
    public void onDeath(Zombie zombie) {
    }
    private boolean canCast(long currentTick) {
        return lastCastTick < 0 || (currentTick - lastCastTick) >= PIANO_MUSIC_COOLDOWN_TICKS;
    }
    private void shiftToNeighborRow(Zombie zombie) {
        int y = (int) zombie.getPosition().getY();
        int newY;
        if (y <= 0) {
            newY = 1;
        } else if (y >= 4) {
            newY = 3;
        } else {
            newY = ThreadLocalRandom.current().nextBoolean() ? y - 1 : y + 1;
        }
        Position oldPos = zombie.getPosition();
        zombie.setPosition(new Position(oldPos.getX(), newY));
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\game\entity\zombie\ability\PushObjectAbility.java ---
package game.entity.zombie.ability;
import common.model.Position;
import game.entity.obstacle.Obstacle;
import game.entity.plant.Plant;
import game.entity.zombie.Zombie;
import game.service.Zombie.ZombieService;
import game.world.GameWorld;
import game.world.playground.Cell;
public class PushObjectAbility extends AbstractZombieAbility{
    private Obstacle pushedObstacle;
    private double offset = 1;
    public PushObjectAbility(Obstacle pushedObstacle) {
        this.pushedObstacle = pushedObstacle;
    }
    @Override
    public void update(Zombie zombie, ZombieService zombieService, long currentTick) {
        if (pushedObstacle == null) {
            return;
        }
        GameWorld world = zombie.getWorld();
        Position zombiePos = zombie.getPosition();
        Position newPos = new Position(zombiePos.getX() - offset, zombiePos.getY());
        Cell oldCell = world.getGrid().getCellAt(pushedObstacle.getPosition());
        Cell newCell = world.getGrid().getCellAt(newPos);
        pushedObstacle.setPosition(newPos);
        if (oldCell != newCell) {
            if (oldCell != null && oldCell.getObstacle() == pushedObstacle) {
                oldCell.setObstacle(null);
            }
            if (newCell == null) {
                pushedObstacle.die();
                pushedObstacle = null;
                return;
            }
            newCell.setObstacle(pushedObstacle);
            Plant plant = newCell.getPlant();
            if (plant != null && !plant.isDead()) {
                plant.getHealth().die();
            }
        }
    }
    @Override
    public void onDeath(Zombie zombie) {
        stopPushing(zombie.getWorld());
    }
    public void stopPushing(GameWorld world) {
        this.pushedObstacle = null;
    }
}

package game.entity.zombie.ability;

    private long lastSpawnedTick = -1;
    private SunSpawner sunSpawner;

    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\game\entity\zombie\ability\SunStealAbility.java ---
package game.entity.zombie.ability;
import common.model.Position;
import common.model.Vector;
import game.entity.sun.Sun;
import game.entity.zombie.Zombie;
import game.service.Zombie.ZombieService;
import game.world.GameWorld;
public class SunStealAbility extends AbstractZombieAbility{
    public static final long SUN_STEAL_COOLDOWN_TICKS = 100;
    public static final double STEAL_RANGE = 150f;
    private long lastStealTick = -1;
    private int maxClaimedSunCurrency;
    private int totalSunStolen = 0;
    public SunStealAbility(int maxClaimedSunCurrency) {
        this.maxClaimedSunCurrency = maxClaimedSunCurrency;
    }
    public SunStealAbility() {
        this.maxClaimedSunCurrency = Integer.MAX_VALUE;
    }
    @Override
    public void update(Zombie zombie, ZombieService zombieService, long currentTick) {
        if(!canSteal(currentTick) || hasReachedMaxClaimedSun()) {
            return;
        }
        GameWorld world = zombie.getWorld();
        Sun nearestSun = findNearestStealableSun(zombie, world);
        if(nearestSun != null) {
            stealSun(zombie, nearestSun, world, currentTick);
        }
    }
    @Override
    public void onDeath(Zombie zombie) {
        GameWorld world = zombie.getWorld();
        world.addEvent("Ra zombie died and it gave you back " + totalSunStolen + " suns!");
        world.addSunAmount(totalSunStolen);
    }
    private boolean canSteal(long currentTick) {
        if(lastStealTick < 0) {
            return true;
        }
        return (currentTick - lastStealTick) >= SUN_STEAL_COOLDOWN_TICKS;
    }
    private boolean hasReachedMaxClaimedSun() {
        return maxClaimedSunCurrency != Integer.MAX_VALUE && totalSunStolen >= maxClaimedSunCurrency;
    }
    private Sun findNearestStealableSun(Zombie zombie, GameWorld world) {
        Sun nearestSun = null;
        double nearestDistance = Double.MAX_VALUE;
        Position zombiePosition = zombie.getPosition();
        for(Sun sun : world.getSuns()) {
            if(!sun.isCollectable()){continue;}
            double distance = Vector.findDist(zombiePosition, sun.getPosition());
            if(distance <= STEAL_RANGE && distance < nearestDistance) {
                nearestSun = sun;
                nearestDistance = distance;
            }
        }
        return nearestSun;
    }
    private void stealSun(Zombie zombie, Sun targetSun, GameWorld world, long currentTick) {
        int stolenValue = targetSun.getValue();
        world.getSuns().remove(targetSun);
        totalSunStolen += stolenValue;
        lastStealTick = currentTick;
        world.addEvent("Ra Zombie stole " + stolenValue + " sun!");
    }
    public void setMaxClaimedSunCurrency(int maxClaimedSunCurrency) {
        this.maxClaimedSunCurrency = maxClaimedSunCurrency;
    }
    public int getTotalSunStolen() {
        return totalSunStolen;
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\game\entity\zombie\ability\TombRaiserAbility.java ---
package game.entity.zombie.ability;
import game.entity.obstacle.TombStone;
import game.entity.zombie.Zombie;
import game.service.Zombie.ZombieService;
import game.world.GameWorld;
import game.world.playground.Cell;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.Collections;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Random;
public class TombRaiserAbility extends AbstractZombieAbility{
    private final long COOLDOWN_TICKS = 60;
    private final int TOMBS_PER_CAST;
    private long lastCastTick = -1;
    private int remainingAmmo;
    private final int TIME_BETWEEN_RAISING;
    private final long lastSpawnTick = -1;
    public TombRaiserAbility(int ammo, int tombsPerCast, int timeBetweenCasts, int timeBetweenRaisings) {
        this.TOMBS_PER_CAST = 2;
        this.remainingAmmo = ammo;
        this.TIME_BETWEEN_RAISING = timeBetweenRaisings;
    }
    @Override
    public void update(Zombie zombie, ZombieService zombieService, long currentTick) {
        if(canRaiseTomb(currentTick)) {
            raiseTomb(zombie, zombieService, currentTick);
        }
    }
    @Override
    public void onDeath(Zombie zombie) {
    }
    private List<Cell> pickRandomCells(List<Cell> pool, int count) {
        List<Cell> result = new ArrayList<>();
        if(pool.isEmpty()) return result;
        List<Cell> shuffled = new ArrayList<>(pool);
        Collections.shuffle(shuffled, new Random());
        for(int i = 0; i < Math.min(count, shuffled.size()); i++) {
            result.add(shuffled.get(i));
        }
        return result;
    }
    private boolean canRaiseTomb(long currentTick) {
        if (remainingAmmo <= 0) {
            return false;
        }
        return lastCastTick < 0 || (currentTick - lastCastTick) >= COOLDOWN_TICKS;
    }
    private void raiseTomb(Zombie zombie, ZombieService zombieService, long currentTick) {
        GameWorld world = zombie.getWorld();
        List<Cell> legalCells = new ArrayList<>();
        for(Cell cell : world.getGrid().getAllCells()) {
            if(!cell.hasObstacle() && cell.getPlant() == null) {
                legalCells.add(cell);
            }
        }
        if(legalCells.isEmpty()) return;
        int numOfTombs = Math.min(TOMBS_PER_CAST, legalCells.size());
        numOfTombs = Math.min(numOfTombs , remainingAmmo);
        List<Cell> chosenCells = pickRandomCells(legalCells, numOfTombs);
        lastCastTick = currentTick;
        remainingAmmo -= chosenCells.size();
    }
    private List<Cell> giveTombCells(GameWorld world) {
        List<Cell> tombCells = new ArrayList<>();
        for(Cell cell : world.getGrid().getAllCells()) {
            if(cell.getObstacle() instanceof TombStone) {
                tombCells.add(cell);
            }
        }
        return tombCells;
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\game\entity\zombie\ability\TurquoiseAbility.java ---
package game.entity.zombie.ability;
import game.entity.plant.Plant;
import game.entity.zombie.Zombie;
import game.entity.zombie.strategy.movement.NormalMovement;
import game.entity.zombie.strategy.movement.StationaryMovement;
import game.service.Zombie.ZombieService;
import game.service.sun.SunSpawner;
public class TurquoiseAbility extends AbstractZombieAbility{
    private enum State { MOVING, CHARGING, COOLDOWN }
    private State currentState = State.MOVING;
    private long stateEndTick = 0;
    private long lastStealTick = 0;
    private final double chargingTimeSeconds = 5.0;
    private final double cooldownTimeSeconds = 5.0;
    private final int ticksPerSecond = 10;
    private int collectedSunAmount = 0;
    @Override
    public void update(Zombie zombie, ZombieService zombieService, long currentTick) {
        if (zombie.isDead()) return;
        switch (currentState) {
            case MOVING:
                handleMovingState(zombie, zombieService, currentTick);
                break;
            case CHARGING:
                handleChargingState(zombie, zombieService, currentTick);
                break;
            case COOLDOWN:
                handleCooldownState(zombie, currentTick);
                break;
        }
    }
    private void handleMovingState(Zombie zombie, ZombieService zombieService, long currentTick) {
        if (isTherePlantInRange(zombie, zombieService)) {
            currentState = State.CHARGING;
            stateEndTick = currentTick + (long)(chargingTimeSeconds * ticksPerSecond);
            lastStealTick = currentTick;
            zombie.setMovementStrategy(new StationaryMovement());
            zombie.getWorld().addEvent("💎 Turquoise Zombie spotted a plant! Charging laser...");
        }
    }
    private void handleChargingState(Zombie zombie, ZombieService zombieService, long currentTick) {
        if (currentTick - lastStealTick >= ticksPerSecond) {
            zombieService.getWorld().removeSunAmount(25);
            zombie.getWorld().addEvent("☀️ SUN STOLEN BY TURQUOISE! (-25)");
            collectedSunAmount += 25;
            lastStealTick = currentTick;
        }
        if (currentTick >= stateEndTick) {
            executeAttack(zombie, zombieService);
            currentState = State.COOLDOWN;
            stateEndTick = currentTick + (long)(cooldownTimeSeconds * ticksPerSecond);
            zombie.getWorld().addEvent("⚡ LASER FIRED! Turquoise is cooling down...");
        }
    }
    private void handleCooldownState(Zombie zombie, long currentTick) {
        if (currentTick >= stateEndTick) {
            currentState = State.MOVING;
            zombie.setMovementStrategy(new NormalMovement());
            zombie.getWorld().addEvent("💎 Turquoise Zombie is moving again.");
        }
    }
    private boolean isTherePlantInRange(Zombie zombie, ZombieService zombieService) {
        for (Plant plant : zombieService.getWorld().getPlants()) {
            if ((int)plant.getPosition().getY() == (int)zombie.getPosition().getY()) {
                if (inRange(plant, zombie)) {
                    return true;
                }
            }
        }
        return false;
    }
    private boolean inRange(Plant plant, Zombie zombie) {
        double distance = zombie.getPosition().getX() - plant.getPosition().getX();
        double cellWidth = zombie.getWorld().getGrid().getConfig().getCellWidth();
        return distance > 0 && distance < cellWidth * 4;
    }
    private void executeAttack(Zombie zombie, ZombieService zombieService) {
        for (Plant plant : zombieService.getWorld().getPlants()) {
            if ((int)plant.getPosition().getY() == (int)zombie.getPosition().getY()) {
                if (inRange(plant, zombie)) {
                    plant.getHealth().die();
                }
            }
        }
    }
    @Override
    public void onDeath(Zombie zombie) {
        SunSpawner sunSpawner = new SunSpawner(zombie.getWorld());
        sunSpawner.spawnZombieSun(zombie, (int)(collectedSunAmount / 2.0f));
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\game\entity\zombie\ability\WizardAbility.java ---
package game.entity.zombie.ability;
import game.entity.effect.Effect;
import game.entity.effect.NoActEffect;
import game.entity.plant.Plant;
import game.entity.zombie.Zombie;
import game.service.Zombie.ZombieService;
import game.world.GameWorld;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Random;
public class WizardAbility extends AbstractZombieAbility{
    List<Plant> castedPlants = new ArrayList<>();
    private long lastThrow = -1;
    private final long cooldown = 100;
    @Override
    public void update(Zombie zombie, ZombieService zombieService, long currentTick) {
        if(zombie.getTargetPlant() != null) {
            Plant target = zombie.getTargetPlant();
            target.addEffect(new NoActEffect());
            zombie.setTargetPlant(null);
            zombie.getWorld().addEvent("Wizard turned " + target.getName() + " to !CAT!");
            castedPlants.add(target);
        }
        if(canCast(currentTick)) {
            lastThrow = currentTick;
            Plant plant = getRandomPlant(zombie);
            if(plant == null) return;
            plant.addEffect(new NoActEffect());
            castedPlants.add(plant);
            zombie.getWorld().addEvent("Wizard turned " + plant.getName() + " to !CAT!");
        }
    }
    private boolean canCast(long currentTick) {
        return lastThrow < 0 || currentTick - lastThrow > cooldown;
    }
    @Override
    public void onDeath(Zombie zombie) {
        zombie.getWorld().addEvent("Wizard died. all casted plants will return back.");
        for(Plant plant : castedPlants) {
            for(Effect effect : plant.getEffects()) {
                if(effect instanceof NoActEffect noAct) {
                    noAct.setExpired(true);
                }
            }
        }
    }
    private Plant getRandomPlant(Zombie zombie) {
        GameWorld world = zombie.getWorld();
        List<Plant> legal = new ArrayList<>();
        for(Plant plant : world.getPlants()) {
            if(!castedPlants.contains(plant)) {
                legal.add(plant);
            }
        }
        Random random = new Random();
        if(legal.isEmpty()) {
            return null;
        }
        return legal.get(random.nextInt(legal.size()));
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\game\entity\zombie\data\FilterList.java ---
package game.entity.zombie.data;
import app.enums.zombie.FilterListType;
import java.util.List;
public class FilterList {
    private List<String> list;
    private FilterListType listType;
    private String blockedPlantingReason;
    public List<String> getList() { return list; }
    public void setList(List<String> list) { this.list = list; }
    public FilterListType getListType() { return listType; }
    public void setListType(FilterListType listType) { this.listType = listType; }
    public boolean contains(String targetId) {
        boolean inList = list != null && list.contains(targetId);
        if (listType == FilterListType.INCLUDE)
        {
            return inList;
        }
        else if (listType == FilterListType.EXCLUDE)
        {
            return !inList;
        }
        return false;
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\game\entity\zombie\data\StatsData.java ---
package game.entity.zombie.data;
import app.enums.zombie.ZombieStatTier;
import app.enums.zombie.ZombieStatsType;
import java.util.HashMap;
import java.util.Map;
public class StatsData {
    private final Map<ZombieStatsType, ZombieStatTier> stats;
    public StatsData() {
        this.stats = new HashMap<>();
    }
    public void addStats(ZombieStatsType type, ZombieStatTier tier)
    {
        stats.put(type,tier);
    }
    public ZombieStatTier getStatTier(ZombieStatsType type) {
        return stats.getOrDefault(type, ZombieStatTier.TIER_1);
    }
    public boolean hasStat(ZombieStatsType type) {
        return stats.containsKey(type);
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\game\entity\zombie\data\properties\ZombieProperty.java ---
package game.entity.zombie.data.properties;
import app.enums.zombie.ZombieSize;
import common.model.Offset;
import common.model.Rect;
import common.model.ShadowOffset;
import common.model.Vector;
import game.entity.zombie.data.StatsData;
import game.entity.zombie.data.properties.armor.ArmorProperty;
import java.util.List;
public abstract class ZombieProperty implements Property{
    private String alias;
    private int EatDPS;
    private int Cost;
    private Vector ArtCenter;
    private Rect AttackRect;
    private String GroundTrackName;
    private boolean CanSpawnPlantFood;
    private Rect HitRect;
    private int Hitpoints;
    private ScalingProperty ScaledProps;
    private ShadowOffset ShadowOffset;
    private ZombieSize zombieSize;
    private double Speed;
    private int WavePointCost;
    private int Weight;
    private StatsData ZombieStats;
    private Offset hypnoShroomOffset;
    private List<ArmorProperty> armor;
    private float FireDamageMultiplier;
    @Override
    public float getFireDamageMultiplier() {
        return FireDamageMultiplier;
    }
    @Override
    public String getAlias() {
        return alias;
    }
    @Override
    public void setAlias(String alias) {
        this.alias = alias;
    }
    @Override
    public double getSpeed() {
        return Speed;
    }
    @Override
    public int getInitialHealth() {
        return Hitpoints;
    }
    @Override
    public double getRunningSpeedScale() {
        return 1f;
    }
    @Override
    public int getZombieWeight() {
        return Weight;
    }
    @Override
    public int getZombieWaveCost() {
        return WavePointCost;
    }
    @Override
    public int getEatDPS() {
        if(EatDPS >= 0) {
            return EatDPS;
        }
        else {
            return 150;
        }
    }
    @Override
    public int getSmashDamage() {
        return 0;
    }
    @Override
    public float getSmashDuration() {
        return 0;
    }
    @Override
    public int getMaxClaimedSunCurrency() {
        return 0;
    }
    @Override
    public int getAmmo() {
        return 0;
    }
    @Override
    public int getNumberOfTombsToSpawn() {
        return 0;
    }
    @Override
    public float getTimeBetweenCasts() {
        return 0;
    }
    @Override
    public int getTimeBetweenRaisings() {
        return 0;
    }
    @Override
    public String printInfo() {
        StringBuilder zombieInfo = new StringBuilder();
        String border = "🧟════════════════════════════════════════🧟";
        String separator = "┠────────────────────────────────────────┨";
        zombieInfo.append(border).append("\n");
        zombieInfo.append(String.format("┃ 🧠 ZOMBIE ALIAS: %-21s ┃\n", (alias != null ? alias : "Unknown")));
        zombieInfo.append(separator).append("\n");
        zombieInfo.append(String.format("┃ 🩸 Hitpoints (Initial): %-16d ┃\n", Hitpoints));
        zombieInfo.append(String.format("┃ 💨 Speed: %-28.2f ┃\n", Speed));
        zombieInfo.append(String.format("┃ 🦷 Eat DPS: %-26d ┃\n", EatDPS));
        zombieInfo.append(String.format("┃ ⚖️ Chance of appearance: %-27d ┃\n", Weight));
        zombieInfo.append(String.format("┃ 📏 Size: %-29s ┃\n", (zombieSize != null ? zombieSize.name() : "N/A")));
        zombieInfo.append(String.format("┃ 🍀 Spawns Plant Food: %-15s ┃\n", (CanSpawnPlantFood ? "Yes ✅" : "No ❌")));
        zombieInfo.append(String.format("┃ $$ Cost : %-16d ┃\n", getZombieWaveCost()));
        if (armor != null && !armor.isEmpty()) {
            zombieInfo.append(separator).append("\n");
            zombieInfo.append("┃ 🛡️ Armor Equipped:                       ┃\n");
            for (ArmorProperty piece : armor) {
                zombieInfo.append(String.format("┃   🔸 %-34s┃\n", piece.getClass().getSimpleName()));
            }
        }
        zombieInfo.append("╚════════════════════════════════════════╝\n");
        return zombieInfo.toString();
    }
    @Override
    public boolean canSpawnPlantFood() {
        return CanSpawnPlantFood;
    }
}

        return 0;
    }
    @Override
    public String printInfo() {
        StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
        String border = "════════════════════════════════════════════════";
        sb.append(String.format("🧟👑 %s 👑🧟\n", alias != null ? alias.toUpperCase() : "UNKNOWN BOSS"));
        sb.append(border).append("\n");
        sb.append(String.format("┠─ 🧠 Hitpoints: %d\n", getInitialHealth()));
        sb.append(String.format("┠─ 💨 Speed: %.2f (Run Scale: %.2f)\n", getSpeed(), getRunningSpeedScale()));
        sb.append(String.format("┠─ 🦷 Eat DPS: %d\n", getEatDPS()));
        sb.append(String.format("┠─ ⚖️ Weight: %d\n", getZombieWeight()));
        sb.append(String.format("┠─ 🌊 Wave Cost: %d\n", getZombieWaveCost()));
        sb.append(String.format("┠─ 🔥 Fire DMG Multiplier: %.2fx\n", getFireDamageMultiplier()));
        sb.append(String.format("┠─ 🔨 Smash: %d dmg / %.1fs\n", getSmashDamage(), getSmashDuration()));
        sb.append(String.format("┠─ 💰 Max Sun Claimed: %d\n", getMaxClaimedSunCurrency()));
        sb.append(String.format("┠─ 🪦 Tombs Spawned: %d (Every %d ticks)\n", getNumberOfTombsToSpawn(), getTimeBetweenRaisings()));
        sb.append("┠─ 📋 BOSS TRAITS:\n");
        sb.append(String.format("   🔸 Plant Food Drop: %b\n", canSpawnPlantFood));
        sb.append(String.format("   🔸 Triggers Zombie Win: %b\n", canTriggerZombieWin));
        sb.append(String.format("   🔸 Squash Grid Items: %b\n", squashGridItems));
        sb.append(String.format("   🔸 Squash Other Zombies: %b\n", squashZombies));
        sb.append(String.format("   🔸 Damage Reflection: %.1f%%\n", foeEatDamageReflectPct * 100));
        sb.append("┠─ 🛡️ IMMUNITIES:\n");
        sb.append(String.format("   🔸 Vulnerable to Flick: %b\n", canBeFlicked));
        sb.append(String.format("   🔸 Vulnerable to Toss (Strong): %b\n", canBePlantTossedStrong));
        sb.append(String.format("   🔸 Vulnerable to Toss (Weak): %b\n", canBePlantTossedWeak));
        sb.append(String.format("   🔸 Valid Pinch Target: %b\n", isValidPinchTarget));
        if (stages != null && !stages.isEmpty()) {
            sb.append(String.format("┠─ 📈 Stages: %d phase(s) defined\n", stages.size()));
        } else {
            sb.append("┠─ 📈 Stages: None defined\n");
        }
        sb.append("╚").append(border);
        return sb.toString();
    }
    @Override
    public boolean canSpawnPlantFood() {
        return canSpawnPlantFood;
    }
}

package game.entity.zombie.strategy.attack;
import game.entity.plant.Plant;
import game.entity.zombie.Zombie;
import game.service.Zombie.ZombieService;
public class EatAttack implements AttackStrategy{
    @Override
    public void executeAttack(Zombie zombie, Plant targetPlant, ZombieService zombieService, long currentTick) {
        double damagePerSecond = zombie.getPropertyData().getEatDPS() * zombie.getEatSpeedMultiplier();
        targetPlant.takeDamage(damagePerSecond / 10.0f , zombie , currentTick);
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\game\entity\zombie\strategy\attack\SmashAttack.java ---
package game.entity.zombie.strategy.attack;
import game.entity.plant.Plant;
import game.entity.zombie.Zombie;
import game.service.Zombie.ZombieService;
public class SmashAttack implements AttackStrategy{
    @Override
    public void executeAttack(Zombie zombie, Plant targetPlant, ZombieService zombieService, long currentTick) {
        if (targetPlant == null || targetPlant.isDead()) {
            System.out.println("returned because target plant is null");
            return;
        }
        if (currentTick < zombie.getNextAttackTick()) {
            System.out.println("returned because currentTick < zombie.getNextAttackTick()");
            return;
        }
        int damage = zombie.getPropertyData().getSmashDamage();
        System.out.println("plant took smash damage");
        long smashDurationTicks = (long) (zombie.getPropertyData().getSmashDuration() * 10);
        targetPlant.takeDamage(damage , zombie , currentTick);
        zombie.setNextAttackTick(currentTick + smashDurationTicks);
        System.out.printf("Smash damage = %d, smash duration = %d", damage, smashDurationTicks );
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\game\entity\zombie\strategy\attack\SwapAfterAttack.java ---
package game.entity.zombie.strategy.attack;
import game.entity.plant.Plant;
import game.entity.zombie.Zombie;
import game.entity.zombie.strategy.movement.MovementStrategy;
import game.service.Zombie.ZombieService;
public class SwapAfterAttack implements AttackStrategy{
    private final AttackStrategy initialAttack;
    private final AttackStrategy nextAttack;
    private final MovementStrategy nextMovement;
    public SwapAfterAttack(AttackStrategy initialAttack,
                                   AttackStrategy nextAttack,
                                   MovementStrategy nextMovement) {
        this.initialAttack = initialAttack;
        this.nextAttack = nextAttack;
        this.nextMovement = nextMovement;
    }
    @Override
    public void executeAttack(Zombie zombie, Plant targetPlant, ZombieService zombieService, long currentTick) {
        initialAttack.executeAttack(zombie, targetPlant, zombieService, currentTick);
        zombie.setMovementStrategy(nextMovement);
        zombie.setAttackStrategy(nextAttack);
        zombie.setMoveSpeedMultiplier(1);
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\game\entity\zombie\strategy\movement\ChargeMovement.java ---
package game.entity.zombie.strategy.movement;
import game.entity.zombie.Zombie;
import game.service.Zombie.ZombieService;
public class ChargeMovement implements MovementStrategy{
    @Override
    public boolean move(Zombie zombie, ZombieService zombieService, long currentTick) {
        double multiplier = zombie.getPropertyData().getRunningSpeedScale();
        double speed = zombie.getSpeed() * multiplier;
        double currentX = zombie.getPosition().getX();
        double nextX = currentX - (speed / 10.0f);
        zombie.getPosition().setX(nextX);
        return true;
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\game\entity\zombie\strategy\movement\NormalMovement.java ---
package game.entity.zombie.strategy.movement;
import common.model.Position;
import game.entity.zombie.Zombie;
import game.service.Zombie.ZombieService;
public class NormalMovement implements MovementStrategy{
    @Override
    public boolean move(Zombie zombie, ZombieService zombieService, long currentTick) {
        if (zombie.getBody() != null) {
            float vx = (float) -(zombie.getSpeed() * zombie.getMoveSpeedMultiplier());
            zombie.getBody().setLinearVelocity(vx, 0f);
            return true;
        }
        return false;
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\game\entity\zombie\strategy\movement\ReverseMovement.java ---
package game.entity.zombie.strategy.movement;
import game.entity.zombie.Zombie;
import game.service.Zombie.ZombieService;
public class ReverseMovement implements MovementStrategy{
    @Override
    public boolean move(Zombie zombie, ZombieService zombieService, long currentTick) {
        double speed = zombie.getSpeed() * zombie.getMoveSpeedMultiplier();
        double currentX = zombie.getPosition().getX();
        double nextX = currentX + (speed / 10.0f);
        zombie.getPosition().setX(nextX);
        if(!zombieService.getWorld().getGrid().isInsideGrid(zombie.getPosition())) {
            zombie.die();
        }
        return true;
    }
}

package game.entity.zombie.strategy.takedamage;
import game.entity.zombie.Zombie;
public class ElementalTakingDamage implements TakingDamageStrategy{
    @Override
    public void takeDamage(Zombie zombie, long currentTick, double amount) {
    }
    @Override
    public void takeDamage(Zombie zombie, long currentTick, double amount, boolean ignoreArmor) {
        if(ignoreArmor) {
            double health = zombie.getCurrentHealth();

        }
        else {
            takeDamage(zombie, currentTick, amount);
        }
    }

package game.entity.zombie.strategy.takedamage;
import game.entity.zombie.Zombie;
import game.world.playground.Cell;
import game.world.playground.celltypes.WaterCellType;
public class SnorkelTakingDamage implements TakingDamageStrategy{
    @Override
    public void takeDamage(Zombie zombie, long currentTick, double amount) {
        Cell cell = zombie.getWorld().getGrid().getCellAt(zombie.getPosition());
        if(cell.getCellType() instanceof WaterCellType) {
            zombie.getWorld().addEvent("Snorkel is under water , it wont take damage!");
        }
        else {
            double health = zombie.getCurrentHealth();
            zombie.setCurrentHealth(health - amount);
        }
    }
    @Override
    public void takeDamage(Zombie zombie, long currentTick, double amount, boolean ignoreArmor) {
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\game\entity\zombie\strategy\takedamage\StandardTakingDamage.java ---
package game.entity.zombie.strategy.takedamage;
import game.entity.zombie.Zombie;
import game.entity.zombie.ZombieArmor;
import java.util.Iterator;
public class StandardTakingDamage implements TakingDamageStrategy{
    @Override
    public void takeDamage(Zombie zombie, long currentTick, double amount) {
        if (amount <= 0 || zombie.isDead()) {
            return;
        }
        double remainingDamage = amount;
        Iterator<ZombieArmor> iterator = zombie.getArmors().iterator();
        while (iterator.hasNext() && remainingDamage > 0) {
            ZombieArmor armor = iterator.next();
            remainingDamage = armor.takeDamage(remainingDamage);
            if (armor.isBroken()) {
                iterator.remove();
                zombie.getWorld().addEvent(
                        "Armor " + armor.getName() + " broke on " + zombie.getName()
                );
            }
        }
        if (remainingDamage > 0) {
            double health = zombie.getCurrentHealth();
            zombie.setCurrentHealth(health - remainingDamage);
        }
    }
    @Override
    public void takeDamage(Zombie zombie, long currentTick, double amount, boolean ignoreArmor) {
        if(ignoreArmor) {
            double health = zombie.getCurrentHealth();
            zombie.setCurrentHealth(health - amount);
        }
        else {
            takeDamage(zombie, currentTick, amount);
        }
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\game\entity\zombie\strategy\takedamage\TakingDamageStrategy.java ---

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\game\graphics\AnimationEntry.java ---
package game.graphics;
import java.util.Map;
public class AnimationEntry {
    private final String name;
    private final String path;
    private final float[] canvas;
    private final Map<String, Float> clips;
    public AnimationEntry(String name, String path, float[] canvas, Map<String, Float> clips) {
        this.name = name;
        this.path = path;
        this.canvas = canvas;
        this.clips = clips;
    }
    public String getName() { return name; }
    public String getPath() { return path; }
    public float getCanvasWidth() { return canvas[0]; }
    public float getCanvasHeight() { return canvas[1]; }
    public Map<String, Float> getClips() { return clips; }
    public boolean hasClip(String clipName) { return clips.containsKey(clipName); }
    public float getClipDuration(String clipName) { return clips.getOrDefault(clipName, 1.0f); }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\game\graphics\AnimationRegistry.java ---
package game.graphics;
import com.google.gson.Gson;
import com.google.gson.reflect.TypeToken;
import java.lang.reflect.Type;
import java.util.HashMap;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;
public class AnimationRegistry {
    private static AnimationRegistry INSTANCE;
    private final Map<String, AnimationEntry> entriesByName = new HashMap<>();
    private final Map<String, AnimationEntry> entriesByPath = new HashMap<>();
    private AnimationRegistry() {}
    public static AnimationRegistry getInstance() {
        if (INSTANCE == null) {
            throw new IllegalStateException("AnimationRegistry not loaded. Call load() first.");
        }
        return INSTANCE;
    }
    public static void load(String jsonContent) {
        INSTANCE = new AnimationRegistry();
        Gson gson = new Gson();
        Type manifestType = new TypeToken<Map<String, Object>>(){}.getType();
        Map<String, Object> root = gson.fromJson(jsonContent, manifestType);
        @SuppressWarnings("unchecked")
        List<Map<String, Object>> animations = (List<Map<String, Object>>) root.get("animations");
        for (Map<String, Object> animData : animations) {
            String name = (String) animData.get("name");
            String path = (String) animData.get("path");
            @SuppressWarnings("unchecked")
            List<Double> canvasRaw = (List<Double>) animData.get("canvas");
            float[] canvas = new float[]{
                canvasRaw.get(0).floatValue(),
                canvasRaw.get(1).floatValue()
            };
            @SuppressWarnings("unchecked")
            Map<String, Double> clipsRaw = (Map<String, Double>) animData.get("clips");
            Map<String, Float> clips = new HashMap<>();
            for (Map.Entry<String, Double> entry : clipsRaw.entrySet()) {
                clips.put(entry.getKey(), entry.getValue().floatValue());
            }
            AnimationEntry entry = new AnimationEntry(name, path, canvas, clips);
            INSTANCE.entriesByName.put(name, entry);
            INSTANCE.entriesByPath.put(path, entry);
        }
    }
    public AnimationEntry getByName(String name) {
        return entriesByName.get(name);
    }
    public AnimationEntry getByPath(String path) {
        return entriesByPath.get(path);
    }
    public boolean hasEntry(String name) {
        return entriesByName.containsKey(name);
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\game\service\lawnmower\LawnMowerService.java ---
package game.service.lawnmower;
import app.enums.quest.EventType;
import app.model.App;
import common.model.Position;
import game.entity.lawnmower.LawnMower;
import game.entity.quest.GameEvent;
import game.entity.zombie.Zombie;
import game.world.GameWorld;
import java.util.ArrayList;
public class LawnMowerService
{
    private final GameWorld gameWorld;
    private final ArrayList<LawnMower> lawnMowers;
    public LawnMowerService(GameWorld gameWorld , ArrayList<LawnMower> lawnMowers) {
        this.gameWorld = gameWorld;
        this.lawnMowers = lawnMowers;
    }
    public void update()
    {
        boolean active = false;
        Zombie targetZombie = null;
        ArrayList<Zombie> zombies = new ArrayList<>();
        if (active)
        {
            int row = 0;
            Position position = targetZombie.getPosition();
            position.setX(2);
            if (gameWorld.getGrid().getCellAt(position) == null) return;
            LawnMower lawnMower = getLawnMowerInRow(row);
            if (lawnMower != null)
            {
                destroyZombiesInRow(gameWorld , row);
                lawnMower.trigger();
                gameWorld.addEvent("The lawnmower is activated!");
            }
        }
    }
    private void destroyZombiesInRow(GameWorld gameWorld , int row)
    {
        int killedZombiesCount = 0;
        for (Zombie zombie: gameWorld.getZombiesInRow(row))
        {
            zombie.die();
            killedZombiesCount++;
        }
        App.getCurrentUser().getQuestManager().fireEvent(new GameEvent(EventType.LAWN_MOWER_TRIGGERED, killedZombiesCount));
    }
    private LawnMower getLawnMowerInRow(int row){
        for (LawnMower lawnMower: lawnMowers)
        {
            if (lawnMower.getRow() == row && !lawnMower.isUsed()) return lawnMower;
        }
        return null;
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\game\service\minigame\VaseBreakerService.java ---
package game.service.minigame;
import app.model.App;
import game.service.Zombie.ZombieService;
import game.world.minigame.vasebreaker.Vase;
import game.world.minigame.vasebreaker.VaseBreakerWorld;
import game.world.playground.Cell;
public class VaseBreakerService {
    private final VaseBreakerWorld world;
    private final ZombieService zombieService;
    public VaseBreakerService(VaseBreakerWorld world) {
        this.world = world;
        this.zombieService = new ZombieService(world);
    }
    public void breakVase(Vase vase) {
        Cell vaseCell = world.getGrid().getCellAt(vase.getPosition());
        vaseCell.setObstacle(null);
        if(vase.getContainedPlantName() == null) {
            zombieService.spawnZombie(vase.getContainedZombieAlias(), vase.getPosition());
            world.addEvent("Zombie " + vase.getContainedZombieAlias()
                    + " got spawned in position " +
                    vase.getPosition().getX() + "," +
                    vase.getPosition().getY() + " due to vase break!");
        }
        else if(vase.getContainedZombieAlias() == null) {
            long currentTick = App.getCurrentGameEngine().getGameClock().getCurrentTick();
            world.addPlantToInventory(vase.getContainedPlantName(), currentTick);
            world.addEvent("Plant " + vase.getContainedPlantName().getLabel() + " spawned due to vase break and added to inventory!");
        }
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\game\service\plant\AttackService.java ---
package game.service.plant;
import app.enums.effect.EffectType;
import game.entity.effect.FireEffect;
import game.entity.effect.PoisonEffect;
import game.entity.effect.SlowEffect;
import game.entity.zombie.Zombie;
import game.world.GameWorld;
import game.world.playground.Cell;
import game.world.playground.Grid;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;
public class AttackService {
    private final GameWorld gameWorld;
    private final Grid grid;
    public AttackService(GameWorld gameWorld) {
        this.gameWorld = gameWorld;
        this.grid = gameWorld.getGrid();
    }
    public boolean hasActiveZombieInCell(Cell cell) {
        return false;
    }
    public Zombie getFrontZombie(List<Zombie> zombies) {
        if (zombies == null || zombies.isEmpty()) return null;
        Zombie frontZombie = null;
        for (Zombie z : zombies) {
            if (z != null && !z.isDead()) {
                if (frontZombie == null || z.getPosition().getX() < frontZombie.getPosition().getX()) {
                    frontZombie = z;
                }
            }
        }
        return frontZombie;
    }
    public boolean hasZombiesInArea(int centerRow, int centerCol, int radius) {
        for (int r = centerRow - radius; r <= centerRow + radius; r++) {
            for (int c = centerCol - radius; c <= centerCol + radius; c++) {
                Cell cell = grid.getCell(r, c);
                if (hasActiveZombieInCell(cell)) {
                    return true;
                }
            }
        }
        return false;
    }
    public void damageZombie(Zombie zombie, long currentTick, int damage) {
        if (zombie == null || zombie.isDead()) return;
        zombie.getTakingDamageStrategy().takeDamage(zombie, currentTick, damage);
        gameWorld.addEvent(zombie.getName() + " took " + damage + " damage.");
    }
    public void damageFrontZombie(List<Zombie> zombies, long currentTick, int damage) {
        Zombie frontZombie = getFrontZombie(zombies);
        if (frontZombie != null) {
            damageZombie(frontZombie, currentTick, damage);
        }
    }
    public void damageZombiesInCell(int row, int col, int damage, long currentTick) {
    }
    public void damageZombiesInRow(int row, int damage, long currentTick) {
        List<Zombie> zombiesInRow = gameWorld.getZombiesInRow(row);
        if (zombiesInRow == null) return;
        List<Zombie> zombiesToDamage = new ArrayList<>(zombiesInRow);
        for (Zombie zombie : zombiesToDamage) {
            if (zombie != null && !zombie.isDead()) {
                damageZombie(zombie, currentTick, damage);
            }
        }
    }
    public void damageArea(int centerRow, int centerCol, int radius, int damage, long currentTick) {
        for (int r = centerRow - radius; r <= centerRow + radius; r++) {
            for (int c = centerCol - radius; c <= centerCol + radius; c++) {
                damageZombiesInCell(r, c, damage, currentTick);
            }
        }
    }
    private void applyFireEffect(Zombie zombie, long currentTick, int fireDuration) {
        zombie.removeEffect(EffectType.ICE);
        zombie.addEffect(new FireEffect(currentTick, fireDuration));
    }
    public void damageFrontZombieWithFire(List<Zombie> zombies, long currentTick, int damage, int fireDuration) {
        Zombie frontZombie = getFrontZombie(zombies);
        if (frontZombie != null) {
            applyFireEffect(frontZombie, currentTick, fireDuration);
            damageZombie(frontZombie, currentTick, damage);
        }
    }
    public void damageZombiesInCellWithFire(int row, int col, int damage, long currentTick, int fireDuration) {
    }
    public void damageZombiesInRowWithFire(int row, int damage, long currentTick, int fireDuration) {
        List<Zombie> zombiesInRow = gameWorld.getZombiesInRow(row);
        if (zombiesInRow == null) return;
        List<Zombie> zombiesToDamage = new ArrayList<>(zombiesInRow);
        for (Zombie zombie : zombiesToDamage) {
            if (zombie != null && !zombie.isDead()) {
                applyFireEffect(zombie, currentTick, fireDuration);
                damageZombie(zombie, currentTick, damage);
            }
        }
    }
    public void damageAreaWithFire(int centerRow, int centerCol, int radius, int damage, long currentTick, int fireDuration) {
        for (int r = centerRow - radius; r <= centerRow + radius; r++) {
            for (int c = centerCol - radius; c <= centerCol + radius; c++) {
                damageZombiesInCellWithFire(r, c, damage, currentTick, fireDuration);
            }
        }
    }
    public void damageFrontZombieWithIce(List<Zombie> zombies, long currentTick, int damage, double slowFactor, int iceDuration) {
        Zombie frontZombie = getFrontZombie(zombies);
        if (frontZombie != null) {
            frontZombie.addEffect(new SlowEffect(currentTick, iceDuration, (float) slowFactor, EffectType.ICE));
            damageZombie(frontZombie, currentTick, damage);
        }
    }
    public void damageZombiesInCellWithIce(int row, int col, int damage, long currentTick, double slowFactor, int iceDuration) {
    }
    public void damageZombiesInRowWithIce(int row, int damage, long currentTick, double slowFactor, int iceDuration) {
        List<Zombie> zombiesInRow = gameWorld.getZombiesInRow(row);
        if (zombiesInRow == null) return;
        List<Zombie> zombiesToDamage = new ArrayList<>(zombiesInRow);
        for (Zombie zombie : zombiesToDamage) {
            if (zombie != null && !zombie.isDead()) {
                zombie.addEffect(new SlowEffect(currentTick, iceDuration, (float) slowFactor, EffectType.ICE));
                damageZombie(zombie, currentTick, damage);
            }
        }
    }
    public void damageAreaWithIce(int centerRow, int centerCol, int radius, int damage, long currentTick, double slowFactor, int iceDuration) {
        for (int r = centerRow - radius; r <= centerRow + radius; r++) {
            for (int c = centerCol - radius; c <= centerCol + radius; c++) {
                damageZombiesInCellWithIce(r, c, damage, currentTick, slowFactor, iceDuration);
            }
        }
    }
    public void damageFrontZombieWithPoison(List<Zombie> zombies, long currentTick, int damage, int poisonDuration, int damagePerTick) {
        Zombie frontZombie = getFrontZombie(zombies);
        if (frontZombie != null) {
            frontZombie.addEffect(new PoisonEffect(currentTick, poisonDuration, damagePerTick));
            damageZombie(frontZombie, currentTick, damage);
        }
    }
    public void damageZombiesInCellWithPoison(int row, int col, int damage, long currentTick, int poisonDuration, int damagePerTick) {
    }
    public void damageZombiesInRowWithPoison(int row, int damage, long currentTick, int poisonDuration, int damagePerTick) {
        List<Zombie> zombiesInRow = gameWorld.getZombiesInRow(row);
        if (zombiesInRow == null) return;
        List<Zombie> zombiesToDamage = new ArrayList<>(zombiesInRow);
        for (Zombie zombie : zombiesToDamage) {
            if (zombie != null && !zombie.isDead()) {
                zombie.addEffect(new PoisonEffect(currentTick, poisonDuration, damagePerTick));
                damageZombie(zombie, currentTick, damage);
            }
        }
    }
    public void damageAreaWithPoison(int centerRow, int centerCol, int radius, int damage, long currentTick, int poisonDuration, int damagePerTick) {
        for (int r = centerRow - radius; r <= centerRow + radius; r++) {
            for (int c = centerCol - radius; c <= centerCol + radius; c++) {
                damageZombiesInCellWithPoison(r, c, damage, currentTick, poisonDuration, damagePerTick);
            }
        }
    }
    public void freezeZombie(Zombie zombie, long currentTick, int icebergFreezeDurationTicks) {
        if (zombie == null || zombie.isDead()) return;
        zombie.addEffect(new SlowEffect(currentTick, icebergFreezeDurationTicks, 0.0f, EffectType.ICE));
        gameWorld.addEvent(zombie.getName() + " is completely frozen by Iceberg Lettuce.");
    }
    public void freezeZombieInCell(Cell cell, long currentTick, int icebergFreezeDurationTicks) {
    }
    public void freezeZombiesInMap(long currentTick, int icebergFreezeDurationTicks) {
    }
    public void freezeZombiesInRow(long currentTick, int icebergFreezeDurationTicks, int row) {
        List<Zombie> zombiesInRow = gameWorld.getZombiesInRow(row);
        if (zombiesInRow == null) return;
        List<Zombie> zombies = new ArrayList<>(zombiesInRow);
        for (Zombie zombie : zombies) {
            freezeZombie(zombie, currentTick, icebergFreezeDurationTicks);
        }
    }
    public Zombie getClosestZombieInFront(int row, double plantX, double maxDistanceX) {
        List<Zombie> zombies = gameWorld.getZombiesInRow(row);
        if (zombies == null) return null;
        Zombie closest = null;
        for (Zombie z : zombies) {
            if (z != null && !z.isDead()) {
                double zx = z.getPosition().getX();
                if (zx >= plantX && zx <= plantX + maxDistanceX) {
                    if (closest == null || zx < closest.getPosition().getX()) {
                        closest = z;
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        return closest;
    }
    public Zombie getClosestZombieBehind(int row, double plantX, double maxDistanceX) {
        List<Zombie> zombies = gameWorld.getZombiesInRow(row);
        if (zombies == null) return null;
        Zombie closest = null;
        for (Zombie z : zombies) {
            if (z != null && !z.isDead()) {
                double zx = z.getPosition().getX();
                if (zx < plantX && zx >= plantX - maxDistanceX) {
                    if (closest == null || zx > closest.getPosition().getX()) {
                        closest = z;
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        return closest;
    }
    public Zombie getClosestZombieInFront(int centerRow, int centerCol, int radius) {
        Zombie closest = null;
        return closest;
    }
    public Zombie getClosestZombieBehind(int centerRow, int centerCol, int radius) {
        Zombie closest = null;
        return closest;
    }
    public Zombie getClosestZombieAbove(int centerRow, int centerCol, int radius) {
        Zombie closest = null;
        return closest;
    }
    public Zombie getClosestZombieBelow(int centerRow, int centerCol, int radius) {
        Zombie closest = null;
        return closest;
    }
    private double getZombieDistance(Zombie zombie, int centerRow, int centerCol, int radius) {
        return Double.MAX_VALUE;
    }
    public Zombie getClosestZombieFromSides(int centerRow, int centerCol, int radius) {
        Zombie above = getClosestZombieAbove(centerRow, centerCol, radius);
        Zombie below = getClosestZombieBelow(centerRow, centerCol, radius);
        double distAbove = getZombieDistance(above, centerRow, centerCol, radius);
        double distBelow = getZombieDistance(below, centerRow, centerCol, radius);
        return distAbove <= distBelow ? above : below;
    }
    public Zombie getClosestZombieFromFrontAndBehind(int centerRow, int centerCol, int radius) {
        Zombie front = getClosestZombieInFront(centerRow, centerCol, radius);
        Zombie behind = getClosestZombieBehind(centerRow, centerCol, radius);
        double distFront = getZombieDistance(front, centerRow, centerCol, radius);
        double distBehind = getZombieDistance(behind, centerRow, centerCol, radius);
        return distFront <= distBehind ? front : behind;
    }
    public void damageClosestZombieInRadius(int centerRow, int centerCol, int radius, int damage, long currentTick) {
        Zombie sides = getClosestZombieFromSides(centerRow, centerCol, radius);
        Zombie frontBehind = getClosestZombieFromFrontAndBehind(centerRow, centerCol, radius);
        double distSides = getZombieDistance(sides, centerRow, centerCol, radius);
        double distFrontBehind = getZombieDistance(frontBehind, centerRow, centerCol, radius);
        Zombie closest = distSides <= distFrontBehind ? sides : frontBehind;
        if (closest != null) {
            damageZombie(closest, currentTick, damage);
        }
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\game\service\plant\PlantDefinitionBuilder.java ---
package game.service.plant;
import app.enums.plant.*;
import game.entity.plant.PlantDefinition;
import game.entity.plant.PlantTags;
import game.entity.plant.ability.PlantAbility;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;
public class PlantDefinitionBuilder {
    private PlantName name;
    private PlantCategory category;
    private int sunCost;
    private int maxHealth;
    private int cooldown;
    private PlantTags tags;
    private final List<PlantAbility> abilities = new ArrayList<>();
    private int attackDamage;
    private float actionInterval;
    public PlantDefinitionBuilder name(PlantName name) {
        this.name = name;
        return this;
    }
    public PlantDefinitionBuilder category(PlantCategory category) {
        this.category = category;
        return this;
    }
    public PlantDefinitionBuilder sunCost(int sunCost) {
        this.sunCost = sunCost;
        return this;
    }
    public PlantDefinitionBuilder maxHealth(int maxHealth) {
        this.maxHealth = maxHealth;
        return this;
    }
    public PlantDefinitionBuilder cooldown(int cooldown) {
        this.cooldown = cooldown;
        return this;
    }
    public PlantDefinitionBuilder tags(PlantTags tags) {
        this.tags = tags;
        return this;
    }
    public PlantDefinitionBuilder addAbilities(PlantAbility ability) {
        this.abilities.add(ability);
        return this;
    }
    public PlantDefinitionBuilder attackDamage(int attackDamage) {
        this.attackDamage = attackDamage;
        return this;
    }
    public PlantDefinitionBuilder actionInterval(float actionInterval) {
        this.actionInterval = actionInterval;
        return this;
    }
    public PlantDefinition build() {
        return new PlantDefinition(name, category, sunCost, maxHealth,
                cooldown, tags, abilities, attackDamage, actionInterval);
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\game\service\plant\PlantFactory.java ---
package game.service.plant;
import app.enums.plant.PlantName;
import app.enums.plant.PlantState;
import app.model.App;
import com.badlogic.gdx.physics.box2d.Body;
import common.model.Position;
import game.component.physic.BodyFactory;
import game.entity.plant.*;
import game.entity.registry.PlantRegistry;
import game.world.playground.MapConfig;
public final class PlantFactory
{
    private PlantFactory() {}
    public static Plant create(PlantName name)
    {
        Plant plant = new Plant();
        PlantDefinition plantDefinition = PlantRegistry.get(name);
        plant.setDefinition(plantDefinition);
        plant.setHealth(new PlantHealth(plantDefinition.getMaxHealth() + UpgradePlant.getExtraHP(name)));
        plant.setState(PlantState.ALIVE);
        return plant;
    }
    public static Plant create(PlantName name , Position position , long plantedTick )
    {
        Plant plant = new Plant();
        PlantDefinition plantDefinition = PlantRegistry.get(name);
        plant.setDefinition(plantDefinition);
        plant.setHealth(new PlantHealth(plantDefinition.getMaxHealth() + UpgradePlant.getExtraHP(name)));
        plant.setState(PlantState.ALIVE);
        plant.setPosition(position);
        plant.setPlantedTick(plantedTick);
        plant.setAbilities(AbilityFactory.getAbilities(name));
        MapConfig config = App.getCurrentGameEngine().getGameWorld().getGrid().getConfig();
        float size = 2.3f;
        float bodyWidth = config.getCellWidth();
        float bodyHeight = config.getCellHeight();
        Body plantBody = BodyFactory.getInstance().createPlantBody(
            (float) position.getX(),
            (float) position.getY(),
            bodyWidth/size,
            bodyHeight/size
        );
        plantBody.setUserData(plant);
        plant.setBody(plantBody);
        return plant;
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\game\service\plant\PlantPlacementResult.java ---
package game.service.plant;
public enum PlantPlacementResult {
    SUCCESS(true, "Plant planted successfully."),
    PLANT_NOT_SELECTED(false, "No plant has been selected."),
    INVALID_CELL(false, "The selected cell is invalid."),
    INVALID_PLANT(false, "The selected plant is invalid."),
    COOLDOWN(false, "This plant is still on cooldown."),
    NOT_ENOUGH_SUN(false, "You do not have enough sun."),
    CELL_OCCUPIED(false, "This cell cannot contain this plant.");
    private final boolean success;
    private final String message;
    PlantPlacementResult(boolean success, String message) {
        this.success = success;
        this.message = message;
    }
    public boolean isSuccess() {
        return success;
    }
    public String getMessage() {
        return message;
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\game\service\plant\PlantPlacementService.java ---
package game.service.plant;
import app.enums.plant.PlantName;
import app.model.App;
import com.badlogic.gdx.math.Vector2;
import common.model.Position;
import game.engine.RealTimeGameEngine;
import game.entity.plant.Plant;
import game.entity.plant.PlantCooldown;
import game.entity.plant.PlantDefinition;
import game.entity.registry.PlantRegistry;
import game.world.GameWorld;
import game.world.playground.Cell;
import game.world.playground.MapConfig;
public class PlantPlacementService {
    private final GameWorld world;
    private final RealTimeGameEngine engine;
    private final PlantPlacementValidator validator;
    public PlantPlacementService(GameWorld world, RealTimeGameEngine engine) {
        this.world = world;
        this.engine = engine;
        this.validator = new PlantPlacementValidator(world);
    }
    public PlantPlacementResult placePlant(
        PlantName plantName,
        Cell targetCell
    ) {
        PlantPlacementResult validation =
            validator.validate(plantName, targetCell);
        if (!validation.isSuccess()) {
            return validation;
        }
        PlantDefinition definition = PlantRegistry.get(plantName);
        MapConfig config = world.getMap().getMapConfig();
        Vector2 center = config.gridToWorld(
            targetCell.getRow(),
            targetCell.getCol()
        );
        long currentTick = engine.getClock().getCurrentTick();
        Plant plant = PlantFactory.create(
            plantName,
            new Position(center.x, center.y),
            currentTick
        );
        if (plant == null) {
            return PlantPlacementResult.INVALID_PLANT;
        }
        if (App.getCurrentUser().isBoosted(definition.getName())) {
            plant.setBoosted(true);
        }
        targetCell.setPlant(plant);
        world.addPlant(plant);
        world.removeSunAmount(definition.getSunCost());
        PlantCooldown cooldown = world.getCoolDownPlant(plantName);
        if (cooldown != null) {
            cooldown.startCooldown(currentTick);
        } else {
            System.out.println("!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!cooldown");
        }
        engine.getGraphicsEngine().registerPlant(plant);
        world.getObserver().onPlantPlaced(
            plantName,
            targetCell.getRow(),
            targetCell.getCol()
        );
        return PlantPlacementResult.SUCCESS;
    }
    public PlantPlacementValidator getValidator() {
        return validator;
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\game\service\plant\PlantPlacementValidator.java ---
package game.service.plant;
import app.enums.plant.PlantName;
import game.entity.plant.PlantCooldown;
import game.entity.plant.PlantDefinition;
import game.entity.registry.PlantRegistry;
import game.world.GameWorld;
import game.world.playground.Cell;
public class PlantPlacementValidator {
    private final GameWorld world;
    public PlantPlacementValidator(GameWorld world) {
        this.world = world;
    }
    public PlantPlacementResult validate(PlantName plantName, Cell targetCell) {
        if (plantName == null) {
            return PlantPlacementResult.PLANT_NOT_SELECTED;
        }
        if (targetCell == null) {
            return PlantPlacementResult.INVALID_CELL;
        }
        PlantDefinition definition = PlantRegistry.get(plantName);
        if (definition == null) {
            return PlantPlacementResult.INVALID_PLANT;
        }
        if (!isCooldownAvailable(plantName)) {
            return PlantPlacementResult.COOLDOWN;
        }
        if (!hasEnoughSun(definition)) {
            return PlantPlacementResult.NOT_ENOUGH_SUN;
        }
        if (!targetCell.isPlantable(definition)) {
            return PlantPlacementResult.CELL_OCCUPIED;
        }
        return PlantPlacementResult.SUCCESS;
    }
    public boolean canPlant(PlantName plantName, Cell targetCell) {
        return validate(plantName, targetCell).isSuccess();
    }
    private boolean isCooldownAvailable(PlantName plantName) {
        PlantCooldown cooldown = world.getCoolDownPlant(plantName);
        return cooldown != null && cooldown.isAvailable();
    }
    private boolean hasEnoughSun(PlantDefinition definition) {
        return world.getSunAmount() >= definition.getSunCost();
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\game\service\plant\UpgradePlant.java ---
package game.service.plant;
import app.enums.plant.PlantName;
import app.model.App;
import app.model.user.User;
import game.entity.plant.PlantLevel;
public class UpgradePlant {
    final static int TICKS_PER_SECOND = 10;
    public static int getLevel(PlantName plantName) {
        User user = App.getCurrentUser();
        if (user == null || user.getPlantLevelMap() == null) return 1;
        PlantLevel pl = user.getPlantLevelMap().get(plantName);
        return pl != null ? pl.getLevel() : 1;
    }
    public static int getExtraHP(PlantName plantName) {
        int level = getLevel(plantName), extraHp = 0;
        if (level >= 2) extraHp += switch (plantName) {
            case WALL_NUT, SWEET_POTATO, EXPLODE_O_NUT, PUMPKIN -> 1000;
            case TALL_NUT -> 2000;
            case KIWIBEAST -> 200;
            case TORCHWOOD -> 300;
            case GARLIC -> 150;
            default -> 0;
        };
        if (level >= 3) extraHp += switch (plantName) {
            case SUNFLOWER, TWIN_SUNFLOWER, SUN_SHROOM, PRIMAL_SUNFLOWER, PEASHOOTER, ROTOBAGA, CAULIPOWER, GOO_PEASHOOTER, SUN_BEAN -> 150;
            case REPEATER, PEA_POD, SPLIT_PEA, FIRE_PEASHOOTER, CHOMPER, CAT_TAIL, LILY_PAD -> 200;
            case ENDURIAN -> 1000;
            default -> 0;
        };
        if (level >= 4) extraHp += switch (plantName) {
            case CABBAGE_PULT, KERNEL_PULT -> 150;
            case THREEPEATER, BONK_CHOY, PHAT_BEET, WASABI_WHIP, MAGNET_SHROOM -> 200;
            case WALL_NUT, SWEET_POTATO, PUMPKIN -> 1500;
            case TALL_NUT -> 3000;
            case GARLIC -> 250;
            default -> 0;
        };
        return extraHp;
    }
    public static int getCostReduction(PlantName plantName) {
        int level = getLevel(plantName), reduction = 0;
        if (level >= 2) reduction += switch (plantName) {
            case THREEPEATER, MELON_PULT, HYPNO_SHROOM, LILY_PAD -> 25;
            case WINTER_MELON -> 50;
            default -> 0;
        };
        if (level >= 3 && plantName == PlantName.IMITATER) reduction += 25;
        if (level >= 4) reduction += switch (plantName) {
            case TWIN_SUNFLOWER, PRIMAL_SUNFLOWER, GOLD_BLOOM, PEASHOOTER, REPEATER, SNOW_PEA, ROTOBAGA, PEA_POD, SPLIT_PEA, ELECTRIC_BLUEBERRY, BOWLING_BULB, CACTUS, FIRE_PEASHOOTER, STARFRUIT, GOO_PEASHOOTER, FUME_SHROOM, WINTER_MELON, PEPPER_PULT, CHERRY_BOMB, GRAPESHOT, JALAPENO, TANGLE_KELP, ENDURIAN, EXPLODE_O_NUT, SUN_BEAN, TORCHWOOD, CAT_TAIL -> 25;
            case CITRON, CAULIPOWER, MEGA_GATLING_PEA, DOOM_SHROOM -> 50;
            default -> 0;
        };
        return reduction;
    }
    public static int getExtraDamage(PlantName plantName)
    {
        return getExtraAttackerDamage(plantName) + getExtraAoEDamage(plantName) +
                getExtraPoisonDamageTick(plantName) + getExtraReflectDamage(plantName) + getExtraExplodeDamage(plantName);
    }
    public static int getExtraAttackerDamage(PlantName plantName) {
        int level = getLevel(plantName), damage = 0;
        if (level >= 2) damage += switch (plantName) {
            case PEASHOOTER, REPEATER, SNOW_PEA, ROTOBAGA, PEA_POD, SPLIT_PEA, FIRE_PEASHOOTER, MEGA_GATLING_PEA, CABBAGE_PULT, CAT_TAIL, WASABI_WHIP, PHAT_BEET -> 10;
            case BONK_CHOY -> 5;
            case PEPPER_PULT -> 15;
            case GRAPESHOT -> 600;
            default -> 0;
        };
        if (level >= 3) damage += switch (plantName) {
            case CITRON -> 150;
            case THREEPEATER, CACTUS, STARFRUIT, PUFF_SHROOM, FUME_SHROOM, KERNEL_PULT -> 10;
            case BOWLING_BULB, KIWIBEAST -> 15;
            case SEA_SHROOM -> 5;
            case CHERRY_BOMB, SQUASH, JALAPENO -> 600;
            case DOOM_SHROOM -> 800;
            default -> 0;
        };
        if (level >= 4) damage += switch (plantName) {
            case POTATO_MINE -> 600;
            case PRIMAL_POTATO_MINE -> 400;
            case MELON_PULT -> 30;
            case ICE_SHROOM -> 50;
            default -> 0;
        };
        return damage;
    }
    public static int getCooldownReduction(PlantName plantName) {
        int level = getLevel(plantName), reduction = 0;
        if (level >= 2) reduction += switch (plantName) {
            case CAULIPOWER, ELECTRIC_BLUEBERRY, ICEBERG_LETTUCE, IMITATER, HOT_POTATO -> 2;
            case SQUASH -> 3;
            case GOLD_BLOOM, CHERRY_BOMB, JALAPENO, DOOM_SHROOM, TANGLE_KELP -> 5;
            default -> 0;
        };
        if (level >= 3) reduction += switch (plantName) {
            case GRAVE_BUSTER -> 2;
            case PRIMAL_POTATO_MINE, GARLIC -> 3;
            case POTATO_MINE, WALL_NUT, TALL_NUT, SWEET_POTATO, PUMPKIN, MAGNET_SHROOM, ICE_SHROOM, ENLIGHTEN_MINT, APPEASE_MINT, ARMA_MINT, BOMBARD_MINT, ENFORCE_MINT, REINFORCE_MINT, ENCHANT_MINT, PIERCE_MINT, CAT_TAIL_MINT -> 5;
            default -> 0;
        };
        if (level >= 4 && plantName == PlantName.LILY_PAD) reduction += 2;
        return reduction * TICKS_PER_SECOND;
    }
    public static double getAttackSpeedBoostPercentage(PlantName plantName) {
        int level = getLevel(plantName);
        double boost = (level >= 2 && plantName == PlantName.STARFRUIT) ? 0.10 : 0.0;
        if (level >= 3) {
            if (plantName == PlantName.BONK_CHOY || plantName == PlantName.PHAT_BEET) boost += 0.10;
            else if (plantName == PlantName.CABBAGE_PULT) boost += 0.15;
        }
        return boost;
    }
    public static double getProductionTimeReduction(PlantName plantName) {
        return getLevel(plantName) >= 2 && switch (plantName) {
            case SUNFLOWER, TWIN_SUNFLOWER, PRIMAL_SUNFLOWER -> true;
            default -> false;
        } ? 2.0 : 0.0;
    }
    public static double getGrowTimeReduction(PlantName plantName) {
        return (getLevel(plantName) >= 2 && plantName == PlantName.SUN_SHROOM) ? 5.0 : 0.0;
    }
    public static double getChargeTimeReduction(PlantName plantName) {
        return (getLevel(plantName) >= 2 && plantName == PlantName.CITRON) ? 1.0 : 0.0;
    }
    public static double getRegenTimeReduction(PlantName plantName) {
        return (getLevel(plantName) >= 2 && plantName == PlantName.BOWLING_BULB) ? 1.0 : 0.0;
    }
    public static double getArmTimeReduction(PlantName plantName) {
        if (getLevel(plantName) < 2) return 0.0;
        return plantName == PlantName.POTATO_MINE ? 3.0 : plantName == PlantName.PRIMAL_POTATO_MINE ? 1.0 : 0.0;
    }
    public static double getEatTimeReduction(PlantName plantName) {
        return (getLevel(plantName) >= 2 && plantName == PlantName.GRAVE_BUSTER) ? 1.0 : 0.0;
    }
    public static double getDigestTimeReduction(PlantName plantName) {
        if (plantName != PlantName.CHOMPER) return 0.0;
        int level = getLevel(plantName);
        return level >= 4 ? 5.0 : level >= 2 ? 2.0 : 0.0;
    }
    public static int getExtraRange(PlantName plantName) {
        int level = getLevel(plantName), extra = 0;
        if (level >= 2 && switch (plantName) { case SEA_SHROOM, FUME_SHROOM, MAGNET_SHROOM -> true; default -> false; }) extra++;
        if (level >= 3 && plantName == PlantName.WASABI_WHIP) extra++;
        if (level >= 4 && plantName == PlantName.PUFF_SHROOM) extra++;
        return extra;
    }
    public static int getExtraPierce(PlantName plantName) {
        return (getLevel(plantName) >= 2 && plantName == PlantName.CACTUS) ? 1 : 0;
    }
    public static int getExtraFreezeOrChillTime(PlantName plantName) {
        int level = getLevel(plantName);
        if (level >= 2 && plantName == PlantName.ICE_SHROOM) return 2;
        if (level >= 3 && (plantName == PlantName.SNOW_PEA || plantName == PlantName.ICEBERG_LETTUCE)) return 2;
        return 0;
    }
    public static int getExtraMintDuration(PlantName plantName) {
        return (getLevel(plantName) >= 2 && isMint(plantName)) ? 1 : 0;
    }
    public static int getExtraLifeSpan(PlantName plantName) {
        int level = getLevel(plantName);
        return ((level >= 2 && plantName == PlantName.PUFF_SHROOM) || (level >= 4 && plantName == PlantName.SEA_SHROOM)) ? 10 : 0;
    }
    public static double getExtraButterChance(PlantName plantName) {
        return (getLevel(plantName) >= 2 && plantName == PlantName.KERNEL_PULT) ? 0.05 : 0.0;
    }
    public static double getExtraPlantFoodChance(PlantName plantName) {
        return (getLevel(plantName) >= 3 && plantName == PlantName.MEGA_GATLING_PEA) ? 0.05 : 0.0;
    }
    public static int getExtraBounces(PlantName plantName) {
        return (getLevel(plantName) >= 3 && plantName == PlantName.GRAPESHOT) ? 1 : 0;
    }
    public static int getExtraTargets(PlantName plantName) {
        return (getLevel(plantName) >= 3 && plantName == PlantName.TANGLE_KELP) ? 1 : 0;
    }
    public static int getExtraMaxSize(PlantName plantName) {
        return (getLevel(plantName) >= 4 && plantName == PlantName.KIWIBEAST) ? 1 : 0;
    }
    public static int getExtraWarmthRadius(PlantName plantName) {
        return (getLevel(plantName) >= 3 && plantName == PlantName.PEPPER_PULT) ? 1 : 0;
    }
    public static int getZombieHpBuffLevel(PlantName plantName) {
        return (getLevel(plantName) >= 3 && plantName == PlantName.HYPNO_SHROOM) ? 1 : 0;
    }
    public static int getZombieDmgBuffLevel(PlantName plantName) {
        return (getLevel(plantName) >= 4 && plantName == PlantName.HYPNO_SHROOM) ? 1 : 0;
    }
    public static int getExtraSunDrop(PlantName plantName) {
        return (getLevel(plantName) >= 2 && plantName == PlantName.SUN_BEAN) ? 5 : 0;
    }
    public static int getExtraImmediateSun(PlantName plantName) {
        return (getLevel(plantName) >= 3 && plantName == PlantName.GOLD_BLOOM) ? 50 : 0;
    }
    public static int getExtraAoEDamage(PlantName plantName) {
        return (getLevel(plantName) >= 3 && (plantName == PlantName.MELON_PULT || plantName == PlantName.WINTER_MELON)) ? 15 : 0;
    }
    public static int getExtraExplodeDamage(PlantName plantName) {
        return (getLevel(plantName) >= 3 && plantName == PlantName.EXPLODE_O_NUT) ? 200 : 0;
    }
    public static int getExtraReflectDamage(PlantName plantName) {
        return (getLevel(plantName) >= 2 && plantName == PlantName.ENDURIAN) ? 5 : 0;
    }
    public static int getExtraPoisonDamageTick(PlantName plantName) {
        return (getLevel(plantName) >= 2 && plantName == PlantName.GOO_PEASHOOTER) ? 5 : 0;
    }
    public static boolean hasDoubleSunChance(PlantName plantName) {
        return getLevel(plantName) >= 4 && (plantName == PlantName.SUNFLOWER || plantName == PlantName.SUN_SHROOM);
    }
    public static boolean hasTargetPriorityUp(PlantName plantName) {
        return getLevel(plantName) >= 3 && plantName == PlantName.ELECTRIC_BLUEBERRY;
    }
    public static boolean canCrushTwice(PlantName plantName) {
        return getLevel(plantName) >= 4 && plantName == PlantName.SQUASH;
    }
    public static boolean hasPlantFoodOnEntrance(PlantName plantName) {
        return getLevel(plantName) >= 4 && plantName == PlantName.IMITATER;
    }
    public static boolean hasExplodeOnFinish(PlantName plantName) {
        return getLevel(plantName) >= 4 && (plantName == PlantName.HOT_POTATO || plantName == PlantName.GRAVE_BUSTER);
    }
    public static boolean hasMeltArea3x3(PlantName plantName) {
        return getLevel(plantName) >= 3 && plantName == PlantName.HOT_POTATO;
    }
    public static boolean hasAoEOnDeath(PlantName plantName) {
        return getLevel(plantName) >= 3 && plantName == PlantName.TORCHWOOD;
    }
    public static boolean canResetFamilyCooldowns(PlantName plantName) {
        return getLevel(plantName) >= 4 && isMint(plantName);
    }
    private static boolean isMint(PlantName plantName) {
        return switch (plantName) {
            case ENLIGHTEN_MINT, APPEASE_MINT, ARMA_MINT, BOMBARD_MINT, ENFORCE_MINT, REINFORCE_MINT, ENCHANT_MINT, PIERCE_MINT, CAT_TAIL_MINT -> true;
            default -> false;
        };
    }
}
