--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\game\service\quest\GameObserver.java ---
package game.service.quest;
import app.enums.plant.PlantCategory;
import app.enums.plant.PlantName;
import app.enums.plant.PlantTag;
import app.model.App;
import game.entity.plant.Plant;
import game.entity.registry.PlantRegistry;
import game.world.GameMap;
import game.world.playground.Cell;
import game.world.playground.Grid;
import java.util.Objects;
public class GameObserver {
    private final GameMap map;
    private int lostPlantsCount = 0;
    private boolean hasUsedForbiddenFamily = false;
    private int sunProducerCount = 0;
    private boolean killedOnlyWithTargetFamily = true;
    private boolean plantedInForbiddenRow = false;
    private boolean plantedInForbiddenCol = false;
    public GameObserver(GameMap map) {
        this.map = map;
    }
    public void onPlantPlaced(PlantName name, int row, int col) {
        QuestManager questManager = App.getCurrentUser().getQuestManager();
        PlantCategory forbiddenCategory = questManager.getActiveForbiddenCategory();
        PlantCategory hunterCategory = questManager.getActiveHunterCategory();
        PlantCategory category = PlantRegistry.get(name) != null ?
                PlantRegistry.get(name).getCategory() : null;
        if (category != null) {
            if (forbiddenCategory != null && category == forbiddenCategory) {
                this.hasUsedForbiddenFamily = true;
            }
            if (category == PlantCategory.SUN_PRODUCER) {
                this.sunProducerCount++;
            }
            if (hunterCategory != null && category != hunterCategory
                    && category != PlantCategory.SUN_PRODUCER
                    && category != PlantCategory.WALL_NUT) {
                this.killedOnlyWithTargetFamily = false;
            }
        }
        int targetCol = questManager.getActiveTargetCol();
        int targetRow = questManager.getActiveTargetRow();
        if (targetCol != -1 && col == targetCol) {
            this.plantedInForbiddenCol = true;
        }
        if (targetRow != -1 && row == targetRow) {
            this.plantedInForbiddenRow = true;
        }
    }
    public void addLostPlantsCount(int amount) {
        if (amount > 0) {
            this.lostPlantsCount += amount;
        }
    }
    public boolean isSymmetric() {
        if (map == null || map.getGrid() == null) return false;
        Grid grid = map.getGrid();
        int rowCount = grid.getRowCount();
        int colCount = grid.getColCount();
        for (int row = 0; row < rowCount / 2; row++) {
            int oppositeRow = rowCount - 1 - row;
            for (int col = 0; col < colCount; col++) {
                Cell cell1 = grid.getCell(row, col);
                Cell cell2 = grid.getCell(oppositeRow, col);
                if (!arePlantsEqual(cell1, cell2)) {
                    return false;
                }
            }
        }
        return true;
    }
    public boolean isAsymmetricExceptMiddle() {
        if (map == null || map.getGrid() == null) return false;
        Grid grid = map.getGrid();
        int rowCount = grid.getRowCount();
        int colCount = grid.getColCount();
        for (int row = 0; row < rowCount / 2; row++) {
            int oppositeRow = rowCount - 1 - row;
            for (int col = 0; col < colCount; col++) {
                Cell cell1 = grid.getCell(row, col);
                Cell cell2 = grid.getCell(oppositeRow, col);
                if (arePlantsEqual(cell1, cell2)) {
                    return false;
                }
            }
        }
        return true;
    }
    public boolean isRowEmpty(int row) {
        if (map == null || map.getGrid() == null) return true;
        Grid grid = map.getGrid();
        if (row < 0 || row >= grid.getRowCount()) return true;
        for (int col = 0; col < grid.getColCount(); col++) {
            Cell cell = grid.getCell(row, col);
            if (cell != null && cell.getPlant() != null) {
                return false;
            }
        }
        return true;
    }
    public boolean isColumnEmpty(int col) {
        if (map == null || map.getGrid() == null) return true;
        Grid grid = map.getGrid();
        if (col < 0 || col >= grid.getColCount()) return true;
        for (int row = 0; row < grid.getRowCount(); row++) {
            Cell cell = grid.getCell(row, col);
            if (cell != null && cell.getPlant() != null) {
                return false;
            }
        }
        return true;
    }
    public boolean isCrossEmpty(int n) {
        return isRowEmpty(n) && isColumnEmpty(n);
    }
    public boolean allPlantsAreMushrooms() {
        if (map == null || map.getPlants() == null) return true;
        for (Plant plant : map.getPlants()) {
            if (plant != null && plant.getDefinition() != null && plant.getDefinition().getTags() != null) {
                if (!plant.getDefinition().getTags().hasTag(PlantTag.NIGHT)) {
                    return false;
                }
            }
        }
        return true;
    }
    private boolean arePlantsEqual(Cell cell1, Cell cell2) {
        String name1 = getPlantName(cell1);
        String name2 = getPlantName(cell2);
        return Objects.equals(name1, name2);
    }
    private String getPlantName(Cell cell) {
        if (cell == null || cell.getPlant() == null) {
            return null;
        }
        return cell.getPlant().getName();
    }
    public int getLostPlantsCount() { return lostPlantsCount; }
    public boolean hasUsedForbiddenFamily() { return hasUsedForbiddenFamily; }
    public int getSunProducerCount() { return sunProducerCount; }
    public boolean isKilledOnlyWithTargetFamily() { return killedOnlyWithTargetFamily; }
    public boolean isPlantedInForbiddenRow() { return plantedInForbiddenRow; }
    public boolean isPlantedInForbiddenCol() { return plantedInForbiddenCol; }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\game\service\quest\QuestLoad.java ---
package game.service.quest;
import app.model.App;
import java.io.File;
public class QuestLoad {
    private static final String SAVE_FILE_PATH = "quest_progress.json";
    public static void load() {
        QuestSaveService saveService = new QuestSaveService();
        File saveFile = new File(SAVE_FILE_PATH);
        if (saveFile.exists()) {
            QuestManager loadedManager = saveService.loadQuests(SAVE_FILE_PATH);
            if (loadedManager != null &&
                    (!loadedManager.getActiveQuests().isEmpty()
                            || !loadedManager.getCompletedQuests().isEmpty())) {
                App.getCurrentUser().setQuestManager(loadedManager);
                loadedManager.checkDailyReset();
                saveService.saveQuests(loadedManager, SAVE_FILE_PATH);
                return;
            }
        }
        QuestManager questManager = new QuestManager();
        initializeDefaultQuests(questManager);
        questManager.resetDailyQuests();
        App.getCurrentUser().setQuestManager(questManager);
        saveService.saveQuests(questManager, SAVE_FILE_PATH);
    }
    private static void initializeDefaultQuests(QuestManager questManager) {
        questManager.addQuest(QuestFactory.createChapterHunter(1));
        questManager.addQuest(QuestFactory.createEconomicalGardener(3));
        questManager.addQuest(QuestFactory.createQuickAction());
        questManager.addQuest(QuestFactory.createDefenseMaster());
        questManager.addQuest(QuestFactory.createDayOrNight());
        questManager.addQuest(QuestFactory.createLawnMowerTime(10));
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\game\service\quest\QuestManager.java ---
package game.service.quest;
import app.enums.plant.PlantCategory;
import app.enums.plant.PlantName;
import app.enums.quest.QuestCategory;
import app.enums.quest.QuestName;
import app.model.App;
import app.model.News;
import app.model.user.User;
import game.entity.quest.GameBackup;
import game.entity.quest.GameEvent;
import game.entity.quest.Quest;
import game.entity.quest.QuestReward;
import game.world.GameWorld;
import java.time.LocalDate;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.Iterator;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Random;
public class QuestManager {
    private final List<Quest> activeQuests;
    private final List<Quest> completedQuests;
    private LocalDate lastResetDate;
    private final Random random;
    private final QuestSaveService questSaveService;
    public QuestManager() {
        this.activeQuests = new ArrayList<>();
        this.completedQuests = new ArrayList<>();
        this.lastResetDate = LocalDate.now();
        this.random = new Random();
        this.questSaveService = new QuestSaveService();
    }
    public void addQuest(Quest quest) {
        activeQuests.add(quest);
    }
    public void fireEvent(GameEvent event) {
        checkDailyReset();
        Iterator<Quest> iterator = activeQuests.iterator();
        List<Quest> newlyCompleted = new ArrayList<>();
        while (iterator.hasNext()) {
            Quest quest = iterator.next();
            quest.updateProgress(event);
            if (quest.checkAndAdvanceTier()) {
                iterator.remove();
                newlyCompleted.add(quest);
            }
        }
        for (Quest quest : newlyCompleted) {
            completeQuest(quest);
        }
    }
    public void evaluateLevelEnd(GameBackup finalState) {
        checkDailyReset();
        Iterator<Quest> iterator = activeQuests.iterator();
        List<Quest> newlyCompleted = new ArrayList<>();
        while (iterator.hasNext()) {
            Quest quest = iterator.next();
            if (quest.evaluateEndState(finalState)) {
                if (quest.checkAndAdvanceTier()) {
                    iterator.remove();
                    newlyCompleted.add(quest);
                }
            }
        }
        for (Quest quest : newlyCompleted) {
            completeQuest(quest);
        }
    }
    private void completeQuest(Quest quest) {
        completedQuests.add(quest);
        grantReward(quest);
        saveQuests();
        GameWorld gameWorld = App.getCurrentGameEngine().getGameWorld();
        String massage = String.format(">>> %s is complete <<<" , quest.getName().toString());
        if (gameWorld != null) gameWorld.addEvent(massage);
        News news = new News(massage , false);
        App.getCurrentUser().getNewsList().add(news);
    }
    private void grantReward(Quest quest) {
        if (quest == null || quest.getRewards() == null) return;
        User currentUser = App.getCurrentUser();
        if (currentUser == null) return;
        for (QuestReward reward : quest.getRewards()) {
            if (reward == null || reward.type() == null) continue;
            switch (reward.type()) {
                case COIN:
                    currentUser.addCoins(reward.amount());
                    break;
                case GEM:
                    currentUser.addGems(reward.amount());
                    break;
                case SEED:
                    currentUser.addCoins(reward.amount() * 10);
                    break;
                case PLANT_UNLOCK:
                    currentUser.unlockRandomPlant();
                    break;
            }
        }
    }
    public void checkDailyReset() {
        LocalDate today = LocalDate.now();
        if (today.isAfter(lastResetDate)) {
            resetDailyQuests();
            lastResetDate = today;
            saveQuests();
        }
    }
    public void resetDailyQuests() {
        activeQuests.removeIf(quest -> quest.getCategory() == QuestCategory.DAILY);
        completedQuests.removeIf(quest -> quest.getCategory() == QuestCategory.DAILY);
        PlantName randomPlant = getRandomPlantName();
        PlantCategory randomCategoryForLimited = getRandomPlantCategory();
        PlantCategory randomCategoryForHunter = getRandomPlantCategory();
        int randomCol = random.nextInt(9);
        int randomRow = random.nextInt(5);
        int randomCross = random.nextInt(5);
        addQuest(QuestFactory.createDailySunCollector());
        addQuest(QuestFactory.createCactusOnly());
        addQuest(QuestFactory.createDemolitionExpert());
        addQuest(QuestFactory.createSymmetry());
        addQuest(QuestFactory.createLimitedBloom(randomCategoryForLimited));
        addQuest(QuestFactory.createWinStreak());
        addQuest(QuestFactory.createAlmostVictory());
        addQuest(QuestFactory.createOcdMaster());
        addQuest(QuestFactory.createCloudyDay());
        addQuest(QuestFactory.createOneColumnLess(randomCol));
        addQuest(QuestFactory.createDefenselessRow(randomRow));
        addQuest(QuestFactory.createDefenselessCross(randomCross));
        addQuest(QuestFactory.createPlantExpert(randomPlant));
        addQuest(QuestFactory.createFamilyHunter(randomCategoryForHunter));
    }
    private PlantName getRandomPlantName() {
        PlantName[] values = PlantName.values();
        return values[random.nextInt(values.length)];
    }
    private PlantCategory getRandomPlantCategory() {
        PlantCategory[] values = PlantCategory.values();
        return values[random.nextInt(values.length)];
    }
    public PlantName getActiveTargetPlant() {
        for (Quest quest : activeQuests) {
            if (quest.getName() == QuestName.PLANT_EXPERT && quest.getTargetPlant() != null) {
                try {
                    return PlantName.valueOf(quest.getTargetPlant());
                } catch (Exception ignored) {}
            }
        }
        return null;
    }
    public List<Quest> getActiveQuests() {
        return activeQuests;
    }
    public List<Quest> getCompletedQuests() {
        return completedQuests;
    }
    public List<Quest> getQuestsByPriority() {
        List<Quest> sorted = new ArrayList<>(activeQuests);
        sorted.sort((q1, q2) -> q1.getPriority().compareTo(q2.getPriority()));
        return sorted;
    }
    public List<Quest> getQuestsByCategory(QuestCategory category) {
        List<Quest> filtered = new ArrayList<>();
        for (Quest quest : activeQuests) {
            if (quest.getCategory() == category) {
                filtered.add(quest);
            }
        }
        return filtered;
    }
    public LocalDate getLastResetDate() {
        return lastResetDate;
    }
    public void setLastResetDate(LocalDate lastResetDate) {
        this.lastResetDate = lastResetDate;
    }
    public int getActiveTargetCol() {
        for (Quest quest : activeQuests) {
            if (quest.getName() == QuestName.ONE_COLUMN_LESS && quest.getTargetValue() != -1) {
                return quest.getTargetValue();
            }
        }
        return -1;
    }
    public int getActiveTargetRow() {
        for (Quest quest : activeQuests) {
            if (quest.getName() == QuestName.DEFENSELESS_ROW && quest.getTargetValue() != -1) {
                return quest.getTargetValue();
            }
        }
        return -1;
    }
    public PlantCategory getActiveForbiddenCategory() {
        for (Quest quest : activeQuests) {
            if (quest.getName() == QuestName.LIMITED_BLOOM && quest.getTargetCategory() != null) {
                try { return PlantCategory.valueOf(quest.getTargetCategory()); } catch (Exception ignored) {}
            }
        }
        return null;
    }
    public int getActiveTargetCross() {
        for (Quest quest : activeQuests) {
            if (quest.getName() == QuestName.DEFENSELESS_CROSS && quest.getTargetValue() != -1) {
                return quest.getTargetValue();
            }
        }
        return -1;
    }
    public PlantCategory getActiveHunterCategory() {
        for (Quest quest : activeQuests) {
            if (quest.getName() == QuestName.FAMILY_HUNTER && quest.getTargetCategory() != null) {
                try { return PlantCategory.valueOf(quest.getTargetCategory()); } catch (Exception ignored) {}
            }
        }
        return null;
    }
    public QuestSaveService getQuestSaveService() {
        return questSaveService;
    }
    public void saveQuests() {
        this.questSaveService.saveQuests(this);
    }
}

package game.service.quest;
import java.util.List;
public class QuestManagerSaveDTO {
    private String lastResetDate;
    private List<QuestStateDTO> activeQuests;
    private List<QuestStateDTO> completedQuests;
    public QuestManagerSaveDTO() {}
    public QuestManagerSaveDTO(String lastResetDate, List<QuestStateDTO> activeQuests, List<QuestStateDTO> completedQuests) {
        this.lastResetDate = lastResetDate;
        this.activeQuests = activeQuests;
        this.completedQuests = completedQuests;
    }
    public String getLastResetDate() { return lastResetDate; }
    public void setLastResetDate(String lastResetDate) { this.lastResetDate = lastResetDate; }
    public List<QuestStateDTO> getActiveQuests() { return activeQuests; }
    public void setActiveQuests(List<QuestStateDTO> activeQuests) { this.activeQuests = activeQuests; }
    public List<QuestStateDTO> getCompletedQuests() { return completedQuests; }
    public void setCompletedQuests(List<QuestStateDTO> completedQuests) { this.completedQuests = completedQuests; }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\game\service\quest\QuestSaveService.java ---
package game.service.quest;
import app.enums.plant.PlantCategory;
import app.enums.plant.PlantName;
import app.enums.quest.QuestName;
import com.google.gson.Gson;
import com.google.gson.GsonBuilder;
import game.entity.quest.Quest;
import java.io.FileReader;
import java.io.FileWriter;
import java.io.IOException;
import java.time.LocalDate;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;
public class QuestSaveService {
    private static final String DEFAULT_SAVE_PATH = "quest_progress.json";
    private final Gson gson;
    public QuestSaveService() {
        this.gson = new GsonBuilder().setPrettyPrinting().create();
    }
    public void saveQuests(QuestManager manager) {
        saveQuests(manager, DEFAULT_SAVE_PATH);
    }
    public void saveQuests(QuestManager manager, String filePath) {
        if (manager == null || filePath == null) return;
        List<QuestStateDTO> activeDTOs = convertToDTOList(manager.getActiveQuests());
        List<QuestStateDTO> completedDTOs = convertToDTOList(manager.getCompletedQuests());
        String resetDateStr = manager.getLastResetDate() != null ? manager.getLastResetDate().toString() : LocalDate.now().toString();
        QuestManagerSaveDTO saveDTO = new QuestManagerSaveDTO(resetDateStr, activeDTOs, completedDTOs);
        writeJsonToFile(saveDTO, filePath);
    }
    public QuestManager loadQuests() {
        return loadQuests(DEFAULT_SAVE_PATH);
    }
    public QuestManager loadQuests(String filePath) {
        if (filePath == null) return null;
        try (FileReader reader = new FileReader(filePath)) {
            QuestManagerSaveDTO saveDTO = gson.fromJson(reader, QuestManagerSaveDTO.class);
            if (saveDTO == null) return null;
            QuestManager manager = new QuestManager();
            if (saveDTO.getLastResetDate() != null) {
                manager.setLastResetDate(LocalDate.parse(saveDTO.getLastResetDate()));
            }
            populateQuests(manager.getActiveQuests(), saveDTO.getActiveQuests());
            populateQuests(manager.getCompletedQuests(), saveDTO.getCompletedQuests());
            return manager;
        } catch (IOException e) {
            return null;
        }
    }
    private List<QuestStateDTO> convertToDTOList(List<Quest> quests) {
        List<QuestStateDTO> dtos = new ArrayList<>();
        if (quests == null) return dtos;
        for (Quest q : quests) {
            dtos.add(new QuestStateDTO(
                    q.getName().name(),
                    q.getCondition() != null ? q.getCondition().getCurrentProgress() : 0,
                    q.getCurrentTierIndex(),
                    q.isCompleted(),
                    q.getTargetPlant(),
                    q.getTargetCategory(),
                    q.getTargetValue()
            ));
        }
        return dtos;
    }
    private void writeJsonToFile(QuestManagerSaveDTO saveDTO, String filePath) {
        try (FileWriter writer = new FileWriter(filePath)) {
            gson.toJson(saveDTO, writer);
        } catch (IOException e) {
            e.printStackTrace();
        }
    }
    private void populateQuests(List<Quest> targetList, List<QuestStateDTO> dtoList) {
        if (dtoList == null || targetList == null) return;
        for (QuestStateDTO dto : dtoList) {
            Quest quest = recreateQuest(dto);
            if (quest != null) {
                restoreQuestState(quest, dto);
                targetList.add(quest);
            }
        }
    }
    private Quest recreateQuest(QuestStateDTO dto) {
        if (dto == null || dto.getQuestName() == null) return null;
        try {
            QuestName name = QuestName.valueOf(dto.getQuestName());
            switch (name) {
                case DAILY_SUN_COLLECTOR: return QuestFactory.createDailySunCollector();
                case CHAPTER_HUNTER: return QuestFactory.createChapterHunter(dto.getTargetValue() != -1 ? dto.getTargetValue() : 1);
                case PLANT_EXPERT: return QuestFactory.createPlantExpert(parsePlantName(dto.getTargetPlant()));
                case CACTUS_ONLY: return QuestFactory.createCactusOnly();
                case ECONOMICAL_GARDENER: return QuestFactory.createEconomicalGardener(dto.getTargetValue() != -1 ? dto.getTargetValue() : 3);
                case DEFENSE_MASTER: return QuestFactory.createDefenseMaster();
                case QUICK_ACTION: return QuestFactory.createQuickAction();
                case DEMOLITION_EXPERT: return QuestFactory.createDemolitionExpert();
                case SYMMETRY: return QuestFactory.createSymmetry();
                case FAMILY_HUNTER: return QuestFactory.createFamilyHunter(parsePlantCategory(dto.getTargetCategory()));
                case LIMITED_BLOOM: return QuestFactory.createLimitedBloom(parsePlantCategory(dto.getTargetCategory()));
                case DAY_OR_NIGHT: return QuestFactory.createDayOrNight();
                case WIN_STREAK: return QuestFactory.createWinStreak();
                case ALMOST_VICTORY: return QuestFactory.createAlmostVictory();
                case OCD_MASTER: return QuestFactory.createOcdMaster();
                case CLOUDY_DAY: return QuestFactory.createCloudyDay();
                case ONE_COLUMN_LESS: return QuestFactory.createOneColumnLess(dto.getTargetValue() != -1 ? dto.getTargetValue() : 8);
                case DEFENSELESS_ROW: return QuestFactory.createDefenselessRow(dto.getTargetValue() != -1 ? dto.getTargetValue() : 4);
                case DEFENSELESS_CROSS: return QuestFactory.createDefenselessCross(dto.getTargetValue() != -1 ? dto.getTargetValue() : 2);
                case LAWN_MOWER_TIME: return QuestFactory.createLawnMowerTime(dto.getTargetValue() != -1 ? dto.getTargetValue() : 10);
                default: return null;
            }
        } catch (IllegalArgumentException e) {
            return null;
        }
    }
    private PlantName parsePlantName(String raw) {
        if (raw != null) {
            try { return PlantName.valueOf(raw); } catch (Exception ignored) {}
        }
        return PlantName.values()[0];
    }
    private PlantCategory parsePlantCategory(String raw) {
        if (raw != null) {
            try { return PlantCategory.valueOf(raw); } catch (Exception ignored) {}
        }
        return PlantCategory.values()[0];
    }
    private void restoreQuestState(Quest quest, QuestStateDTO dto) {
        for (int i = 0; i < dto.getCurrentTierIndex(); i++) {
            quest.checkAndAdvanceTier();
        }
        if (quest.getCondition() != null && dto.getCurrentProgress() > 0) {
            quest.getCondition().setCurrentProgress(dto.getCurrentProgress());
        }
        if (dto.isCompleted()) {
            quest.checkAndAdvanceTier();
        }
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\game\service\quest\QuestStateDTO.java ---
package game.service.quest;
public class QuestStateDTO {
    private String questName;
    private int currentProgress;
    private int currentTierIndex;
    private boolean completed;
    private String targetPlant;
    private String targetCategory;
    private int targetValue;
    public QuestStateDTO() {}
    public QuestStateDTO(String questName, int currentProgress, int currentTierIndex, boolean completed,
                         String targetPlant, String targetCategory, int targetValue) {
        this.questName = questName;
        this.currentProgress = currentProgress;
        this.currentTierIndex = currentTierIndex;
        this.completed = completed;
        this.targetPlant = targetPlant;
        this.targetCategory = targetCategory;
        this.targetValue = targetValue;
    }
    public String getQuestName() { return questName; }
    public void setQuestName(String questName) { this.questName = questName; }
    public int getCurrentProgress() { return currentProgress; }
    public void setCurrentProgress(int currentProgress) { this.currentProgress = currentProgress; }
    public int getCurrentTierIndex() { return currentTierIndex; }
    public void setCurrentTierIndex(int currentTierIndex) { this.currentTierIndex = currentTierIndex; }
    public boolean isCompleted() { return completed; }
    public void setCompleted(boolean completed) { this.completed = completed; }
    public String getTargetPlant() { return targetPlant; }
    public void setTargetPlant(String targetPlant) { this.targetPlant = targetPlant; }
    public String getTargetCategory() { return targetCategory; }
    public void setTargetCategory(String targetCategory) { this.targetCategory = targetCategory; }
    public int getTargetValue() { return targetValue; }
    public void setTargetValue(int targetValue) { this.targetValue = targetValue; }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\game\service\special_levels\ConveyorBeltService.java ---
package game.service.special_levels;
import app.enums.plant.PlantName;
import game.world.GameWorld;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Random;
public class ConveyorBeltService {
    private final GameWorld world;
    private final List<PlantName> plantPool;
    private final List<PlantName> beltPlants;
    private final Random random;
    private static final int MAX_CAPACITY = 10;
    private int tickInterval = 120;
    public ConveyorBeltService(GameWorld world, List<PlantName> plantPool) {
        this.world = world;
        this.plantPool = new ArrayList<>(plantPool);
        this.beltPlants = new ArrayList<>();
        this.random = new Random();
    }
    public boolean rollPlant() {
        if (beltPlants.size() >= MAX_CAPACITY) {
            return false;
        }
        if (plantPool.isEmpty()) {
            return false;
        }
        PlantName rolledPlant = plantPool.get(random.nextInt(plantPool.size()));
        beltPlants.add(rolledPlant);
        world.addEvent("🎁 [Conveyor Belt] A new " + rolledPlant.toString() + " has arrived!");
        return true;
    }
    public void consumePlant(PlantName plantName) {
        beltPlants.remove(plantName);
    }
    public List<PlantName> getBeltPlants() {
        return beltPlants;
    }
    public int getCapacity() {
        return MAX_CAPACITY;
    }
    public int getTickInterval() {
        return tickInterval;
    }
    public void setTickInterval(int tickInterval) {
        this.tickInterval = tickInterval;
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\game\service\sun\SunCollector.java ---
package game.service.sun;
import app.enums.quest.EventType;
import app.enums.sun.SunState;
import app.model.App;
import common.model.Position;
import game.entity.quest.GameEvent;
import game.entity.sun.Sun;
import game.world.GameWorld;
public class SunCollector {
    private final GameWorld gameWorld;
    public SunCollector(GameWorld gameWorld) {
        this.gameWorld = gameWorld;
    }
    public Sun collect(Position position) {
        for (Sun sun : gameWorld.getSuns()) {
            if (sun.getPosition().equals(position) && sun.isCollectable()) {
                sun.setState(SunState.COLLECTED);
                int value = sun.getValue();
                gameWorld.addSunAmount(value);
                gameWorld.removeSun(sun);
                GameEvent event = new GameEvent(EventType.SUN_COLLECTED, value);
                App.getCurrentUser().getQuestManager().fireEvent(event);
                return sun;
            }
        }
        return null;
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\game\service\sun\SunDropManager.java ---
package game.service.sun;
import app.enums.sun.SunState;
import common.model.Position;
import game.entity.sun.SkySun;
import game.entity.sun.Sun;
import game.world.GameWorld;
public class SunDropManager {
    private final GameWorld gameWorld;
    private final int groundY;
    private final long moveInterval;
    private final long landingInterval;
    public SunDropManager(GameWorld gameWorld) {
        this.gameWorld = gameWorld;
        this.groundY = gameWorld.getGrid().getRowCount() - 1;
        this.moveInterval = 25;
        this.landingInterval = 50;
    }
    public void update(long currentTick) {
        for (Sun sun : gameWorld.getSuns()) {
            if (!(sun instanceof SkySun skySun)) {
                continue;
            }
            if (skySun.getState() == SunState.COLLECTED || skySun.getState() == SunState.ON_GROUND) {
                continue;
            }
            if (currentTick >= skySun.getSpawnTick() + landingInterval && skySun.getState() != SunState.ON_GROUND) {
                skySun.land();
                Position position = skySun.getPosition();
                gameWorld.addEvent(
                        "New " +
                                skySun.getType().name().toLowerCase() +
                                " Sun reached the ground at position (<" +
                                (int) position.getX() +
                                ">, <" +
                                (int) position.getY() +
                                ">)"
                );
                continue;
            }
            if (currentTick - skySun.getLastMoveTick() < moveInterval) {
                continue;
            }
            if ((int) skySun.getPosition().getY() < groundY) {
                skySun.getPosition().setY(skySun.getPosition().getY() + 1);
            }
            skySun.setLastMoveTick(currentTick);
        }
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\game\service\sun\SunSpawner.java ---
package game.service.sun;
import app.enums.sun.SunType;
import common.model.Position;
import game.entity.plant.Plant;
import game.entity.sun.PlantSun;
import game.entity.sun.SkySun;
import game.entity.sun.Sun;
import game.entity.zombie.Zombie;
import game.world.GameWorld;
import java.util.concurrent.ThreadLocalRandom;
public class SunSpawner {
    private final GameWorld gameWorld;
    private long lastSkySunSpawnTime = 0;
    public SunSpawner(GameWorld gameWorld) {
        this.gameWorld = gameWorld;
    }
    public long calculateNextSkySunDelay(long elapsedTime) {
        return (long) Math.max(6 + (0.05 * elapsedTime), 12);
    }
    public Sun spawnSkySun(long currentTick) {
        long delay = calculateNextSkySunDelay(lastSkySunSpawnTime);
        long tickDelay = delay*10;
        if (currentTick - lastSkySunSpawnTime < tickDelay) {
            return null;
        }
        SunType sunType = generateRandomSunType();
        Position position = generateRandomPosition();
        int value = switch (sunType) {
            case NORMAL, RADIOACTIVE -> 25;
            case SPECIAL -> 100;
        };
        Sun sun = new SkySun(sunType, position, value, currentTick);
        gameWorld.addSun(sun);
        gameWorld.addEvent(
                "New " +
                        sunType.name().toLowerCase() +
                        " sun is dropping at position (<" +
                        (int) position.getX() +
                        ">, <" +
                        (int) position.getY() +
                        ">)"
        );
        lastSkySunSpawnTime = currentTick;
        return sun;
    }
    private Position generateRandomPosition() {
        int x = ThreadLocalRandom.current().nextInt(0, 9);
        int y = ThreadLocalRandom.current().nextInt(0, 5);
        return new Position(x, y);
    }
    private SunType generateRandomSunType() {
        int random = ThreadLocalRandom.current().nextInt(100);
        if (random < 80) {
            return SunType.NORMAL;
        }
        if (random < 95) {
            return SunType.SPECIAL;
        }
        return SunType.RADIOACTIVE;
    }
    public Sun spawnPlantSun(Plant plant, int amount) {
        Sun sun = new PlantSun(plant.getPosition(), amount);
        gameWorld.addSun(sun);
        gameWorld.addEvent(
                "plant " +
                        plant.getName() +
                        " produced a sun at (<" +
                        (int) plant.getPosition().getX() +
                        ">, <" +
                        (int) plant.getPosition().getY() +
                        ">)"
        );
        return sun;
    }
    public Sun spawnZombieSun(Zombie zombie, int amount) {
        Sun sun = new PlantSun(zombie.getPosition(), amount);
        gameWorld.addSun(sun);
        gameWorld.addEvent
                ("Zombie " + zombie.getName() +
                " dropped sun at " + zombie.getPosition().toString() +
                " with amount " + amount);
        return sun;
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\game\service\Zombie\WaveService.java ---
package game.service.Zombie;
import app.model.App;
import com.badlogic.gdx.math.Vector2;
import common.model.Position;
import game.entity.wave.Wave;
import game.entity.zombie.Zombie;
import game.entity.zombie.data.properties.Property;
import game.world.GameWorld;
import game.world.chapter.Chapter;
import game.world.playground.Grid;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Random;
import java.util.Vector;
public class WaveService {
    private final GameWorld world;
    private final ZombieService zombieService;
    private final Chapter chapter;
    private final Random random;
    public WaveService(GameWorld world, ZombieService zombieService, Chapter chapter) {
        this.world = world;
        this.zombieService = zombieService;
        this.chapter = chapter;
        this.random = new Random();
    }
    public Wave startNextWave(int waveNumber) {
        Wave wave = new Wave();
        wave.setNumber(waveNumber);
        wave.setFinalWave(waveNumber == getTotalWavesCount());
        double cost = chapter.getWaveFormula().calculateWaveCost(
            waveNumber,
            getTotalWavesCount(),
            App.getCurrentUser().getDifficultyLevel()
        );
        spawnZombiesForWave(cost, wave);
        world.getMap().setCurrentWave(wave);
        return wave;
    }
    public Zombie spawnRandomZombie() {
        List<Property> availableTypes = chapter.getAvailableZombies();
        if (availableTypes.isEmpty()) return null;
        int totalWeight = 0;
        for (Property p : availableTypes) totalWeight += p.getZombieWeight();
        if (totalWeight <= 0) return null;
        int rand = random.nextInt(totalWeight);
        Property selected = null;
        for (Property p : availableTypes) {
            rand -= p.getZombieWeight();
            if (rand < 0) { selected = p; break; }
        }
        if (selected == null) return null;
        List<Vector2> spawnPoints = world.getMap().getZombieSpawns();
        if (spawnPoints.isEmpty()) return null;
        Vector2 spawnPos = spawnPoints.get(random.nextInt(spawnPoints.size()));
        return zombieService.spawnZombie(selected.getAlias(), new Position(spawnPos.x, spawnPos.y));
    }
    private void spawnZombiesForWave(double cost, Wave wave) {
        double accumulatedCost = 0;
        List<Property> availableTypes = chapter.getAvailableZombies();
        while (accumulatedCost < cost) {
            Property selected = selectWeightedRandom(availableTypes);
            if (selected == null) break;
            List<Vector2> spawnPoints = world.getMap().getZombieSpawns();
            Vector2 pos = spawnPoints.get(random.nextInt(spawnPoints.size()));
            Zombie zombie = zombieService.spawnZombie(selected.getAlias(), new Position(pos.x, pos.y));
            if (zombie != null) {
                wave.addZombie(zombie);
                accumulatedCost += selected.getZombieWaveCost();
            }
        }
    }
    private Property selectWeightedRandom(List<Property> types) {
        int total = 0;
        for (Property p : types) total += p.getZombieWeight();
        int rand = random.nextInt(total);
        for (Property p : types) {
            rand -= p.getZombieWeight();
            if (rand < 0) return p;
        }
        return types.get(0);
    }
    public int getTotalWavesCount() {
        return chapter.getTotalWaves();
    }
    public boolean isCurrentWaveFinished() {
        Wave wave = world.getMap().getCurrentWave();
        if (wave == null) return true;
        for (Zombie z : wave.getZombies()) {
            if (!z.isDead() && z.getCurrentHealth() > 0) return false;
        }
        return true;
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\game\service\Zombie\ZombieJsonParserService.java ---
package game.service.Zombie;
import app.enums.zombie.Armor.ArmorFlag;
import app.enums.zombie.Armor.ArmorType;
import app.enums.zombie.ZombieStatTier;
import app.enums.zombie.ZombieStatsType;
import com.badlogic.gdx.Gdx;
import com.google.gson.*;
import common.model.Rect;
import game.entity.registry.ZombieRegistry;
import game.entity.zombie.data.StatsData;
import game.entity.zombie.data.properties.*;
import game.entity.zombie.data.properties.DarkAges.JugglerProperty;
import game.entity.zombie.data.properties.DarkAges.KingProperty;
import game.entity.zombie.data.properties.DarkAges.WizardProperty;
import game.entity.zombie.data.properties.ancientEgypt.ExplorerProperty;
import game.entity.zombie.data.properties.ancientEgypt.RaProperty;
import game.entity.zombie.data.properties.ancientEgypt.TombRaiserProperty;
import game.entity.zombie.data.properties.armor.ArmorProperty;
import game.entity.zombie.data.properties.beach.FishermanProperty;
import game.entity.zombie.data.properties.beach.OctopusProperty;
import game.entity.zombie.data.properties.beach.SnorkelProperty;
import game.entity.zombie.data.properties.iceAge.HunterProperty;
import game.entity.zombie.data.properties.iceAge.IceAgeDodoProperty;
import game.entity.zombie.data.properties.iceAge.TroglobiteProperty;
import java.io.FileReader;
import java.io.Reader;
import java.lang.reflect.Type;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.HashMap;
import java.util.Map;
public class ZombieJsonParserService {
    private static final Map<String, Class<? extends Property>> CLASS_MAP = new HashMap<>();
    static {
        CLASS_MAP.put("ZombiePropertySheet", OrdinaryZombieProperty.class);
        CLASS_MAP.put("ZombieGargantuarProps", GargantuarProperty.class);
        CLASS_MAP.put("ZombieRaProps", RaProperty.class);
        CLASS_MAP.put("ZombieExplorerProps", ExplorerProperty.class);
        CLASS_MAP.put("ZombieTombRaiserProps", TombRaiserProperty.class);
        CLASS_MAP.put("ZombieIceAgeDodoProps", IceAgeDodoProperty.class);
        CLASS_MAP.put("ZombieIceAgeHunterProps", HunterProperty.class);
        CLASS_MAP.put("ZombieIceAgeTroglobiteProps", TroglobiteProperty.class);
        CLASS_MAP.put("ZombieBeachFishermanProps", FishermanProperty.class);
        CLASS_MAP.put("ZombieBeachOctopusProps", OctopusProperty.class);
        CLASS_MAP.put("ZombieBeachSnorkelProps", SnorkelProperty.class);
        CLASS_MAP.put("ZombieDarkJugglerProps", JugglerProperty.class);
        CLASS_MAP.put("ZombieDarkWizardProps", WizardProperty.class);
        CLASS_MAP.put("ZombieDarkKingProps", KingProperty.class);
        CLASS_MAP.put("ZombieModernAllStarProps", AllStarProperty.class);
        CLASS_MAP.put("ZombieLostCityJaneProps", LostCityProperty.class);
        CLASS_MAP.put("ZombieCrystalSkullProps", CrystalSkullProperty.class);
        CLASS_MAP.put("ZombieProspectorProps", ProspectorProperty.class);
        CLASS_MAP.put("ZombiePianoProps", PianoProperty.class);
        CLASS_MAP.put("ZombieNewspaperProps", NewspaperProperty.class);
        CLASS_MAP.put("ZombieArcadeProps", ArcadeProperty.class);
    }
    public static void loadZombiesFromJson(String filePath) {
        Gson gson = new GsonBuilder()
                .registerTypeAdapter(Property.class, new PropertyDeserializer())
                .registerTypeAdapter(Rect.class, new RectDeserializer())
                .registerTypeAdapter(StatsData.class, new StatsDataDeserializer())
                .registerTypeAdapter(ScalingProperty.class, new ScalingPropertyDeserializer())
                .create();
        try (Reader reader = Gdx.files.internal(filePath).reader()){
            JsonArray jsonArray = JsonParser.parseReader(reader).getAsJsonArray();
            for (JsonElement element : jsonArray) {
                Property property = gson.fromJson(element, Property.class);
                if (property != null) {
                    ZombieRegistry.register(property);
                }
            }
        }
        catch (Exception e){
            System.err.println("Failed to read JSON file: " + e.getMessage());
        }
    }
    public static void loadArmorsFromJson(String filePath) {
        Gson gson = new GsonBuilder()
                .registerTypeAdapter(ArmorProperty.class, new ArmorPropertyDeserializer())
                .create();
        try (Reader reader = Gdx.files.internal(filePath).reader()) {
            JsonArray jsonArray = JsonParser.parseReader(reader).getAsJsonArray();
            for (JsonElement element : jsonArray) {
                ArmorProperty armorProperty = gson.fromJson(element, ArmorProperty.class);
                if (armorProperty != null) {
                    ZombieRegistry.register(armorProperty);
                }
            }
        } catch (Exception e) {
            System.err.println("Failed to read armor JSON file: " + e.getMessage());
        }
    }
    private static class PropertyDeserializer implements JsonDeserializer<Property> {
        @Override
        public Property deserialize(JsonElement json, Type typeOfT, JsonDeserializationContext context) throws JsonParseException {
            JsonObject jsonObject = json.getAsJsonObject();
            String objClass = jsonObject.get("objclass").getAsString();
            JsonArray aliasesArray = jsonObject.getAsJsonArray("aliases");
            JsonObject objData = jsonObject.getAsJsonObject("objdata");
            Class<? extends Property> targetClass = CLASS_MAP.get(objClass);
            if (targetClass == null) {
                System.out.println("Warning: Unmapped objclass: " + objClass + ". Skipping.");
                return null;
            }
            Property property = context.deserialize(objData, targetClass);
            if (aliasesArray != null && !aliasesArray.isEmpty()) {
                String alias = aliasesArray.get(0).getAsString();
                property.setAlias(alias);
            }
            return property;
        }
    }
    private static class RectDeserializer implements JsonDeserializer<Rect> {
        @Override
        public Rect deserialize(JsonElement json, Type typeOfT, JsonDeserializationContext context) throws JsonParseException {
            JsonObject obj = json.getAsJsonObject();
            float w = obj.has("mWidth") ? obj.get("mWidth").getAsFloat() : 0;
            float h = obj.has("mHeight") ? obj.get("mHeight").getAsFloat() : 0;
            float x = obj.has("mX") ? obj.get("mX").getAsFloat() : 0;
            float y = obj.has("mY") ? obj.get("mY").getAsFloat() : 0;
            return new Rect(x, y, w, h);
        }
    }
    private static class StatsDataDeserializer implements JsonDeserializer<StatsData> {
        @Override
        public StatsData deserialize(JsonElement json, Type typeOfT, JsonDeserializationContext context) throws JsonParseException {
            StatsData statsData = new StatsData();
            if (json.isJsonArray()) {
                JsonArray array = json.getAsJsonArray();
                for (JsonElement element : array) {
                    JsonObject obj = element.getAsJsonObject();
                    String typeStr = obj.get("Type").getAsString().toUpperCase();
                    String valStr = obj.get("Value").getAsString().toUpperCase();
                    try {
                        ZombieStatsType type = ZombieStatsType.valueOf(typeStr);
                        char tierLevel = valStr.charAt(valStr.length()-1);
                        String enumName = "TIER_" + tierLevel;
                        ZombieStatTier tier = ZombieStatTier.valueOf(enumName);
                        statsData.addStats(type, tier);
                    } catch (IllegalArgumentException e) {
                        System.err.println("Unrecognized Stat Enum: " + typeStr + " or " + valStr);
                    }
                }
            }
            return statsData;
        }
    }
    private static class ScalingPropertyDeserializer implements JsonDeserializer<ScalingProperty> {
        @Override
        public ScalingProperty deserialize(JsonElement json, Type typeOfT, JsonDeserializationContext context) throws JsonParseException {
            ScalingProperty scalingProp = new ScalingProperty();
            return scalingProp;
        }
    }
    private static class ArmorPropertyDeserializer implements JsonDeserializer<ArmorProperty> {
        @Override
        public ArmorProperty deserialize(JsonElement json, Type typeOfT, JsonDeserializationContext context) throws JsonParseException {
            JsonObject jsonObject = json.getAsJsonObject();
            JsonArray aliasesArray = jsonObject.getAsJsonArray("aliases");
            JsonObject objData = jsonObject.getAsJsonObject("objdata");
            if (objData == null) {
                return null;
            }
            ArmorProperty armorProperty = new ArmorProperty();
            if (aliasesArray != null && !aliasesArray.isEmpty()) {
                armorProperty.setAlias(aliasesArray.get(0).getAsString());
            }
            if (objData.has("ArmorType")) {
                armorProperty.setArmorType(ArmorType.valueOf(objData.get("ArmorType").getAsString()));
            }
            if (objData.has("BaseHealth")) {
                armorProperty.setBaseHealth(objData.get("BaseHealth").getAsInt());
            }
            if (objData.has("ArmorFlags")) {
                ArrayList<ArmorFlag> flags = new ArrayList<>();
                for (JsonElement flagElement : objData.getAsJsonArray("ArmorFlags")) {
                    flags.add(ArmorFlag.valueOf(flagElement.getAsString()));
                }
                armorProperty.setArmorFlags(flags);
            }
            return armorProperty;
        }
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\game\service\Zombie\ZombieService.java ---
package game.service.Zombie;
import common.model.Position;
import game.entity.plant.Plant;
import game.entity.zombie.Zombie;
import game.entity.zombie.ZombieFactory;
import game.world.GameWorld;
public class ZombieService {
    private final GameWorld world;
    private final WaveService waveService;
    public ZombieService(GameWorld world) {
        this.world = world;
        waveService = new WaveService(world, this, world.getCurrentChapter());
    }
    public Zombie spawnZombie(String alias, Position position) {
        Zombie newZombie = ZombieFactory.buildZombie(alias, world, position);
        if(newZombie != null) {
            world.addZombie(newZombie);
            world.addEvent("Zombie spawned at position " + position.toString());
            return newZombie;
        }
        return null;
    }
    public Plant getFirstPlantInRow(int lane) {
        Plant target = null;
        for(Plant plant : world.getPlants()) {
            if((int)plant.getPosition().getY() == lane) {
                if(target == null || target.getPosition().getX() < plant.getPosition().getX()) {
                    target = plant;
                }
            }
        }
        return target;
    }
    public GameWorld getWorld() {
        return world;
    }
    public WaveService getWaveService() {
        return waveService;
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\game\world\GameMap.java ---
package game.world;
import app.model.App;
import com.badlogic.gdx.maps.tiled.TiledMap;
import com.badlogic.gdx.math.Vector2;
import game.entity.sun.Sun;
import game.entity.lawnmower.LawnMower;
import game.entity.plant.Plant;
import game.entity.projectile.Projectile;
import game.entity.wave.Wave;
import game.entity.zombie.Zombie;
import game.world.playground.Grid;
import game.world.playground.MapConfig;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;
public class GameMap {
    private final List<Sun> suns;
    private Grid grid;
    private final ArrayList<Plant> plants;
    private final ArrayList<Zombie> zombies;
    private final ArrayList<Zombie> spawnedZombiesDuringGame;
    private final List<Projectile> projectiles;
    private ArrayList<LawnMower> lawnMowers;
    private Wave currentWave;
    private TiledMap tiledMap;
    private MapConfig mapConfig;
    public GameMap() {
        this.suns = new ArrayList<>();
        this.plants = new ArrayList<>();
        this.zombies = new ArrayList<>();
        this.projectiles = new ArrayList<>();
        this.spawnedZombiesDuringGame = new ArrayList<>();
        currentWave = new Wave();
    }
    public void initFromChapter(TiledMap tiledMap, int rows, int cols) {
        this.tiledMap = tiledMap;
        this.mapConfig = MapConfig.fromTiledMap(tiledMap, rows, cols);
        this.grid = new Grid(mapConfig);
        if (!mapConfig.getLawnMowerSpawns().isEmpty()) {
            initLawnMowers();
        }
    }
    private void initLawnMowers() {
        List<Vector2> spawns = mapConfig.getLawnMowerSpawns();
        for (int i = 0; i < spawns.size(); i++) {
            Vector2 pos = spawns.get(i);
            lawnMowers.add(new LawnMower(new Vector2(pos.x, pos.y)));
        }
    }
    public List<Sun> getSuns() {
        return suns;
    }
    public void addSun(Sun sun) {
        suns.add(sun);
    }
    public void removeSun(Sun sun) {
        suns.remove(sun);
    }
    public Grid getGrid() {
        return grid;
    }
    public void setGrid(Grid grid) {
        this.grid = grid;
        this.lawnMowers = new ArrayList<>();
        for (int row = 0; row < grid.getRowCount(); row++) {
            lawnMowers.add(new LawnMower(new Vector2(0, row)));
        }
    }
    public ArrayList<Plant> getPlants() {
        return plants;
    }
    public void addPlant(Plant plant) {
        plants.add(plant);
    }
    public ArrayList<Zombie> getZombies() {
        return zombies;
    }
    public void addZombie(Zombie zombie) {
        spawnedZombiesDuringGame.add(zombie);
    }
    public void flushPendingZombies() {
        if (!spawnedZombiesDuringGame.isEmpty()) {
            zombies.addAll(spawnedZombiesDuringGame);
            for(Zombie zombie : spawnedZombiesDuringGame) {
                App.getRealTimeEngine().getGraphicsEngine().registerZombie(zombie);
            }
            spawnedZombiesDuringGame.clear();
        }
    }
    public List<Projectile> getProjectiles() {
        return projectiles;
    }
    public void addProjectile(Projectile projectile) {
        projectiles.add(projectile);
    }
    public ArrayList<LawnMower> getLawnMowers() {
        return lawnMowers;
    }
    public ArrayList<Zombie> getSpawnedZombiesDuringGame() {
        return spawnedZombiesDuringGame;
    }
    public Wave getCurrentWave() {
        return currentWave;
    }
    public void setCurrentWave(Wave currentWave) {
        this.currentWave = currentWave;
    }
    public TiledMap getTiledMap() {
        return tiledMap;
    }
    public MapConfig getMapConfig() {
        return mapConfig;
    }
    public List<Vector2> getZombieSpawns() {
        return mapConfig.getZombieSpawns();
    }
    public float getLoseLineX() {
        return mapConfig.getLoseLineX();
    }
    public Vector2 getCameraCenter() {
        Vector2 center = mapConfig.getCameraCenter();
        if (center == null) {
            return new Vector2(
                mapConfig.getPlaygroundX() + mapConfig.getPlaygroundWidth() / 2f,
                mapConfig.getPlaygroundY() + mapConfig.getPlaygroundHeight() / 2f
            );
        }
        return center;
    }
    public LawnMower getLawnMowerInRow(int row) {
        for (LawnMower lm : lawnMowers) {
            if (lm.getRow() == row) return lm;
        }
        return null;
    }
    public List<Zombie> getZombiesInRow(int row) {
        List<Zombie> result = new ArrayList<>();
        float cellHeight = mapConfig.getCellHeight();
        float rowTop = mapConfig.getPlaygroundY() + (row + 1) * cellHeight;
        float rowBottom = mapConfig.getPlaygroundY() + row * cellHeight;
        for (Zombie z : zombies) {
            float zY = (float) z.getPosition().getY();
            if (zY >= rowBottom && zY < rowTop) {
                result.add(z);
            }
        }
        return result;
    }
    public boolean hasCrossedLoseLine(Zombie zombie) {
        float loseLine = mapConfig.getLoseLineX();
        if (loseLine == 0) return false;
        return zombie.getPosition().getX() < loseLine;
    }
    public void initFromGrid(TiledMap tiledMap, Grid grid) {
        this.tiledMap = tiledMap;
        this.grid = grid;
        this.mapConfig = grid.getConfig();
        if (!mapConfig.getLawnMowerSpawns().isEmpty()) {
            lawnMowers = new ArrayList<>();
            initLawnMowers();
        }
    }
}

package game.world;
import app.controller.menu.MenuNavigator;
import app.enums.GameState;
import app.enums.Menu;
import app.enums.plant.PlantName;
import app.enums.quest.EventType;
import app.model.App;
import app.model.News;
import game.entity.lawnmower.LawnMower;

public class GameWorld {
    private Season currentSeason;
    private Chapter currentChapter;
    private int sunAmount;
    private GameState gameState;
    private final List<String> events;
    private int currentWaveNumber;
    private Map<PlantName, PlantCooldown> selectedGamePlants;
    private final GameObserver observer;
    private final GameMap map;
    private int difficultyLevel = 1;
    public GameWorld() {
        this.sunAmount = 5000;
        this.map = new GameMap();
        this.gameState = GameState.PLAYING;
        this.events = new ArrayList<>();
        this.currentWaveNumber = 0;
        this.observer = new GameObserver(map);
    }
    public void loadSelectedGamePlants(List<PlantName> selectedPlants) {
        this.selectedGamePlants = new HashMap<>();
        for (PlantName name : selectedPlants)
            selectedGamePlants.put(name, new PlantCooldown(name));
    }
    public PlantCooldown getCoolDownPlant(PlantName name) {
        return selectedGamePlants.getOrDefault(name, null);
    }
    public int getCurrentWaveNumber() {
        return currentWaveNumber;
    }

        this.currentWaveNumber = currentWaveNumber;
    }
    public Season getSeason() {
        return currentSeason;
    }
    public int getSunAmount() {
        return sunAmount;
    }
    public void addSunAmount(int amount) {
        this.sunAmount += amount;
    }
    public void removeSunAmount(int amount) {
        this.sunAmount = Math.max(0, this.sunAmount - amount);
    }
    public List<Sun> getSuns() {
        return map.getSuns();
    }
    public void addSun(Sun sun) {
        map.addSun(sun);
    }
    public void removeSun(Sun sun) {
        map.removeSun(sun);
    }
    public Grid getGrid() {
        return map.getGrid();
    }
    public ArrayList<LawnMower> getLawnMowers() {
        return map.getLawnMowers();
    }
    public ArrayList<Plant> getPlants() {
        return map.getPlants();
    }
    public ArrayList<Zombie> getZombies() {
        return map.getZombies();
    }
    public List<Projectile> getProjectiles() {
        return map.getProjectiles();
    }
    public void addEvent(String message) {
        events.add(message);
    }
    public List<String> getEvents() {
        return events;
    }
    public void clearEventLog() {
        events.clear();
    }
    public void addPlant(Plant plant) {
        map.addPlant(plant);
    }
    public void addZombie(Zombie zombie) {
        map.addZombie(zombie);
    }
    public void flushPendingZombies() {
        map.flushPendingZombies();
    }
    public void addProjectile(Projectile projectile) {
        map.addProjectile(projectile);
    }
    public void setCurrentSeason(Season currentSeason) {
        this.currentSeason = currentSeason;
    }
    public Chapter getCurrentChapter() {
        return currentChapter;
    }
    public void setCurrentChapter(Chapter currentChapter) {
        this.currentChapter = currentChapter;
    }
    public void setGrid(Grid grid) {
        map.setGrid(grid);
    }
    public int getDifficultyLevel() {
        return difficultyLevel;
    }
    public void setDifficultyLevel(int difficultyLevel) {
        this.difficultyLevel = difficultyLevel;
    }
    public void checkZombieWinCondition(Zombie zombie) {
        int lane = (int) zombie.getPosition().getY();
        LawnMower lawnMower = map.getLawnMowerInRow(lane);
        if (lawnMower == null) {
            triggerZombieVictory();
        }
    }
    public void triggerZombieVictory() {
        gameState = GameState.LOST;
        App.getCurrentUser().getQuestManager().fireEvent(new GameEvent(EventType.LEVEL_LOST, 1));
        App.getCurrentUser().getQuestManager().saveQuests();
        addEvent("The zombie ate your brain; LOSER!!!");
        map.getZombies().clear();
        map.getPlants().clear();
        map.getProjectiles().clear();
        MenuNavigator.goTo(Menu.GameMenu);
    }
    public void triggerPlantVictory() {
        gameState = GameState.WON;
        onLevelWin();
        addEvent("Dear humanz, zis is not done yet; we will come back to eat your brainz, humanz.");
        App.getCurrentUser().addNews(
                new News("Congratulations, You completed chapter "
                        + currentChapter.getChapterNumber()
                        + " from season " + currentChapter.getSeason().getName(), false));
        App.getCurrentUser().getGameProgress()
                .markLevelCompleted(
                currentChapter.getSeason().getSeasonType(),
                currentChapter.getChapterNumber()
                );
        MenuNavigator.goTo(Menu.GameMenu);
    }
    public List<Zombie> getZombiesInRow(int row) {
        return map.getZombiesInRow(row);
    }
    public GameState getGameState() {
        return gameState;
    }
    public void setGameState(GameState gameState) {
        this.gameState = gameState;
    }
    public Season getCurrentSeason() {
        return currentSeason;
    }
    public List<PlantName> getSelectedGamePlants() {
        return List.of(selectedGamePlants.keySet().toArray(new PlantName[0]));
    }
    public Map<PlantName, PlantCooldown> getCooldownPlants() {
        return selectedGamePlants;
    }
    public ArrayList<PlantCooldown> getPlantCooldowns() {
        return new ArrayList<>(selectedGamePlants.values());
    }
    public GameObserver getObserver() {
        return observer;
    }
    public GameMap getMap() {
        return map;
    }
    private void onLevelWin() {
        QuestManager questManager = App.getCurrentUser().getQuestManager();
        questManager.fireEvent(new GameEvent(
                EventType.LEVEL_WON,
                1,
                null, null, -1, -1,
                difficultyLevel,
                false
        ));
        int targetCol = questManager.getActiveTargetCol();
        int targetRow = questManager.getActiveTargetRow();
        int targetCrossIndex = questManager.getActiveTargetCross();
        boolean isDay = !isNight();
        GameBackup finalState = new GameBackup(
                sunAmount,
                observer.getLostPlantsCount(),
                observer.isSymmetric(),
                observer.isAsymmetricExceptMiddle(),
                observer.hasUsedForbiddenFamily(),
                observer.allPlantsAreMushrooms(),
                isDay,
                observer.getSunProducerCount(),
                !observer.isPlantedInForbiddenCol() && observer.isColumnEmpty(targetCol),
                !observer.isPlantedInForbiddenRow() && observer.isRowEmpty(targetRow),
                observer.isCrossEmpty(targetCrossIndex),
                observer.isKilledOnlyWithTargetFamily()
        );
        questManager.evaluateLevelEnd(finalState);
        questManager.saveQuests();
    }
    public boolean isConveyorActive() {
        for(ChapterRule rule : currentChapter.getRules()) {
            if(rule instanceof ConveyorBeltRules) {
                return true;
            }
        }
        return false;
    }
    public boolean isNight() {
        for(ChapterRule rule : currentChapter.getRules()) {
            if(rule instanceof NightOpsRules) {
                return true;
            }
        }
        return false;
    }
    public boolean isFinalWave() {
        return map.getCurrentWave().isFinalWave();
    }
    public Wave getCurrentWave() {
        return map.getCurrentWave();
    }
}

package game.world.chapter;
import app.enums.plant.PlantName;
import app.model.user.User;
import com.badlogic.gdx.maps.tiled.TiledMap;
import com.badlogic.gdx.maps.tiled.TmxMapLoader;
import game.entity.zombie.data.properties.Property;
import game.world.chapter.rule.ChapterRule;
import game.world.chapter.wave.WaveConfig;
import game.world.chapter.wave.WaveFormula;
import game.world.minigame.Minigame;
import game.world.playground.GridConfig;
import game.world.seasons.Season;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;
public class Chapter {
    private final int chapterNumber;
    private final ChapterType chapterType;
    private final Season season;
    private final int totalWaves;
    private final GridConfig gridConfig;
    private final List<PlantName> availablePlants;
    private final List<Property> availableZombies;
    private final WaveFormula waveFormula;
    private final WaveConfig waveConfig;
    private final List<ChapterRule> rules;
    private final Minigame minigameLogic;
    private final boolean needsPlantSelection;
    private final int maxPlantsAvailable;
    private String mapPath;
    private TiledMap map;
    public Chapter(int chapterNumber, ChapterType chapterType, Season season, int totalWaves,
                   GridConfig gridConfig, List<PlantName> availablePlants, List<Property> availableZombies,
                   WaveFormula waveFormula, WaveConfig waveConfig,List<ChapterRule> rules, Minigame minigameLogic, boolean needsPlantSelection, String mapPath) {
        this.chapterNumber = chapterNumber;
        this.chapterType = chapterType;
        this.season = season;
        this.totalWaves = totalWaves;
        this.gridConfig = gridConfig;
        this.availablePlants = availablePlants;
        this.availableZombies = availableZombies;
        this.waveFormula = waveFormula;
        this.waveConfig = waveConfig;
        this.rules = rules;
        this.minigameLogic = minigameLogic;
        this.needsPlantSelection = needsPlantSelection;
        this.maxPlantsAvailable = 8;
        this.mapPath = mapPath;
        TmxMapLoader loader = new TmxMapLoader();
        this.map = loader.load(mapPath);
    }
    public int getChapterNumber() {
        return chapterNumber;
    }
    public ChapterType getChapterType() {
        return chapterType;
    }
    public Season getSeason() {
        return season;
    }
    public int getTotalWaves() {
        return totalWaves;
    }
    public GridConfig getGridConfig() {
        return gridConfig;
    }
    public List<PlantName> getAvailablePlants() {
        return availablePlants;
    }
    public List<Property> getAvailableZombies() {
        return availableZombies;
    }
    public WaveFormula getWaveFormula() {
        return waveFormula;
    }
    public List<ChapterRule> getRules() {
        return rules;
    }
    public boolean isLocked(User user) {
        int highestCompleted = user.getGameProgress().getHighestCompletedLevel(season.getSeasonType());
        return chapterNumber > highestCompleted + 1;
    }
    public List<PlantName> getSelectablePlants(User user) {
        List<PlantName> result = new ArrayList<>();
        for (PlantName plant : availablePlants) {
            if (user.isUnlocked(plant)) {
                result.add(plant);
            }
        }
        return result;
    }
    public List<PlantName> getAllUnlockedPlants(User user) {
        return user.getUnlockedPlants();
    }
    public Minigame getMinigameLogic() {
        return minigameLogic;
    }
    public boolean isMinigame() {
        return chapterType == ChapterType.MINIGAME;
    }
    public boolean isNeedsPlantSelection() {
        return needsPlantSelection;
    }
    public int getMaxPlantsAvailable() {
        return maxPlantsAvailable;
    }
    public TiledMap getMap() {
        return map;
    }
    public void setMapPath(TiledMap map) {
        this.map = map;
    }
    public WaveConfig getWaveConfig() {
        return waveConfig;
    }
}

package game.world.chapter;
import app.enums.plant.PlantName;
import game.entity.registry.ZombieRegistry;
import game.entity.zombie.data.properties.Property;
import game.world.chapter.rule.ChapterRule;
import game.world.chapter.rule.speciallevelRules.*;
import game.world.chapter.wave.DefaultWaveFormula;
import game.world.chapter.wave.WaveConfig;
import game.world.chapter.wave.WaveFormula;
import game.world.minigame.Minigame;
import game.world.minigame.izombie.IZombieMode;
import game.world.minigame.scoringgame.ScoringGameMode;
import game.world.minigame.vasebreaker.VaseBreakerMode;
import game.world.minigame.wallnut_bowling.WallNutBowlingMode;
import game.world.playground.GridConfig;
import game.world.seasons.*;
import java.util.List;
public class ChapterFactory {
    public Chapter createAncientEgyptChapter1() {
        Season season = new AncientEgyptSeason();
        return createChapter(
                1,
                ChapterType.NORMAL,
                season,
                2,

    private Chapter createPlaceholder(Season season, int chapterNumber, ChapterType type) {
        return createChapter(
                chapterNumber,
                type,
                season,
                type == ChapterType.BOSS ? 1 : 3,
                GridConfig.defaultConfig(),
                List.of(),
                List.of(),
                new DefaultWaveFormula(),
                List.of(),
                null,
                true
        );
    }
    private Chapter createChapter(int chapterNumber,
                                  ChapterType chapterType,
                                  Season season,
                                  int totalWaves,
                                  GridConfig gridConfig,
                                  List<PlantName> availablePlants,
                                  List<Property> availableZombies,
                                  WaveFormula waveFormula,
                                  List<ChapterRule> rules, Minigame logic, boolean needsPlantSelection) {
        return new Chapter(
                chapterNumber,
                chapterType,
                season,
                totalWaves,
                gridConfig,
                availablePlants,
                availableZombies,
                waveFormula,
                WaveConfig.getDefaultWaveConfig(),
                rules,
                logic,
                needsPlantSelection,
            "Maps/map1.tmx"
        );
    }

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\game\world\chapter\ChapterId.java ---
package game.world.chapter;
import app.enums.SeasonType;
public record ChapterId(SeasonType seasonType, int chapterNumber) {
    public static ChapterId parseNormalChapter(String input) {
        if (input == null) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("Chapter name cannot be null.");
        }
        String[] parts = input.trim().split("\\.");
        if (parts.length != 2) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("Chapter name must be in format season.chapter like 1.1");
        }
        int season = Integer.parseInt(parts[0]);
        SeasonType type;
        switch (season) {
            case 1 -> type = SeasonType.ANCIENT_EGYPT;
            case 2 -> type = SeasonType.DARK_AGES;
            case 3 -> type = SeasonType.BIG_WAVE_BEACH;
            case 4 -> type = SeasonType.FROSTBITE_CAVES;
            default -> throw new IllegalArgumentException("incompatible season type");
        }
        int chapter = Integer.parseInt(parts[1]);
        return new ChapterId(type, chapter);
    }
    public static ChapterId parseMinigameChapter(String name) {
        switch (name) {
            case "VASE_BREAKER" -> {
                return new ChapterId(SeasonType.MINIGAME, 1);
            }
            case "SCORING_GAME" -> {
                return new ChapterId(SeasonType.MINIGAME, 2);
            }
            case "I_ZOMBIE" -> {
                return new ChapterId(SeasonType.MINIGAME, 3);
            }
            case "WALLNUT_BOWLING" -> {
                return new ChapterId(SeasonType.MINIGAME, 4);
            }
            default -> throw new IllegalArgumentException("couldn't find minigame with name : " + name);
        }
    }
    @Override
    public boolean equals(Object o) {
        if (!(o instanceof ChapterId other)) return false;
        return seasonType == other.seasonType && chapterNumber == other.chapterNumber;
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\game\world\chapter\ChapterRegistry.java ---
package game.world.chapter;
import app.enums.SeasonType;
import java.util.HashMap;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.function.Supplier;
public class ChapterRegistry {
    private static final ChapterFactory FACTORY = new ChapterFactory();
    private static final Map<ChapterId, Supplier<Chapter>> CHAPTERS = new HashMap<>();
    static {
        CHAPTERS.put(new ChapterId(SeasonType.ANCIENT_EGYPT, 1), FACTORY::createAncientEgyptChapter1);
        CHAPTERS.put(new ChapterId(SeasonType.ANCIENT_EGYPT, 2), FACTORY::createAncientEgyptChapter2);
        CHAPTERS.put(new ChapterId(SeasonType.ANCIENT_EGYPT, 3), FACTORY::createAncientEgyptChapter3);
        CHAPTERS.put(new ChapterId(SeasonType.ANCIENT_EGYPT, 4), FACTORY::createAncientEgyptChapter4);
        CHAPTERS.put(new ChapterId(SeasonType.DARK_AGES, 1), FACTORY::createDarkAgesChapter1);
        CHAPTERS.put(new ChapterId(SeasonType.DARK_AGES, 2), FACTORY::createDarkAgesChapter2);
        CHAPTERS.put(new ChapterId(SeasonType.DARK_AGES, 3), FACTORY::createDarkAgesChapter3);
        CHAPTERS.put(new ChapterId(SeasonType.DARK_AGES, 4), FACTORY::createDarkAgesChapter4);
        CHAPTERS.put(new ChapterId(SeasonType.BIG_WAVE_BEACH, 1), FACTORY::createBigWaveBeachChapter1);
        CHAPTERS.put(new ChapterId(SeasonType.BIG_WAVE_BEACH, 2), FACTORY::createBigWaveBeachChapter2);
        CHAPTERS.put(new ChapterId(SeasonType.BIG_WAVE_BEACH, 3), FACTORY::createBigWaveBeachChapter3);
        CHAPTERS.put(new ChapterId(SeasonType.BIG_WAVE_BEACH, 4), FACTORY::createBigWaveBeachChapter4);
        CHAPTERS.put(new ChapterId(SeasonType.FROSTBITE_CAVES, 1), FACTORY::createFrostBiteCavesChapter1);
        CHAPTERS.put(new ChapterId(SeasonType.FROSTBITE_CAVES, 2), FACTORY::createFrostBiteCavesChapter2);
        CHAPTERS.put(new ChapterId(SeasonType.FROSTBITE_CAVES, 3), FACTORY::createFrostBiteCavesChapter3);
        CHAPTERS.put(new ChapterId(SeasonType.FROSTBITE_CAVES, 4), FACTORY::createFrostBiteCavesChapter4);
        CHAPTERS.put(new ChapterId(SeasonType.MINIGAME, 1), FACTORY::createVaseBreakerChapter);
        CHAPTERS.put(new ChapterId(SeasonType.MINIGAME, 2), FACTORY::createScoringGameChapter);
        CHAPTERS.put(new ChapterId(SeasonType.MINIGAME, 3), FACTORY::createIZombieChapter);
        CHAPTERS.put(new ChapterId(SeasonType.MINIGAME, 4), FACTORY::createWallNutBowlingChapter);
    }
    private ChapterRegistry() {}
    public static Chapter getChapterByName(String name) {
        ChapterId id = ChapterId.parseNormalChapter(name);
        Supplier<Chapter> supplier = CHAPTERS.get(id);
        if (supplier == null) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("Unknown chapter: " + name);
        }
        return supplier.get();
    }
    public static Chapter getMinigameChapterByName(String minigame) {
        ChapterId id = ChapterId.parseMinigameChapter(minigame);
        Supplier<Chapter> supplier = CHAPTERS.get(id);
        if (supplier == null) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("Unknown Minigame: " + minigame);
        }
        return supplier.get();
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\game\world\chapter\ChapterWorldBuilder.java ---
package game.world.chapter;
import common.model.Position;
import game.world.GameWorld;
import game.world.chapter.rule.ChapterRule;
import game.world.minigame.MinigameWorld;
import game.world.minigame.izombie.IZombieMode;
import game.world.minigame.izombie.IZombieWorld;
import game.world.minigame.scoringgame.ScoringGameMode;
import game.world.minigame.scoringgame.ScoringGameWorld;
import game.world.minigame.vasebreaker.VaseBreakerMode;
import game.world.minigame.vasebreaker.VaseBreakerWorld;
import game.world.minigame.wallnut_bowling.WallNutBowingWorld;
import game.world.minigame.wallnut_bowling.WallNutBowlingMode;
import game.world.playground.Grid;
import game.world.playground.MapConfig;
public class ChapterWorldBuilder {
    public static GameWorld buildWorld(Chapter chapter) {
        GameWorld world = null;
        Grid grid =
            new Grid(
                MapConfig.fromTiledMap(
                    chapter.getMap(),
                    5,
                    9
                )
            );
        chapter.getSeason().setupMap(grid);
        if (chapter.isMinigame() && chapter.getMinigameLogic() != null) {
            MinigameWorld minigameWorld;
            if (chapter.getMinigameLogic() instanceof VaseBreakerMode) {
                minigameWorld = new VaseBreakerWorld(
                        chapter.getMinigameLogic(),
                        chapter.getSeason()
                );
            } else if (chapter.getMinigameLogic() instanceof ScoringGameMode) {
                minigameWorld = new ScoringGameWorld(
                        chapter.getMinigameLogic(),
                        chapter.getSeason()
                );
            } else if (chapter.getMinigameLogic() instanceof IZombieMode) {
                minigameWorld = new IZombieWorld(
                        chapter.getMinigameLogic(),
                        chapter.getSeason(),
                        chapter.getMinigameLogic().getAvailableZombies()
                );
            } else if (chapter.getMinigameLogic() instanceof WallNutBowlingMode) {
                minigameWorld = new WallNutBowingWorld(
                        chapter.getMinigameLogic(),
                        chapter.getSeason()
                );
            } else {
                minigameWorld = new MinigameWorld(
                        chapter.getMinigameLogic(),
                        chapter.getSeason()
                );
            }
            minigameWorld.setCurrentChapter(chapter);
            minigameWorld.setCurrentSeason(chapter.getSeason());
            minigameWorld.setGrid(grid);
            chapter.getMinigameLogic().setWorld(minigameWorld);
            chapter.getMinigameLogic().onMinigameStart(minigameWorld);
            world = minigameWorld;
        } else {
            world = new GameWorld();
            world.setCurrentChapter(chapter);
            world.setCurrentSeason(chapter.getSeason());
            world.setGrid(grid);
        }
        for (ChapterRule rule : chapter.getRules()) {
            rule.onChapterStart(world);
        }
        world.getMap().initFromGrid(
            chapter.getMap(),
            grid
        );
        return world;
    }
}
