--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\game\world\chapter\rule\speciallevelRules\ConveyorBeltRules.java ---
package game.world.chapter.rule.speciallevelRules;
import app.enums.plant.PlantName;
import game.engine.GameSimulationEngine;
import game.engine.system.special_levels.ConveyorBeltSystem;
import game.service.special_levels.ConveyorBeltService;
import game.world.GameWorld;
import game.world.chapter.rule.ChapterRule;
import java.util.List;
public class ConveyorBeltRules implements ChapterRule {
    private final String CONVEYOR_BELT_TEXT ="";
    private final List<PlantName> plantPool;
    private ConveyorBeltService service;
    public ConveyorBeltRules(List<PlantName> plantPool) {
        this.plantPool = plantPool;
    }
    @Override
    public void onChapterStart(GameWorld world) {
        world.loadSelectedGamePlants(List.of());
        this.service = new ConveyorBeltService(world, plantPool);
        this.service.rollPlant();
        world.addEvent(CONVEYOR_BELT_TEXT);
    }
    @Override
    public void onEngineStart(GameSimulationEngine engine) {
        engine.addSystem(new ConveyorBeltSystem(this.service));
    }
    @Override
    public void onTick(GameWorld world, long currentTick) {
    }
    public ConveyorBeltService getService() {
        return service;
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\game\world\chapter\rule\speciallevelRules\DeadLineRules.java ---
package game.world.chapter.rule.speciallevelRules;
import game.engine.GameSimulationEngine;
import game.entity.zombie.Zombie;
import game.world.GameWorld;
import game.world.chapter.rule.ChapterRule;
public class DeadLineRules implements ChapterRule {
    private final int deadLineColumn;
    private final String WARNING_TEXT;
    public DeadLineRules(int deadLineColumn) {
        this.deadLineColumn = deadLineColumn;
        this.WARNING_TEXT =
                "\n╔═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╗\n" +
                        "║                                                                     ║\n" +
                        "║  ☠️   W A R N I N G :  D E A D   L I N E   E N G A G E D   ☠️       ║\n" +
                        "║                                                                     ║\n" +
                        "╠═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╣\n" +
                        "║                                                                     ║\n" +
                        "║  A critical perimeter has been established on the lawn!             ║\n" +
                        "║                                                                     ║\n" +
                        "║  [ TACTICAL RULES ]                                                 ║\n" +
                        "║  🛑 THE RED LINE: Do not let ANY zombie cross Column " + deadLineColumn + ".             ║\n" +
                        "║  ⚠️ INSTANT FAILURE: If a single zombie steps past this boundary,   ║\n" +
                        "║     the defense is breached and you will instantly lose the game.   ║\n" +
                        "║  🛡️ STRATEGY: Plant heavy defenses ahead of the line!               ║\n" +
                        "║                                                                     ║\n" +
                        "║  Hold the line at all costs. Good luck.                             ║\n" +
                        "║                                                                     ║\n" +
                        "╚═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╝";
    }
    @Override
    public void onChapterStart(GameWorld world) {
        world.addEvent(WARNING_TEXT);
    }
    @Override
    public void onTick(GameWorld world, long currentTick) {
        for (Zombie zombie : new java.util.ArrayList<>(world.getZombies())) {
            double zombieX = zombie.getPosition().getX();
            if (zombieX < deadLineColumn * world.getGrid().getConfig().getCellWidth()) {
                world.addEvent("A zombie crossed the Dead Line! GAME OVER!");
                world.triggerZombieVictory();
                return;
            }
        }
    }
    @Override
    public void onEngineStart(GameSimulationEngine engine) {
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\game\world\chapter\rule\speciallevelRules\LockedPlantsRules.java ---
package game.world.chapter.rule.speciallevelRules;
import app.enums.plant.PlantName;
import game.engine.GameSimulationEngine;
import game.world.GameWorld;
import game.world.chapter.rule.ChapterRule;
import java.util.List;

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\game\world\chapter\rule\speciallevelRules\TimedWarRules.java ---
package game.world.chapter.rule.speciallevelRules;
import game.engine.GameSimulationEngine;
import game.entity.zombie.Zombie;
import game.world.GameWorld;
import game.world.chapter.rule.ChapterRule;
import java.util.HashSet;
import java.util.Set;
public class TimedWarRules implements ChapterRule {
    private final int totalSecondsLimit;
    private final int targetKillCount;
    private long startTick = -1;
    private int zombiesKilled = 0;
    private final Set<Zombie> countedDeadZombies = new HashSet<>();
    public TimedWarRules(int secondsLimit, int targetKillCount) {
        this.totalSecondsLimit = secondsLimit;
        this.targetKillCount = targetKillCount;
    }
    @Override
    public void onChapterStart(GameWorld world) {
        String introMessage =
                "\n╔═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╗\n" +
                        "║                                                                     ║\n" +
                        "║  ⏳  T I M E D   W A R   A C T I V E  ⏳                            ║\n" +
                        "║                                                                     ║\n" +
                        "╠═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╣\n" +
                        "║                                                                     ║\n" +
                        "║  Defeat " + targetKillCount + " zombies before time runs out!                     ║\n" +
                        "║                                                                     ║\n" +
                        "║  ⏱️ TIME LIMIT: " + totalSecondsLimit + " seconds                                          ║\n" +
                        "║  💀 TARGET KILLS: " + targetKillCount + " zombies                                       ║\n" +
                        "║                                                                     ║\n" +
                        "╚═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╝";
        world.addEvent(introMessage);
    }
    @Override
    public void onTick(GameWorld world, long currentTick) {
        if (startTick == -1) {
            startTick = currentTick;
        }
        long ticksElapsed = currentTick - startTick;
        long secondsElapsed = ticksElapsed / 10;
        long secondsRemaining = totalSecondsLimit - secondsElapsed;
        for (Zombie zombie : world.getZombies()) {
            if (zombie.isDead() && !countedDeadZombies.contains(zombie)) {
                countedDeadZombies.add(zombie);
                zombiesKilled++;
            }
        }
        if (zombiesKilled >= targetKillCount) {
            world.addEvent("🏆 Target reached! Defeated " + zombiesKilled + "/" + targetKillCount + " zombies!");
            world.triggerPlantVictory();
            return;
        }
        if (secondsRemaining <= 0) {
            world.addEvent("💀 TIME IS UP! You failed to defeat the target number of zombies! 💀");
            world.triggerZombieVictory();
            return;
        }
        if (ticksElapsed % 10 == 0) {
            String status = String.format("⏱️ Time Left: %ds | 💀 Kills: %d/%d",
                    secondsRemaining, zombiesKilled, targetKillCount);
            world.addEvent(status);
        }
    }
    @Override
    public void onEngineStart(GameSimulationEngine engine) {}
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\game\world\minigame\izombie\IZombieMode.java ---
package game.world.minigame.izombie;
import app.enums.plant.PlantName;
import common.model.Position;
import game.engine.GameSimulationEngine;
import game.engine.system.lawnmower.LawnMowerSimulationSystem;
import game.engine.system.sun.SunSimulationSystem;
import game.engine.system.wave.WaveSimulationSystem;
import game.entity.plant.Plant;
import game.entity.zombie.Zombie;
import game.entity.zombie.ZombieFactory;
import game.entity.zombie.ability.SunProductionAbility;
import game.entity.zombie.strategy.movement.StationaryMovement;
import game.service.plant.PlantFactory;
import game.world.GameWorld;
import game.world.minigame.Minigame;
import game.world.playground.Cell;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Random;
public class IZombieMode implements Minigame {
    private final List<PlantName> availablePlants;
    private final List<String> availableZombieIds;
    private final Random random = new Random();
    private IZombieWorld world;
    private final int RED_LINE;
    private boolean spawnedRandomPlant;
    private List<Zombie> sunProducerZombies;
    public IZombieMode(List<PlantName> availablePlants, List<String> availableZombieIds, int redline) {
        this.availablePlants = availablePlants;
        this.availableZombieIds = availableZombieIds;
        this.RED_LINE = redline;
        this.spawnedRandomPlant = false;
        this.sunProducerZombies = new ArrayList<>();
    }
    @Override
    public void onMinigameStart(GameWorld world) {
        world.addEvent("-----I ZOMBIE MINIGAME STARTED-----");
    }
    private Plant spawnRandomPlant(Position position) {
        PlantName plantName = availablePlants.get(random.nextInt(availablePlants.size()));
        return PlantFactory.create(plantName , position , 0);
    }
    @Override
    public void onTick(GameWorld world, long currentTick) {
        if(!spawnedRandomPlant) {
            IZombieWorld zWorld = (IZombieWorld) world;
            for (int r = 0; r < 5; r++) {
                for (int c = 0; c < RED_LINE; c++) {
                    Position position = new Position(c,r);
                    Plant plant = spawnRandomPlant(position);
                    Cell cell = zWorld.getGrid().getCellAt(position);
                    cell.setPlant(plant);
                    zWorld.addPlant(plant);
                }
            }
            for(int r = 0; r < 5; r++) {
                Zombie zombie = ZombieFactory.buildZombie("ZombieDefault", zWorld, new Position(8,r));
                zombie.addAbility(new SunProductionAbility(world));
                zombie.setMovementStrategy(new StationaryMovement());
                zWorld.addZombie(zombie);
                sunProducerZombies.add(zombie);
            }
            spawnedRandomPlant = true;
        }
        for(Zombie zombie : sunProducerZombies) {
            if(!zombie.isDead())return;
        }
        if(world.getSunAmount() < 100) {
            world.addEvent("!LOSER!");
            world.triggerZombieVictory();
        }
    }
    @Override
    public void onEngineStart(GameSimulationEngine engine) {
        engine.removeSystem(SunSimulationSystem.class);
        engine.removeSystem(WaveSimulationSystem.class);
        engine.removeSystem(LawnMowerSimulationSystem.class);
    }
    @Override
    public void setWorld(GameWorld world) {
        this.world = (IZombieWorld) world;
    }
    @Override
    public List<PlantName> getPlants() {
        return List.of();
    }
    @Override
    public List<String> getAvailableZombies() {
        return availableZombieIds;
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\game\world\minigame\scoringgame\patterns\ExactDamagePattern.java ---
package game.world.minigame.scoringgame.patterns;
import app.model.App;
import game.entity.zombie.Zombie;
import game.world.minigame.scoringgame.ScoringGameWorld;
public class ExactDamagePattern implements ScoringPattern{
    @Override
    public String getName() { return "Exact Damage Kill!"; }
    @Override
    public int getMiopoints() { return 50; }
    @Override
    public void processTick(ScoringGameWorld world, long currentTick) {
        for (Zombie z : world.getZombies()) {
            if (z.isDead() && world.getZombieHealthSnapshot().containsKey(z)) {
                double lastHealth = world.getZombieHealthSnapshot().get(z);
                if (lastHealth > 0 && z.getCurrentHealth() == 0) {
                    trigger(world);
                }
            }
            world.getZombieHealthSnapshot().put(z, z.getCurrentHealth());
        }
    }
    private void trigger(ScoringGameWorld world) {
        world.addEvent(getName());
        App.getCurrentUser().addMioPoint(getMiopoints());
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\game\world\minigame\scoringgame\patterns\LastStandRecoveryPattern.java ---
package game.world.minigame.scoringgame.patterns;
import app.model.App;
import game.entity.zombie.Zombie;
import game.world.minigame.scoringgame.ScoringGameWorld;
public class LastStandRecoveryPattern implements ScoringPattern{
    @Override
    public String getName() { return "Last Stand Recovery!"; }
    @Override
    public int getMiopoints() { return 100; }
    @Override
    public void processTick(ScoringGameWorld world, long currentTick) {
        for (Zombie z : world.getZombies()) {
            if (z.isDead()) {
                int col = world.getGrid().getCellAt(z.getPosition()).getCol();
                if (col == 0) {
                    world.addEvent(getName());
                    App.getCurrentUser().addMioPoint(getMiopoints());
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

        }
    }

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\game\world\minigame\vasebreaker\Vase.java ---
package game.world.minigame.vasebreaker;
import app.enums.plant.PlantName;
import common.model.Position;
import game.entity.obstacle.Obstacle;
import game.service.minigame.VaseBreakerService;
public class Vase extends Obstacle {
    private boolean broken = false;
    private PlantName containedPlant;
    private String containedZombieAlias;
    private Position position;
    private boolean spawned;
    private VaseType vaseType;
    public Vase(PlantName plant, Position position) {
        super(position, "Vase");
        this.containedPlant = plant;
        this.vaseType = VaseType.PLANT_VASE;
        this.position = position;
        this.spawned = false;
    }
    public Vase(String zombieAlias, Position position) {
        super(position, "Vase");
        this.containedZombieAlias = zombieAlias;
        this.vaseType = VaseType.GHOUL_VASE;
        this.position = position;
        this.spawned = false;
    }
    public void breakVase() {
        this.broken = true;
    }
    public boolean isBroken() { return broken; }
    public void update(long currentTick, VaseBreakerService service) {
        if(!spawned) {
            if(broken) {
                service.breakVase(this);
                spawned = true;
            }
        }
    }
    public PlantName getContainedPlantName() {
        return containedPlant;
    }
    public String getContainedZombieAlias() {
        return containedZombieAlias;
    }
    public Position getPosition() {
        return position;
    }
    public void setPosition(Position position) {
        this.position = position;
    }
    public VaseType getVaseType() {
        return vaseType;
    }
    public void setVaseType(VaseType vaseType) {
        this.vaseType = vaseType;
    }
    @Override
    public void onHit(long currentTick, int damage) {
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\game\world\minigame\vasebreaker\VaseBreakerInventoryPlant.java ---
package game.world.minigame.vasebreaker;
import app.enums.plant.PlantName;
public class VaseBreakerInventoryPlant {
    private final PlantName plantName;
    private final long addedTick;
    public static final long LIFETIME_TICKS = 50;
    public VaseBreakerInventoryPlant(PlantName plantName, long addedTick) {
        this.plantName = plantName;
        this.addedTick = addedTick;
    }
    public PlantName getPlantName() {
        return plantName;
    }
    public long getAddedTick() {
        return addedTick;
    }
    public boolean isExpired(long currentTick) {
        return currentTick - addedTick >= LIFETIME_TICKS;
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\game\world\minigame\vasebreaker\VaseBreakerMode.java ---
package game.world.minigame.vasebreaker;
import app.enums.plant.PlantName;
import common.model.Position;
import game.engine.GameSimulationEngine;
import game.engine.system.lawnmower.LawnMowerSimulationSystem;
import game.engine.system.minigame.VaseBreakerSystem;
import game.engine.system.sun.SunSimulationSystem;
import game.engine.system.wave.WaveSimulationSystem;
import game.world.GameWorld;
import game.world.minigame.Minigame;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Random;
public class VaseBreakerMode implements Minigame {
    private final List<PlantName> availablePlants;
    private final List<String> availableZombieIds;
    private final Random random = new Random();
    private VaseBreakerWorld world;
    public VaseBreakerMode(List<PlantName> availablePlants, List<String> availableZombieIds) {
        this.availablePlants = availablePlants;
        this.availableZombieIds = availableZombieIds;
    }
    @Override
    public void onMinigameStart(GameWorld world) {
        VaseBreakerWorld vbWorld = (VaseBreakerWorld) world;
        for (int r = 0; r < 5; r++) {
            for (int c = 4; c < 9; c++) {
                Vase v = spawnRandomVase(new Position(c,r));
                vbWorld.addVase(v);
            }
        }
        world.addEvent(
                        "╔══════════════════════════════════════════════╗\n" +
                        "║           🏺 VASEBREAKER RULES 🏺            ║\n" +
                        "╠══════════════════════════════════════════════╣\n" +
                        "║ 🚫☀️ No sun falls from the sky!              ║\n" +
                        "║ 🔨🏺 Smash vases for Plants 🌱 or Zombies 🧟!║\n" +
                        "║ ⏱️🌱 Plant seeds QUICKLY before they vanish! ║\n" +
                        "║ ⚠️👾 Beware the Purple Vases (Gargantuar)!   ║\n" +
                        "╚══════════════════════════════════════════════╝"
        );
    }
    private Vase spawnRandomVase(Position position) {
        int chance = random.nextInt(100);
        if (chance < 10) return new Vase("ZombieGargantuar", position);
        if (chance < 40) return new Vase(availablePlants.get(random.nextInt(availablePlants.size())), position);
        Vase vase;
        vase = random.nextBoolean() ?
                new Vase(availableZombieIds.get(random.nextInt(availableZombieIds.size())), position) :
                new Vase(availablePlants.get(random.nextInt(availablePlants.size())), position);
        vase.setVaseType(VaseType.RANDOM_VASE);
        return vase;
    }
    @Override
    public void onTick(GameWorld world, long currentTick) {
        this.world.removeExpiredPlants(currentTick);
        if(isAllVasesBroken() &&
                world.getZombies().isEmpty() &&
                world.getMap().getSpawnedZombiesDuringGame().isEmpty()) {
            world.triggerPlantVictory();
        }
    }
    @Override
    public void onEngineStart(GameSimulationEngine engine) {
        engine.removeSystem(SunSimulationSystem.class);
        engine.removeSystem(WaveSimulationSystem.class);
        engine.removeSystem(LawnMowerSimulationSystem.class);
        engine.addSystem(new VaseBreakerSystem(world));
    }
    @Override
    public void setWorld(GameWorld world) {
        this.world = (VaseBreakerWorld) world;
    }
    @Override
    public List<PlantName> getPlants() {
        return List.of();
    }
    @Override
    public List<String> getAvailableZombies() {
        return List.of();
    }
    private boolean isAllVasesBroken() {
        for(Vase vase : world.getVases()) {
            if(!vase.isBroken()) {
                return false;
            }
        }
        return true;
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\game\world\minigame\vasebreaker\VaseBreakerWorld.java ---
package game.world.minigame.vasebreaker;
import app.enums.plant.PlantName;
import game.entity.zombie.Zombie;
import game.world.minigame.Minigame;
import game.world.minigame.MinigameWorld;
import game.world.playground.Cell;
import game.world.seasons.Season;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.Iterator;
import java.util.List;
public class VaseBreakerWorld extends MinigameWorld {
    private final List<Vase> vases = new ArrayList<>();
    private final List<VaseBreakerInventoryPlant> plantInventory;
    public VaseBreakerWorld(Minigame logic, Season season) {
        super(logic, season);
        plantInventory = new ArrayList<>();
    }
    public List<Vase> getVases() {
        return vases;
    }
    public void addPlantToInventory(PlantName plantName, long currentTick) {
        plantInventory.add(new VaseBreakerInventoryPlant(plantName, currentTick));
    }
    public List<VaseBreakerInventoryPlant> getPlantInventory() {
        return plantInventory;
    }
    public boolean hasPlantInInventory(PlantName name) {
        for (VaseBreakerInventoryPlant entry : plantInventory) {
            if (entry.getPlantName() == name) {
                return true;
            }
        }
        return false;
    }
    public boolean removePlantFromInventory(PlantName name) {
        for (int i = 0; i < plantInventory.size(); i++) {
            if (plantInventory.get(i).getPlantName() == name) {
                plantInventory.remove(i);
                return true;
            }
        }
        return false;
    }
    public void removeExpiredPlants(long currentTick) {
        Iterator<VaseBreakerInventoryPlant> iterator = plantInventory.iterator();
        while (iterator.hasNext()) {
            VaseBreakerInventoryPlant entry = iterator.next();
            if (entry.isExpired(currentTick)) {
                iterator.remove();
                addEvent(entry.getPlantName().name() + " disappeared from inventory after 50 ticks.");
            }
        }
    }
    public void addVase(Vase vase) {
        vases.add(vase);
        Cell vaseCell = getGrid().getCellAt(vase.getPosition());
        vaseCell.setObstacle(vase);
    }
    @Override
    public void checkZombieWinCondition(Zombie zombie) {
        addEvent("Brainzzz! zombies Ate YOUR BRAIN");
        triggerZombieVictory();
    }
}

package game.world.minigame.wallnut_bowling;
import app.enums.plant.PlantName;
import game.engine.GameSimulationEngine;
import game.engine.system.lawnmower.LawnMowerSimulationSystem;
import game.entity.plant.Plant;
import game.world.GameWorld;
import game.world.minigame.Minigame;
import game.world.minigame.wallnut_bowling.entity.ExplodeONutBowling;
import game.world.minigame.wallnut_bowling.entity.TallNutBowling;
import game.world.minigame.wallnut_bowling.entity.WallNutBowling;
import game.world.playground.Cell;
import java.util.List;
public class WallNutBowlingMode implements Minigame {
    private WallNutBowingWorld world;
    @Override
    public void onMinigameStart(GameWorld world) {
        world.addEvent("WELCOME TO WALLNUT BOWLING! Stay behind the Red Line!");
    }
    @Override
    public void onTick(GameWorld world, long currentTick) {
        List<Plant> plants = world.getPlants();
        for (int i = 0; i < plants.size(); i++) {
            Plant plant = plants.get(i);
            Plant bowlingPlant = convertToBowlingPlant(plant);
            if (bowlingPlant != null) {
                plants.set(i, bowlingPlant);
                System.out.println("Converted " + plant.getName() + " to bowling version.");
            }
        }
    }
    private Plant convertToBowlingPlant(Plant plant) {
        if (plant instanceof WallNutBowling
                || plant instanceof TallNutBowling
                || plant instanceof ExplodeONutBowling) {
            return null;
        }
        Plant bowlingPlant = switch (plant.getDefinition().getName()) {
            case WALL_NUT -> new WallNutBowling();
            case TALL_NUT -> new TallNutBowling();
            case EXPLODE_O_NUT -> new ExplodeONutBowling();
            default -> null;
        };
        if (bowlingPlant == null) {
            return null;
        }
        bowlingPlant.setDefinition(plant.getDefinition());
        bowlingPlant.setHealth(plant.getHealth());
        bowlingPlant.setPosition(plant.getPosition());
        bowlingPlant.setState(plant.getState());
        bowlingPlant.setPlantedTick(plant.getPlantedTick());
        bowlingPlant.setBoosted(plant.isBoosted());
        bowlingPlant.setAbilities(plant.getAbilities());
        Cell currentCell = world.getGrid().getCellAt(plant.getPosition());
        currentCell.setPlant(bowlingPlant);
        return bowlingPlant;
    }
    @Override
    public void onEngineStart(GameSimulationEngine engine) {
        engine.removeSystem(LawnMowerSimulationSystem.class);
    }
    @Override
    public void setWorld(GameWorld world) {
        this.world = (WallNutBowingWorld) world;
    }
    @Override public List<PlantName> getPlants() { return List.of(); }
    @Override public List<String> getAvailableZombies() { return List.of("ZombieDefault"); }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\game\world\minigame\wallnut_bowling\entity\BowlingMovement.java ---
package game.world.minigame.wallnut_bowling.entity;
import game.entity.plant.Plant;
import game.entity.zombie.Zombie;
import game.service.plant.PlantService;
public interface BowlingMovement {
    double getSpeed();
    void move(long currentTick, PlantService plantService);
    void onHit(PlantService service, long currentTick);
    double getDamage();
    default boolean isColliding(Zombie z, Plant p) {
        return Math.abs(z.getPosition().getX() - p.getPosition().getX()) < 0.5 &&
                Math.abs(z.getPosition().getY() - p.getPosition().getY()) < 0.5;
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\game\world\minigame\wallnut_bowling\entity\ExplodeONutBowling.java ---
package game.world.minigame.wallnut_bowling.entity;
import common.model.Position;
import game.entity.plant.Plant;
import game.entity.plant.ability.PlantAbility;
import game.entity.zombie.Zombie;
import game.service.plant.PlantService;
import game.world.GameWorld;
import game.world.playground.Cell;
import game.world.playground.Grid;
public class ExplodeONutBowling extends Plant implements BowlingMovement{
    @Override
    public double getDamage() {
        return 0;
    }
    @Override
    public double getSpeed() {
        return 0.5;
    }
    @Override
    public void move(long currentTick, PlantService plantService) {
        GameWorld world = plantService.getGameWorld();
        Grid grid = world.getGrid();
        if (grid.isInsideGrid(getPosition())) {
            Cell currentCell = grid.getCellAt(getPosition());
            if (currentCell != null && currentCell.getPlant() == this) {
                currentCell.setPlant(null);
            }
        }
        Position newPosition = new Position(getPosition().getX() + getSpeed() / 10.0, getPosition().getY());
        setPosition(newPosition);
        if (grid.isInsideGrid(getPosition())) {
            Cell nextCell = grid.getCellAt(getPosition());
            if (nextCell != null && nextCell.getPlant() == null) {
                nextCell.setPlant(this);
            }
        }
    }
    @Override
    public void onHit(PlantService service, long currentTick) {
        GameWorld world = service.getGameWorld();
        for (Zombie zombie : world.getZombies()) {
            if (isColliding(zombie,this)) {
                for (PlantAbility ability : getAbilities())
                {
                    ability.update(this , service , currentTick);
                }
                markDead(world);
            }
        }
        if (getPosition().getX() > world.getGrid().getPlaygroundWidth()) markDead(world);
    }
    @Override
    public void update(PlantService plantService, long currentTick) {
        move(currentTick, plantService);
        onHit(plantService,currentTick);
    }
}

            }
        }
        Position newPosition = new Position(getPosition().getX() + getSpeed() / 10.0, getPosition().getY());
        setPosition(newPosition);
        if (grid.isInsideGrid(getPosition())) {
            Cell nextCell = grid.getCellAt(getPosition());
            if (nextCell != null && nextCell.getPlant() == null) {
                nextCell.setPlant(this);
            }
        }
    }
    @Override
    public void update(PlantService plantService, long currentTick) {
        move(currentTick, plantService);
        onHit(plantService, currentTick);
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\game\world\minigame\wallnut_bowling\entity\WallNutBowling.java ---
package game.world.minigame.wallnut_bowling.entity;
import common.model.Position;
import game.entity.plant.Plant;
import game.entity.zombie.Zombie;
import game.service.plant.PlantService;
import game.world.GameWorld;
import game.world.playground.Cell;
import game.world.playground.Grid;
public class WallNutBowling extends Plant implements BowlingMovement{
    private int bounceCount = 0;
    private double speedY = 0;
    @Override
    public double getDamage() {
        return 300;
    }
    @Override
    public double getSpeed() {
        return 0.5;
    }
    @Override
    public void onHit(PlantService service, long currentTick) {
        GameWorld world = service.getGameWorld();
        for (Zombie zombie : world.getZombies()) {
            if (isColliding(zombie,this)) {
                zombie.getTakingDamageStrategy().takeDamage(zombie, currentTick,getDamage());
                handleBounce();
                break;
            }
        }
        if (getPosition().getX() > world.getGrid().getPlaygroundWidth()) markDead(world);
    }
    @Override
    public void move(long currentTick, PlantService plantService) {
        GameWorld world = plantService.getGameWorld();
        Grid grid = world.getGrid();
        if (grid.isInsideGrid(getPosition())) {
            Cell currentCell = grid.getCellAt(getPosition());
            if (currentCell != null && currentCell.getPlant() == this) {
                currentCell.setPlant(null);
            }
        }
        Position newPosition = new Position(getPosition().getX() + getSpeed() / 10.0, getPosition().getY() + speedY / 10.0f);
        setPosition(newPosition);
        double gridHeight = plantService.getGameWorld().getGrid().getPlaygroundHeight();
        if (getPosition().getY() <= 0 || getPosition().getY() >= gridHeight) {
            speedY = -speedY;
            setPosition(new Position(
                    getPosition().getX(),
                    Math.max(0.1, Math.min(gridHeight - 0.1, getPosition().getY()))
            ));
        }
        if (grid.isInsideGrid(getPosition())) {
            Cell nextCell = grid.getCellAt(getPosition());
            if (nextCell != null && nextCell.getPlant() == null) {
                nextCell.setPlant(this);
            }
        }
    }
    private void handleBounce() {
        if (bounceCount == 0) {
            speedY = (Math.random() > 0.5) ? 0.5 : -0.5;
        } else {
            speedY = -speedY;
        }
        bounceCount++;
    }
    @Override
    public void update(PlantService plantService, long currentTick) {
        move(currentTick, plantService);
        onHit(plantService, currentTick);
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\game\world\playground\Cell.java ---
package game.world.playground;
import com.badlogic.gdx.math.Rectangle;
import com.badlogic.gdx.math.Vector2;
import game.entity.obstacle.Obstacle;
import game.entity.plant.Plant;
import game.entity.plant.PlantDefinition;
import game.entity.zombie.Zombie;
import game.world.playground.celltypes.CellType;
import game.world.playground.celltypes.WaterCellType;
import java.util.List;
public class Cell {
    private final int row;
    private final int col;
    private final Rectangle bounds;
    private final Vector2 worldPosition;
    private CellType cellType;
    private Obstacle obstacle;
    private Plant currentPlant;
    public Cell(
        int row,
        int col,
        float x,
        float y,
        float width,
        float height
    ) {
        this.row = row;
        this.col = col;
        this.bounds = new Rectangle(x, y, width, height);
        this.worldPosition = new Vector2(
            x + width / 2f,
            y + height / 2f
        );
    }
    public int getRow() {
        return row;
    }
    public int getCol() {
        return col;
    }
    public Vector2 getWorldPosition() {
        return worldPosition;
    }
    public float getWorldX() {
        return worldPosition.x;
    }
    public float getWorldY() {
        return worldPosition.y;
    }
    public Rectangle getBounds() {
        return bounds;
    }
    public float getX() {
        return bounds.x;
    }
    public float getY() {
        return bounds.y;
    }
    public float getWidth() {
        return bounds.width;
    }
    public float getHeight() {
        return bounds.height;
    }
    public boolean contains(float worldX, float worldY) {
        return bounds.contains(worldX, worldY);
    }
    public CellType getCellType() {
        return cellType;
    }
    public void setCellType(CellType cellType) {
        this.cellType = cellType;
    }
    public boolean isPlantable() {
        return obstacle == null
            && currentPlant == null
            && (cellType == null || cellType.canPlant());
    }
    public boolean isPlantable(PlantDefinition plantDefinition) {
        return isPlantable();
    }
    public Obstacle getObstacle() {
        return obstacle;
    }
    public void setObstacle(Obstacle obstacle) {
        this.obstacle = obstacle;
    }
    public boolean hasObstacle() {
        return obstacle != null;
    }
    public void clearObstacle() {
        obstacle = null;
    }
    public Plant getPlant() {
        return currentPlant;
    }
    public void setPlant(Plant plant) {
        currentPlant = plant;
    }
    public boolean hasPlant() {
        return currentPlant != null;
    }
    public void removePlant() {
        currentPlant = null;
    }
    public List<Zombie> getZombies() {
        return null;
    }
    public boolean isBeach() {
        return cellType instanceof WaterCellType;
    }
    public void setBeach(boolean beach) {
        if (beach) {
            cellType = new WaterCellType();
        }
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\game\world\playground\Grid.java ---
package game.world.playground;
import com.badlogic.gdx.math.Vector2;
import common.model.Position;
import game.entity.Entity;
public class Grid {
    private final MapConfig config;
    private final Cell[][] cells;
    public Grid(MapConfig config) {
        this.config = config;
        this.cells = new Cell[config.getRows()][config.getCols()];
        initializeCells();
    }
    private void initializeCells() {
        for (int row = 0; row < config.getRows(); row++) {
            for (int col = 0; col < config.getCols(); col++) {
                float x = config.getPlaygroundX() + col * config.getCellWidth();
                float y = config.getPlaygroundY() + row * config.getCellHeight();
                cells[row][col] = new Cell(
                    row,
                    col,
                    x,
                    y,
                    config.getCellWidth(),
                    config.getCellHeight()
                );
            }
        }
    }
    public Cell getCell(int row, int col) {
        if (row < 0 || row >= config.getRows() || col < 0 || col >= config.getCols()) {
            return null;
        }
        return cells[row][col];
    }
    public Cell getCellAt(float worldX, float worldY) {
        if (
            worldX < config.getPlaygroundX()
                || worldX >= config.getPlaygroundX() + config.getPlaygroundWidth()
                || worldY < config.getPlaygroundY()
                || worldY >= config.getPlaygroundY() + config.getPlaygroundHeight()
        ) {
            return null;
        }
        int col = (int) (
            (worldX - config.getPlaygroundX())
                / config.getCellWidth()
        );
        int row = (int) (
            (worldY - config.getPlaygroundY())
                / config.getCellHeight()
        );
        return getCell(row, col);
    }
    public Cell getCellAt(Position position) {
        return getCell(
            (int) position.getX(),
            (int) position.getY()
        );
    }
    public Vector2 getCellCenter(int row, int col) {
        Cell cell = getCell(row, col);
        if (cell == null) {
            return null;
        }
        return cell.getWorldPosition();
    }
    public int getRowCount() {
        return config.getRows();
    }
    public int getColCount() {
        return config.getCols();
    }
    public MapConfig getConfig() {
        return config;
    }
    public Cell[] getAllCells() {
        Cell[] result = new Cell[config.getRows() * config.getCols()];
        int index = 0;
        for (int row = 0; row < config.getRows(); row++) {
            for (int col = 0; col < config.getCols(); col++) {
                result[index++] = cells[row][col];
            }
        }
        System.out.println(
            "WORLD: x=" + config.getPlaygroundX()
                + " y=" + config.getPlaygroundY()
                + " | PLAYGROUND: x=" + config.getPlaygroundX()
                + " y=" + config.getPlaygroundY()
                + " width=" + config.getPlaygroundWidth()
                + " height=" + config.getPlaygroundHeight()
        );
        return result;
    }
    public boolean isInsideGrid(Position position) {
        return getCellAt(position) != null;
    }
    public float getPlaygroundWidth() {
        return config.getPlaygroundWidth();
    }
    public float getPlaygroundHeight() {
        return config.getPlaygroundHeight();
    }
    public int getEntityLane(Entity entity) {
        Position position = entity.getPosition();
        if (position == null) {
            return -1;
        }
        float worldY = (float) position.getY();
        float cellHeight = config.getCellHeight();
        float playgroundY = config.getPlaygroundY();
        int row = (int) Math.floor((worldY - playgroundY) / cellHeight);
        return Math.max(0, Math.min(row, config.getRows() - 1));
    }
    public int getEntityLane(Position position) {
        if (position == null) {
            return -1;
        }
        float worldY = (float) position.getY();
        float cellHeight = config.getCellHeight();
        float playgroundY = config.getPlaygroundY();
        int row = (int) Math.floor((worldY - playgroundY) / cellHeight);
        return Math.max(0, Math.min(row, config.getRows() - 1));
    }
    public int getEntityLane(float x, float y) {
        return getEntityLane(new Position(x, y));
    }
}

package game.world.playground;
public record GridConfig(int rowCount, int columnCount, double cellWidth, double cellHeight) {
    public static GridConfig defaultConfig() {
        return new GridConfig(5, 9, 1f, 1f);
    }
    public double getGridWidth() {
        return columnCount * cellWidth;
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\game\world\playground\MapConfig.java ---
package game.world.playground;
import com.badlogic.gdx.maps.MapLayer;
import com.badlogic.gdx.maps.MapObject;
import com.badlogic.gdx.maps.objects.RectangleMapObject;
import com.badlogic.gdx.maps.tiled.TiledMap;
import com.badlogic.gdx.math.Rectangle;
import com.badlogic.gdx.math.Vector2;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.Collections;
import java.util.List;
public class MapConfig {
    private final int rows;
    private final int cols;
    private final float cellWidth;
    private final float cellHeight;
    private final float playgroundX;
    private final float playgroundY;
    private final float playgroundWidth;
    private final float playgroundHeight;
    private final float loseLineX;
    private final Vector2 cameraCenter;
    private final List<Vector2> lawnMowerSpawns;
    private final List<Vector2> zombieSpawns;
    private MapConfig(Builder builder) {
        this.rows = builder.rows;
        this.cols = builder.cols;
        this.cellWidth = builder.cellWidth;
        this.cellHeight = builder.cellHeight;
        this.playgroundX = builder.playgroundX;
        this.playgroundY = builder.playgroundY;
        this.playgroundWidth = builder.playgroundWidth;
        this.playgroundHeight = builder.playgroundHeight;
        this.loseLineX = builder.loseLineX;
        this.cameraCenter = builder.cameraCenter;
        this.lawnMowerSpawns = Collections.unmodifiableList(builder.lawnMowerSpawns);
        this.zombieSpawns = Collections.unmodifiableList(builder.zombieSpawns);
    }
    public static MapConfig fromTiledMap(TiledMap tiledMap, int rows, int cols) {
        Builder builder = new Builder();
        builder.rows = rows;
        builder.cols = cols;
        Rectangle playground = extractSingleRectangle(tiledMap, "playground");
        if (playground != null) {
            builder.playgroundX = playground.x;
            builder.playgroundY = playground.y;
            builder.playgroundWidth = playground.width;
            builder.playgroundHeight = playground.height;
            builder.cellWidth = playground.width / cols;
            builder.cellHeight = playground.height / rows;
        }
        Rectangle loseRect = extractSingleRectangle(tiledMap, "lose");
        if (loseRect != null) {
            builder.loseLineX = loseRect.x;
        }
        Rectangle mapRect = extractSingleRectangle(tiledMap, "map");
        if (mapRect != null) {
            builder.cameraCenter = new Vector2(
                mapRect.x + mapRect.width / 2f,
                mapRect.y + mapRect.height / 2f
            );
        }
        builder.lawnMowerSpawns = extractAllRectanglePositions(tiledMap, "lawn_mower");
        builder.zombieSpawns = extractAllRectanglePositions(tiledMap, "zombie");
        return new MapConfig(builder);
    }
    public Vector2 gridToWorld(int row, int col) {
        float x = playgroundX + col * cellWidth + cellWidth / 2f;
        float y = playgroundY + row * cellHeight + cellHeight / 2f;
        return new Vector2(x, y);
    }
    public GridCoordinate worldToGrid(float worldX, float worldY) {
        if (worldX < playgroundX || worldX >= playgroundX + playgroundWidth ||
            worldY < playgroundY || worldY >= playgroundY + playgroundHeight) {
            return null;
        }
        int col = (int) ((worldX - playgroundX) / cellWidth);
        int row = (int) ((worldY - playgroundY) / cellHeight);
        col = Math.max(0, Math.min(col, cols - 1));
        row = Math.max(0, Math.min(row, rows - 1));
        return new GridCoordinate(row, col);
    }
    public boolean isPastLoseLine(float worldX) {
        return worldX < loseLineX;
    }
    public int getRows() { return rows; }
    public int getCols() { return cols; }
    public float getCellWidth() { return cellWidth; }
    public float getCellHeight() { return cellHeight; }
    public float getPlaygroundX() { return playgroundX; }
    public float getPlaygroundY() { return playgroundY; }
    public float getPlaygroundWidth() { return playgroundWidth; }
    public float getPlaygroundHeight() { return playgroundHeight; }
    public float getLoseLineX() { return loseLineX; }
    public Vector2 getCameraCenter() { return cameraCenter; }
    public List<Vector2> getLawnMowerSpawns() { return lawnMowerSpawns; }
    public List<Vector2> getZombieSpawns() { return zombieSpawns; }
    private static Rectangle extractSingleRectangle(TiledMap map, String layerName) {
        MapLayer layer = map.getLayers().get(layerName);
        if (layer == null || layer.getObjects().getCount() == 0) return null;
        MapObject obj = layer.getObjects().get(0);
        if (obj instanceof RectangleMapObject) {
            return ((RectangleMapObject) obj).getRectangle();
        }
        return null;
    }
    private static List<Vector2> extractAllRectanglePositions(TiledMap map, String layerName) {
        List<Vector2> positions = new ArrayList<>();
        MapLayer layer = map.getLayers().get(layerName);
        if (layer == null) return positions;
        for (MapObject obj : layer.getObjects()) {
            if (obj instanceof RectangleMapObject) {
                Rectangle rect = ((RectangleMapObject) obj).getRectangle();
                positions.add(new Vector2(
                    rect.x + rect.width / 2f,
                    rect.y + rect.height / 2f
                ));
            }
        }
        return positions;
    }
    public record GridCoordinate(int row, int col) {}
    private static class Builder {
        int rows, cols;
        float cellWidth, cellHeight;
        float playgroundX, playgroundY, playgroundWidth, playgroundHeight;
        float loseLineX;
        Vector2 cameraCenter;
        List<Vector2> lawnMowerSpawns = new ArrayList<>();
        List<Vector2> zombieSpawns = new ArrayList<>();
    }
}

package game.world.seasons;
import app.enums.SeasonType;
import app.model.App;
import common.model.Position;
import game.entity.obstacle.TombStone;
import game.service.Zombie.ZombieService;
import game.world.playground.Cell;
import game.world.playground.Grid;
import game.world.playground.celltypes.GrassCellType;
import java.util.Random;
public class AncientEgyptSeason extends Season{
    private final int RANDOM_TOMB_COUNT = 3;
    public AncientEgyptSeason() {
        seasonName = "Ancient Egypt 🏜️";
    }
    @Override
    public String getGrassDisplay() {
        return Color.YELLOW + "░" + Color.RESET;
    }
    @Override
    public String getWaterDisplay() {
        return Color.BLUE + "≈" + Color.RESET;
    }
    @Override
    public void updateEnvironment(Grid grid) {
    }
    @Override
    public void setupMap(Grid grid) {
        for (Cell cell : grid.getAllCells()) {
            cell.setCellType(new GrassCellType());
        }
        placeRandomGraves(grid, RANDOM_TOMB_COUNT);
    }
    private void placeRandomGraves(Grid grid, int count) {
        Random random = new Random();
        int placedGrave = 0;
        while (placedGrave < count) {
            int row = random.nextInt(grid.getRowCount());
            int col = random.nextInt(grid.getColCount());
            Cell cell = grid.getCell(row, col);
            if (cell.getObstacle() != null)
                continue;
            placedGrave++;
        }
    }
    @Override
    public SeasonType getSeasonType() {
        return SeasonType.ANCIENT_EGYPT;
    }
    @Override
    public void onWaveStart(Grid grid) {
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\game\world\seasons\BigWaveBeachSeason.java ---
package game.world.seasons;
import app.enums.SeasonType;
import app.model.App;
import game.entity.plant.Plant;
import game.service.Zombie.ZombieService;
import game.world.playground.Cell;
import game.world.playground.Grid;
import game.world.playground.celltypes.GrassCellType;
import game.world.playground.celltypes.WaterCellType;
import java.util.Random;
public class BigWaveBeachSeason extends Season{
    private int seaStartColumn;
    private final int beachCount = 5;
    public BigWaveBeachSeason() {
        seasonName = "Big Wave Beach 🌊";
    }
    @Override
    public void updateEnvironment(Grid grid) {
    }
    @Override
    public String getGrassDisplay() {
        return Color.GREEN + "░" + Color.RESET;
    }
    @Override
    public String getWaterDisplay() {
        return Color.CYAN + "≈" + Color.RESET;
    }
    @Override
    public void onWaveStart(Grid grid) {
        Random random = new Random();
        seaStartColumn = random.nextInt(4, 7);
        updateWaterCells(grid);
        destroyInvalidPlants(grid);
        for (Cell cell : grid.getAllCells()) {
            if (cell.isBeach() && (cell.getCellType() instanceof WaterCellType)) {
                ZombieService service = new ZombieService(App.getCurrentGameEngine().getGameWorld());
            }
        }
    }
    private void destroyInvalidPlants(Grid grid) {
        for (Cell cell : grid.getAllCells()) {
            Plant plant = cell.getPlant();
            if (plant == null)
                continue;
            if (!cell.isPlantable()) {
            }
        }
    }
    @Override
    public void setupMap(Grid grid) {
        placeBeachCells(grid, beachCount);
        seaStartColumn = 6;
        updateWaterCells(grid);
    }
    private void placeBeachCells(Grid grid, int count) {
        Random random = new Random();
        int placed = 0;
        while (placed < count) {
            int row = random.nextInt(grid.getRowCount());
            int col = random.nextInt(grid.getColCount());
            Cell cell = grid.getCell(row, col);
            if (cell.isBeach())
                continue;
            cell.setBeach(true);
            placed++;
        }
    }
    private void updateWaterCells(Grid grid) {
        for (Cell cell : grid.getAllCells()) {
            if (cell.getCol() >= seaStartColumn)
                cell.setCellType(new WaterCellType());
            else
                cell.setCellType(new GrassCellType());
        }
    }
    @Override
    public SeasonType getSeasonType() {
        return SeasonType.BIG_WAVE_BEACH;
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\game\world\seasons\Color.java ---
package game.world.seasons;
public class Color {
    public static final String BLACK = "\u001B[30m";
    public static final String RED = "\u001B[31m";
    public static final String GREEN = "\u001B[32m";
    public static final String YELLOW = "\u001B[33m";
    public static final String BLUE = "\u001B[34m";
    public static final String MAGENTA = "\u001B[35m";
    public static final String CYAN = "\u001B[36m";
    public static final String WHITE = "\u001B[37m";
    public static final String RESET = "\u001B[0m";
    public static final String BRIGHT_BLACK = "\u001B[90m";
    public static final String BRIGHT_RED = "\u001B[91m";
    public static final String BRIGHT_GREEN = "\u001B[92m";
    public static final String BRIGHT_YELLOW = "\u001B[93m";
    public static final String BRIGHT_BLUE = "\u001B[94m";
    public static final String BRIGHT_MAGENTA = "\u001B[95m";
    public static final String BRIGHT_CYAN = "\u001B[96m";
    public static final String BRIGHT_WHITE = "\u001B[97m";
    public static final String PURPLE = "\u001B[38;5;93m";
    public static final String ORANGE = "\u001B[38;5;214m";
    public static final String PURPLE_ALT = "\u001B[38;5;129m";
    public static final String ORANGE_ALT = "\u001B[38;5;208m";
    public static String rgb(int r, int g, int b) {
        return String.format("\u001B[38;2;%d;%d;%dm", r, g, b);
    }
    public static final String PURPLE_RGB = rgb(128, 0, 128);
    public static final String DARK_PURPLE = rgb(75, 0, 130);
    public static final String ORANGE_RGB = rgb(255, 165, 0);
    public static final String DARK_ORANGE = rgb(255, 140, 0);
}

package game.world.seasons;
import app.enums.SeasonType;
import app.model.App;
import game.entity.obstacle.TombStone;
import game.service.Zombie.ZombieService;
import game.world.playground.Cell;
import game.world.playground.Grid;
import game.world.playground.celltypes.GrassCellType;
import java.util.Random;
public class DarkAgesSeason extends Season {
    private final int RANDOM_TOMB_COUNT = 3;
    private final int RANDOM_NECROMANCY_COUNT = 5;
    public DarkAgesSeason() {
        seasonName = "Dark Ages 🏰";
    }
    @Override
    public void updateEnvironment(Grid grid) {
    }
    @Override
    public String getGrassDisplay() {
        return Color.GREEN + "▓" + Color.RESET;
    }
    @Override
    public String getWaterDisplay() {
        return "";
    }
    @Override
    public void setupMap(Grid grid) {
        for (Cell cell : grid.getAllCells()) {
            cell.setCellType(new GrassCellType());
        }
        placeNecromancyCells(grid, RANDOM_NECROMANCY_COUNT);
        placeRandomGraves(grid, RANDOM_TOMB_COUNT);
    }
    private void placeNecromancyCells(Grid grid, int count) {
        Random random = new Random();
        int placed = 0;
        while (placed < count) {
            int row = random.nextInt(grid.getRowCount());
            int col = random.nextInt(grid.getColCount());
            Cell cell = grid.getCell(row, col);
            placed++;
        }
    }
    private void placeRandomGraves(Grid grid, int count) {
        Random random = new Random();
        int placedGrave = 0;
        while (placedGrave < count) {
            int row = random.nextInt(grid.getRowCount());
            int col = random.nextInt(grid.getRowCount()) + 4;
            Cell cell = grid.getCell(row, col);
            if (cell.getObstacle() != null)
                continue;
            if (cell.hasPlant())
                continue;
            placedGrave++;
        }
    }
    @Override
    public void onWaveStart(Grid grid) {
        placeRandomGraves(grid, RANDOM_TOMB_COUNT);
        for (Cell cell : grid.getAllCells()) {
        }
    }
    @Override
    public SeasonType getSeasonType() {
        return SeasonType.DARK_AGES;
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\game\world\seasons\FrostBiteCavesSeason.java ---
package game.world.seasons;
import app.enums.SeasonType;
import app.model.App;
import common.model.Position;
import game.entity.obstacle.IceBlock;
import game.entity.plant.Plant;
import game.entity.zombie.Zombie;
import game.service.Zombie.ZombieService;
import game.world.GameWorld;
import game.world.playground.Cell;
import game.world.playground.Grid;
import game.world.playground.celltypes.GrassCellType;
import game.world.playground.celltypes.IceCellType;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Random;
public class FrostBiteCavesSeason extends Season{
    private final int RANDOM_ICE_COUNT = 5;
    private final int RANDOM_ZOMBIE_COUNT = 3;
    private boolean spawnedFreezedZombie = false;
    public FrostBiteCavesSeason() {
        seasonName = "Frostbite Caves ❄️";
    }
    @Override
    public void updateEnvironment(Grid grid) {
        for (Cell cell : grid.getAllCells()) {
            if (!(cell.getCellType() instanceof IceCellType))
                continue;
            for (Zombie zombie : cell.getZombies()) {
            }
        }
        if(!spawnedFreezedZombie) {
            Random random = new Random();
            GameWorld world = App.getCurrentGameEngine().getGameWorld();
            ZombieService service = new ZombieService(world);
            for (int i = 0; i < RANDOM_ZOMBIE_COUNT; i++) {
                int row = random.nextInt(grid.getRowCount());
                int col = random.nextInt(grid.getRowCount()) + 4;
                Position position = new Position(col, row);
                if(grid.isInsideGrid(position)) {
                    service.spawnZombie("ZombieDefault", position);
                }
            }
            world.addEvent("######## FROZEN ZOMBIES GOT SPAWNED ON THE MAP");
            spawnedFreezedZombie = true;
        }
    }
    @Override
    public String getGrassDisplay() {
        return Color.WHITE + "░" + Color.RESET;
    }
    @Override
    public String getWaterDisplay() {
        return "";
    }
    @Override
    public void onWaveStart(Grid grid) {
        applyIceWind(grid);
    }
    private void applyIceWind(Grid grid) {
        List<Integer> rows = selectRandomRows(grid);
        for (int row : rows) {
            for (int col = 0; col < grid.getColCount(); col++) {
                Cell cell = grid.getCell(row, col);
                Plant plant = cell.getPlant();
            }
        }
    }
    private List<Integer> selectRandomRows(Grid grid) {
        Random random = new Random();
        List<Integer> rows = new ArrayList<>();
        int count = random.nextInt(grid.getRowCount()) + 1;
        while (rows.size() < count) {
            int row = random.nextInt(grid.getRowCount());
            if (!rows.contains(row)) {
                rows.add(row);
            }
        }
        return rows;
    }
    @Override
    public void setupMap(Grid grid) {
        for (Cell cell : grid.getAllCells()) {
            cell.setCellType(new GrassCellType());
        }
        placeRandomIces(grid, RANDOM_ICE_COUNT);
    }
    public void placeRandomIces(Grid grid, int count) {
        Random random = new Random();
        int placed = 0;
        while (placed < count) {
            int row = random.nextInt(grid.getRowCount());
            int col = random.nextInt(grid.getColCount());
            Cell cell = grid.getCell(row, col);
            if (cell.getCellType() instanceof IceCellType)
                continue;
            cell.setCellType(new IceCellType());
            placed++;
        }
    }
    @Override
    public SeasonType getSeasonType() {
        return SeasonType.FROSTBITE_CAVES;
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\game\world\seasons\Season.java ---
package game.world.seasons;
import app.enums.SeasonType;
import game.entity.obstacle.Obstacle;
import game.world.chapter.Chapter;
import game.world.playground.Grid;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;
public abstract class Season {
    protected String seasonName;
    private ArrayList<Obstacle> seasonObstacles;
    protected List<Chapter> chapters = new ArrayList<>();
    public abstract void updateEnvironment(Grid grid);
    public abstract void setupMap(Grid grid);
    public abstract SeasonType getSeasonType();
    public List<Chapter> getChapters() {
        return chapters;
    }
    public Chapter getChapter(int number) {
        for (Chapter chapter : chapters) {
            if (chapter.getChapterNumber() == number) {
                return chapter;
            }
        }
        return null;
    }
    public abstract String getGrassDisplay();
    public abstract String getWaterDisplay();
    public String getName() {
        return seasonName;
    }
    public abstract void onWaveStart(Grid grid);
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\graphics\GraphicsEngine.java ---
package graphics;
import app.enums.plant.PlantName;
import com.badlogic.gdx.graphics.g2d.SpriteBatch;
import game.entity.plant.Plant;
import game.entity.zombie.Zombie;
import graphics.animation.*;
import graphics.animation.zombie.*;
import graphics.instance.PlantRenderInstance;
import graphics.instance.ZombieRenderInstance;
import graphics.renderer.PlantRenderer;
import graphics.renderer.ZombieRenderer;
import graphics.resolver.PlantVisualStateResolver;
import graphics.resolver.ZombieVisualStateResolver;
import graphics.state.PlantVisualState;
import graphics.state.ZombieVisualState;
import pvz.libpvz.pam.ClipRef;
import pvz.libpvz.pam.PamPlayer;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.HashMap;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.Set;
public class GraphicsEngine {
    private final AnimationLibrary animationLibrary;
    private final PlantAnimationRegistry plantAnimationRegistry;
    private final ZombieAnimationRegistry zombieAnimationRegistry;
    private final AnimationSelector plantAnimationSelector;
    private final ZombieAnimationSelector zombieAnimationSelector;
    private final PlantVisualStateResolver plantStateResolver;
    private final ZombieVisualStateResolver zombieStateResolver;
    private final PlantRenderer plantRenderer;
    private final ZombieRenderer zombieRenderer;
    private final Map<Plant, PlantRenderInstance> activePlantInstances;
    private final Map<Zombie, ZombieRenderInstance> activeZombieInstances;
    public GraphicsEngine(PamPlayer pamPlayer) {
        animationLibrary = new AnimationLibrary(pamPlayer);
        plantAnimationRegistry = new PlantAnimationRegistry();
        zombieAnimationRegistry = new ZombieAnimationRegistry();
        plantAnimationSelector = new AnimationSelector();
        zombieAnimationSelector = new ZombieAnimationSelector();
        plantStateResolver = new PlantVisualStateResolver();
        zombieStateResolver = new ZombieVisualStateResolver();
        plantRenderer = new PlantRenderer(pamPlayer);
        zombieRenderer = new ZombieRenderer(pamPlayer);
        activePlantInstances = new HashMap<>();
        activeZombieInstances = new HashMap<>();
    }
    public void prepareLevelAssets(Set<PlantName> levelPlants, Set<String> levelZombies) {
        animationLibrary.unloadAllPlantsExcept(levelPlants);
        for (PlantName plantName : levelPlants) {
            AnimationProfile profile = plantAnimationRegistry.getProfile(plantName);
            if (profile != null) {
                animationLibrary.loadPlantAssets(plantName, profile.getPamPath());
            }
        }
        animationLibrary.unloadAllZombiesExcept(levelZombies);
        for (String alias : levelZombies) {
            ZombieAnimationProfile profile = zombieAnimationRegistry.getProfile(alias);
            if (profile != null) {
                animationLibrary.loadZombieAssets(alias, profile.getPamPath());
            }
        }
    }
    public void registerPlant(Plant plant) {
        if (plant == null || activePlantInstances.containsKey(plant)) {
            return;
        }
        PlantName plantName = plant.getDefinition().getName();
        AnimationProfile profile = plantAnimationRegistry.getProfile(plantName);
        if (profile != null) {
            animationLibrary.loadPlantAssets(plantName, profile.getPamPath());
        }
        activePlantInstances.put(plant, new PlantRenderInstance(plant));
    }
    public void unregisterPlant(Plant plant) {
        if (plant != null) {
            activePlantInstances.remove(plant);
        }
    }
    public void registerZombie(Zombie zombie) {
        if (zombie == null || activeZombieInstances.containsKey(zombie)) {
            return;
        }
        String name = zombie.getName();
        ZombieAnimationProfile profile = zombieAnimationRegistry.getProfile(name);
        if (profile != null) {
            animationLibrary.loadZombieAssets(name, profile.getPamPath());
        }
        activeZombieInstances.put(zombie, new ZombieRenderInstance(zombie));
    }
    public void unregisterZombie(Zombie zombie) {
        if (zombie != null) {
            activeZombieInstances.remove(zombie);
        }
    }
    public void updateAndRender(SpriteBatch batch, float delta) {
        renderPlants(batch, delta);
        renderZombies(batch, delta);
    }
    private void renderPlants(SpriteBatch batch, float delta) {
        if (activePlantInstances.isEmpty()) {
            return;
        }
        List<PlantRenderInstance> instances =
            new ArrayList<>(activePlantInstances.values());
        for (PlantRenderInstance instance : instances) {
            instance.update(delta);
            updateInstanceState(instance);
            updateInstanceAnimation(instance);
            plantRenderer.render(batch, instance);
        }
    }
    private void renderZombies(SpriteBatch batch, float delta) {
        if (activeZombieInstances.isEmpty()) {
            return;
        }
        List<ZombieRenderInstance> instances =
            new ArrayList<>(activeZombieInstances.values());
        for (ZombieRenderInstance instance : instances) {
            instance.update(delta);
            updateInstanceState(instance);
            updateInstanceAnimation(instance);
            zombieRenderer.render(batch, instance);
        }
    }
    private void updateInstanceState(PlantRenderInstance instance) {
        PlantVisualState state = plantStateResolver.resolve(
            instance.getPlant(),
            instance,
            instance.getCurrentVisualState(),
            instance.getAnimationController().isFinished()
        );
        instance.setCurrentVisualState(state);
    }
    private void updateInstanceState(ZombieRenderInstance instance) {
        ZombieVisualState state = zombieStateResolver.resolve(
            instance.getZombie(),
            instance,
            instance.getCurrentVisualState(),
            instance.getAnimationController().isFinished()
        );
        instance.setCurrentVisualState(state);
    }
    private void updateInstanceAnimation(PlantRenderInstance instance) {
        PlantName name = instance.getPlant().getDefinition().getName();
        AnimationProfile profile = plantAnimationRegistry.getProfile(name);
        if (profile == null) {
            return;
        }
        AnimationVariant variant = plantAnimationSelector.selectVariant(
            profile,
            instance,
            instance.getCurrentVisualState()
        );
        if (variant == null) {
            return;
        }
        PlantClipKey key = new PlantClipKey(
            name,
            variant.getClipName(),
            profile.getPamPath()
        );
        ClipRef clip = animationLibrary.getClip(key);
        if (clip == null) {
            clip = animationLibrary.getPamPlayer().getClip(
                profile.getPamPath(),
                variant.getClipName()
            );
            if (clip == null) {
                return;
            }
            animationLibrary.putClip(key, clip);
        }
        instance.getAnimationController().changeVariant(
            variant,
            clip,
            profile.getPamPath()
        );
    }
    private void updateInstanceAnimation(ZombieRenderInstance instance) {
        String alias = instance.getZombie().getName();
        ZombieAnimationProfile profile = zombieAnimationRegistry.getProfile(alias);
        if (profile == null) {
            return;
        }
        ZombieAnimationVariant variant = zombieAnimationSelector.selectVariant(
            profile,
            instance,
            instance.getCurrentVisualState()
        );
        if (variant == null) {
            return;
        }
        ZombieClipKey key = new ZombieClipKey(
            alias,
            variant.getClipName(),
            profile.getPamPath()
        );
        ClipRef clip = animationLibrary.getClip(key);
        if (clip == null) {
            clip = animationLibrary.getPamPlayer().getClip(
                profile.getPamPath(),
                variant.getClipName()
            );
            if (clip == null) {
                return;
            }
            animationLibrary.putClip(key, clip);
        }
        instance.getAnimationController().changeVariant(
            variant,
            clip,
            profile.getPamPath()
        );
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\graphics\animation\AnimationController.java ---
package graphics.animation;
import pvz.libpvz.pam.ClipRef;
import java.util.Objects;
public class AnimationController {
    private AnimationVariant currentVariant;
    private ClipRef currentClip;
    private String currentPamPath;
    private float stateTime;
    private boolean finished;
    public AnimationController() {
        this.stateTime = 0.0f;
        this.finished = false;
    }
    public void update(float delta) {
        if (finished) {
            return;
        }
        this.stateTime += delta;
        checkAnimationCompletion();
    }
    public boolean changeVariant(AnimationVariant newVariant, ClipRef newClip, String pamPath) {
        if (newVariant == null) {
            return false;
        }
        if (isSameVariant(newVariant) && Objects.equals(this.currentClip, newClip)) {
            return false;
        }
        this.currentVariant = newVariant;
        this.currentClip = newClip;
        this.currentPamPath = pamPath;
        this.stateTime = 0.0f;
        this.finished = false;
        return true;
    }
    private void checkAnimationCompletion() {
        if (isNonLoopingFinished()) {
            this.finished = true;
        }
    }
    private boolean isNonLoopingFinished() {
        return currentVariant != null
            && !currentVariant.isLoop()
            && currentClip != null
            && stateTime >= currentClip.duration;
    }
    private boolean isSameVariant(AnimationVariant newVariant) {
        return currentVariant != null && currentVariant.equals(newVariant);
    }
    public AnimationVariant getCurrentVariant() {
        return currentVariant;
    }
    public ClipRef getCurrentClip() {
        return currentClip;
    }
    public String getCurrentPamPath() {
        return currentPamPath;
    }
    public float getStateTime() {
        return stateTime;
    }
    public boolean isFinished() {
        return finished;
    }
    public void setFinished(boolean finished) {
        this.finished = finished;
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\graphics\animation\AnimationLibrary.java ---
package graphics.animation;
import app.enums.plant.PlantName;
import graphics.animation.zombie.ZombieClipKey;
import pvz.libpvz.pam.ClipRef;
import pvz.libpvz.pam.PamPlayer;
import java.util.HashMap;
import java.util.HashSet;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.Set;
public class AnimationLibrary {
    private final PamPlayer pamPlayer;
    private final Map<PlantClipKey, ClipRef> plantClipCache;
    private final Map<ZombieClipKey, ClipRef> zombieClipCache;
    private final Set<PlantName> loadedPlants;
    private final Set<String> loadedZombies;
    public AnimationLibrary(PamPlayer pamPlayer) {
        this.pamPlayer = pamPlayer;
        this.plantClipCache = new HashMap<>();
        this.zombieClipCache = new HashMap<>();
        this.loadedPlants = new HashSet<>();
        this.loadedZombies = new HashSet<>();
    }
    public void loadPlantAssets(PlantName plantName, String pamPath) {
        if (loadedPlants.contains(plantName)) {
            return;
        }
        pamPlayer.loadSync(pamPath);
        loadedPlants.add(plantName);
    }
    public void loadZombieAssets(String alias, String pamPath) {
        if(loadedZombies.contains(alias)) {
            return;
        }
        pamPlayer.loadSync(pamPath);
        loadedZombies.add(alias);
    }
    public ClipRef getClip(PlantClipKey key) {
        return plantClipCache.get(key);
    }
    public ClipRef getClip(ZombieClipKey key) {
        return zombieClipCache.get(key);
    }
    public void putClip(PlantClipKey key, ClipRef clip) {
        plantClipCache.put(key, clip);
    }
    public void putClip(ZombieClipKey key, ClipRef clip) {
        zombieClipCache.put(key, clip);
    }
    public void unloadPlantAssets(PlantName plantName) {
        plantClipCache.keySet().removeIf(key -> key.plantName() == plantName);
        loadedPlants.remove(plantName);
    }
    public void unloadZombieAssets(String alias) {
        zombieClipCache.keySet().removeIf(key -> key.alias().equals(alias));
        loadedZombies.remove(alias);
    }
    public void unloadAllPlantsExcept(Set<PlantName> activePlants) {
        plantClipCache.keySet().removeIf(key -> !activePlants.contains(key.plantName()));
        loadedPlants.retainAll(activePlants);
    }
    public void unloadAllZombiesExcept(Set<String> activeZombies) {
        zombieClipCache.keySet().removeIf(key -> !activeZombies.contains(key.alias()));
        loadedZombies.retainAll(activeZombies);
    }
    public PamPlayer getPamPlayer() {
        return pamPlayer;
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\graphics\animation\AnimationProfile.java ---
package graphics.animation;
import app.enums.plant.PlantName;
import graphics.state.PlantVisualState;
import java.util.*;
public class AnimationProfile {
    private final PlantName plantName;
    private final String pamPath;
    private final Map<PlantVisualState, List<AnimationVariant>> variantsMap;
    public AnimationProfile(PlantName plantName, String pamPath) {
        this.plantName = plantName;
        this.pamPath = pamPath;
        this.variantsMap = new EnumMap<>(PlantVisualState.class);
    }
    public void addVariant(PlantVisualState state, AnimationVariant variant) {
        variantsMap.computeIfAbsent(state, k -> new ArrayList<>()).add(variant);
    }
    public List<AnimationVariant> getVariants(PlantVisualState state) {
        return variantsMap.getOrDefault(state, Collections.emptyList());
    }
    public Map<PlantVisualState, List<AnimationVariant>> getAllVariantsMap() {
        return Collections.unmodifiableMap(variantsMap);
    }
    public PlantName getPlantName() {
        return plantName;
    }
    public String getPamPath() {
        return pamPath;
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\graphics\animation\AnimationSelector.java ---
package graphics.animation;
import graphics.animation.strategy.*;
import graphics.instance.PlantRenderInstance;
import graphics.state.PlantVisualState;
import java.util.EnumMap;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;
public class AnimationSelector {
    private final Map<PlantVisualState, VariantSelectionStrategy> strategyRegistry;
    private final VariantSelectionStrategy defaultStrategy;
    public AnimationSelector() {
        this.strategyRegistry = new EnumMap<>(PlantVisualState.class);
        this.defaultStrategy = new FirstSelectionStrategy();
        registerDefaults();
    }
    public void registerStrategy(PlantVisualState state, VariantSelectionStrategy strategy) {
        strategyRegistry.put(state, strategy);
    }
    public AnimationVariant selectVariant(AnimationProfile profile, PlantRenderInstance instance, PlantVisualState state) {
        List<AnimationVariant> variants = profile.getVariants(state);
        if (variants.isEmpty()) {
            return null;
        }
        VariantSelectionStrategy strategy = strategyRegistry.getOrDefault(state, defaultStrategy);
        return strategy.select(variants, instance);
    }
    private void registerDefaults() {
        strategyRegistry.put(PlantVisualState.IDLE, new IdleSelectionStrategy());
        strategyRegistry.put(PlantVisualState.HURT, new ConditionSelectionStrategy());
        strategyRegistry.put(PlantVisualState.PLANT_FOOD, new SequenceSelectionStrategy());
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\graphics\animation\AnimationVariant.java ---
package graphics.animation;
import game.entity.plant.Plant;
import graphics.animation.condition.PlantAnimationCondition;
import java.util.Objects;
public class AnimationVariant {
    private final String clipName;
    private final boolean loop;
    private final int priority;
    private final AnimationTag tag;
    private final PlantAnimationCondition condition;
    public AnimationVariant(String clipName, boolean loop, int priority, AnimationTag tag, PlantAnimationCondition condition) {
        this.clipName = clipName;
        this.loop = loop;
        this.priority = priority;
        this.tag = tag;
        this.condition = condition;
    }
    public AnimationVariant(String clipName, boolean loop, int priority, AnimationTag tag) {
        this(clipName, loop, priority, tag, plant -> true);
    }
    public String getClipName() {
        return clipName;
    }
    public boolean isLoop() {
        return loop;
    }
    public int getPriority() {
        return priority;
    }
    public AnimationTag getTag() {
        return tag;
    }
    public boolean matchesCondition(Plant plant) {
        return condition == null || condition.matches(plant);
    }
    @Override
    public boolean equals(Object o) {
        if (this == o) return true;
        if (o == null || getClass() != o.getClass()) return false;
        AnimationVariant that = (AnimationVariant) o;
        return Objects.equals(clipName, that.clipName);
    }
    @Override
    public int hashCode() {
        return Objects.hash(clipName);
    }
}

package graphics.animation;
import app.enums.plant.PlantName;
import graphics.animation.loader.AnimationProfileLoader;
import java.util.HashMap;
import java.util.Map;
public class PlantAnimationRegistry {
    private final Map<PlantName, AnimationProfile> profileRegistry;
    public PlantAnimationRegistry() {
        this.profileRegistry = new HashMap<>();
        registerInitialProfiles();
    }
    public AnimationProfile getProfile(PlantName plantName) {
        return profileRegistry.get(plantName);
    }
    public void registerProfile(AnimationProfile profile) {
        profileRegistry.put(profile.getPlantName(), profile);
    }
    private void registerInitialProfiles() {
        AnimationProfile profile =
            AnimationProfileLoader.load("plants/sunflower.json");
        registerProfile(profile);
    }
}

public record PlantClipKey(PlantName plantName, String clipName, String pamPath) {
    @Override
    public boolean equals(Object o) {
        if (this == o) return true;
        if (o == null || getClass() != o.getClass()) return false;
        PlantClipKey clipKey = (PlantClipKey) o;
        return plantName == clipKey.plantName &&
            Objects.equals(clipName, clipKey.clipName) &&
            Objects.equals(pamPath, clipKey.pamPath);
