--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\graphics\animation\loader\AnimationProfileLoader.java ---
package graphics.animation.loader;
import app.enums.plant.PlantName;
import com.badlogic.gdx.Gdx;
import com.badlogic.gdx.files.FileHandle;
import com.google.gson.JsonObject;
import com.google.gson.JsonParser;
import graphics.animation.AnimationProfile;
import graphics.animation.AnimationTag;
import graphics.animation.AnimationVariant;
import graphics.state.PlantVisualState;
public final class AnimationProfileLoader {
    private AnimationProfileLoader() {
    }
    public static AnimationProfile load(String path) {
        FileHandle file = Gdx.files.internal(path);
        if (!file.exists()) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("Resource not found: " + path);
        }
        JsonObject root = JsonParser
            .parseReader(file.reader())
            .getAsJsonObject();
        PlantName plantName = PlantName.valueOf(root.get("plant").getAsString());
        String pamPath = root.get("pamPath").getAsString();
        AnimationProfile profile = new AnimationProfile(plantName, pamPath);
        JsonObject states = root.getAsJsonObject("states");
        for (String stateName : states.keySet()) {
            PlantVisualState state = PlantVisualState.valueOf(stateName);
            states.getAsJsonArray(stateName).forEach(element -> {
                JsonObject obj = element.getAsJsonObject();
                profile.addVariant(
                    state,
                    new AnimationVariant(
                        obj.get("clip").getAsString(),
                        obj.get("loop").getAsBoolean(),
                        obj.get("priority").getAsInt(),
                        AnimationTag.valueOf(obj.get("tag").getAsString())
                    )
                );
            });
        }
        return profile;
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\graphics\animation\loader\ZombieAnimationProfileLoader.java ---
package graphics.animation.loader;
import com.badlogic.gdx.Gdx;
import com.badlogic.gdx.files.FileHandle;
import com.google.gson.JsonObject;
import com.google.gson.JsonParser;
import graphics.animation.zombie.ZombieAnimationProfile;
import graphics.animation.zombie.ZombieAnimationTag;
import graphics.animation.zombie.ZombieAnimationVariant;
import graphics.state.ZombieVisualState;
public final class ZombieAnimationProfileLoader {
    private ZombieAnimationProfileLoader() {
    }
    public static ZombieAnimationProfile load(String path) {
        FileHandle file = Gdx.files.internal(path);
        if (!file.exists()) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("Resource not found: " + path);
        }
        JsonObject root = JsonParser
            .parseReader(file.reader())
            .getAsJsonObject();
        String alias = root.get("zombie").getAsString();
        String pamPath = root.get("pamPath").getAsString();
        ZombieAnimationProfile profile = new ZombieAnimationProfile(alias, pamPath);
        JsonObject states = root.getAsJsonObject("states");
        for (String stateName : states.keySet()) {
            ZombieVisualState state = ZombieVisualState.valueOf(stateName);
            states.getAsJsonArray(stateName).forEach(jsonElement -> {
                JsonObject object = jsonElement.getAsJsonObject();
                profile.addVariant(
                    state,
                    new ZombieAnimationVariant(
                        object.get("clip").getAsString(),
                        object.get("loop").getAsBoolean(),
                        object.get("priority").getAsInt(),
                        ZombieAnimationTag.valueOf(object.get("tag").getAsString())
                    )
                );
            });
        }
        return profile;
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\graphics\animation\strategy\ConditionSelectionStrategy.java ---
package graphics.animation.strategy;
import graphics.animation.AnimationVariant;
import graphics.animation.zombie.ZombieAnimationVariant;
import graphics.instance.PlantRenderInstance;
import graphics.instance.ZombieRenderInstance;
import java.util.List;
public class ConditionSelectionStrategy implements VariantSelectionStrategy {
    @Override
    public AnimationVariant select(List<AnimationVariant> variants, PlantRenderInstance instance) {
        if (isPlantVariantsInvalid(variants)) {
            return null;
        }
        return findMatchingConditionVariant(variants, instance);
    }
    private AnimationVariant findMatchingConditionVariant(List<AnimationVariant> variants, PlantRenderInstance instance) {
        for (AnimationVariant variant : variants) {
            if (variant.matchesCondition(instance.getPlant())) {
                return variant;
            }
        }
        return variants.get(0);
    }
    private ZombieAnimationVariant findMatchingConditionVariant(List<ZombieAnimationVariant> variants, ZombieRenderInstance instance) {
        for(ZombieAnimationVariant variant : variants) {
            if(variant.matchesCondition(instance.getZombie())) {
                return variant;
            }
        }
        return variants.get(0);
    }
    @Override
    public ZombieAnimationVariant select(List<ZombieAnimationVariant> variants, ZombieRenderInstance instance) {
        if(isZombieVariantsInvalid(variants)) {
            return null;
        }
        return findMatchingConditionVariant(variants, instance);
    }
}

package graphics.animation.strategy;
import graphics.animation.AnimationVariant;
import graphics.animation.zombie.ZombieAnimationVariant;
import graphics.instance.PlantRenderInstance;
import graphics.instance.ZombieRenderInstance;

public class FirstSelectionStrategy implements VariantSelectionStrategy {
    @Override
    public AnimationVariant select(List<AnimationVariant> variants, PlantRenderInstance instance) {
        if (isPlantVariantsInvalid(variants)) {
            return null;
        }
        return variants.get(0);
    }
    @Override
    public ZombieAnimationVariant select(List<ZombieAnimationVariant> variants, ZombieRenderInstance instance) {
        if(isZombieVariantsInvalid(variants)) {
            return null;
        }
        return variants.get(0);
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\graphics\animation\strategy\IdleSelectionStrategy.java ---
package graphics.animation.strategy;
import graphics.animation.AnimationTag;
import graphics.animation.AnimationVariant;
import graphics.animation.zombie.ZombieAnimationTag;
import graphics.animation.zombie.ZombieAnimationVariant;
import graphics.instance.PlantRenderInstance;
import graphics.instance.ZombieRenderInstance;
import java.util.List;
public class IdleSelectionStrategy implements VariantSelectionStrategy {
    @Override
    public AnimationVariant select(List<AnimationVariant> variants, PlantRenderInstance instance) {
        if (isPlantVariantsInvalid(variants)) {
            return null;
        }
        AnimationVariant current = instance.getAnimationController().getCurrentVariant();
        if (current != null && isVariationTag(current.getTag()) && !instance.getAnimationController().isFinished()) {
            return current;
        }
        if (instance.shouldTriggerIdleVariation()) {
            AnimationVariant variation = findPlantVariationVariant(variants);
            instance.resetIdleTimer();
            if (variation != null) {
                return variation;
            }
        }
        return findPlantDefaultVariant(variants);
    }
    private AnimationVariant findPlantVariationVariant(List<AnimationVariant> variants) {
        for (AnimationVariant variant : variants) {
            if (isVariationTag(variant.getTag())) {
                return variant;
            }
        }
        return null;
    }
    private ZombieAnimationVariant findZombieVariationVariant(List<ZombieAnimationVariant> variants) {
        for (ZombieAnimationVariant variant : variants) {
            if (isVariationTag(variant.getTag())) {
                return variant;
            }
        }
        return null;
    }
    private boolean isVariationTag(AnimationTag tag) {
        return tag == AnimationTag.BLINK || tag == AnimationTag.SPECIAL;
    }
    private boolean isVariationTag(ZombieAnimationTag tag) {
        return tag == ZombieAnimationTag.BLINK || tag == ZombieAnimationTag.ABILITY;
    }
    private AnimationVariant findPlantDefaultVariant(List<AnimationVariant> variants) {
        for (AnimationVariant variant : variants) {
            if (variant.getTag() == AnimationTag.DEFAULT) {
                return variant;
            }
        }
        return variants.get(0);
    }
    private ZombieAnimationVariant findZombieDefaultVariant(List<ZombieAnimationVariant> variants) {
        for (ZombieAnimationVariant variant : variants) {
            if (variant.getTag() == ZombieAnimationTag.DEFAULT) {
                return variant;
            }
        }
        return variants.get(0);
    }
    @Override
    public ZombieAnimationVariant select(List<ZombieAnimationVariant> variants, ZombieRenderInstance instance) {
        if(isZombieVariantsInvalid(variants)) {
            return null;
        }
        ZombieAnimationVariant current = instance.getAnimationController().getCurrentVariant();
        if(current != null && isVariationTag(current.getTag()) && !instance.getAnimationController().isFinished()) {
            return current;
        }
        if(instance.shouldTriggerVariation()) {
            ZombieAnimationVariant variation = findZombieVariationVariant(variants);
            instance.resetVariationTimer();
            if(variation != null) {
                return variation;
            }
        }
        return findZombieDefaultVariant(variants);
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\graphics\animation\strategy\SequenceSelectionStrategy.java ---
package graphics.animation.strategy;
import graphics.animation.AnimationVariant;
import graphics.animation.zombie.ZombieAnimationVariant;
import graphics.instance.PlantRenderInstance;
import graphics.instance.ZombieRenderInstance;
import java.util.List;
public class SequenceSelectionStrategy implements VariantSelectionStrategy {
    @Override
    public AnimationVariant select(List<AnimationVariant> variants, PlantRenderInstance instance) {
        if (isPlantVariantsInvalid(variants)) {
            return null;
        }
        int stage = instance.getAnimationStage();
        int safeIndex = Math.min(stage, variants.size() - 1);
        return variants.get(safeIndex);
    }
    @Override
    public ZombieAnimationVariant select(List<ZombieAnimationVariant> variants, ZombieRenderInstance instance) {
        if(isZombieVariantsInvalid(variants)) {
            return null;
        }
        int stage = instance.getAnimationStage();
        int safeIndex = Math.min(stage, variants.size() - 1);
        return variants.get(safeIndex);
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\graphics\animation\strategy\VariantSelectionStrategy.java ---
package graphics.animation.strategy;
import graphics.animation.AnimationVariant;
import graphics.animation.zombie.ZombieAnimationVariant;
import graphics.instance.PlantRenderInstance;
import graphics.instance.ZombieRenderInstance;
import java.util.List;
public interface VariantSelectionStrategy {
    AnimationVariant select(List<AnimationVariant> variants, PlantRenderInstance instance);
    ZombieAnimationVariant select(List<ZombieAnimationVariant> variants, ZombieRenderInstance instance);
    default boolean isPlantVariantsInvalid(List<AnimationVariant> variants) {
        return variants == null || variants.isEmpty();
    }
    default boolean isZombieVariantsInvalid(List<ZombieAnimationVariant> variants) {
        return variants == null || variants.isEmpty();
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\graphics\animation\zombie\ZombieAnimationController.java ---
package graphics.animation.zombie;
import pvz.libpvz.pam.ClipRef;
import java.util.Objects;
public class ZombieAnimationController {
    private ZombieAnimationVariant currentVariant;
    private ClipRef currentClip;
    private String currentPamPath;
    private float stateTime;
    private boolean finished;
    public ZombieAnimationController() {
        this.stateTime = 0.0f;
        this.finished = false;
    }
    public void update(float delta) {
        if (finished) {
            return;
        }
        this.stateTime += delta;
        checkAnimationCompletion();
    }
    public boolean changeVariant(ZombieAnimationVariant newVariant, ClipRef newClip, String pamPath) {
        if (newVariant == null) {
            return false;
        }
        if (isSameVariant(newVariant) && Objects.equals(this.currentClip, newClip)) {
            return false;
        }
        this.currentVariant = newVariant;
        this.currentClip = newClip;
        this.currentPamPath = pamPath;
        this.stateTime = 0.0f;
        this.finished = false;
        return true;
    }
    private void checkAnimationCompletion() {
        if (isNonLoopingFinished()) {
            this.finished = true;
        }
    }
    private boolean isNonLoopingFinished() {
        return currentVariant != null
            && !currentVariant.isLoop()
            && currentClip != null
            && stateTime >= currentClip.duration;
    }
    private boolean isSameVariant(ZombieAnimationVariant newVariant) {
        return currentVariant != null && currentVariant.equals(newVariant);
    }
    public ZombieAnimationVariant getCurrentVariant() {
        return currentVariant;
    }
    public ClipRef getCurrentClip() {
        return currentClip;
    }
    public String getCurrentPamPath() {
        return currentPamPath;
    }
    public float getStateTime() {
        return stateTime;
    }
    public boolean isFinished() {
        return finished;
    }
    public void setFinished(boolean finished) {
        this.finished = finished;
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\graphics\animation\zombie\ZombieAnimationProfile.java ---
package graphics.animation.zombie;
import graphics.state.ZombieVisualState;
import java.util.*;
public class ZombieAnimationProfile {
    private final String alias;
    private final String pamPath;
    private final Map<ZombieVisualState, List<ZombieAnimationVariant>> variantsMap;
    public ZombieAnimationProfile(String alias, String pamPath) {
        this.alias = alias;
        this.pamPath = pamPath;
        this.variantsMap = new EnumMap<>(ZombieVisualState.class);
    }
    public void addVariant(ZombieVisualState state, ZombieAnimationVariant variant) {
        variantsMap.computeIfAbsent(state, z -> new ArrayList<>()).add(variant);
    }
    public List<ZombieAnimationVariant> getVariants(ZombieVisualState state) {
        return variantsMap.getOrDefault(state, Collections.emptyList());
    }
    public Map<ZombieVisualState, List<ZombieAnimationVariant>> getAllVariantsMap() {
        return Collections.unmodifiableMap(variantsMap);
    }
    public String getAlias() {
        return alias;
    }
    public String getPamPath() {
        return pamPath;
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\graphics\animation\zombie\ZombieAnimationSelector.java ---
package graphics.animation.zombie;
import graphics.animation.strategy.FirstSelectionStrategy;
import graphics.animation.strategy.VariantSelectionStrategy;
import graphics.instance.ZombieRenderInstance;
import graphics.state.ZombieVisualState;
import java.util.EnumMap;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;
public class ZombieAnimationSelector {
    private final Map<ZombieVisualState, VariantSelectionStrategy> strategyRegistry;
    private final VariantSelectionStrategy defaultStrategy;
    public ZombieAnimationSelector() {
        this.strategyRegistry = new EnumMap<>(ZombieVisualState.class);
        this.defaultStrategy = new FirstSelectionStrategy();
        registerDefaults();
    }
    public void registerStrategy(ZombieVisualState state, VariantSelectionStrategy strategy) {
        strategyRegistry.put(state, strategy);
    }
    public ZombieAnimationVariant selectVariant(ZombieAnimationProfile profile, ZombieRenderInstance instance, ZombieVisualState state) {
        List<ZombieAnimationVariant> variants = profile.getVariants(state);
        if(variants.isEmpty()) {
            return null;
        }
        VariantSelectionStrategy strategy = strategyRegistry.getOrDefault(state, defaultStrategy);
        return strategy.select(variants, instance);
    }
    private void registerDefaults() {
        strategyRegistry.put(ZombieVisualState.IDLE, new FirstSelectionStrategy());
        strategyRegistry.put(ZombieVisualState.WALK, new FirstSelectionStrategy());
        strategyRegistry.put(ZombieVisualState.ATTACK, new FirstSelectionStrategy());
        strategyRegistry.put(ZombieVisualState.DEATH, new FirstSelectionStrategy());
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\graphics\animation\zombie\ZombieAnimationVariant.java ---
package graphics.animation.zombie;
import game.entity.zombie.Zombie;
import graphics.animation.condition.ZombieAnimationCondition;
import java.util.Objects;
public class ZombieAnimationVariant {
    private final String clipName;
    private final boolean loop;
    private final int priority;
    private final ZombieAnimationTag tag;
    private final ZombieAnimationCondition condition;
    public ZombieAnimationVariant(String clipName, boolean loop, int priority, ZombieAnimationTag tag, ZombieAnimationCondition condition) {
        this.clipName = clipName;
        this.loop = loop;
        this.priority = priority;
        this.tag = tag;
        this.condition = condition;
    }
    public ZombieAnimationVariant(String clipName, boolean loop, int priority, ZombieAnimationTag tag) {
        this(clipName, loop, priority, tag, zombie -> true);
    }
    public String getClipName() {
        return clipName;
    }
    public boolean isLoop() {
        return loop;
    }
    public int getPriority() {
        return priority;
    }
    public ZombieAnimationTag getTag() {
        return tag;
    }
    public boolean matchesCondition(Zombie zombie) {
        return condition == null || condition.matches(zombie);
    }
    @Override
    public boolean equals(Object obj) {
        if(this == obj) return true;
        if(obj == null || obj.getClass() != getClass()) {
            return false;
        }
        ZombieAnimationVariant that = (ZombieAnimationVariant) obj;
        return clipName.equals(that.clipName);
    }
    @Override
    public int hashCode() {
        return Objects.hash(clipName);
    }
}

public record ZombieClipKey(String alias, String clipName, String pamPath) {

    public boolean equals(Object obj) {
        if(this == obj) return true;
        if(obj == null || getClass() != obj.getClass()) return false;
        ZombieClipKey clipKey = (ZombieClipKey) obj;
        return  alias.equals(clipKey.alias) &&
                Objects.equals(clipName, clipKey.clipName) &&

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\graphics\instance\PlantRenderInstance.java ---
package graphics.instance;
import game.entity.plant.Plant;
import graphics.animation.AnimationController;
import graphics.event.PlantVisualEvent;
import graphics.state.PlantVisualState;
import java.util.ArrayDeque;
import java.util.Queue;
public class PlantRenderInstance {
    private final Plant plant;
    private final AnimationController animationController;
    private final VisibilityController visibilityController;
    private final Queue<PlantVisualEvent> eventQueue;
    private PlantVisualState currentVisualState;
    private int animationStage;
    private float idleTimer;
    private float nextIdleInterval;
    public PlantRenderInstance(Plant plant) {
        this.plant = plant;
        this.animationController = new AnimationController();
        this.visibilityController = new VisibilityController();
        this.eventQueue = new ArrayDeque<>();
        this.currentVisualState = PlantVisualState.IDLE;
        this.animationStage = 0;
        this.idleTimer = 0.0f;
        this.nextIdleInterval = generateRandomIdleInterval();
    }
    public void update(float delta) {
        animationController.update(delta);
        idleTimer += delta;
    }
    public boolean shouldTriggerIdleVariation() {
        return idleTimer >= nextIdleInterval;
    }
    public void resetIdleTimer() {
        this.idleTimer = 0.0f;
        this.nextIdleInterval = generateRandomIdleInterval();
    }
    private float generateRandomIdleInterval() {
        return 4.0f + (float) (Math.random() * 4.0f);
    }
    public void pushEvent(PlantVisualEvent event) {
        eventQueue.add(event);
    }
    public PlantVisualEvent pollEvent() {
        return eventQueue.poll();
    }
    public Plant getPlant() {
        return plant;
    }
    public AnimationController getAnimationController() {
        return animationController;
    }
    public VisibilityController getVisibilityController() {
        return visibilityController;
    }
    public PlantVisualState getCurrentVisualState() {
        return currentVisualState;
    }
    public void setCurrentVisualState(PlantVisualState currentVisualState) {
        this.currentVisualState = currentVisualState;
    }
    public int getAnimationStage() {
        return animationStage;
    }
    public void setAnimationStage(int animationStage) {
        this.animationStage = animationStage;
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\graphics\map\hover\CellHoverRenderer.java ---
package graphics.map.hover;
import com.badlogic.gdx.Gdx;
import com.badlogic.gdx.graphics.Color;
import com.badlogic.gdx.graphics.GL20;
import com.badlogic.gdx.graphics.OrthographicCamera;
import com.badlogic.gdx.graphics.glutils.ShapeRenderer;
import game.world.playground.Cell;
import game.world.playground.Grid;
import game.world.playground.MapConfig;
public class CellHoverRenderer {
    private static final float DEFAULT_ROW_ALPHA = 0.16f;
    private static final float DEFAULT_COLUMN_ALPHA = 0.16f;
    private static final float DEFAULT_CELL_ALPHA = 0.30f;
    private static final float DEFAULT_ERROR_ROW_ALPHA = 0.12f;
    private static final float DEFAULT_ERROR_COLUMN_ALPHA = 0.12f;
    private static final float DEFAULT_ERROR_CELL_ALPHA = 0.28f;
    private static final float DEFAULT_INSET_RATIO = 0.90f;
    private static final float DEFAULT_PULSE_SPEED = 5.5f;
    private static final float DEFAULT_PULSE_AMOUNT = 0.08f;
    private final ShapeRenderer shapeRenderer;
    private Color highlightColor = Color.WHITE.cpy();
    private Color invalidColor = new Color(1f, 0.18f, 0.18f, 1f);
    private float rowAlpha = DEFAULT_ROW_ALPHA;
    private float columnAlpha = DEFAULT_COLUMN_ALPHA;
    private float cellAlpha = DEFAULT_CELL_ALPHA;
    private float invalidRowAlpha = DEFAULT_ERROR_ROW_ALPHA;
    private float invalidColumnAlpha = DEFAULT_ERROR_COLUMN_ALPHA;
    private float invalidCellAlpha = DEFAULT_ERROR_CELL_ALPHA;
    private float insetRatio = DEFAULT_INSET_RATIO;
    private float pulseSpeed = DEFAULT_PULSE_SPEED;
    private float pulseAmount = DEFAULT_PULSE_AMOUNT;
    private boolean highlightRow = true;
    private boolean highlightColumn = true;
    private boolean emphasizeHoveredCell = true;
    private boolean pulsing = true;
    private float time;
    public CellHoverRenderer() {
        shapeRenderer = new ShapeRenderer();
    }
    public void render(
        OrthographicCamera camera,
        Grid grid,
        Cell hoveredCell,
        boolean canPlant,
        float delta
    ) {
        if (camera == null || grid == null || hoveredCell == null) {
            return;
        }
        time += delta;
        MapConfig config = grid.getConfig();
        float cellWidth = config.getCellWidth();
        float cellHeight = config.getCellHeight();
        float pulse = getPulse();
        Color color = canPlant ? highlightColor : invalidColor;
        Gdx.gl.glEnable(GL20.GL_BLEND);
        Gdx.gl.glBlendFunc(
            GL20.GL_SRC_ALPHA,
            GL20.GL_ONE_MINUS_SRC_ALPHA
        );
        shapeRenderer.setProjectionMatrix(camera.combined);
        shapeRenderer.begin(ShapeRenderer.ShapeType.Filled);
        renderRow(
            grid,
            hoveredCell,
            cellWidth,
            cellHeight,
            color,
            canPlant,
            pulse
        );
        renderColumn(
            grid,
            hoveredCell,
            cellWidth,
            cellHeight,
            color,
            canPlant,
            pulse
        );
        renderHoveredCell(
            hoveredCell,
            cellWidth,
            cellHeight,
            color,
            canPlant,
            pulse
        );
        shapeRenderer.end();
        Gdx.gl.glDisable(GL20.GL_BLEND);
    }
    private void renderRow(
        Grid grid,
        Cell hoveredCell,
        float width,
        float height,
        Color color,
        boolean valid,
        float pulse
    ) {
        if (!highlightRow) {
            return;
        }
        float alpha = valid ? rowAlpha : invalidRowAlpha;
        alpha *= pulse;
        shapeRenderer.setColor(
            color.r,
            color.g,
            color.b,
            alpha
        );
        int row = hoveredCell.getRow();
        int targetColumn = hoveredCell.getCol();
        for (int col = 0; col < grid.getColCount(); col++) {
            if (col == targetColumn) {
                continue;
            }
            Cell cell = grid.getCell(row, col);
            if (cell != null) {
                drawCell(cell, width, height);
            }
        }
    }
    private void renderColumn(
        Grid grid,
        Cell hoveredCell,
        float width,
        float height,
        Color color,
        boolean valid,
        float pulse
    ) {
        if (!highlightColumn) {
            return;
        }
        float alpha = valid ? columnAlpha : invalidColumnAlpha;
        alpha *= pulse;
        shapeRenderer.setColor(
            color.r,
            color.g,
            color.b,
            alpha
        );
        int column = hoveredCell.getCol();
        int targetRow = hoveredCell.getRow();
        for (int row = 0; row < grid.getRowCount(); row++) {
            if (row == targetRow) {
                continue;
            }
            Cell cell = grid.getCell(row, column);
            if (cell != null) {
                drawCell(cell, width, height);
            }
        }
    }
    private void renderHoveredCell(
        Cell cell,
        float width,
        float height,
        Color color,
        boolean valid,
        float pulse
    ) {
        if (!emphasizeHoveredCell) {
            return;
        }
        float alpha = valid ? cellAlpha : invalidCellAlpha;
        alpha *= pulse;
        shapeRenderer.setColor(
            color.r,
            color.g,
            color.b,
            alpha
        );
        drawCell(cell, width, height);
    }
    private void drawCell(Cell cell, float width, float height) {
        float drawWidth = width * insetRatio;
        float drawHeight = height * insetRatio;
        float x = cell.getWorldX() - drawWidth / 2f;
        float y = cell.getWorldY() - drawHeight / 2f;
        shapeRenderer.rect(
            x,
            y,
            drawWidth,
            drawHeight
        );
    }
    private float getPulse() {
        if (!pulsing) {
            return 1f;
        }
        return 1f + pulseAmount *
            (float) Math.sin(time * pulseSpeed);
    }
    public void setHighlightColor(Color color) {
        if (color != null) {
            highlightColor.set(color);
        }
    }
    public void setInvalidColor(Color color) {
        if (color != null) {
            invalidColor.set(color);
        }
    }
    public void setRowAlpha(float alpha) {
        rowAlpha = clampAlpha(alpha);
    }
    public void setColumnAlpha(float alpha) {
        columnAlpha = clampAlpha(alpha);
    }
    public void setCellAlpha(float alpha) {
        cellAlpha = clampAlpha(alpha);
    }
    public void setInvalidRowAlpha(float alpha) {
        invalidRowAlpha = clampAlpha(alpha);
    }
    public void setInvalidColumnAlpha(float alpha) {
        invalidColumnAlpha = clampAlpha(alpha);
    }
    public void setInvalidCellAlpha(float alpha) {
        invalidCellAlpha = clampAlpha(alpha);
    }
    public void setInsetRatio(float ratio) {
        insetRatio = Math.max(0f, Math.min(1f, ratio));
    }
    public void setPulseSpeed(float speed) {
        pulseSpeed = Math.max(0f, speed);
    }
    public void setPulseAmount(float amount) {
        pulseAmount = Math.max(0f, amount);
    }
    public void setHighlightRow(boolean enabled) {
        highlightRow = enabled;
    }
    public void setHighlightColumn(boolean enabled) {
        highlightColumn = enabled;
    }
    public void setEmphasizeHoveredCell(boolean enabled) {
        emphasizeHoveredCell = enabled;
    }
    public void setPulsing(boolean enabled) {
        pulsing = enabled;
    }
    private float clampAlpha(float alpha) {
        return Math.max(0f, Math.min(1f, alpha));
    }
    public void resetPulse() {
        time = 0f;
    }
    public void dispose() {
        shapeRenderer.dispose();
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\graphics\renderer\PlantRenderer.java ---
package graphics.renderer;
import com.badlogic.gdx.graphics.g2d.SpriteBatch;
import graphics.animation.AnimationController;
import graphics.animation.AnimationVariant;
import graphics.instance.PlantRenderInstance;
import pvz.libpvz.pam.ClipRef;
import pvz.libpvz.pam.PamPlayer;
public class PlantRenderer {
    private final PamPlayer pamPlayer;
    public PlantRenderer(PamPlayer pamPlayer) {
        this.pamPlayer = pamPlayer;
    }
    public void render(SpriteBatch batch, PlantRenderInstance instance) {
        AnimationController controller = instance.getAnimationController();
        ClipRef clip = controller.getCurrentClip();
        AnimationVariant variant = controller.getCurrentVariant();
        String pamPath = controller.getCurrentPamPath();
        if (clip == null || variant == null || pamPath == null) {
            return;
        }
        float x = (float) instance.getPlant().getPosition().getX();
      float y = (float) instance.getPlant().getPosition().getY();
        pamPlayer.draw(
            batch,
            pamPath,
            variant.getClipName(),
            controller.getStateTime(),
            x,
            y,
            true
        );
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\graphics\resolver\EventStateTransitionEvaluator.java ---
package graphics.resolver;
import graphics.event.PlantVisualEvent;
import graphics.event.PlantVisualEventType;
import graphics.event.ZombieVisualEvent;
import graphics.instance.PlantRenderInstance;
import graphics.instance.ZombieRenderInstance;
import graphics.state.PlantVisualState;
import graphics.state.ZombieVisualState;
public class EventStateTransitionEvaluator {
    public PlantVisualState evaluateNextEventState(PlantRenderInstance instance) {
        PlantVisualEvent event = instance.pollEvent();
        if (event == null) {
            return null;
        }
        return switch (event.getType()) {
            case DIED -> PlantVisualState.DEATH;
            case PLANT_FOOD_ACTIVATED -> PlantVisualState.PLANT_FOOD;
            case ATTACK_TRIGGERED -> PlantVisualState.ATTACK;
            case DAMAGE_TAKEN -> PlantVisualState.HURT;
            case SLEPT -> PlantVisualState.SLEEP;
            case FROZEN -> PlantVisualState.FROZEN;
            default -> null;
        };
    }
    public ZombieVisualState evaluateNextEventState(ZombieRenderInstance instance) {
        ZombieVisualEvent event = instance.pollEvent();
        if(event == null) {
            return null;
        }
        return switch (event.getType()) {
            case DIED -> ZombieVisualState.DEATH;
            case BOMBED -> ZombieVisualState.BOMBED;
            case DAMAGE_TAKEN -> ZombieVisualState.HURT;
            default -> null;
        };
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\graphics\resolver\PlantVisualStateResolver.java ---
package graphics.resolver;
import game.entity.plant.Plant;
import graphics.instance.PlantRenderInstance;
import graphics.state.PlantVisualState;
public class PlantVisualStateResolver {
    private final StatusConditionEvaluator conditionEvaluator;
    private final EventStateTransitionEvaluator eventEvaluator;
    public PlantVisualStateResolver() {
        this.conditionEvaluator = new StatusConditionEvaluator();
        this.eventEvaluator = new EventStateTransitionEvaluator();
    }
    public PlantVisualState resolve(Plant plant, PlantRenderInstance instance, PlantVisualState currentState, boolean isAnimationFinished) {
        PlantVisualState forcedConditionState = conditionEvaluator.evaluateEntityConditions(plant);
        if (forcedConditionState != null) {
            return forcedConditionState;
        }
        PlantVisualState eventState = eventEvaluator.evaluateNextEventState(instance);
        if (eventState != null) {
            return eventState;
        }
        if (isAnimationFinished && currentState.isActionState()) {
            return PlantVisualState.IDLE;
        }
        return currentState;
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\graphics\resolver\StatusConditionEvaluator.java ---
package graphics.resolver;
import game.entity.plant.Plant;
import game.entity.zombie.Zombie;
import graphics.state.PlantVisualState;
import graphics.state.ZombieVisualState;
public class StatusConditionEvaluator {
    public PlantVisualState evaluateEntityConditions(Plant plant) {
        if (plant == null) {
            return null;
        }
        if (plant.getHealth() != null && plant.getHealth().isDead()) {
            return PlantVisualState.DEATH;
        }
        if (plant.isFrozen()) {
            return PlantVisualState.FROZEN;
        }
        if (plant.isSleeping()) {
            return PlantVisualState.SLEEP;
        }
        return null;
    }
    public ZombieVisualState evaluateEntityConditions(Zombie zombie) {
        if(zombie == null) {
            return null;
        }
        if(zombie.isDead()) {
            return ZombieVisualState.DEATH;
        }
        if(zombie.isAttacking()) {
            return ZombieVisualState.ATTACK;
        }
        if(zombie.isWalking()) {
            return ZombieVisualState.WALK;
        }
        return null;
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\graphics\resolver\ZombieVisualStateResolver.java ---
package graphics.resolver;
import game.entity.zombie.Zombie;
import graphics.instance.ZombieRenderInstance;
import graphics.state.ZombieVisualState;
public class ZombieVisualStateResolver {
    private final StatusConditionEvaluator conditionEvaluator;
    private final EventStateTransitionEvaluator eventEvaluator;
    public ZombieVisualStateResolver() {
        this.conditionEvaluator = new StatusConditionEvaluator();
        this.eventEvaluator = new EventStateTransitionEvaluator();
    }
    public ZombieVisualState resolve(Zombie zombie, ZombieRenderInstance instance, ZombieVisualState currentState, boolean isAnimationFinished) {
        ZombieVisualState forcedConditionState = conditionEvaluator.evaluateEntityConditions(zombie);
        if(forcedConditionState != null) {
            return forcedConditionState;
        }
        ZombieVisualState eventState = eventEvaluator.evaluateNextEventState(instance);
        if(eventState != null) {
            return eventState;
        }
        if(isAnimationFinished && currentState.isActionState()) {
            return ZombieVisualState.WALK;
        }
        return currentState;
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\graphics\state\PlantVisualState.java ---
package graphics.state;
public enum PlantVisualState {
    IDLE,
    ATTACK,
    HURT,
    PLANT_FOOD,
    DEATH,
    SLEEP,
    FROZEN;
    public boolean isInterruptible() {
        return this == IDLE || this == SLEEP;
    }
    public boolean isActionState() {
        return this == ATTACK || this == PLANT_FOOD;
    }
}

--- FILE: .\core\src\main\java\graphics\state\ZombieVisualState.java ---
package graphics.state;
public enum ZombieVisualState {
    WALK,
    ATTACK,
    HURT,
    DEATH,
    FROZEN,
    BURNING,
    IDLE,
    BOMBED,
    ;
    public boolean isInterruptible() {
        return this == IDLE;
    }
    public boolean isActionState() {
        return this == ATTACK || this == DEATH;
    }
}

package plants_vs_zombies;
import app.view.AppLoop;
import com.badlogic.gdx.Game;
import com.badlogic.gdx.graphics.g2d.SpriteBatch;
public class PlantsVSZombies extends Game {
    public SpriteBatch batch;
    private static PlantsVSZombies pvz;
    @Override
    public void create() {
        batch = new SpriteBatch();
        pvz = this;
        AppLoop.init();
    }
    @Override
    public void render() {
        AppLoop.update();
        super.render();
    }
    @Override
    public void dispose() {
        super.dispose();
        batch.dispose();
    }
    public static PlantsVSZombies getPvz() {return pvz;}
}

--- FILE: .\lwjgl3\src\main\java\com\plants_vs_zombies\lwjgl3\Lwjgl3Launcher.java ---
package com.plants_vs_zombies.lwjgl3;
import com.badlogic.gdx.backends.lwjgl3.Lwjgl3Application;
import com.badlogic.gdx.backends.lwjgl3.Lwjgl3ApplicationConfiguration;
import plants_vs_zombies.PlantsVSZombies;
public class Lwjgl3Launcher {
    public static void main(String[] args) {
        if (StartupHelper.startNewJvmIfRequired()) return;
        createApplication();
    }
    private static Lwjgl3Application createApplication() {
        return new Lwjgl3Application(new PlantsVSZombies(), getDefaultConfiguration());
    }
    private static Lwjgl3ApplicationConfiguration getDefaultConfiguration() {
        Lwjgl3ApplicationConfiguration configuration = new Lwjgl3ApplicationConfiguration();
        configuration.setTitle("Plants VS Zombies");
        configuration.useVsync(true);
        configuration.setForegroundFPS(Lwjgl3ApplicationConfiguration.getDisplayMode().refreshRate + 1);
        configuration.setWindowedMode(640, 480);
        configuration.setWindowIcon("libgdx128.png", "libgdx64.png", "libgdx32.png", "libgdx16.png");
        return configuration;
    }
}

--- FILE: .\lwjgl3\src\main\java\com\plants_vs_zombies\lwjgl3\StartupHelper.java ---
package com.plants_vs_zombies.lwjgl3;
import com.badlogic.gdx.Version;
import com.badlogic.gdx.backends.lwjgl3.Lwjgl3NativesLoader;
import org.lwjgl.system.macosx.LibC;
import org.lwjgl.system.macosx.ObjCRuntime;
import java.io.BufferedReader;
import java.io.File;
import java.io.InputStreamReader;
import java.lang.management.ManagementFactory;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import static org.lwjgl.system.JNI.invokePPP;
import static org.lwjgl.system.JNI.invokePPZ;
import static org.lwjgl.system.macosx.ObjCRuntime.objc_getClass;
import static org.lwjgl.system.macosx.ObjCRuntime.sel_getUid;
public class StartupHelper {
    private static final String JVM_RESTARTED_ARG = "jvmIsRestarted";
    private StartupHelper() {
        throw new UnsupportedOperationException();
    }
    public static boolean startNewJvmIfRequired(boolean redirectOutput) {
        String osName = System.getProperty("os.name").toLowerCase();
        if (!osName.contains("mac")) {
            if (osName.contains("windows")) {
                String programData = System.getenv("ProgramData");
                if(programData == null) programData = "C:\\Temp\\";
                String prevTmpDir = System.getProperty("java.io.tmpdir", programData);
                String prevUser = System.getProperty("user.name", "libGDX_User");
                System.setProperty("java.io.tmpdir", programData + "/libGDX-temp");
                System.setProperty("user.name", ("User_" + prevUser.hashCode() + "_GDX" + Version.VERSION).replace('.', '_'));
                Lwjgl3NativesLoader.load();
                System.setProperty("java.io.tmpdir", prevTmpDir);
                System.setProperty("user.name", prevUser);
            }
            return false;
        }
        if (!System.getProperty("org.graalvm.nativeimage.imagecode", "").isEmpty()) {
            return false;
        }
        long objc_msgSend = ObjCRuntime.getLibrary().getFunctionAddress("objc_msgSend");
        long NSThread      = objc_getClass("NSThread");
        long currentThread = invokePPP(NSThread, sel_getUid("currentThread"), objc_msgSend);
        boolean isMainThread = invokePPZ(currentThread, sel_getUid("isMainThread"), objc_msgSend);
        if(isMainThread) return false;
        long pid = LibC.getpid();
        if ("1".equals(System.getenv("JAVA_STARTED_ON_FIRST_THREAD_" + pid))) {
            return false;
        }
        if ("true".equals(System.getProperty(JVM_RESTARTED_ARG))) {
            System.err.println(
                    "There was a problem evaluating whether the JVM was started with the -XstartOnFirstThread argument.");
            return false;
        }
        ArrayList<String> jvmArgs = new ArrayList<>();
        String separator = System.getProperty("file.separator", "/");
        String javaExecPath = System.getProperty("java.home") + separator + "bin" + separator + "java";
        if (!(new File(javaExecPath)).exists()) {
            System.err.println(
                    "A Java installation could not be found. If you are distributing this app with a bundled JRE, be sure to set the -XstartOnFirstThread argument manually!");
            return false;
        }
        jvmArgs.add(javaExecPath);
        jvmArgs.add("-XstartOnFirstThread");
        jvmArgs.add("-D" + JVM_RESTARTED_ARG + "=true");
        jvmArgs.addAll(ManagementFactory.getRuntimeMXBean().getInputArguments());
        jvmArgs.add("-cp");
        jvmArgs.add(System.getProperty("java.class.path"));
        String mainClass = System.getenv("JAVA_MAIN_CLASS_" + pid);
        if (mainClass == null) {
            StackTraceElement[] trace = Thread.currentThread().getStackTrace();
            if (trace.length > 0) {
                mainClass = trace[trace.length - 1].getClassName();
            } else {
                System.err.println("The main class could not be determined.");
                return false;
            }
        }
        jvmArgs.add(mainClass);
        try {
            if (!redirectOutput) {
                ProcessBuilder processBuilder = new ProcessBuilder(jvmArgs);
                processBuilder.start();
            } else {
                Process process = (new ProcessBuilder(jvmArgs))
                        .redirectErrorStream(true).start();
                BufferedReader processOutput = new BufferedReader(
                        new InputStreamReader(process.getInputStream()));
                String line;
                while ((line = processOutput.readLine()) != null) {
                    System.out.println(line);
                }
                process.waitFor();
            }
        } catch (Exception e) {
            System.err.println("There was a problem restarting the JVM");
            e.printStackTrace();
        }
        return true;
    }
    public static boolean startNewJvmIfRequired() {
        return startNewJvmIfRequired(true);
    }
}
